const knownRoles = ['Chatnext AI User', 'Funnel Admin', 'Funnel Manager']

const knownFixtures = {
    custom_field: knownCustomFields,
    funnel_node: knownFunnelNodes,
    role: knownRoles,
}

try {
    const knownFixtureFiles = Object.keys(knownFixtures)
    for (const fixtureFile of knownFixtureFiles) {
        const fixturePath = path.join(fixturesPath, `${fixtureFile}.json`)
        const fixture = JSON.parse(readFileSync(fixturePath, 'utf8'))
        console.log(`Total ${fixtureFile}s: ${fixture.length}`)

        const fields = []
        const repeatedFields = []
        const unknownFields = []
        const missingFields = []
        for (const field of fixture) {
            if (!fields.includes(field.name)) {
                fields.push(field.name)
            } else {
                repeatedFields.push(field.name)
            }
            if (!knownFixtures[fixtureFile].includes(field.name)) {
                unknownFields.push(field.name)
            }
        }

        for (const field of knownFixtures[fixtureFile])
            if (!fields.includes(field)) missingFields.push(field)

        if (repeatedFields.length > 0) {
            throw new Error(`Repeated ${fixtureFile}s: ${repeatedFields.join(', ')}`)
        }

        if (unknownFields.length > 0) {
            throw new Error(`Unknown ${fixtureFile}s: ${unknownFields.join(', ')}`)
        }

        if (missingFields.length > 0) {
            throw new Error(`Missing ${fixtureFile}s: ${missingFields.join(', ')}`)
        }
    }
} catch (e) {
    console.error(e)
    process.exit(1)
}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/agent_api.py
import json

import frappe
import requests

from nextai.config.api import API_URL
from nextai.utils import create_response


@frappe.whitelist(allow_guest=True)
def get_agent_response(**kwargs):
    try:
        kwargs.pop('cmd')
        if not kwargs.get('site_url'): return create_response("Error", "Please Specify Site URL")
        session_id = kwargs.get('session_id')
        chat_history = get_user_chat_history(session_id)
        route_data = get_route_data(kwargs.get("route_path"))
        kwargs['chat_history'] = chat_history
        kwargs['route_data'] = route_data
        payload = json.dumps(kwargs)
        url = f"{API_URL}/api/method/chatnext_core.api.get_message"
        headers = {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        }
        response = requests.request("POST", url, headers=headers, data=payload)
        frappe.logger('utils').exception(response.json())
        return response.json().get('message')
    except Exception as e:
        frappe.logger('utils').exception(e)
        return e


def get_user_chat_history(session_id):
    return frappe.db.get_list('Chatnext Conversation', {'session': session_id}, ['sender', 'message'],
                              order_by='creation', ignore_permissions=True)


def get_route_data(route_path):
    if not route_path: return {}
    # Route Path Will be in this format => ['Form', 'DocType', 'CP2P Settings']
    if route_path[0] == 'Form':
        return {route_path[1]: route_path[2]}
    elif route_path[0] == 'Workspaces':
        return {route_path[0]: route_path[1]}
    else:
        # TODO: Extract Report Name etc
        return {}

import frappe


@frappe.whitelist()
def link_doctype_with_contact(doc_type, doc_name, contact):
    # print("link_doctype_with_contact", doc_type, doc_name, contact)
    contact = frappe.get_doc("Contact", contact)
    contact.append("links", {"link_doctype": doc_type, "link_name": doc_name})
    contact.save()
    return True

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/api/chatnext/form_conversation.py
import frappe


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_all_from_doc(doctype: str, docname: str):
    permitted_docs = frappe.get_list(doctype, filters={"name": docname})
    if not permitted_docs: return []
    reference_doc = f"{doctype}::{docname}"
    cna_conversations = frappe.get_all(
        "CNA Conversation", filters={
            "reference_doc": reference_doc, 'message_type': "form", 'status': "Submitted"
        }, fields=['user', 'message', 'creation', 'modified']
    )
    return cna_conversations

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/api/communication.py
import ast
import frappe
import json


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_communications(
    email_ids, limit_start=0, limit_page_length=25, order_by="creation DESC"
):
    or_filters = []
    email_ids = email_ids if isinstance(email_ids, list) else json.loads(email_ids)
    for email_id in email_ids:
        or_filters.append(["Communication", "sender", "=", email_id])
        or_filters.append(["Communication", "recipients", "=", email_id])

    communications = frappe.get_all(
        "Communication",
        fields="*",
        or_filters=or_filters,
        order_by=order_by,
        limit_start=limit_start,
        limit_page_length=limit_page_length,
    )

    for communication in communications:
        files = frappe.get_all(
            "File",
            fields="*",
            filters=[
                ["attached_to_doctype", "=", "Communication"],
                ["attached_to_name", "=", communication.name],
            ],
        )
        communication["attachments"] = files

    return communications

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/api/doctype_meta.py
import frappe

minimal_fields_keys = ['fieldname', 'fieldtype', 'label', 'description', 'options', 'reqd', 'is_virtual', 'hidden', 'in_list_view', 'read_only', 'set_only_once']


def pick_fields(obj: dict, fields: list[str]):
    return {f: obj[f] for f in fields if f in obj}


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_meta(doctype):
    return frappe.get_meta(doctype)


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_fields(
    doctype, docname=None,
    field_name_only: int | bool = False,
    include_breaks: int | bool = True,
    all_fields: int | bool = False,
    detailed: int | bool = False,
    field_keys=None,
):
    if field_keys is None:
        field_keys = minimal_fields_keys
    if docname and not frappe.db.exists(doctype, docname):
        return None
    meta = frappe.get_doc(doctype, docname).meta if docname else frappe.get_meta(doctype)
    fields = meta.fields
    table_fields = [f for f in fields if f.fieldtype.startswith("Table")]
    table_fields = [f for f in table_fields if f.permlevel != 1000]
    for f in table_fields:
        if f.permlevel == 1001:
            f.read_only = True
    if not all_fields:
        permitted_fields = meta.get_permitted_fieldnames()
        fields = [f for f in fields if f.fieldname in permitted_fields or f.fieldtype.endswith("Break")]
    if not include_breaks:
        fields = [f for f in fields if not f.fieldtype.endswith("Break")]
    if field_name_only:
        fields = [f.fieldname for f in fields]
    else:
        if "read_only" in field_keys:
            writable_fields = meta.get_permitted_fieldnames(permission_type='write')
            for f in fields:
                if f.fieldname in writable_fields:
                    f.read_only = False
                else:
                    f.read_only = True
        if not detailed:
            fields = [pick_fields(f.as_dict(), field_keys) for f in fields]
    return fields + table_fields

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/api/download.py
import frappe
from frappe.utils.pdf import get_pdf


@frappe.whitelist()
def download_pdf(**kwargs):
    try:
        args = frappe._dict(kwargs)

        doc = frappe.get_doc(args.docType, args.name, filters=args.filters)
        html = frappe.get_print(args.docType, args.name, None, doc=doc, no_letterhead="no_letterhead")
        pdf_file = get_pdf(html)
        frappe.local.response.fileContent = pdf_file
        frappe.local.response.filename = f"{args.name}.pdf"
        frappe.local.response.responseType = "download"
    except Exception as e:
        print("Exception Occurred", e)
        frappe.logger('utils').exception(e)
        return e

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/api/fix_contact_message_order.py
import frappe


@frappe.whitelist()
def fix_up():
    values = {}
    frappe.db.sql("""
             UPDATE    tabContact c
                SET       custom_last_message_interaction = (
                  select creation from `tabWhatsApp Message` w
                                  where w.`to` = c.name or w.`from` = c.name
                                  order by creation desc limit 1
                  );
            """, values=values, as_dict=1)
    frappe.db.sql("""
               UPDATE    tabContact c
                    SET       custom_last_message_interaction = '1999-01-01 21:39:15.000000'
                 where custom_last_message_interaction is null;

            """, values=values, as_dict=1)
    return "done"

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/api/get_doc/get_lead_contact.py
import frappe


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_contact(lead_name):
    try:
        contacts = frappe.get_all(doctype="Contact", filters={'link_doctype': 'Lead', 'link_name': lead_name}, fields=["whatsapp_id", "name"])
        if len(contacts) > 0:
            contact = contacts[0]
            doc = frappe.get_doc("Contact", contact.name)

            primary_email = None
            for email in doc.get("email_ids"):
                if email.get("is_primary"):
                    primary_email = email.get("email_id")
                    break
            resp_data = {
                'whatsapp_id': doc.get("whatsapp_id"),
                'name': contact.name,
                'email_id': primary_email
            }

            return resp_data
    except Exception as e:
        print("Error:", e)
    return None

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/api/get_doc_fields_list.py
import frappe

not_allowed_fields = [
    "Section Break",
    "Column Break",
    "Page Break",
    "Tab Break",
]


# nextai.api.get_doc_fields_list.get_doc_fields_list
@frappe.whitelist()
def get_doc_fields_list(doctype, include_breaks=False):
    meta_data = frappe.get_meta(doctype)
    fields = meta_data.get("fields")

    all_fields = []

    if fields:
        for field in fields:
            field_name = field.get("fieldname")
            field_type = field.get("fieldtype")
            label = field.get("label")

            if include_breaks or field_type not in not_allowed_fields:
                all_fields.append(
                    {"label": label, "value": field_name, "type": field_type}
                )

    return all_fields

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/api/get_print_formats.py
import frappe


@frappe.whitelist()
# nextai.api.get_print_formats.get_print_formats
def get_print_formats(doctype):
    values = {
        'doctype': doctype,
    }
    formats = frappe.db.sql("""
        SELECT *
        FROM `tabPrint Format`
        WHERE `doc_type` = %(doctype)s
            and "disabled" = 0
    """, values=values, as_dict=1)
    return ['standard'] + [n.get('name') for n in formats]

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/api/get_whatsapp.py
import frappe

@frappe.whitelist()
def get_whatsapp(docname,doctype="Lead"):
    values = {
        'doctype': doctype,
        'docname': docname
    }
    contacts = frappe.db.sql("""
       select whatsapp_id
        from tabContact
        where name in (select parent
                       from `tabDynamic Link`
                       where link_doctype = %(doctype)s
                         and link_name = %(docname)s
                         and parenttype = 'Contact')
          and whatsapp_id is not null
        order by tabContact.creation desc
        limit 1
    """, values=values, as_dict=1)
    if not len(contacts): return None
    return contacts[0].get('whatsapp_id')

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/api/webhook/personal_whatsapp.py
import json
from datetime import datetime
from urllib.parse import urlparse

import frappe
import requests
from frappe.utils import now

from nextai.personal_whatsapp_integration.doctype.utils import waha_api_dispatcher

sent_status_map = {
    "PENDING": "Sent",
    "SERVER": "Sent",
    "DEVICE": "Delivered",
    "READ": "Read",
}

received_status_map = {
    "SERVER": "Received",
    "DEVICE": "Received",
    "READ": "Marked Read",
}


def handle_session_status_event(data):
    session_doctype = "CN PW Whatsapp Session"
    payload = data["payload"]

    record_name = frappe.db.get_value(
        session_doctype, {"name": data["session"]}, "name"
    )

    session_id = data["session"]
    if record_name:
        if not data["me"]:
            doc = frappe.get_doc(session_doctype, record_name)
            doc.update(
                {
                    "status": payload["status"],
                    "qr_image_link": f"/api/{session_id}/auth/qr?t = {datetime.now()}",
                }
            )
            doc.save(ignore_permissions=True)
        if data["me"]:
            if not is_chat_available(data["me"]["id"]):
                save_contact_by_session(data["me"]["id"], session_id)
            doc = frappe.get_doc(session_doctype, record_name)
            doc.update(
                {
                    "status": payload["status"],
                    "user_id": data["me"]["id"],
                    "user_name": data["me"]["pushName"],
                    "qr_image_link": f"/api/{session_id}/auth/qr?t = {datetime.now()}",
                }
            )
            doc.save(ignore_permissions=True)

    else:
        # create new session
        session_data = {
            "status": payload["status"],
            "session_id": session_id,
            "qr_image_link": f"/api/{session_id}/auth/qr?t = {datetime.now()}",
        }
        doc = frappe.new_doc("CN PW Whatsapp Session")
        doc.update(session_data)
        doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)


def download_media(url):
    parsed_url = urlparse(url)
    file_name = parsed_url.path.split("/")[-1]
    response = waha_api_dispatcher("GET", url)

    if response.status_code == 200:
        file_doc = frappe.get_doc(
            {
                "doctype": "File",
                "file_name": file_name,
                "content": response.content,
            }
        ).insert(ignore_permissions=True)
        return file_doc
    else:
        return


def download_profile_picture(url):
    parsed_url = urlparse(url)
    file_name = parsed_url.path.split("/")[-1]
    response = requests.request("GET", url)
    if response.status_code == 200:
        file_doc = frappe.get_doc(
            {
                "doctype": "File",
                "file_name": file_name,
                "content": response.content,
            }
        ).insert(ignore_permissions=True)
        return file_doc


def get_profile_picture(session: str, chat_id: str):
    url = f"/api/contacts/profile-picture?session={session}&contactId={chat_id}"
    data = waha_api_dispatcher("GET", url).json()
    profile_picture_url = data.get("profilePictureURL")
    if profile_picture_url:
        return download_profile_picture(profile_picture_url)
    else:
        return None


def save_contact_by_session(user_id, session):
    if not user_id:
        return
    url = f"/api/contacts?contactId={user_id}&session={session}"
    response = waha_api_dispatcher("GET", url)
    contact_data = response.json()

    user_name = contact_data.get("name", contact_data.get("pushname", ""))

    file_doc = get_profile_picture(session, user_id)

    data = {
        "chat_id": user_id,
        "chat_name": user_name,
        "profile_picture": file_doc.get("file_url") if file_doc else None,
        "type": "Contact",
    }

    doc = frappe.new_doc("CN PW Chat")
    doc.update(data)
    doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)


def save_group(session, group_id):
    if not group_id:
        return
    url = f"/api/{session}/groups/{group_id}"
    response = waha_api_dispatcher("GET", url)
    group_data = response.json()
    participants = []
    file_url = None
    file_doc = get_profile_picture(session, group_id)
    group_name = group_data.get("name")
    if file_doc:
        file_url = file_doc.file_url
    if not group_name:
        group_name = group_name = group_data.get("groupMetadata", {}).get("subject")
    members = []

    for participant in participants:
        tab = {"user": participant["id"]["user"], "is_admin": participant["isAdmin"]}
        members.append(tab)

    data = {
        "chat_id": group_id,
        "chat_name": group_name,
        "profile_picture": file_url,
        "members": members,
        "type": "Group",
    }
    doc = frappe.new_doc("CN PW Chat")
    doc.update(data)
    doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)


def is_chat_available(contact):
    contact_exists = frappe.db.exists("CN PW Chat", {"chat_id": contact})
    return contact_exists


def update_unread_count(chat_id, session_id):
    chat_doc = frappe.get_doc("CN PW Chat", chat_id)
    entry_found = False
    filters = {"status": "Received"}

    if chat_id.endswith("@c.us"):
        filters["user_id"] = chat_id
    elif chat_id.endswith("@g.us"):
        filters["group_id"] = chat_id
    unread_message_count = frappe.db.count("CN PW Whatsapp Message", filters)
    for entry in chat_doc.unread_count:
        if entry.session == session_id:
            entry.unread_count = unread_message_count
            entry_found = True
            break
    if not entry_found:
        new_entry = {
            "unread_count": unread_message_count,
            "session": session_id,
        }
        chat_doc.append("unread_count", new_entry)
    chat_doc.save(ignore_permissions=True)
    frappe.db.commit()


def update_last_message(
    chat_id,
    session_id,
    message_type,
    send_type,
    message_body=None,
):
    chat_doc = frappe.get_doc("CN PW Chat", chat_id)
    message_time = now()
    entry_found = False
    for entry in chat_doc.last_message:
        if entry.session == session_id:
            entry.message_type = message_type
            entry.message_body = message_body
            entry.message_time = message_time
            entry.type = send_type
            entry_found = True
            break
    if not entry_found:
        new_entry = {
            "session": session_id,
            "message_type": message_type,
            "message_body": message_body,
            "message_time": message_time,
            "type": send_type,
        }
        chat_doc.append("last_message", new_entry)
    chat_doc.save(ignore_permissions=True)
    frappe.db.commit()


def save_message_and_contact_and_group(data, msg_type, user_id):
    group_suffix = "@g.us"
    user_suffix = "@c.us"
    session_id = data["session"]
    payload = data["payload"]
    _data = payload["_data"]
    message_id = _data["id"]["id"]
    author = _data.get("author")
    quoted_msg_id = _data.get("quotedStanzaID")
    if user_id.endswith(group_suffix) or user_id.endswith(user_suffix):
        if user_id.endswith(group_suffix):
            author = (
                frappe.get_doc("CN PW Whatsapp Session", session_id).user_id
                if msg_type == "Outgoing"
                else _data.get("author")
            )
            if not is_chat_available(user_id):
                save_group(session_id, user_id)
            if not is_chat_available(author):
                save_contact_by_session(author, session_id)
        elif user_id.endswith(user_suffix):
            if not is_chat_available(user_id):
                save_contact_by_session(user_id, session_id)

        ack_name = payload.get("ackName", "")
        message_status = (
            sent_status_map.get(ack_name, "Unknown")
            if msg_type == "Outgoing"
            else received_status_map.get(ack_name, "Unknown")
        )

        doc = frappe.new_doc("CN PW Whatsapp Message")
        doc.update(
            {
                "message_id": message_id,
                "session": session_id,
                "group_id": user_id if user_id.endswith(group_suffix) else None,
                "user_id": author if user_id.endswith(group_suffix) else user_id,
                "type": msg_type,
                "conversation_type": (
                    "Group" if user_id.endswith(group_suffix) else "Personal"
                ),
                "status": message_status,
                "quoted_message_id": quoted_msg_id if (quoted_msg_id) else None,
            }
        )
        if quoted_msg_id:
            doc.update(
                {
                    "quoted_message_id": quoted_msg_id,
                }
            )
        message_data = None
        if _data["type"] == "chat":
            message_data = {
                "body": payload["body"],
                "message_type": "Text",
            }
        elif _data["type"] in ["image", "video", "document"]:
            url = payload["media"]["url"].split("3000")[-1]
            file_doc = download_media(url)
            message_data = {
                "caption": _data.get("caption"),
                "message_type": _data["type"].capitalize(),
                "mime_type": payload["media"]["mimetype"],
                "file_name": payload["media"]["filename"],
                "media_url": {file_doc.file_url},
            }
        elif _data["type"] == "audio":
            url = payload["media"]["url"].split("3000")[-1]
            file_doc = download_media(url)
            message_data = {
                "message_type": _data["type"].capitalize(),
                "mime_type": payload["media"]["mimetype"],
                "media_url": {file_doc.file_url},
            }
        elif _data["type"] == "e2e_notification":
            return

        doc.update(message_data)
        doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
        update_unread_count(user_id, session_id)
        update_last_message(
            user_id,
            session_id,
            "Text" if _data["type"] == "chat" else _data["type"].capitalize(),
            msg_type,
            payload["body"] if _data["type"] == "chat" else None,
        )


def handle_message_event(data):
    if data["payload"]["fromMe"]:
        return
    payload = data["payload"]
    msg_type = "Incoming"
    user_id = payload["from"]
    save_message_and_contact_and_group(data, msg_type, user_id)


def handle_message_any_event(data):
    if not data["payload"]["fromMe"]:
        return
    payload = data["payload"]
    msg_type = "Outgoing"
    user_id = payload["to"]
    save_message_and_contact_and_group(data, msg_type, user_id)


def update_message_status(data):
    payload = data["payload"]
    ack_name = payload.get("ackName")
    if not ack_name:
        return
    _data = payload["_data"]
    message_id = _data["id"]["id"]
    if not frappe.db.exists(
        "CN PW Whatsapp Message",
        {
            "message_id": message_id,
        },
    ):
        return
    wa_message = frappe.get_doc(
        "CN PW Whatsapp Message",
        {
            "message_id": message_id,
        },
    )
    wa_message.status = sent_status_map.get(ack_name, "Unknown")
    wa_message.save(ignore_permissions=True)


def handle_message_ack_event(data):
    if not data["payload"]["fromMe"]:
        return
    update_message_status(data)


def handle_chat_archive(data):
    session = data["session"]
    payload = data["payload"]
    chat_id = payload["id"]
    doctype_name = "CN PW Chat"
    chat_doc = frappe.get_doc(doctype_name, chat_id)
    session_exists = any(
        child.session_name == session for child in chat_doc.archived_by
    )
    if payload["archived"]:
        if session_exists:
            return "chat id is already archived"
        else:
            chat_doc.append(
                "archived_by",
                {
                    "session_name": session,
                },
            )
    else:
        if session_exists:
            chat_doc.archived_by = [
                child for child in chat_doc.archived_by if child.session_name != session
            ]
        else:
            return "chat id is not archived"
    chat_doc.save(ignore_permissions=True)


# nextai.api.webhook.personal_whatsapp.handle
@frappe.whitelist(allow_guest=True)
def handle(**kwargs):
    print(kwargs)
    try:
        log = frappe.new_doc("CN PW Webhook Log")
        log.update({"payload": json.dumps(kwargs, indent=2)})
        log.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    except:
        pass
    event = kwargs["event"]
    if event == "session.status":
        handle_session_status_event(kwargs)
    if event == "message":
        handle_message_event(kwargs)
    if event == "message.any":
        handle_message_any_event(kwargs)
    if event == "message.ack":
        handle_message_ack_event(kwargs)
    if event == "chat.archive":
        handle_chat_archive(kwargs)

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/api/webhook/whatsapp.py
import json
from typing import Dict

import frappe
from werkzeug.wrappers import Response

from nextai.utils import send_request

MEDIA_TYPES = ("image", "sticker", "document", "audio", "video")


def create_or_get_contact(wa_id, contact_name):
    if frappe.db.exists("Contact", {"whatsapp_id": wa_id}):
        contact = frappe.get_last_doc("Contact", filters={"whatsapp_id": wa_id})
    else:
        contact = frappe.get_doc(
            {
                "doctype": "Contact",
                "first_name": contact_name,
                "whatsapp_id": wa_id,
            }
        ).insert(ignore_permissions=True)
        frappe.db.commit()
    return contact


def process_status_update(status: Dict):
    message_id = status.get("id")
    status = status.get("status")
    frappe.db.set_value(
        "WhatsApp Message", {"id": message_id}, "status", status.title()
    )


def create_message(message: Dict, contacts: Dict = None, outgoing=False, bot=False):
    # print("create_whatsapp_message")
    message_type = message.get("type")
    # Create whatsapp contact doc if not exists
    received_from = message.get("from")
    sent_to = message.get("to")
    whatsapp_no = sent_to if outgoing else received_from

    contact_name = None
    if contacts:
        contact_name = contacts[whatsapp_no]

    if not contact_name:
        contact_name = "Unknown Number:" + whatsapp_no

    contact = create_or_get_contact(whatsapp_no, contact_name)
    ctwa_clid = message.get("referral", {}).get("ctwa_clid")
    # page_id = frappe.local.form_dict["entry"][0].get("id")
    referral = message.get("referral", {})
    media_type = referral.get("media_type")
    source_id = referral.get("source_id")
    source_type = referral.get("source_type")
    source_url = referral.get("source_url")

    message_data = {
        "doctype": "WhatsApp Message",
        "type": "Outgoing" if outgoing else "Incoming",
        "status": "Sent" if outgoing else "Received",
        "id": message.get("id"),
        "message_type": message_type.title(),
        "is_bot": True if bot else False,
        "referral_ctwa_clid": ctwa_clid,
        # "referral_page_id": page_id,
        "referral_media_type": media_type,
        "referral_source_id": source_id,
        "referral_source_type": source_type,
        "referral_source_url": source_url,
    }

    if outgoing:
        message_data["to"] = contact.name
    else:
        message_data["from"] = contact.name

    if message_type == "text":
        message_data["message_body"] = message.get("text").get("body")
    elif message_type in MEDIA_TYPES:
        message_data["media_id"] = message.get(message_type).get("id")
        message_data["media_mime_type"] = message.get(message_type).get("mime_type")
        message_data["media_hash"] = message.get(message_type).get("sha256")
        message_data["media_filename"] = message.get(message_type).get("filename")
        message_data["media_caption"] = message.get(message_type).get("caption")
    elif message_type == "interactive":
        message_data["interactive_message_data"] = json.dumps(
            message.get("interactive")
        )
    elif message_type == "button":
        message_data["interactive_message_data"] = json.dumps(message.get("button"))

    # print("message_data", json.dumps(message_data, default=str, indent=6))
    wa_message_docs = frappe.get_doc(message_data).insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    wa_message_docs.notify_update()
    return wa_message_docs


@frappe.whitelist(allow_guest=True)
def handle():
    phone_number_id = frappe.db.get_single_value("Whatsapp Settings", "phone_number_id")
    if not phone_number_id:
        frappe.throw("Whatsapp Settings not configured properly")
    if frappe.request.method == "GET":
        return verify_token_and_fulfill_challenge()
    try:
        form_dict = frappe.local.form_dict
        try:
            phone_id = form_dict["entry"][0]["changes"][0]["value"]["metadata"][
                "phone_number_id"
            ]
            if phone_id != phone_number_id:
                return
        except:
            pass
        messages = form_dict["entry"][0]["changes"][0]["value"].get("messages", [])
        statuses = form_dict["entry"][0]["changes"][0]["value"].get("statuses", [])
        contacts = form_dict["entry"][0]["changes"][0]["value"].get("contacts", [])
        contacts_map = {i["wa_id"]: i["profile"]["name"] for i in contacts}

        for status in statuses:
            process_status_update(status)

        message_contact = None
        for message in messages:
            message = create_message(message, contacts_map)
            message_contact = message.get("from")

        frappe.get_doc(
            {"doctype": "Whatsapp Webhook Log", "payload": frappe.as_json(form_dict)}
        ).insert(ignore_permissions=True)

        # TODO: to be removed after testing on production
        forward_to_botpress = frappe.db.get_single_value(
            "Whatsapp Settings", "forward_to_botpress"
        )
        botpress_webhook_url = frappe.db.get_single_value(
            "Whatsapp Settings", "botpress_webhook_url"
        )
        if forward_to_botpress and botpress_webhook_url and message_contact:
            contact_doc = frappe.get_doc("Contact", message_contact)
            if not contact_doc.chatbot_disabled:
                # TODO: use enqueue
                send_request(
                    botpress_webhook_url,
                    {**form_dict, "contact": contact_doc.as_dict()},
                    "POST",
                )

    except Exception:
        frappe.log_error("WhatsApp Webhook Log Error", frappe.get_traceback())


def verify_token_and_fulfill_challenge():
    meta_challenge = frappe.form_dict.get("hub.challenge")
    expected_token = frappe.db.get_single_value(
        "Whatsapp Settings", "webhook_verify_token"
    )

    if frappe.form_dict.get("hub.verify_token") != expected_token:
        frappe.throw("Verify token does not match")

    return Response(meta_challenge, status=200)

import frappe
from frappe.oauth import get_url_delimiter


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_doc_meta(doc_name):
    return frappe.get_meta(doc_name)


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_session():
    if frappe.session.user:
        otoken = frappe.new_doc("OAuth Bearer Token")
        otoken.client = request.client["name"]
        try:
            otoken.user = (
                request.user
                if request.user
                else frappe.db.get_value(
                    "OAuth Bearer Token",
                    {"refresh_token": request.body.get("refresh_token")},
                    "user",
                )
            )
        except Exception:
            otoken.user = frappe.session.user
        otoken.scopes = get_url_delimiter().join(request.scopes)
        otoken.access_token = token["access_token"]
        otoken.refresh_token = token.get("refresh_token")
        otoken.expires_in = token["expires_in"]
        otoken.save(ignore_permissions=True)
        frappe.db.commit()
    return

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/chatapi/chat.py
import frappe
import json
import datetime
import requests
from nextai.config.api import API_URL
from nextai.utils import create_response
import uuid
from werkzeug.wrappers import Response

@frappe.whitelist()
def send_message_to_server(**kwargs):
    kwargs.pop('cmd')
    kwargs=frappe._dict(kwargs)
    try:
        if kwargs.get('id') and kwargs.get('message') and kwargs.get('time'):
            id = kwargs['id']
            message = kwargs['message']
            time = kwargs['time']
            session = kwargs['session_id']

            user_conversation = frappe.get_doc({
                'id': id,
	            'doctype': 'Chatnext Conversation',
	            'channel': frappe.session.user,
	            'sender': 'USER',
                'message': message,
                'created_at': time,
                'session': session
            })
            user_conversation.insert()

            site_url = frappe.utils.get_url()
            chat_history = get_user_chat_history(session)
            route_data = get_route_data(kwargs.get("route_path"))
            payload = json.dumps({
                'chat_history': chat_history,
                'route_data': route_data,
                'site_url': site_url,
                'message': str(message),
                'user_id': frappe.session.user
            })
            url = f"{API_URL}/api/method/chatnext_core.api.get_message"
            headers = {
                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
            }
            response = requests.request("POST", url, headers=headers, data=payload)
            frappe.logger('utils').exception(response.json())

            output_message = response.json()['message']['output']

            bot_conversation_time = datetime.datetime.now().isoformat()
            bot_conversation_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
            bot_conversation = frappe.get_doc({
                'id': bot_conversation_id,
	            'doctype': 'Chatnext Conversation',
	            'channel': frappe.session.user,
	            'sender': 'BOT',
                'message': output_message,
                'created_at': bot_conversation_time,
                'session': session
            })
            bot_conversation.insert()

            data = json.dumps({
                'success': True,
                'id': bot_conversation_id,
                'author': 'BOT',
                'message': output_message,
                'time': bot_conversation_time
            })
            response = Response(data, status=201, mimetype='application/json')
            return response

        else:
            frappe.logger("chatnext").log("API FAILED")
            frappe.logger("chatnext").log(str(payload))
            raise Exception('Invalid request')

    except Exception as e:
        frappe.logger("chatnext").error(e)
        data = json.dumps({'success': False, 'message': str(e)[0:200]})
        response = Response(data, status=400, mimetype='application/json')
        return response

def get_user_chat_history(session_id):
    return frappe.db.get_list('Chatnext Conversation', {'session': session_id}, ['sender','message'], order_by='creation', ignore_permissions=True)

def get_route_data(route_path):
    if not route_path: return {}
    # Route Path Will be in this format => ['Form', 'DocType', 'CP2P Settings']
    if route_path[0] == 'Form':
        return {route_path[1]: route_path[2]}
    elif route_path[0] == 'Workspaces':
        return {route_path[0]: route_path[1]}
    else:
        # TODO: Extract Report Name etc
        return {}

@frappe.whitelist()
def get_older_messages(**kwargs):
    kwargs = frappe._dict(kwargs)
    try:
        if kwargs.get('author'):
            author = kwargs['author']
            older_messages = frappe.get_all('Chatnext Conversation', fields=['*'], order_by='creation desc', filters={
                'channel': author
            })
            return json.dumps({
                'success': True,
                'messages': older_messages
            }, default=str)
        else:
            raise Exception('Invalid request')
    except Exception as e:
        return json.dumps({
            'success': False,
            'message': 'Error: ' + str(e)
        })

import datetime
import json
import uuid

import frappe
from nextai.core_handshake import is_chatbot_enabled, send_authorized_core_request
from nextai.utils import send_core_request

# TODO: validate this - invalid function
def core_request():
    user_id = frappe.session.user
    # get chatnext field from user doctype
    user = frappe.get_doc("User", user_id)
    if not is_chatbot_enabled():
        return False
    if user.chatnext_api_key and user.chatnext_api_secret:
        return True
    return False


# check if chatnext core has access to chatnext
@frappe.whitelist()
def validate_core_access():
    pass


# check if chatnext core has access to chatnext
@frappe.whitelist()
def validate():
    user_id = frappe.session.user
    first_record = frappe.get_all("OAuth Client", {"app_name": "ChatNext"})
    client = frappe.get_doc("OAuth Client", first_record[0].name)
    if not client:
        frappe.throw("Client Not Present")
    host = frappe.get_site_config().get("host_name")
    payload = json.dumps(
        {
            "site_url": host or "http://core.localhost:8000",
            "user_id": user_id,
            "scopes": client.scopes,
        }
    )
    response = send_core_request(
        payload, "GET", "chatnext_core.chatnext_handshake.validate_chatnext_token"
    )
    if not response.json().get("message"):
        frappe.throw(response.json())
    return response.json().get("message")


@frappe.whitelist(methods=["POST"])
def ocr_bot_request(file, doc_name):
    fields = frappe.get_meta(doc_name).fields
    meta = [
        f"{d.fieldname} {'(mandatory)' if d.reqd is True or d.reqd == 1 else ''}"
        + (
            " value one of ({})".format(d.options.replace("\n", ","))
            if d.fieldtype == "Select"
            else ""
        )
        for d in fields
        if (
                d.fieldtype
                not in [
                    "Section Break",
                    "Column Break",
                    "Read Only",
                    "Tab Break",
                    "Button",
                    "Link",
                ]
        )
    ]

    payload_image = {
        "document_image_base64": file,
    }

    headers = {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
    }
    ocr_res = send_authorized_core_request(
        payload_image,
        "POST",
        "chatnext_core.clients.ocr_client.file_to_ocr",
        headers,
    ).json()

    payload_doc = {
        "data": ocr_res["message"],
        "document_meta": ", ".join(meta),
        "doctype": doc_name,
    }

    return send_authorized_core_request(
        payload_doc,
        "POST",
        "chatnext_core.clients.ai_client.create_doctype",
        headers,
    ).json()


@frappe.whitelist()
def bot_response(**kwargs):
    current_time = datetime.datetime.utcnow().isoformat() + "Z"
    id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    session = kwargs.get("session")
    message = kwargs.get("message")
    channel = kwargs.get("user_id")

    doc = frappe.get_doc(
        {
            "doctype": "Chatnext Conversation",
            "id": id,
            "sender": "BOT",
            "created_at": current_time,
            "channel": channel,
            "message": message,
            "session": session,
        }
    )
    doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    doc.reload()

    payload_data = {"message": message, "id": id}

    frappe.publish_realtime(
        event="BOT_RESPONSE",
        message=json.dumps(payload_data, default=str),
        user=frappe.session.user,
    )

@frappe.whitelist()
def bot_reply(**kwargs):
    id = kwargs.get("id")
    sender = kwargs.get("sender")
    message = kwargs.get("message")
    created_at = kwargs.get("created_at")
    session = kwargs.get("session")

    user_id = frappe.session.user
    common_data = {
        "channel": user_id,
        "message": message,
        "session": session,
    }

    host = frappe.get_site_config().get("host_name")
    payload = json.dumps({"site_url": host or "http://127.0.0.1:8000", **common_data})

    # TODO: validate this - invalid function call and usage
    bot_acknowledgement = core_request()

    if (
            "status" in bot_acknowledgement["message"]
            and bot_acknowledgement["message"]["status"] == "OK"
    ):
        doc = frappe.get_doc(
            {
                "doctype": "Chatnext Conversation",
                "id": id,
                "sender": sender,
                "created_at": created_at,
                **common_data,
            }
        )
        doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
        doc.reload()
        return {"success": True, "status": "OK"}

    raise Exception("Unable to connect chatnext bot.")

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/chatnext/auth.py
import frappe
from frappe.auth import LoginManager
from nextai.core_handshake import generate_authorization_code


def create_oauth_code(user_id):
    first_record = frappe.get_all('OAuth Client', {'app_name': 'ChatNext'})
    client = frappe.get_doc('OAuth Client', first_record[0].name)
    redirect_uri = client.default_redirect_uri

    if not client:
        frappe.throw('Unauthorized client')

    oauth_code = generate_authorization_code(user_id, client)
    return {
        'grant_type': 'authorization_code',
        'code': oauth_code.authorization_code,
        'redirect_uri': redirect_uri,
        "client_id": oauth_code.client,
        "scopes": oauth_code.scopes,
    }


@frappe.whitelist(methods=["POST"], allow_guest=True)
def login(userid: str, password: str):
    login_manager = LoginManager()
    user_id = login_manager.check_password(userid, password)
    auth_code = create_oauth_code(user_id)
    login_manager.login_as(user_id)
    return {
        "success": True,
        "auth_code": auth_code
    }


@frappe.whitelist(methods=["GET"])
def get_cookies():
    user_id = frappe.session.user
    login_manager = LoginManager()
    login_manager.login_as(user_id)
    return {
        "success": True,
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/chatnext/data.py
import frappe


def get_fields(doc, fields: list[str]):
    obj = {}
    for field in fields:
        obj[field] = doc.get(field)
    return obj


@frappe.whitelist(methods=['GET'])
def get_boot_data():
    user_id = frappe.session.user
    user_doc = frappe.get_doc("User", user_id)
    user_fields = [
        'fullname', "time_zone", "email", "first_name", "first_name", "language", "user_type", "user_emails",
        "desk_theme", "last_active"
    ]
    micro_apps = frappe.get_all("CN Microapp", fields=["name", "content_route", "icon", "pinned", "title", "is_home"])
    return {
        "user_info": {
            "user_id": user_id,
            **get_fields(user_doc, user_fields),
            "roles": [role.role for role in user_doc.roles]
        },
        "micro_apps": micro_apps
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/core_handshake.py
import json

import requests

import frappe
from frappe.utils import add_days, now_datetime
from nextai.config.api import API_URL
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel.funnel_user import get_funnel_user_id
from nextai.utils import send_authorized_core_request, is_admin_user


# -------------
# for core app
# -------------

def send_handshake_request(payload, request_type, method):
    url = f"{API_URL}/api/method/chatnext_core.chatnext_handshake.{method}"
    headers = {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    }
    response = requests.request(
        request_type, url, headers=headers, data=payload
    )
    return response


def generate_authorization_code(user_id, client):
    # Generate a random authorization code
    if frappe.db.exists('OAuth Authorization Code', {'client': client.name, 'user': user_id, 'validity': 'Valid'}):
        return frappe.get_doc('OAuth Authorization Code', {'client': client.name, 'user': user_id, 'validity': 'Valid'})
    authorization_code = frappe.generate_hash(length=32)
    # Save the authorization code to the database
    auth_code_doc = frappe.new_doc('OAuth Authorization Code')
    auth_code_doc.client = client.name
    auth_code_doc.user = user_id
    auth_code_doc.authorization_code = authorization_code
    auth_code_doc.scopes = client.scopes

    # Get the current datetime object
    now = now_datetime()
    thirty_days_from_now = add_days(now, 30)
    auth_code_doc.expiration_time = thirty_days_from_now
    # auth_code_doc.expires_in = 30
    auth_code_doc.save(ignore_permissions=True)
    auth_code_doc.reload()
    frappe.db.commit()
    # auth_code_doc
    return auth_code_doc


def get_api_access_from_core(auth_code, user_id, redirect_uri):
    host = frappe.get_site_config().get('host_name')
    localhost = frappe.get_site_config().get('localhost_name')
    payload = json.dumps(
        {
            "site_url": host or localhost or "http://localhost:8000",
            "authorization_code": auth_code.authorization_code,
            "client_id": auth_code.client,
            "scopes": auth_code.scopes,
            "redirect_uri": redirect_uri
        }
    )
    response = send_handshake_request(
        payload, "POST", 'create_chatnext_api_access_key'
    )

    if not response.json().get("message"):
        print(response.json())
        frappe.throw("did not receive a valid response from core")

    api_details = response.json().get("message")
    api_key = api_details.get('api_key')
    api_secret = api_details.get('api_secret')
    user = frappe.get_doc('User', user_id)
    user.update(
        {
            'chatnext_api_key': api_key,
            'chatnext_api_secret': api_secret,
        }
    )
    user.save(ignore_permissions=True)
    frappe.db.commit()
    return {
        "success": True,
    }


def is_chatbot_enabled():
    if is_admin_user():
        return True
    user = frappe.session.user
    has_role = frappe.get_all(
        "Has Role", filters={"parent": user, "role": "Chatnext AI User"}
    )
    return len(has_role) > 0


def connect_user_to_core(user_id=None):
    user_id = user_id or frappe.session.user
    first_record = frappe.get_all('OAuth Client', {'app_name': 'ChatNext'})
    client = frappe.get_doc('OAuth Client', first_record[0].name)
    if not client:
        frappe.throw('Unauthorized client')
    auth_code = generate_authorization_code(user_id, client)
    token = get_api_access_from_core(
        auth_code, user_id, client.default_redirect_uri
    )
    raise Exception("sdfhg")
    return token


@frappe.whitelist()
def initiate():
    if not is_chatbot_enabled():
        frappe.throw("User don't have permission to access chatnext ai chatbot")
    try:
        return connect_user_to_core()
    except Exception as e:
        frappe.logger('utils').exception(e)
        return e

    if not is_chatbot_enabled():

    funnel_user = frappe.get_doc('User', funnel_user_id)
    if funnel_user.chatnext_api_key and funnel_user.chatnext_api_secret:
        return True
    return "Chatnext is setup to use this feature. Please contact your admin."

# check if chatnext core has access to chatnext
@frappe.whitelist()
def validate():
    user_id = frappe.session.user
    first_record = frappe.get_all('OAuth Client', {'app_name': 'ChatNext'})
    client = frappe.get_doc('OAuth Client', first_record[0].name)
    if not client:
        frappe.throw('Client Not Present')
    host = frappe.get_site_config().get('host_name')
    payload = json.dumps(
        {
            "site_url": host or "http://core.localhost:8000",
            "user_id": user_id,
            "scopes": client.scopes,
        }
    )
    response = send_authorized_core_request(
        payload, "GET", 'chatnext_core.chatnext_handshake.validate_chatnext_token'
    )
    if not response.json().get("message"):
        frappe.throw(response.json())
    return response.json().get("message")

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/cron.py
import frappe
from frappe.utils import now_datetime


def cron_job():
    current_datetime = now_datetime()
    tasks = frappe.get_all(
        'Automation Task',
        filters={'status': 'Pending', 'start': ['<', current_datetime]},
        fields=['*']
    )

    for task in tasks:
        frappe.enqueue('nextai.cron.start_task', task=task)

def start_task(task):
    try:
        if task.get("type") == "Send WhatsApp":
            # TODO : go send whatsapp
            pass

        if task.get("type") == "Send Email":
            # TODO : go send email
            recipient = frappe.db.get_value("Email Account", {"name": task.get('email_account')}, 'email_id')
            subject = frappe.db.get_value("Email Template", {"name": task.get('email_template')}, 'subject')
            message = frappe.db.get_value("Email Template", {"name": task.get('email_template')}, 'response')
            context = task.get('email_payload')

            frappe.sendmail(recipients=recipient, subject=frappe.render_template(subject, context=context), message=frappe.render_template(message, context=context))



        if task.get("type") == "API Call":
            # TODO : API CALL
            pass

    except Exception as e:
        print(e)


# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/formio_embed.py
import json
import re

import frappe
from werkzeug.wrappers import Response


# Convert camelCase to snake_case


def camel_to_snake(key):
    s1 = re.sub('(.)([A-Z][a-z]+)', r'\1_\2', key)
    return re.sub('([a-z0-9])([A-Z])', r'\1_\2', s1).lower()


@frappe.whitelist(allow_guest=True)
def get_web_form_data(**kwargs):
    try:
        if frappe.request and frappe.request.method == "POST":
            data = kwargs.get("file")

            web_form_name = frappe.request.args.get('web_form_name')

            web_form = frappe.get_doc('Web Form', web_form_name)
            form_doc_type = web_form.doc_type

            data.pop('submit', None)

            snake_case_data = {camel_to_snake(key): value for key, value in data.items()}

            added_properties = {"doctype": form_doc_type, "web_form": web_form_name}
            submitted_data = {**snake_case_data, **added_properties}

            frappe.form_dict.web_form = web_form_name
            frappe.form_dict.data = json.dumps(submitted_data)

            doc = frappe.website.doctype.web_form.web_form.accept(web_form=web_form_name,
                                                                  data=json.dumps(submitted_data))

            response_data = {
                'web_form_name': web_form_name,
                'doc_type': form_doc_type,
                'doc': str(doc)
            }

            return Response(json.dumps(response_data), status=200, content_type='application/json')
        else:
            web_form_name = kwargs.get('web_form_name')

            if web_form_name:
                web_form = frappe.get_doc('Web Form', web_form_name)
                form_data = web_form.custom_form_data

                # Parse the JSON string into a Python dictionary
                parsed_form_data = json.loads(form_data)

                return Response(json.dumps(parsed_form_data), status=200, content_type='application/json')
            else:
                raise Exception('Invalid request. Please provide a web_form_name parameter.')
    except Exception as e:
        response_data = {'success': False, 'message': str(e)}
        return Response(json.dumps(response_data), status=500, content_type='application/json')


@frappe.whitelist(allow_guest=True)
def get_form_styles(**kwargs):
    web_form_name = kwargs.get('web_form_name')

    if web_form_name:
        web_form = frappe.get_doc('Web Form', web_form_name)
        form_styles = web_form.custom_css_style

        if form_styles:
            return Response(form_styles, status=200, content_type='text/plain')
        else:
            return Response("", status=200, content_type='text/plain')

    raise Exception('Invalid request. Please provide a web_form_name parameter.')

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/apis/fetch_data.py
import json

import frappe


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_searched_doc_list(doctype: str, q: str = '', limit=20, fields=None, sort='name', filters=None):
    if fields is None:
        fields = ['name']
    filters = json.loads(filters) if filters else []
    if q:
        filters.append(["name", "like", f"%{q}%"])
    if isinstance(fields, str):
        try:
            fields_list = json.loads(fields)
            fields = fields_list if isinstance(fields_list, list) else fields.split(',')
        except:
            fields = fields.split(',')
    return frappe.get_list(doctype, fields=fields, filters=filters, limit=int(float(limit)), order_by=sort)

@frappe.whitelist()
def get_service_account_cred_list(integration_type=None):
    response = send_authorized_core_request(
        {},
        "GET",
        "chatnext_core.chatnext_core.doctype.customer_service_account_credential.customer_service_account_credential.get_service_account_credential_list",
    )

    data = response.json().get("message", [])

    if integration_type:
        filtered_data = [item for item in data if item.get("integration_type") == integration_type]
        return filtered_data

    return data


# nextai.funnel.apis.funnel_integration_creds.add_update_service_account_cred
@frappe.whitelist()
def add_update_service_account_cred(
        integration_type, integration_name, credentials, name=None
):
    if not integration_type:
        raise ValueError("integration_type cannot be empty.")
    if not integration_name:
        raise ValueError("integration_name cannot be empty.")
    if not credentials:
        raise ValueError("credentials cannot be empty.")

    payload = {
        "integration_type": integration_type,
        "integration_name": integration_name,
        "credentials": credentials,
        "name": name,
    }

    response = send_authorized_core_request(
        payload,
        "POST",
        "chatnext_core.chatnext_core.doctype.customer_service_account_credential.customer_service_account_credential.add_update_service_account_credential",
    )
    if response.status_code // 100 != 2:
        raise Exception(response.json())
    return response.json().get("message")

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/custom_trigger.py
import json

import frappe
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_tasks_from_triggers import create_tasks_from_triggers


@frappe.whitelist()
def trigger_list(event_names, doctype=None):
    if not isinstance(event_names, list):
        if isinstance(event_names, str):
            event_names = event_names.split(",")
        else:
            event_names = [event_names]
    values = {
        'event_names': event_names
    }
    funnel_nodes = frappe.db.sql(
        """
            select name, default_doctype, trigger_event_name
            from `tabFunnel Node`
            where is_custom = 1
            and element_type = 'Trigger'
            and trigger_event_name in %(event_names)s
            """, values=values, as_dict=1
    )
    if not funnel_nodes:
        return []

    values = {
        'funnel_node_names': [node['name'] for node in funnel_nodes]
    }
    trigger_nodes = frappe.db.sql(
        """
                select *
                from `tabFunnel Definition`
                where  type in %(funnel_node_names)s
                and parenttype = 'Funnel Published'
                and parent in (
                    select name from `tabFunnel Published`
                    where published = 1
                )
            """, values=values, as_dict=1
    )

    if not trigger_nodes:
        return []

    for node in trigger_nodes:
        funnel_node = next((funnel_node for funnel_node in funnel_nodes if funnel_node['name'] == node['type']), None)
        node['funnel_node'] = funnel_node
        node_data = json.loads(node['data'])
        node['node_data'] = node_data
        node['target_doctype'] = node_data.get('doctype') or funnel_node.default_doctype
        node['trigger_event_name'] = funnel_node.trigger_event_name
        node['node_label'] = node_data.get('node_label')

    if doctype:
        trigger_nodes = [node for node in trigger_nodes if not node.get('target_doctype') or node['target_doctype'] == doctype]

    published_funnel_names = [node['parent'] for node in trigger_nodes]
    published_funnels = frappe.db.get_list("Funnel Published", filters={"name": ["in", published_funnel_names]}, fields=["*"])
    for node in trigger_nodes:
        funnel_data = next((funnel for funnel in published_funnels if funnel['name'] == node['parent']), None)
        node['funnel_data'] = funnel_data
        node['funnel_name'] = funnel_data.get('funnel')

    return trigger_nodes


def trigger_event(doc, event_name, variables=None, funnel=None, node_label=None):
    variables = variables or {}
    triggers = trigger_list([event_name], doc.doctype)
    data = {
        **variables,
        "doc": doc.as_dict(),
        "event": event_name
    }
    if funnel:
        triggers = [trigger for trigger in triggers if trigger['funnel_name'] == funnel]
    if node_label:
        triggers = [trigger for trigger in triggers if trigger['node_label'] == node_label]
    return create_tasks_from_triggers(triggers, data)

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/docs/check_documentation_updates.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Helper script to check which Funnel node documentation needs updating.
This script compares timestamps between funnel_node.json and documentation files.

Usage:
    python3 check_documentation_updates.py              # Check all nodes
    python3 check_documentation_updates.py node_name    # Check specific node

The script automatically detects your location from:
- Current working directory (if in bench or app directory)
- Script location (when called from elsewhere)
- FRAPPE_BENCH_PATH environment variable (as fallback)

Before updating documentation:
1. Run this script to see what needs updating
2. Review existing similar node documentation for style consistency
3. Check the Python implementation in the corresponding file
4. Update both the raw timestamp comment and formatted date
"""

import json
import os
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path

# Try to determine paths based on current working directory or script location
def get_paths():
    # Start from current working directory
    cwd = os.getcwd()

    # Check if we're in a bench directory (has 'apps' folder)
    apps_dir = os.path.join(cwd, 'apps')
    if os.path.exists(apps_dir) and os.path.isdir(apps_dir):
        bench_path = cwd
        app_path = os.path.join(bench_path, 'apps/nextai')
        if os.path.exists(app_path):
            return bench_path, app_path

    # Check if we're in the nextai app directory
    if 'apps/nextai' in cwd:
        # Extract the bench path from current path
        parts = cwd.split('apps/nextai')
        bench_path = parts[0].rstrip('/')
        app_path = os.path.join(bench_path, 'apps/nextai')
        if os.path.exists(app_path):
            return bench_path, app_path

    # Try from script location as fallback
    script_path = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    if 'apps/nextai' in script_path:
        parts = script_path.split('apps/nextai')
        bench_path = parts[0].rstrip('/')
        app_path = os.path.join(bench_path, 'apps/nextai')
        if os.path.exists(app_path):
            return bench_path, app_path

    # Last resort - check environment variable
    bench_path = os.environ.get('FRAPPE_BENCH_PATH', os.path.expanduser('~/frappe-bench'))
    app_path = os.path.join(bench_path, 'apps/nextai')

    return bench_path, app_path

BENCH_PATH, APP_PATH = get_paths()

def get_node_timestamps():
    """Extract all node timestamps from funnel_node.json"""
    funnel_json_path = os.path.join(APP_PATH, 'nextai/fixtures/funnel_node.json')

    if not os.path.exists(funnel_json_path):
        return None, "funnel_node.json not found"

    try:
        with open(funnel_json_path, 'r') as f:
            nodes = json.load(f)

        timestamps = {}
        for node in nodes:
            name = node.get('name', '')
            modified = node.get('modified', '')
            category = node.get('category', '')
            element_type = node.get('element_type', '')
            label = node.get('label', '')

            if name:
                timestamps[name] = {
                    'modified': modified,
                    'category': category,
                    'element_type': element_type,
                    'label': label
                }

        return timestamps, None
    except Exception as e:
        return None, f"Error reading funnel_node.json: {e}"

def get_doc_timestamp(node_name):
    """Get timestamp from a specific documentation file"""
    doc_path = os.path.join(APP_PATH, f'nextai/funnel/docs/nodes/{node_name}.md')

    if not os.path.exists(doc_path):
        return None, "not_found"

    try:
        with open(doc_path, 'r') as f:
            first_line = f.readline().strip()

        # Extract timestamp from HTML comment
        if first_line.startswith('<!-- Last Updated:'):
            match = re.search(r'<!-- Last Updated: (.+?) -->', first_line)
            if match:
                return match.group(1), "ok"

        return None, "no_comment"
    except Exception as e:
        return None, f"error: {e}"

def check_all_nodes():
    """Check all nodes and return status"""
    node_timestamps, error = get_node_timestamps()
    if error:
        return {"error": error}

    results = {
        "total": len(node_timestamps),
        "up_to_date": [],
        "needs_update": [],
        "missing_docs": [],
        "missing_comment": [],
        "by_category": {}
    }

    for node_name, node_info in node_timestamps.items():
        node_modified = node_info['modified']
        category = node_info['category']

        # Initialize category if not exists
        if category not in results["by_category"]:
            results["by_category"][category] = {
                "up_to_date": [],
                "needs_update": [],
                "missing_docs": []
            }

        doc_timestamp, status = get_doc_timestamp(node_name)

        if status == "not_found":
            results["missing_docs"].append(node_name)
            results["by_category"][category]["missing_docs"].append(node_name)
        elif status == "no_comment":
            results["missing_comment"].append(node_name)
            results["by_category"][category]["needs_update"].append({
                "node": node_name,
                "issue": "missing_comment"
            })
        elif status == "ok":
            if doc_timestamp == node_modified:
                results["up_to_date"].append(node_name)
                results["by_category"][category]["up_to_date"].append(node_name)
            else:
                update_info = {
                    "node": node_name,
                    "label": node_info['label'],
                    "category": category,
                    "current": doc_timestamp,
                    "expected": node_modified
                }
                results["needs_update"].append(update_info)
                results["by_category"][category]["needs_update"].append(update_info)

    return results

def get_specific_node(node_name):
    """Get timestamps for a specific node"""
    node_timestamps, error = get_node_timestamps()
    if error:
        return {"error": error}

    if node_name not in node_timestamps:
        return {"error": f"Node '{node_name}' not found in funnel_node.json"}

    node_info = node_timestamps[node_name]
    doc_timestamp, status = get_doc_timestamp(node_name)

    return {
        "node": node_name,
        "label": node_info['label'],
        "category": node_info['category'],
        "element_type": node_info['element_type'],
        "funnel_json_timestamp": node_info['modified'],
        "doc_timestamp": doc_timestamp,
        "doc_status": status,
        "needs_update": doc_timestamp != node_info['modified'] if doc_timestamp else True
    }

def print_results(results):
    """Pretty print the results"""
    if "error" in results:
        print(f"❌ Error: {results['error']}")
        return

    print(f"\n📊 Documentation Status Summary")
    print(f"{'='*50}")
    print(f"Total Nodes: {results['total']}")
    print(f"✅ Up to date: {len(results['up_to_date'])}")
    print(f"⚠️  Needs update: {len(results['needs_update'])}")
    print(f"❌ Missing docs: {len(results['missing_docs'])}")
    print(f"⚠️  Missing comment: {len(results['missing_comment'])}")

    if results['needs_update']:
        print(f"\n📝 Nodes Needing Documentation Updates:")
        print(f"{'-'*50}")
        for item in results['needs_update']:
            print(f"• {item['node']} ({item['label']})")
            print(f"  Category: {item['category']}")
            print(f"  Current: {item['current']}")
            print(f"  Expected: {item['expected']}")
            print()

    if results['missing_docs']:
        print(f"\n❌ Missing Documentation Files:")
        print(f"{'-'*50}")
        for node in results['missing_docs']:
            print(f"• {node}")

    if results['missing_comment']:
        print(f"\n⚠️  Files Missing Timestamp Comment:")
        print(f"{'-'*50}")
        for node in results['missing_comment']:
            print(f"• {node}")

    # Category breakdown
    print(f"\n📁 Status by Category:")
    print(f"{'-'*50}")
    for category, cat_results in results['by_category'].items():
        up_count = len(cat_results['up_to_date'])
        need_count = len(cat_results['needs_update'])
        miss_count = len(cat_results['missing_docs'])
        total = up_count + need_count + miss_count

        if total > 0:
            print(f"\n{category}:")
            print(f"  ✅ Up to date: {up_count}")
            if need_count > 0:
                print(f"  ⚠️  Needs update: {need_count}")
            if miss_count > 0:
                print(f"  ❌ Missing: {miss_count}")

def main():
    """Main execution"""
    # Show which path is being used (helpful for debugging)
    if not os.path.exists(APP_PATH):
        print(f"⚠️  Warning: App path not found: {APP_PATH}")
        print(f"📍 Current directory: {os.getcwd()}")
        print(f"💡 Tip: Run from your bench directory or inside apps/nextai")
        sys.exit(1)

    # Check if specific node was requested
    if len(sys.argv) > 1:
        node_name = sys.argv[1]
        print(f"\n🔍 Checking specific node: {node_name}")
        node_info = get_specific_node(node_name)

        if "error" in node_info:
            print(f"❌ {node_info['error']}")
        else:
            print(f"\nNode: {node_info['node']}")
            print(f"Label: {node_info['label']}")
            print(f"Category: {node_info['category']}")
            print(f"Type: {node_info['element_type']}")
            print(f"Funnel JSON: {node_info['funnel_json_timestamp']}")
            print(f"Documentation: {node_info['doc_timestamp']}")
            print(f"Status: {node_info['doc_status']}")
            print(f"Needs Update: {'Yes ⚠️' if node_info['needs_update'] else 'No ✅'}")
    else:
        # Check all nodes
        results = check_all_nodes()
        print_results(results)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

import json

import frappe
# import frappe
from frappe.model.document import Document


class CNAConversation(Document):

    def update_reference_doc(self):
        if self.reference_doc: return
        if not self.workflow: return
        funnel_variables = frappe.db.get_value("Funnel Workflow", self.workflow, "variables")
        if not funnel_variables: return
        variables = json.loads(funnel_variables)
        workflow_doc = variables.get("doc")
        if not workflow_doc: return
        reference_type = workflow_doc.get("doctype")
        reference_name = workflow_doc.get("name")
        reference_doc = f"{reference_type}::{reference_name}"
        self.reference_doc = reference_doc

    def before_save(self):
        self.update_reference_doc()

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel/funnel.py
# Copyright (c) 2023, Hybrowlabs Technologies and contributors
# For license information, please see license.txt

import frappe
from frappe.model.document import Document

from nextai.utils import is_admin_user


class Funnel(Document):
    def verify_nodes(self):
        trigger_nodes = [
            node for node in self.funnel_definition if node.element_type == "Trigger"
        ]
        public_nodes = [
            node
            for node in trigger_nodes
            if node.type in ["chatnext_assistant_public_trigger"]
        ]
        if self.funnel_type == "Public":
            if len(public_nodes) != 1:
                raise frappe.ValidationError(
                    "You can/must have only one public trigger node"
                )
            invalid_triggers = [
                node.type
                for node in trigger_nodes
                if node.type
                   not in ["chatnext_assistant_public_trigger", "on_funnel_trigger"]
            ]
            if len(invalid_triggers):
                raise frappe.ValidationError(
                    f"You cannot use these triggers in public funnel: {', '.join(invalid_triggers)}"
                )
        else:
            if len(public_nodes) > 0:
                raise frappe.ValidationError(
                    "You cannot have public trigger node in private funnel"
                )

    @frappe.whitelist()
    def publish(self):
        if self.status == "published" and self.get_doc_before_save():
            raise frappe.ValidationError("Funnel is already published")
        self.reload()
        self.verify_nodes()
        self.status = "published"
        self.archived = 0
        self.save()
        new_published_funnel = frappe.new_doc("Funnel Published")
        new_published_funnel.funnel = self.name
        new_published_funnel.published = 1
        new_published_funnel.insert()

    @frappe.whitelist()
    def unpublish(self):
        previous_published_funnels = frappe.get_all(
            "Funnel Published",
            filters={"funnel": self.name, "published": 1},
            fields=["name"],
            as_list=False,
        )
        for previous_funnel in previous_published_funnels:
            previous_funnel_doc = frappe.get_doc(
                "Funnel Published", previous_funnel.name
            )
            previous_funnel_doc.published = 0
            previous_funnel_doc.save()

    @frappe.whitelist()
    def archive(self):
        self.unpublish()
        self.reload()
        self.archived = 1
        self.save()

    @frappe.whitelist()
    def unarchive(self):
        self.reload()
        self.archived = 0
        self.status = "draft"
        self.save()

    def update_status(self):
        pre_doc = self.get_doc_before_save()
        if self.archived:
            if self.status != "archived":
                self.status = "archived"
                self.save()
        elif not self.status:
            self.status = "draft"
            self.save()
        elif pre_doc:
            if self.status == "published" and pre_doc.status == "published":
                self.status = "updated"
                self.save()

    def can_write(self):
        doc_before_save = self.get_doc_before_save()
        if not doc_before_save:
            doc_before_save = self

        if doc_before_save.funnel_type != self.funnel_type:
            frappe.throw("cannot change funnel type")

        user = frappe.session.user
        if is_admin_user(user):
            return True

        # only admin can manage public funnel
        if doc_before_save.funnel_type == "Public":
            return False

        user_roles = [
            role_dict["role"]
            for role_dict in frappe.get_all(
                "Has Role", filters={"parent": user}, fields=["role"]
            )
        ]

        if not len(user_roles):
            return False

        funnel_access = doc_before_save.funnel_access
        if not len(funnel_access):
            return False

        for access in funnel_access:
            if access.role in user_roles and access.can_modify:
                return True

        return False

    def on_update(self):
        if not self.can_write():
            frappe.throw("You don't have permission to modify this funnel")
        self.update_status()

    def after_insert(self):
        if self.status == "published":
            self.publish()


@frappe.whitelist()
def check_funnel_permission():
    user_id = frappe.session.user
    if is_admin_user():
        return True

    roles = frappe.get_roles(user_id)
    if "Funnel Admin" in roles:
        return True

    funnels = frappe.get_all("Funnel", fields=["*"], filters={"owner": user_id})
    if len(funnels):
        return True

    return False


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_list(limit=0, fields=None):
    if fields is None:
        fields = ["*"]
    funnels = frappe.get_list(
        "Funnel",
        fields=fields,
        limit=limit if limit else None
    )
    # fetch the funnel labels
    funnel_names = [funnel.name for funnel in funnels]
    funnel_labels = frappe.db.sql("""
            SELECT JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`data`, '$.node_label')) as node_label,
             (SELECT name FROM `tabFunnel` WHERE name = fd.parent) AS funnel_name
            FROM `tabFunnel Definition` fd
            where parent IN (
                SELECT name
                FROM `tabFunnel`
                where name in %(funnel_names)s
            )
            and JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`data`, '$.node_label')) is not null
            and type in ('text_label','section_label')
        """, values={
        "funnel_names": funnel_names
    }, as_dict=1)
    for funnel in funnels:
        funnel['funnel_labels'] = [label.node_label for label in funnel_labels if label['funnel_name'] == funnel.name]
    return funnels

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel/funnel_permission.py
import frappe
from nextai.utils import is_admin_user


def funnel_list_query(user):
    if not user:
        return "0"

    if user == "Administrator":
        return ""

    user_roles = [
        role_dict["role"]
        for role_dict in frappe.get_all(
            "Has Role", filters={"parent": user}, fields=["role"]
        )
    ]

    if not len(user_roles):
        return "0"

    if "System Manager" in user_roles:
        return ""

    roles_string = ", ".join([f"'{role}'" for role in user_roles])

    condition = f"""
    name in (select parent
               from `tabFunnel Access`
               where can_view = 1
                 and parenttype = 'Funnel'
                 and role in ({roles_string}))
                 or owner = '{user}'
                 and funnel_type != 'Public'
    """
    return condition


def has_super_permission(user):
    return is_admin_user(user)


def check_funnel_read_permission(doc, user=None, permission_type=None):
    if not user:
        return False

    if frappe.db.exists(doc.doctype, doc.name):
        doc = frappe.get_doc(doc.doctype, doc.name)

    if has_super_permission(user):
        return True

    if doc.funnel_type == "Public":
        return False

    funnel_access = doc.funnel_access
    if not len(funnel_access):
        return False

    user_roles = [
        role_dict["role"]
        for role_dict in frappe.get_all(
            "Has Role", filters={"parent": user}, fields=["role"]
        )
    ]

    for access in funnel_access:
        if access.role in user_roles and access.can_view:
            return True

    return True

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel/funnel_user.py
import frappe

# for backward compatibility
funnel_user_id = 'funnel@chatnext.hybrowlabs.com'


def get_funnel_user_id():
    global funnel_user_id
    funnel_user_id = frappe.db.get_single_value('ChatNext Settings', 'funnel_user')
    return funnel_user_id


def has_funnel_user():
    funnel_user = frappe.db.exists('User', get_funnel_user_id())
    if funnel_user:
        return True
    return False


def create_funnel_user(funnel_userid=None):
    funnel_user = frappe.new_doc('User')
    funnel_user.update(
        {
            'email': funnel_userid or get_funnel_user_id(),
            'first_name': "Frappe",
            'last_name': "Automation",
            'user_type': 'System User',
            "roles": [
                {
                    "role": "System Manager"
                }
            ],
        }
    )
    funnel_user.save(ignore_permissions=True)


def is_handshake_done():
    funnel_user = frappe.get_doc('User', get_funnel_user_id())
    if funnel_user.chatnext_api_key and funnel_user.chatnext_api_secret:
        return True
    return False


@frappe.whitelist()
def check():
    if has_funnel_user() and is_handshake_done():
        return True
    return False


@frappe.whitelist()
def handshake():
    if not has_funnel_user():
        create_funnel_user()
        frappe.db.commit()

    chatnext_clients = frappe.get_all('OAuth Client', {'app_name': 'ChatNext'})
    if not chatnext_clients:
        frappe.throw('Unauthorized client')

    # to avoid circular import
    from nextai.core_handshake import get_api_access_from_core, generate_authorization_code

    client = frappe.get_doc('OAuth Client', chatnext_clients[0].name)
    auth_code = generate_authorization_code(get_funnel_user_id(), client)
    token = get_api_access_from_core(auth_code, get_funnel_user_id(), client.default_redirect_uri)
    return token.get("success")

@frappe.whitelist()
def get_email_fields(doctype):
    values = {
        'doctype': doctype
    }
    fields = frappe.db.sql("""
            select
            	fieldname, fieldtype, label
            from `tabDocField`
            where
            	parent= %(doctype)s
             	and
              	((fieldtype='Link' and options='User') or (fieldtype='Link' and options='email') or (fieldtype='Link' and options='Contact') or (fieldtype='Data'));
    """, values=values, as_dict=1)
    return fields
