# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_published/funnel_published.py
# Copyright (c) 2023, Hybrowlabs Technologies and contributors
# For license information, please see license.txt

import frappe
# import frappe
from frappe.model.document import Document


class FunnelPublished(Document):
    def after_insert(self):
        self.published = 1
        funnel = frappe.get_doc("Funnel", self.funnel)
        definitions = funnel.funnel_definition
        id_maps = {}
        if not definitions:
            return

        for row in definitions:
            id_maps[row.id] = self.name + "-" + row.id

        for row in definitions:
            if row.element_type == "edge":
                try:
                    row.source = id_maps[row.source]
                    row.target = id_maps[row.target]
                except KeyError:
                    # remove edge from definition of main funnel
                    row.delete()
                    # remove edge from definition here
                    definitions.remove(row)
            row.id = id_maps[row.id]

        defination_jsons = [definition.as_dict() for definition in definitions]

        for defination_json in defination_jsons:
            defination_json.pop("name")
            def_doc = frappe.new_doc("Funnel Definition")
            def_doc.update(defination_json)
            self.funnel_definition.append(def_doc)

        # un publish all other published funnels
        previous_published_funnels = frappe.get_all(
            "Funnel Published",
            filters={"funnel": self.funnel, "published": 1, "name": ("!=", self.name)},
            fields=["name"],
            as_list=False,
        )
        self.save()
        for previous_funnel in previous_published_funnels:
            previous_funnel_doc = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Published", previous_funnel.name)
            previous_funnel_doc.published = 0
            previous_funnel_doc.save()

        "example": "example",
        "doc_example_name": src_doc.get("name"),

    # do your things -- end

    return {

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/add_comment.py
import json

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import format_with_variables


def add_comment(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)

    doctype = node_data.get("select_doctype")
    docname = node_data.get("name")
    comment = node_data.get("comment")

    docname = format_with_variables(docname, variables)
    comment = format_with_variables(comment, variables)

    doc = frappe.get_doc(doctype, docname)
    doc.add_comment('Comment', comment)
    doc.save(ignore_permissions=True)

    return {}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/add_tags_to_doc.py
import ast
import json

import frappe

from frappe.desk.doctype.tag.tag import add_tag
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import (
    format_with_variables,
)


def parse_tags(tags):
    try:
        parsed_tags = json.loads(tags)
        if isinstance(parsed_tags, list):
            return parsed_tags
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
        pass

    try:
        parsed_tags = ast.literal_eval(tags)
        if isinstance(parsed_tags, list):
            return parsed_tags
    except (ValueError, SyntaxError):
        pass

    if isinstance(tags, str):
        return [tag.strip() for tag in tags.split(",")]

    if isinstance(tags, list):
        return tags

    return []


def add_tags_to_doc(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)

    tags = format_with_variables(node_data.get("tags", ""), variables)
    doctype = node_data.get("doctype")
    docname = node_data.get("docname")

    tags = parse_tags(tags)

    doctype = format_with_variables(doctype, variables)
    docname = format_with_variables(docname, variables)

    user = frappe.session.user

    frappe.set_user("Administrator")

    for tag in tags:
        add_tag(tag, doctype, docname)

    frappe.set_user(user)

    return {action_return_keys.result: tags}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/assign_user.py
import json

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import format_with_variables


def assign_user(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)

    doctype = node_data.get("select_doctype")
    docname = node_data.get("name")
    assigned_user = node_data.get("user_id")

    if docname and doctype and assigned_user:
        docname = format_with_variables(docname, variables)
        assigned_user = format_with_variables(assigned_user, variables)

        add_function = frappe.get_attr("frappe.desk.form.assign_to.add")
        add_function({
            "assign_to": [assigned_user],
            "doctype": doctype,
            "name": docname,
            "description": f"Assignment for {doctype} {docname}",
        })
    return {}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/call_api.py
import json

import requests

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import (
    format_with_variables,
)


def call_api(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
    api_url = node_data["api_url"]
    method = node_data["method"]
    payload = node_data.get("payload")
    headers = node_data.get("headers")

    api_url = format_with_variables(api_url, variables)
    if payload:
        payload = format_with_variables(payload, variables)
        json.loads(payload)
    if headers:
        headers = format_with_variables(headers, variables)
        headers = json.loads(headers)
    else:
        headers = {}

    response = requests.request(method, api_url, headers=headers, data=payload)

    try:
        returned_data = response.json()
    except:
        returned_data = response.text

    variable_path = node_data.get("variable_path")
    if variable_path:
        variables[variable_path] = returned_data
    return {
        action_return_keys.result: {
            "payload": payload,
            "returned_data": returned_data,
        }
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/chatgpt_message.py
import json

from nextai.config.api import HOST_NAME
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import (
    format_with_variables,
)
from nextai.utils import send_authorized_core_request


def chatgpt_message(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
    prompt = node_data["prompt"]
    system_prompt = node_data.get("system_prompt")

    prompt = format_with_variables(prompt, variables)
    payload = {
        "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
    }

    if system_prompt:
        system_prompt = format_with_variables(system_prompt, variables)
        payload["messages"].insert(0, {"role": "system", "content": system_prompt})

    # # create awaiting task
    headers = {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        "origin": HOST_NAME,
    }

    response = send_authorized_core_request(
        payload,
        "POST",
        "chatnext_core.clients.ai_client.text_answer",
        headers,
    ).json()

    reply = response["message"]
    variables["chatgpt_reply"] = reply

    return {}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/chatgpt_multi_prompt.py
import json

from nextai.config.api import HOST_NAME
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import (
    format_with_variables,
)
from nextai.utils import send_authorized_core_request


def chatgpt_multi_prompt(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
    prompts = node_data["prompts"]
    system_prompt = node_data.get("system_prompt")
    extract_data = node_data.get("extract_data")
    extraction_prompt = node_data.get("extraction_prompt")
    isolate_extraction_prompt = node_data.get("isolate_extraction_prompt")

    messages = []
    if system_prompt:
        system_prompt = format_with_variables(system_prompt, variables)
        messages.append({"role": "system", "content": system_prompt})

    for prompt in prompts:
        prompt = prompt["prompt"]
        prompt = format_with_variables(prompt, variables)
        messages.append({"role": "user", "content": prompt})
        payload = {
            "messages": messages,
        }
        headers = {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            "origin": HOST_NAME,
        }
        response = send_authorized_core_request(
            payload,
            "POST",
            "chatnext_core.clients.ai_client.text_answer",
            headers,
        ).json()

        reply = response["message"]
        messages.append({"role": "assistant", "content": reply})
        variables["chatgpt_reply"] = reply

    if extract_data:
        extraction_prompt = format_with_variables(extraction_prompt, variables)
        if isolate_extraction_prompt:
            messages = []
        messages.append({"role": "user", "content": extraction_prompt})

        payload = {
            "messages": messages,
        }
        headers = {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            "origin": HOST_NAME,
        }
        response = send_authorized_core_request(
            payload,
            "POST",
            "chatnext_core.clients.ai_client.json_answer",
            headers,
        ).json()
        reply = response["message"]
        variables["chatgpt_response"] = json.loads(reply)
    return {}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/chatnext_assistant_ai_qa_agent.py
import json
import uuid

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys, task_states
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.chat_agent_apis import (
    create_thread,
    interact_with_ai,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import (
    update_task_status,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import (
    format_with_variables,
)


def chatnext_assistant_ai_qa_agent(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
    assistant_id = node_data["assistant_id"]
    openai_thread_id = create_thread()

    task_name = variables["task_name"]
    variables["openai_thread_id"] = openai_thread_id
    message_body = node_data.get("first_message_to_send")
    if message_body:
        message_body = format_with_variables(message_body, variables)
    if message_body:
        ai_response = interact_with_ai(
            assistant_id, variables["openai_thread_id"], message_body
        )
        variables["last_ai_response"] = ai_response
        response_type = ai_response.get("type")
        if response_type == "message":
            message_text = ai_response.get("content")
            if message_text:
                user_id = variables.get("user_id")
                session = variables.get("session_id")
                conversation = frappe.get_doc(
                    {
                        "doctype": "CNA Conversation",
                        "status": "pending",
                        "user": user_id,
                        "session": session,
                        "id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
                        "author": "Bot",
                        "message_type": "Text",
                        "message": message_text,
                    }
                )
                conversation.insert()
        else:
            raise Exception("Invalid response type received from AI assistant")
    update_task_status(task_name, status=task_states.AWAITING_TRIGGER)

    return {
        action_return_keys.result: {
            "openai_thread_id": openai_thread_id,
            "ai_response": variables.get("last_ai_response"),
        },
        action_return_keys.skip_task_creation: True,
        action_return_keys.skip_task_update: True,
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/chatnext_assistant_document_approval.py
import json

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import task_states, action_return_keys
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import (
    update_task_status,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import (
    format_with_variables,
)


def chatnext_assistant_document_approval(action_node, variables):
    action_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
    target_doc_type = action_data["doc_type"]
    target_doc_name = action_data["doc_name"]
    is_role_approval = action_data.get("role_approval")
    open_chatnext_assistant = action_data.get("open_chatnext_assistant")
    required_fields = action_data.get("required_fields")

    target_user = None
    target_role = None
    if is_role_approval:
        target_role = format_with_variables(action_data["role_name"], variables)
    else:
        target_user = format_with_variables(action_data["user_id"], variables)

    if required_fields:
        required_fields = format_with_variables(required_fields, variables)

    target_doc_name = format_with_variables(target_doc_name, variables)
    task_name = variables["task_name"]

    approval_doc = frappe.get_doc(
        {
            "doctype": "CN Doc Approval",
            "status": "pending",
            "user": target_user,
            "role": target_role,
            "use_role": bool(is_role_approval),
            "doc_type": target_doc_type,
            "doc_name": target_doc_name,
            "task": task_name,
            "open_chatnext_assistant": bool(open_chatnext_assistant),
            "required_fields": required_fields,
        }
    )
    approval_doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)

    target_users = [target_user]
    if is_role_approval:
        user_roles = frappe.get_all(
            "Has Role",
            filters={"role": target_role, "parenttype": "user"},
            fields=["parent"],
        )
        target_users = [ur.parent for ur in user_roles]

    add_function = frappe.get_attr("frappe.desk.form.assign_to.add")
    for _target_user in target_users:
        add_function(
            {
                "assign_to": [_target_user],
                "doctype": target_doc_type,
                "name": target_doc_name,
                "description": "You have a new approval request",
            }
        )

    update_task_status(task_name, status=task_states.AWAITING_TRIGGER)

    return {
        action_return_keys.skip_task_creation: True,
        action_return_keys.skip_task_update: True,
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/chatnext_assistant_form_question.py
import json
import uuid

import frappe
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys, task_states
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.triggers.chatnext_assistant_trigger import create_form_prefill_data
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import (
    update_task_status,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import (
    format_with_variables,
)


def get_form_json(action_data, variables):
    is_dynamic_form = action_data.get("is_dynamic_form")
    selected_form = action_data.get("selected_form")
    is_variable_form = action_data.get("is_variable_form")
    formio_variable = action_data.get("formio_variable")

    if is_variable_form and formio_variable:
        formio_data = variables[formio_variable]
    else:
        formio_data = (
            action_data.get("formio")
            if is_dynamic_form
            else frappe.get_value("Microapp Form Widget", selected_form, "custom_form_data")
        )
        if not formio_data and not is_dynamic_form and frappe.db.exists("Microapp Form Widget", selected_form):
            raise Exception(f"MicroApp Form {selected_form} not found")
    if isinstance(formio_data, str):
        formio_data = json.loads(formio_data)
    return formio_data


def create_form_conversation(
    action_data, variables, user_id: str, session: str,
    on_submit=None, workflow_id: str = None, prefill_data=None,
    formio_data=None, enable_data_update_on_submit: bool = None,
    show_in_chat_history: bool = False,
):
    formio_data = formio_data or get_form_json(action_data, variables)
    if isinstance(formio_data, str):
        formio_data = json.loads(formio_data)

    prefill_form_data = action_data.get("prefill_data")
    prefill_form_data = format_with_variables(prefill_form_data, variables)
    prefill_form_data = {
        **variables.get("common_assistant_prefill_data", {}),
        **(json.loads(prefill_form_data) if prefill_form_data else {}),
        **(prefill_data or {}),
    }

    workflow_id = workflow_id or variables.get("workflow_name")

    conversation = frappe.get_doc(
        {
            "doctype": "CNA Conversation",
            "status": "pending",
            "user": user_id,
            "session": session,
            "id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
            "author": "Bot",
            "message_type": "Form",
            "workflow": workflow_id,
            "show_in_chat_history": show_in_chat_history,
            "message": json.dumps(
                {
                    "form": formio_data,
                    "prefill_data": prefill_form_data,
                    'on_submit': on_submit,
                    'enable_data_update_on_submit': enable_data_update_on_submit
                },
                indent=2,
                default=str,
            ),
        }
    )
    conversation.insert()


def chatnext_assistant_form_question(action_node, variables):
    action_data = json.loads(action_node.data)

    workflow_id = variables.get("workflow_name")
    session = variables.get("session_id")
    user_id = variables.get("user_id")

    formio_data = get_form_json(action_data, variables)
    prefill_data = create_form_prefill_data(formio_data, variables)
    enable_data_update_on_submit = action_data.get('enableDataUpdateOnSubmit')
    show_in_chat_history = action_data.get("show_in_chat_history")
    create_form_conversation(
        action_data, variables, user_id, session, formio_data=formio_data, workflow_id=workflow_id,
        enable_data_update_on_submit=enable_data_update_on_submit, prefill_data=prefill_data,
        show_in_chat_history=show_in_chat_history
    )

    task_name = variables["task_name"]
    update_task_status(task_name, status=task_states.AWAITING_TRIGGER)

    return {
        action_return_keys.skip_task_creation: True,
        action_return_keys.skip_task_update: True,
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/chatnext_assistant_multi_actions.py
import json
import uuid

import frappe
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import task_states, action_return_keys
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import (
    update_task_status,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import (
    format_with_variables,
)


def chatnext_assistant_multi_actions(action_node, variables):
    action_data = json.loads(action_node.data)

    target_doc_type = action_data["doctype"]
    target_doc_name = action_data["doc_variable"]
    target_user_data = action_data["user_data"]
    open_chatnext_assistant = action_data.get("open_chatnext_assistant")
    post_action_creation_message = action_data.get("postActionCreationMessage")
    options_list = [item['option'] for item in action_data.get('buttons', [])]
    options_list_str = json.dumps(options_list)

    target_user_data = format_with_variables(target_user_data, variables)
    target_doc_name = format_with_variables(target_doc_name, variables)
    task_name = variables["task_name"]

    new_todo_doc = frappe.new_doc("ToDo")
    new_todo_doc.description = f"Action Required for {target_doc_type} {target_doc_name}"
    new_todo_doc.custom_approval_type = "Multi Actions"
    new_todo_doc.reference_type = target_doc_type
    new_todo_doc.reference_name = target_doc_name
    new_todo_doc.status = "Open"
    new_todo_doc.custom_funnel_task = task_name
    new_todo_doc.custom_doctype_actions = options_list_str
    new_todo_doc.custom_open_chatnext_assistant_on_action = bool(open_chatnext_assistant)
    if frappe.db.exists("User", target_user_data):
        new_todo_doc.allocated_to = target_user_data
    else:
        new_todo_doc.role = target_user_data

    new_todo_doc.save(ignore_permissions=True)

    if frappe.db.exists('User', target_user_data):
        target_users = [target_user_data]
    else:
        user_roles = frappe.get_all(
            "Has Role",
            filters={"role": target_user_data, "parenttype": "user"},
            fields=["parent"],
        )
        target_users = [ur.parent for ur in user_roles]

    # share doc
    if not action_data.get('do_not_assign') and target_user_data not in ["System Manager", 'Administrator']:
        user_id = frappe.session.user
        frappe.session.user = 'Administrator'
        for _target_user in target_users:
            if frappe.db.exists("DocShare", {"user": _target_user, "share_doctype": target_doc_type, "share_name": target_doc_name, "read": 1}):
                continue
            share_doc = frappe.new_doc("DocShare")
            share_doc.user = _target_user
            share_doc.share_doctype = target_doc_type
            share_doc.share_name = target_doc_name
            share_doc.read = 1
            share_doc.save(ignore_permissions=True)
        frappe.session.user = user_id

    frappe.enqueue(
        "frappe.desk.doctype.notification_log.notification_log.make_notification_logs",
        enqueue_after_commit=True,
        users=target_users,
        now=False,
        doc={
            "type": "Share",
            "document_type": target_doc_type,
            "subject": f"Action Required for {target_doc_type} {target_doc_name}",
            "document_name": target_doc_name,
            "from_user": frappe.session.user,
        },
    )

    if open_chatnext_assistant:
        if post_action_creation_message:
            session_id = variables.get("session_id")
            conversation = frappe.get_doc(
                {
                    "doctype": "CNA Conversation",
                    "status": "default",
                    "user": variables.get("user_id"),
                    "session": session_id,
                    "workflow": variables.get("workflow_name"),
                    "id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
                    "author": "Bot",
                    "message_type": 'Text',
                    "message": str(post_action_creation_message),
                }
            )
            conversation.insert()

    update_task_status(task_name, status=task_states.AWAITING_TRIGGER)

    return {
        action_return_keys.skip_task_update: True
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/chatnext_assistant_show_file.py
import json
import uuid

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import (
    format_with_variables,
)


def chatnext_assistant_show_file(action_node, variables):
    action_data = json.loads(action_node.data)

    session = variables.get("session_id")
    user_id = variables.get("user_id")

    file_path = action_data.get("file_path")
    open_file_doc = action_data.get("open_file_doc")
    enable_download = action_data.get("enable_download")
    file_path = format_with_variables(file_path, variables)

    filename = None
    file_docname = None
    if frappe.db.exists("File", file_path):
        file_doc = frappe.get_doc("File", file_path)
        file_path = file_doc.file_url
        filename = file_doc.file_name
        file_docname = file_doc.name
    elif open_file_doc:
        file_doc = frappe.get_doc("File", {"file_url": file_path})
        file_path = file_doc.file_url
        filename = file_doc.file_name
        file_docname = file_doc.name

    conversation = frappe.get_doc(
        {
            "doctype": "CNA Conversation",
            "status": "default",
            "user": user_id,
            "session": session,
            "id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
            "author": "Bot",
            "message_type": "Preview",
            "message": json.dumps(
                {
                    "type": "file",
                    "file_url": file_path,
                    "filename": filename,
                    "file_docname": file_docname,
                    "open_file_doc": open_file_doc,
                    "enable_download": enable_download,
                },
                indent=2,
                default=str,
            ),
        }
    )
    conversation.insert()
    return {}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/chatnext_assistant_trigger.py
import json
import uuid

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys, task_states
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import (
    create_new_task,
)

true = True
false = False

get_docname_question = {
    "components": [
        {
            "label": "Select Doc",
            "widget": "choicesjs",
            "tableView": true,
            "dataSrc": "url",
            "data": {
                "url": "/api/resource/{{data.doctype}}?limit=0",
                "headers": [{"key": "", "value": ""}],
            },
            "dataType": "string",
            "idPath": "name",
            "valueProperty": "name",
            "template": "<span>{{ item.name }}</span>",
            "validate": {"required": true},
            "validateWhenHidden": false,
            "key": "docname",
            "type": "select",
            "selectValues": "data",
            "disableLimit": false,
            "noRefreshOnScroll": false,
            "input": true,
        },
        {
            "type": "button",
            "label": "Submit",
            "key": "submit",
            "disableOnInvalid": true,
            "input": true,
            "tableView": false,
        },
    ]
}


def chatnext_assistant_trigger(action_node, variables):
    session = variables.get("session_id")
    user_id = variables.get("user_id")

    conversation = frappe.get_doc(
        {
            "doctype": "CNA Conversation",
            "status": "pending",
            "user": user_id,
            "session": session,
            "id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
            "author": "Bot",
            "message_type": "Form",
            "message": json.dumps(
                {
                    "form": get_docname_question,
                    "prefill_data": {
                        "doctype": variables.get("doctype"),
                    },
                },
                indent=2,
                default=str,
            ),
        }
    )
    conversation.insert()

    create_new_task(
        variables.get("workflow"),
        action_node.id,
        action_node.id,
        "",
        task_info={
            "triggered_by": "ai for docname input",
        },
        status=task_states.AWAITING_TRIGGER,
        target_node_name=action_node.type,
        target_node=action_node,
    )

    return {
        action_return_keys.skip_task_creation: True,
        action_return_keys.skip_task_update: True,
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/chatnext_assistant_ui_action.py
import json
import uuid

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import (
    format_with_variables,
)


def chatnext_assistant_ui_action(action_node, variables):
    action_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
    action_type = action_data.get("action_type")

    session = variables.get("session_id")
    user_id = variables.get("user_id")

    message = dict({"action_type": action_type})

    if action_type == "create_document":
        message["doctype"] = action_data["doctype"]
        prefill_json = action_data["prefill_json"]
        prefill_data = format_with_variables(prefill_json, variables)
        prefill_json = json.loads(prefill_data)
        message["prefill_json"] = prefill_json

    if action_type == "update_document":
        document_data = action_data["document_json"]
        document_data = format_with_variables(document_data, variables)
        document_data = json.loads(document_data)
        message["document_data"] = document_data

    if action_type == "redirect_to":
        route = action_data["route"]
        route = format_with_variables(route, variables)
        message["route"] = route

    conversation = frappe.get_doc(
        {
            "doctype": "CNA Conversation",
            "status": "pending",
            "user": user_id,
            "session": session,
            "id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
            "author": "Bot",
            "message_type": "UI Action",
            "message": json.dumps(
                message,
                indent=2,
                default=str,
            ),
        }
    )
    conversation.insert()
    return {}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/chatnext_dynamic_multi_actions.py
import json
from typing import Optional
import frappe
from frappe.core.doctype.communication.email import make as create_email
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import task_states, action_return_keys
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import update_task_status
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.todo_utils import create_approval_todo
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.actions.fetch_approval_matrix import fetch_approval_matrix
from frappe.utils.jinja import render_template
from frappe import _
def get_employee_or_user_field(doctype: str, name: str, employee_doc_field: str) -> Optional[str]:
    doc = frappe.get_doc(doctype, name)
    emp_name = None
    preferred_fields = ("employee", "employee_id")
    meta = doc.meta

    for fn in preferred_fields:
        if doc.get(fn):
            df = meta.get_field(fn)
            if not df or df.fieldtype != "Link" or df.options != "Employee":
                continue
            emp_name = doc.get(fn)
            break

    if not emp_name:
        for df in meta.fields:
            if getattr(df, "fieldtype", "") == "Link" and getattr(df, "options", "") == "Employee":
                val = doc.get(df.fieldname)
                if val:
                    emp_name = val
                    break

    if not emp_name:
        for df in meta.fields:
            if getattr(df, "fieldtype", "") == "Link" and getattr(df, "options", "") == "User":
                val = doc.get(df.fieldname)
                if val:
                    return val
        return None

    emp_meta = frappe.get_meta("Employee")
    emp_df = emp_meta.get_field(employee_doc_field)
    if not emp_df:
        raise ValueError(_("Field '{0}' does not exist in Employee").format(employee_doc_field))
    employee = frappe.get_doc("Employee", emp_name)
    field_value = employee.get(employee_doc_field)

    if not field_value:
        return None

    if emp_df.fieldtype == "Link" and emp_df.options == "User":
        return field_value

    if emp_df.fieldtype == "Link" and emp_df.options == "Employee":
        return frappe.get_value("Employee", field_value, "user_id")

    if employee_doc_field in {"user_id", "user"}:
        return field_value

    return None


def chatnext_dynamic_multi_actions(action_node, variables):
    try:
        node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
        variable_doc=variables.get("doc")
        variables["doctype"] = variable_doc.get("doctype")
        variables["docname"] = variable_doc.get("name")
        manual_approval_matrix = node_data.get("selectApprovalMatrix")
        check_manual = node_data.get("selectManually")
        variable_matrix = node_data.get("variableMatrix")
        if check_manual and manual_approval_matrix:
            matrix_name = manual_approval_matrix
        elif  not check_manual and variable_matrix:
            variable_matrix_name = node_data.get("variableMatrix")
            matrix_name = variables.get(variable_matrix_name)
        else:
            result = fetch_approval_matrix(action_node, variables)
            matching_rules = result.get("matching_rules") or []
            matrix_name = matching_rules[0].get("name") if matching_rules else None
        if not matrix_name:
            raise ValueError("No matching approval rules found")
        matrix_doc = frappe.get_doc("Approval Policy Matrix", matrix_name)
        approval_mode = matrix_doc.approval_mode or "Sequential"
        allow_skip = matrix_doc.allow_skip_missing_level or 0
        restart_from_rejection = matrix_doc.restart_from_rejection_point or 0
        todo_category=matrix_doc.todo_category or "Approval Matrix"

        approval_stages = [stage.as_dict() for stage in matrix_doc.approval_stages]
        if not approval_stages:
            raise ValueError("No approval stages defined")

        if restart_from_rejection:
            prev = frappe.get_all("Approval Tracker", filters={
                "doc_type": variables["doctype"],
                "doc_name": variables["docname"],
                "approval_matrix": matrix_name,
                "status": "Rejected",
                "restart_from_rejection_point": 1
            }, order_by="creation desc", limit=1)
            if prev:
                tracker = frappe.get_doc("Approval Tracker", prev[0].name)
                rejected = next((l for l in tracker.approval_logs if l.status == "Rejected"), None)
                if rejected:
                    rejected.status = "Pending"
                    rejected.approval_time = None
                    rejected.save()
                    create_approval_todo(tracker.doctype, tracker.name, rejected)
                    update_task_status(variables["task_name"], task_states.AWAITING_TRIGGER)
                    tracker.status = "Pending"
                    tracker.save(ignore_permissions=True)
                    return {
                        action_return_keys.result: {"approval_tracker": tracker.name},
                        action_return_keys.skip_task_creation: True,
                        action_return_keys.skip_task_update: True
                    }

        approval_tracker = frappe.get_doc({
            "doctype": "Approval Tracker",
            "status": "Pending",
            "doc_type": variables["doctype"],
            "doc_name": variables["docname"],
            "task": variables["task_name"],
            "approval_matrix": matrix_name,
            "current_approval_step": 0,
            "approval_mode": approval_mode,
            "approval_stages": approval_stages,
            "allow_skip_missing_level": allow_skip,
            "restart_from_rejection_point": restart_from_rejection,
            "todo_category":todo_category
        }).insert(ignore_permissions=True)

        doc = frappe.get_doc(variables["doctype"], variables["docname"])

        def add_log_or_skip(stage, user=None, role=None):
            approver_type = stage.get("approver_type")
            update_field = stage.get("update_field")
            update_value = stage.get("update_value")
            email_template = stage.get("email_template")
            approval_email_recipient=stage.get("approval_email_recipient")
            field_to_update_on_rejection = stage.get("field_to_update_on_rejection")
            new_value_on_rejection = stage.get("new_value_on_rejection")
            rejection_email_template = stage.get("rejection_email_template")
            rejection_email_recipient = stage.get("rejection_email_recipient")
            form_for_approval = stage.get("form_for_approval")
            update_data_on_approval_form_submit=stage.get("update_data_on_approval_form_submit")
            update_data_on_reject_form_submit=stage.get("update_data_on_reject_form_submit")
            form_for_rejection = stage.get("form_for_rejection")
            approval_timeline_in_days = stage.get("approval_timeline_in_days")
            before_approval_email_template=stage.get("before_approval_email_template")
            cc_applicable=stage.get("cc_applicable")
            cc_based_on=stage.get("cc_based_on")
            cc_employee_field=stage.get("cc_employee_field")
            cc_role=stage.get("cc_role")
            allow_revoke=stage.get("allow_revoke")
            allow_sendback=stage.get("allow_sendback")
            sendback_email_template=stage.get("sendback_email_template")
            escalation_applicable=stage.get("escalation_applicable")
            auto_approvereject_applicable=stage.get("auto_approvereject_applicable")
            auto_action_type=stage.get("auto_action_type")
            custom_escalation_user=""
            if stage.get("escalation_applicable") and stage.get("escalation_employee_field") and stage.get("escalation_based_on")=="Employee Field":
                custom_escalation_user=get_employee_or_user_field(approval_tracker.doc_type,approval_tracker.doc_name,stage.get("escalation_employee_field"))
            if stage.get("escalation_applicable") and stage.get("escalation_based_on")=="User":
                custom_escalation_user=stage.get("escalation_user")
            custom_doctype_actions_with_form=[]
            if form_for_approval:
                custom_doctype_actions_with_form.append("Approve")
            if form_for_rejection:
                custom_doctype_actions_with_form.append("Reject")
            if allow_sendback:
                custom_doctype_actions_with_form.append("Send Back")
            if not user and not role:
                if allow_skip:
                    approval_tracker.append("approval_logs", {
                        "status": "Skipped",
                        "role": None,
                        "user": None,
                        "approver_type": approver_type,
                        "update_field": update_field,
                        "update_value": update_value,
                        "email_template": email_template,
                        "approval_email_recipient": approval_email_recipient,
                        "field_to_update_on_rejection": field_to_update_on_rejection,
                        "new_value_on_rejection": new_value_on_rejection,
                        "rejection_email_template": rejection_email_template,
                        "rejection_email_recipient": rejection_email_recipient,
                        "form_for_approval": form_for_approval,
                        "update_data_on_approval_form_submit":update_data_on_approval_form_submit,
                        "update_data_on_reject_form_submit":update_data_on_reject_form_submit,
                        "form_for_rejection": form_for_rejection,
                        "todo_category":todo_category,
                        "cc_applicable":cc_applicable,
                        "cc_based_on":cc_based_on,
                        "cc_employee_field":cc_employee_field,
                        "cc_role":cc_role,
                        "allow_revoke":allow_revoke,
                        "allow_sendback":allow_sendback,
                        sendback_email_template:sendback_email_template,
                        "approval_timeline_in_days": approval_timeline_in_days,
                        "before_approval_email_template":before_approval_email_template

                    })
                    return False
                else:
                    raise ValueError(f"Approver not found and skipping not allowed for stage: {stage}")

            log = approval_tracker.append("approval_logs", {
                "status": "Pending",
                "role": role,
                "user": user,
                "approver_type": approver_type,
                "update_field": update_field,
                "update_value": update_value,
                "email_template": email_template,
                "approval_email_recipient": approval_email_recipient,
                "field_to_update_on_rejection": field_to_update_on_rejection,
                "new_value_on_rejection": new_value_on_rejection,
                "rejection_email_template": rejection_email_template,
                "rejection_email_recipient": rejection_email_recipient,
                "form_for_approval": form_for_approval,
                "form_for_rejection": form_for_rejection,
                "update_data_on_approval_form_submit":update_data_on_approval_form_submit,
                "update_data_on_reject_form_submit":update_data_on_reject_form_submit,
                "todo_category":todo_category,
                "approval_timeline_in_days": approval_timeline_in_days,
                "before_approval_email_template":before_approval_email_template,
                "cc_applicable":cc_applicable,
                "cc_based_on":cc_based_on,
                "cc_employee_field":cc_employee_field,
                "cc_role":cc_role,
                "allow_revoke":allow_revoke,
                "allow_sendback":allow_sendback,
                "sendback_email_template":sendback_email_template,
                "custom_doctype_actions_with_form": custom_doctype_actions_with_form,
                "escalation_applicable":escalation_applicable,
                "custom_escalation_user":custom_escalation_user,
                "auto_approvereject_applicable":auto_approvereject_applicable,
                "auto_action_type":auto_action_type

            })
            log.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
            create_approval_todo(approval_tracker.doctype, approval_tracker.name, log)

            return True

        def resolve_stage(stage):
            t = stage.get("approver_type")
            if t == "Level":
                level = stage.get("level")
                emp_field = matrix_doc.select_employee_field
                parent_link = matrix_doc.parent_employee_link_field
                emp = fetch_employee_from_doc(doc.doctype, doc.name, emp_field) \
                    if parent_link == "doc" \
                    else fetch_employee_from_linked_doc(doc.doctype, doc.name, emp_field, parent_link)
                user = fetch_reporting_manager_user(emp, level) if emp else None
                return add_log_or_skip(stage, user=user, role=stage.get("role"))

            if t == "User":
                u = stage.get("user")
                return add_log_or_skip(stage, user=u if frappe.db.exists("User", u) else None)

            if t == "Role":
                r = stage.get("role")
                has = frappe.get_all("Has Role", filters={"role": r}, fields=["parent"])
                return add_log_or_skip(stage, user=None, role=r if has else None)

            if t == "Field":
                u = doc.get(stage.get("user_field"))
                return add_log_or_skip(stage, user=u if frappe.db.exists("User", u) else None)

            if t=="Employee Doc Field":
                u = stage.get("employee_doc_field")
                user=get_employee_or_user_field(doc.doctype,doc.name,u)
                return add_log_or_skip(stage, user=user if frappe.db.exists("User", user) else None)

            return add_log_or_skip(stage)

        if approval_mode == "Sequential":
            for idx, stage in enumerate(approval_stages):
                if resolve_stage(stage):
                    approval_tracker.current_approval_step = idx
                    approval_tracker.save(ignore_permissions=True)
                    break
            else:
                approval_tracker.status = "Approved"
                approval_tracker.save(ignore_permissions=True)
        elif approval_mode == "Parallel":
            for stage in approval_stages:
                resolve_stage(stage)
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported approval mode: {approval_mode}")

        update_task_status(variables["task_name"], status=task_states.AWAITING_TRIGGER)
        return {
            action_return_keys.result: {"approval_tracker": approval_tracker.name},
            action_return_keys.skip_task_creation: True,
            action_return_keys.skip_task_update: True
        }

    except Exception as e:
        frappe.log_error(frappe.get_traceback(), "chatnext_dynamic_multi_actions")
        raise Exception(f"[Approval Tracker Node Error] {str(e)}")

# ---------------------- Helper Functions ----------------------

def fetch_employee_from_doc(doctype, docname, field_name):
    try:
        doc = frappe.get_doc(doctype, docname)
        actual_field_name = field_name.split(" (")[0]
        return doc.get(actual_field_name)
    except Exception:
        return None


def fetch_employee_from_linked_doc(doctype, docname, field_name, linked_field_name):
    try:
        doc = frappe.get_doc(doctype, docname)
        actual_field_name = field_name.split(" (")[0]
        meta = frappe.get_meta(doctype)
        linked_field_meta = next((f for f in meta.fields if f.fieldname == linked_field_name), None)
        if not linked_field_meta:
            return None
        linked_doctype = linked_field_meta.options
        linked_docname = doc.get(linked_field_name)
        if not linked_docname:
            return None
        linked_doc = frappe.get_doc(linked_doctype, linked_docname)
        return linked_doc.get(actual_field_name)
    except Exception:
        return None


def fetch_reporting_manager_user(employee_id, level):
    try:
        employee_doc = frappe.get_doc("Employee", employee_id)
        manager_id = employee_doc.reports_to
        if not manager_id:
            return None
        manager_doc = frappe.get_doc("Employee", manager_id)
        if level == "L1":
            return manager_doc.user_id
        if level == "L2":
            return fetch_reporting_manager_user(manager_id, "L1")
        if level == "L3":
            return fetch_reporting_manager_user(manager_id, "L2")
        if level == "L4":
            return fetch_reporting_manager_user(manager_id, "L3")
        if level == "L5":
            return fetch_reporting_manager_user(manager_id, "L4")
        return None
    except Exception:
        return None

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/copy_doc_fields.py
import json

import frappe
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import format_with_variables


def copy_doc_fields(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
    source_doctype = node_data["source_doctype"]
    source_docname = format_with_variables(node_data["source_docname"], variables)
    source_doc = frappe.get_doc(source_doctype, source_docname)

    target_doctype = node_data["target_doctype"]
    target_docname = format_with_variables(node_data["target_docname"], variables)
    target_doc = frappe.get_doc(target_doctype, target_docname)

    doc_fields = node_data['fields']

    for doc_field in doc_fields:
        source_field = doc_field['source_field']
        target_field = doc_field['target_field']
        source_value = source_doc.get(source_field)
        target_doc.set(target_field, source_value)

    target_doc.save()

    return {}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/create_doctype.py
import json
import frappe
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import format_with_variables
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys


def create_doctype(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)

    doctype_info = node_data.get("docTypeJson")
    if doctype_info:
        doctype_info = format_with_variables(doctype_info, variables)

    doctype_info = json.loads(doctype_info)

    new_doc = frappe.new_doc(doctype_info["doctype"])
    doctype_info.pop("doctype")
    new_doc.update(doctype_info)

    new_doc.insert()

    return {
        action_return_keys.result: new_doc.as_dict()
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/create_funnel_trigger_link.py
import json
import uuid

import frappe
from nextai.config.api import HOST_NAME
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import (
    format_with_variables,
)


def get_funnel_definition_id(funnel_name):
    # Fetch the latest record based on creation date
    latest_funnel_published_record = frappe.get_all(
        "Funnel Published",
        filters={"funnel": funnel_name, "published": 1},
        fields=["name"],
        order_by="creation desc",
        limit=1,
    )

    if latest_funnel_published_record:

        doc = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Published", latest_funnel_published_record[0].name)
        child_table = doc.get("funnel_definition")
        for child in child_table:
            if child.type == "chatnext_assistant_public_trigger":
                return child.name
    return None


def create_funnel_trigger_link(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)

    variables_info = node_data.get("variablesJson")
    funnel_name = node_data.get("selectFunnel")
    multiple_usage = node_data.get("multiple_usage")
    variable_to_store_url = node_data.get("variable")
    if variables_info:
        variables_info = format_with_variables(variables_info, variables)
    funnel_definition = get_funnel_definition_id(funnel_name)

    new_doc = frappe.new_doc("CN Link Funnel Trigger")
    new_doc.assistant_trigger_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    new_doc.execution_count = 0
    if variables_info:
        new_doc.variables = json.loads(variables_info)
    new_doc.multiple_usage = 1 if multiple_usage else 0
    new_doc.funnel_definition = funnel_definition
    new_doc.insert()
    url = HOST_NAME + "/chatnext_assistant?trigger=" + new_doc.assistant_trigger_id
    variables[variable_to_store_url] = url
    return {action_return_keys.result: url}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/create_google_meet_link.py
import datetime
import json

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import (
    format_with_variables,
)
from nextai.utils import send_authorized_core_request


def create_google_meet_link(action_node, variables):
    # Load the action node data
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
    meet_title = node_data.get("meet_title")
    description = node_data.get("description")
    meeting_date_time = node_data.get("meeting_date_time")
    duration = node_data.get("duration_in_minutes")
    creator_mail_id = node_data.get("creator_mail_id")
    attendees_list = node_data.get("attendees_list")
    credential = node_data.get("credential")

    # Format variables in the data
    meet_title = format_with_variables(meet_title, variables)
    description = format_with_variables(description, variables)
    meeting_date_time = format_with_variables(meeting_date_time, variables)
    duration = format_with_variables(duration, variables)
    creator_mail_id = format_with_variables(creator_mail_id, variables)
    attendees_list = format_with_variables(attendees_list, variables)
    credential = format_with_variables(credential, variables)

    # Prepare the attendees list
    attendees_list = [x.strip() for x in attendees_list.split(",")]

    try:
        data_time = datetime.datetime.strptime(meeting_date_time, '%Y-%m-%d %H:%M') \
            if len(meeting_date_time) == 16 else \
            datetime.datetime.strptime(meeting_date_time, '%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')
        meeting_date_time = data_time.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')
    except ValueError:
        raise ValueError(f"Invalid meeting datetime format: {meeting_date_time}")

    payload = {
        "credential": credential,
        "summary": meet_title,
        "description": description,
        "duration_minutes": duration,
        "impersonate_user": creator_mail_id,
        "user_mails": attendees_list,
        "meeting_datetime": meeting_date_time,
    }
    # Send the request to the Google Meet API
    response = send_authorized_core_request(
        payload, "POST", "chatnext_core.clients.google_client.generate_google_meet_link"
    )

    if response.status_code != 200:
        error_message = response.text or "An error occurred while generating the Google Meet link."
        raise Exception(f"Error {response.status_code}: {error_message}")

    meet_link = response.json().get("message")

    meet_link_variable = node_data.get("answer_var")
    variables[meet_link_variable] = meet_link

    return {action_return_keys.result: meet_link}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/create_print_file.py

def create_print_file(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
    doctype = node_data["doctype"]
    docname = format_with_variables(node_data["docname"], variables)
    variable_path = node_data["variable_path"]

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/data_to_doc.py
import json

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import (
    format_with_variables,
)
from nextai.utils import send_authorized_core_request


def data_to_doc(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)

    if not node_data.get("doctype_data") or not node_data.get("doctype"):
        raise Exception("No data or doctype found in variables")

    doctype = node_data.get("doctype")
    option_data = node_data.get("doctype_data")
    select_doctype_fields = node_data.get("select_doctype_fields")
    data = format_with_variables(option_data, variables)

    fields = frappe.get_meta(doctype).fields
    meta = [
        # add mandatory if field is required
        field_str
        for d in fields
        if (
            d.fieldtype
            not in [
                "Section Break",
                "Column Break",
                "Read Only",
                "Tab Break",
                "Button",
                "Link",
            ]
        )
        and (
            field_str := (
                f"{d.fieldname} {'(mandatory)' if d.reqd is True or d.reqd == 1 else ''}"
                + (
                    " value one of ({})".format(d.options.replace("\n", ","))
                    if d.fieldtype == "Select"
                    else ""
                )
                if d.fieldname in select_doctype_fields
                else ""
            )
        )
        and field_str
    ]

    host = frappe.get_site_config().get("host_name")
    payload = {
        "data": data,
        "document_meta": ", ".join(meta),
        "doctype": doctype,
    }

    # # create awaiting task
    headers = {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        "origin": host or "http://127.0.0.1:8000",
    }

    response = send_authorized_core_request(
        payload,
        "POST",
        "chatnext_core.clients.ai_client.create_doctype",
        headers,
    ).json()

    doc_info = response["message"]

    if (
        node_data.get("doc_variable") is not None
        and node_data.get("doc_variable").strip() != ""
    ):
        ocr_path = node_data.get("doc_variable")
        variables[ocr_path] = json.loads(doc_info)

    return {action_return_keys.result: json.loads(doc_info)}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/exec_python.py
import json

from frappe.utils.safe_exec import safe_exec

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys


def exec_py_exp(py_exp, variables):
    py_exp = py_exp.replace('\n', '\n  ')
    py_exp = "def returned_function(variables):\n  " + py_exp
    _g, _l = safe_exec(py_exp)
    returned_function = _g.get("returned_function")
    return returned_function(variables)


def exec_python(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
    py_code = node_data["python_code"]
    result = exec_py_exp(py_code, variables)
    return {
        action_return_keys.result: result
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/fetch_approval_matrix.py
import frappe
from datetime import datetime
import json

EMPLOYEE_FIELDNAME_GUESSES = ("employee", "employee_id", "emp", "emp_id")
def get_user_from_doc(doctype: str, docname: str) -> str | None:
    if not (doctype and docname):
        return None
    meta = frappe.get_meta(doctype)
    employee_field = next(
        (
            df.fieldname
            for df in meta.fields
            if df.fieldname in EMPLOYEE_FIELDNAME_GUESSES
            or (df.fieldtype == "Link" and df.options in ("Employee", "Employee Master"))
        ),
        None,
    )
    if employee_field:
        employee_id = frappe.db.get_value(doctype, docname, employee_field)
        if employee_id:
            for emp_dt in ("Employee", "Employee Master"):
                user_vals = frappe.db.get_value(emp_dt, employee_id, "user_id")
                if isinstance(user_vals, (tuple, list)):
                    user_vals = next((u for u in user_vals if u), None)
                if user_vals:
                    return user_vals
    return frappe.db.get_value(doctype, docname, "owner")

def check_employee_user(docname, doctype, user_id):
    if not (doctype and docname):
        return None
    meta = frappe.get_meta(doctype)
    employee_field = next(
        (
            df.fieldname
            for df in meta.fields
            if df.fieldname in EMPLOYEE_FIELDNAME_GUESSES
            or (df.fieldtype == "Link" and df.options in ("Employee", "Employee Master"))
        ),
        None,
    )
    if employee_field:
        employee_id = frappe.db.get_value(doctype, docname, employee_field)
        if employee_id:
            for emp_dt in ("Employee", "Employee Master"):
                user_vals = frappe.db.get_value(emp_dt, employee_id, "user_id")
                if user_id == user_vals:
                    return user_id
                else:
                    return user_vals
def fetch_approval_matrix(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
    doctype = variables.get("doctype")
    docname = variables.get("docname")
    event = variables.get("event")
    if event == "chatnext_trigger":
        user_id = variables.get("user_id")
        user_id= check_employee_user(docname,doctype,user_id)
    else:
        user_id = get_user_from_doc(doctype, docname)
    if not doctype or not docname:
        return {"matching_rules": []}

    try:
        doc = frappe.get_doc(doctype, docname)
    except:
        frappe.log_error(f"Could not fetch document {doctype} {docname}")
        return {"matching_rules": []}

    rules = frappe.get_all(
        "Approval Policy Matrix",
        filters={"target_doctype": doctype, "is_active": 1},
        fields=["name", "matrix_name", "priority_level"],
        order_by="priority_level asc"
    )

    matching_rules = []
    for rule in rules:
        try:
            rule_doc = frappe.get_doc("Approval Policy Matrix", rule.name)
            user_exists_in_assignments = checck_user_in_assignments(user_id, rule_doc)
            if not user_exists_in_assignments:
                continue
            conditions = rule_doc.approval_rule_condition
            matched_conditions = []

            for cond in conditions:
                target_type = cond.target_type
                field_name = cond.field_name
                operator = cond.operator or "="
                expected_value = cond.value
                actual_value = None

                # Resolve actual_value from doc or linked doc
                if target_type == "doc":
                    actual_value = doc.get(field_name)
                else:
                    linked_doc = resolve_linked_doc(doc, target_type)
                    if linked_doc:
                        actual_value = linked_doc.get(field_name)

                if actual_value is None:
                    matched_conditions.append(False)
                    continue

                # Field-to-field condition support
                if isinstance(expected_value, str) and expected_value.startswith("field:"):
                    compare_field = expected_value.replace("field:", "").strip()
                    expected_value = doc.get(compare_field)

                if evaluate_condition(actual_value, expected_value, operator):
                    matched_conditions.append(True)
                else:
                    matched_conditions.append(False)

            if conditions:
                join_types = [c.join_type or "AND" for c in conditions]
                if "OR" in join_types:
                    if any(matched_conditions):
                        matching_rules.append(rule)
                else:
                    if all(matched_conditions):
                        matching_rules.append(rule)
        except:
            continue
    return {"matching_rules": matching_rules}


def checck_user_in_assignments(user_id, rule_doc):
    if not user_id or len(rule_doc.user_assingment)==0:
        return True

    for assignment in rule_doc.user_assingment:
        get_assignment_doc=frappe.get_doc("Dynamic User Assignment", assignment.select_visibility_restriction)
        for user in get_assignment_doc.get("assigned_users"):
            if user.user_id == user_id:
                return True
    return False

def resolve_linked_doc(main_doc, link_fieldname):
    try:
        link_field_value = main_doc.get(link_fieldname)
        if not link_field_value:
            return None

        field_meta = main_doc.meta.get_field(link_fieldname)
        if not field_meta or field_meta.fieldtype != "Link":
            return None

        linked_doctype = field_meta.options
        return frappe.get_doc(linked_doctype, link_field_value)
    except:
        return None

def evaluate_condition(actual, expected, operator):
    try:
        if operator == "=":
            return str(actual) == str(expected)
        elif operator == "!=":
            return str(actual) != str(expected)
        elif operator == ">":
            return float(actual) > float(expected)
        elif operator == "<":
            return float(actual) < float(expected)
        elif operator == ">=":
            return float(actual) >= float(expected)
        elif operator == "<=":
            return float(actual) <= float(expected)
        elif operator == "in":
            return str(actual) in expected.split(",")
        elif operator == "not in":
            return str(actual) not in expected.split(",")
        elif operator == "like":
            return expected.lower() in str(actual).lower()
        elif operator == "not like":
            return expected.lower() not in str(actual).lower()
        elif operator == "between":
            if ":" not in expected:
                return False
            start, end = expected.split(":")
            try:
                actual_dt = datetime.strptime(str(actual), "%Y-%m-%d")
                start_dt = datetime.strptime(start.strip(), "%Y-%m-%d")
                end_dt = datetime.strptime(end.strip(), "%Y-%m-%d")
                return start_dt <= actual_dt <= end_dt
            except:
                try:
                    return float(start.strip()) <= float(actual) <= float(end.strip())
                except:
                    return False
    except:
        return False

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/file_to_ocr.py
import base64
import json
import mimetypes

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import (
    format_with_variables,
)
from nextai.utils import send_authorized_core_request


def file_to_ocr(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)

    if (
        node_data.get("file_url_path") is None
        or node_data.get("file_url_path").strip() == ""
    ):
        raise Exception("No file path found in variables")

    uri_path = format_with_variables(node_data.get("file_url_path"), variables)

    file_path = get_absolute_path(uri_path)

    mime_type, _ = mimetypes.guess_type(file_path)

    with open(file_path, "rb") as file:
        file_content = file.read()

    base64_data = base64.b64encode(file_content).decode("utf-8")
    data_uri = f"data:{mime_type};base64,{base64_data}"

    host = frappe.get_site_config().get("host_name")
    payload = {
        "document_image_base64": data_uri,
    }

    # create awaiting task
    headers = {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        "origin": host or "http://127.0.0.1:8000",
    }

    response = send_authorized_core_request(
        payload,
        "POST",
        "chatnext_core.clients.ocr_client.file_to_ocr",
        headers,
    ).json()

    ocr_data = response["message"]
    ocr_path = node_data.get("ocr_variable")
    variables[ocr_path] = ocr_data

    return {action_return_keys.result: ocr_data}


def get_absolute_path(file_url):
    site_path = frappe.get_site_path()
    if "private" in file_url:
        file_path = site_path + file_url
    else:
        file_path = site_path + "/public" + file_url
    return file_path

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/frappe_exec_dotted_path.py
import json

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import format_with_variables


def frappe_exec_dotted_path(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
    dotted_path = node_data["dotted_path"]
    payload = node_data.get("payload")
    enqueue = node_data.get("enqueue")
    payload = format_with_variables(payload, variables)
    payload = json.loads(payload) if payload else {}
    payload = {
        "variables": variables,
        **payload
    }

    if enqueue:
        frappe.enqueue(
            dotted_path,
            queue="long",
            is_async=True,
            **payload
        )
        return {
            action_return_keys.result: "Enqueued"
        }
    result = frappe.call(dotted_path, **payload)

    return {
        action_return_keys.result: result
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/personal_whatsapp_add_members_to_group.py
import json

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.actions.personal_whatsapp_create_group import (
    create_participants_array,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import (
    format_with_variables,
)
from nextai.personal_whatsapp_integration.doctype.cn_pw_whatsapp_message.cn_pw_whatsapp_message import (
    format_whatsapp_group_id,
)
from nextai.personal_whatsapp_integration.doctype.utils import waha_api_dispatcher


def personal_whatsapp_add_members_to_group(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
    session_id = node_data["session_id"]
    group_id = format_with_variables(node_data["group_id"], variables)

    session_id = format_with_variables(session_id, variables)
    group_id = format_whatsapp_group_id(group_id)
    participants = format_with_variables(node_data["participants"], variables)
    member_list = participants.split(",") if participants else []
    participants = create_participants_array(participants)
    url = f"/api/{session_id}/groups/{group_id}/participants/add"

    payload = json.dumps({"participants": participants})

    waha_api_dispatcher("POST", url, payload).json()
    group_doc = frappe.get_doc("CN PW Chat", {"chat_id": group_id})
    for user in member_list:
        new_member = group_doc.append("members", {})
        new_member.user = user
    group_doc.save()
    frappe.db.commit()

    return {"result": node_data}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/personal_whatsapp_ai_qa_agent.py
import json

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import task_states, action_return_keys
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.chat_agent_apis import (
    create_thread,
    interact_with_ai,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import (
    update_task_status,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import (
    format_with_variables,
)
from nextai.personal_whatsapp_integration.doctype.cn_pw_whatsapp_message.cn_pw_whatsapp_message import (
    send_personal_whatsapp_message,
)


def get_contact_id(node_data, variables):
    conversation_type = node_data["conversation_type"]
    conversation_type = format_with_variables(conversation_type, variables)
    contact_id = None
    if conversation_type == "personal":
        user_id = node_data["user_id"]
        user_id = format_with_variables(user_id, variables)
        contact_id = user_id
    elif conversation_type == "group":
        group_id = node_data["group_id"]
        group_id = format_with_variables(group_id, variables)
        contact_id = group_id
    else:
        receiver_id = node_data["receiver_id"]
        receiver_id = format_with_variables(receiver_id, variables)
        contact_id = receiver_id
    return contact_id


def personal_whatsapp_ai_qa_agent(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
    assistant_id = node_data["assistant_id"]
    openai_thread_id = create_thread()

    task_name = variables["task_name"]
    variables["openai_thread_id"] = openai_thread_id
    message_body = node_data.get("first_message_to_send")

    if message_body:
        message_body = format_with_variables(message_body, variables)
    if message_body:
        ai_response = interact_with_ai(
            assistant_id, variables["openai_thread_id"], message_body
        )
        variables["last_ai_response"] = ai_response
        response_type = ai_response.get("type")
        if response_type == "message":
            message_text = ai_response.get("content")
            if message_text:
                node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
                session_id = node_data["session_id"]
                session_id = format_with_variables(session_id, variables)
                contact_id = get_contact_id(node_data, variables)
                send_personal_whatsapp_message(contact_id, session_id, message_text)

        else:
            raise Exception("Invalid response type received from AI assistant")
    update_task_status(task_name, status=task_states.AWAITING_TRIGGER)

    return {
        action_return_keys.result: {
            "openai_thread_id": openai_thread_id,
            "ai_response": variables.get("last_ai_response"),
        },
        action_return_keys.skip_task_creation: True,
        action_return_keys.skip_task_update: True,
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/personal_whatsapp_create_group.py
import json

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import (
    format_with_variables,
)
from nextai.personal_whatsapp_integration.doctype.cn_pw_whatsapp_message.cn_pw_whatsapp_message import (
    format_whatsapp_id,
)
from nextai.personal_whatsapp_integration.doctype.utils import waha_api_dispatcher


def create_participants_array(participants: str):
    ids = participants.split(",")
    participant_list = []
    for id_ in ids:
        trimmed_id = id_.strip()
        user_id = format_whatsapp_id(trimmed_id)
        participant_list.append({"id": user_id})

    return participant_list


def personal_whatsapp_create_group(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
    session_id = node_data["session_id"]
    group_name = node_data["group_name"]
    participants = node_data["participants"]

    session_id = format_with_variables(session_id, variables)
    group_name = format_with_variables(group_name, variables)
    participants = format_with_variables(participants, variables)

    url = f"/api/{session_id}/groups"

    participants = create_participants_array(participants)

    payload = json.dumps({"name": group_name, "participants": participants})
    res = waha_api_dispatcher("POST", url, payload).json()
    group_id = res["gid"]["_serialized"]

    variables["group_id"] = group_id
    return {"result": group_id}

import json

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.actions.personal_whatsapp_create_group import (

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import (
    format_with_variables,
)

    format_whatsapp_group_id,
)

def personal_whatsapp_remove_members_from_group(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)

    session_id = format_with_variables(node_data["session_id"], variables)
    group_id = format_with_variables(node_data["group_id"], variables)
