# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/personal_whatsapp_select_session.py
import json

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import (
    format_with_variables,
)


def personal_whatsapp_select_session(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)

    chosen_session_id = node_data["select_session"]
    default = node_data["default"]
    chosen_session_id = format_with_variables(chosen_session_id, variables)
    default_session_id = frappe.db.get_value("CN PW Whatsapp Session", {"default": 1})

    if default:
        variables["session_id"] = default_session_id
    else:
        variables["session_id"] = chosen_session_id

    return {"result": default_session_id}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/personal_whatsapp_send_media_file.py
import json
import mimetypes

import frappe

from nextai.config.api import HOST_NAME
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import (
    format_with_variables,
)
from nextai.personal_whatsapp_integration.doctype.cn_pw_whatsapp_message.cn_pw_whatsapp_message import (
    format_whatsapp_id,
    format_whatsapp_group_id,
)


def personal_whatsapp_send_media_file(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)

    chat_type = node_data["chatType"]
    session_id = node_data["session_id"]
    file_path = node_data["media_file_path"]
    message_type = node_data["messageType"]
    file_name = node_data.get("file_name", "")
    caption = node_data.get("caption", "")

    file_name = format_with_variables(file_name, variables)
    caption = format_with_variables(caption, variables)
    session_id = format_with_variables(session_id, variables)
    file_path = format_with_variables(file_path, variables)

    if frappe.db.exists("File", {"file_url": file_path}):
        file_doc = frappe.get_doc("File", {"file_url": file_path})
    else:
        file_doc = frappe.get_doc("File", {"file_url": file_path}).insert(
            ignore_permissions=True
        )

    media_url = f"{HOST_NAME}{file_doc.file_url}"

    mime_type = mimetypes.guess_type(file_doc.file_url)[0]
    user_id = frappe.db.get_value(
        "CN PW Whatsapp Session", {"session_id": session_id}, "user_id"
    )
    doc = frappe.new_doc("CN PW Whatsapp Message")
    doc.update(
        {
            "session": session_id,
            "user_id": user_id,
            "type": "Outgoing",
            "message_type": message_type.capitalize(),
            "mime_type": mime_type,
            "media_url": media_url,
        }
    )
    if not media_url:
        raise Exception("No file Found in this address")

    if message_type == "audio":
        if chat_type == "personal":
            user_id = format_with_variables(node_data["user_id"], variables)
            user_id = format_whatsapp_id(user_id)
            doc.update(
                {
                    "conversation_type": "Personal",
                    "user_id": user_id,
                }
            )
        elif chat_type == "group":
            group_id = format_with_variables(node_data["group_id"], variables)
            group_id = format_whatsapp_group_id(group_id)
            doc.update(
                {
                    "conversation_type": "Group",
                    "group_id": group_id,
                }
            )

        else:
            receiver_id = format_with_variables(node_data["receiver_id"], variables)

            if receiver_id.endswith("@g.us"):
                doc.update({"group_id": receiver_id, "conversation_type": "Group"})
            else:
                receiver_id = format_with_variables(node_data["receiver_id"], variables)
                receiver_id = format_whatsapp_id(receiver_id)
                doc.update(
                    {
                        "user_id": receiver_id,
                        "conversation_type": "Personal",
                    }
                )
    else:

        if chat_type == "personal":
            user_id = format_with_variables(node_data["user_id"], variables)
            user_id = format_whatsapp_id(user_id)
            doc.update(
                {
                    "conversation_type": "Personal",
                    "user_id": user_id,
                    "caption": caption,
                    "file_name": file_name,
                }
            )

        elif chat_type == "group":
            group_id = format_with_variables(node_data["group_id"], variables)
            group_id = format_whatsapp_group_id(group_id)
            doc.update(
                {
                    "conversation_type": "Group",
                    "group_id": group_id,
                    "caption": caption,
                    "file_name": file_name,
                }
            )
        else:
            receiver_id = format_with_variables(node_data["receiver_id"], variables)
            if receiver_id.endswith("@g.us"):
                doc.update(
                    {
                        "group_id": receiver_id,
                        "conversation_type": "Group",
                        "caption": caption,
                        "file_name": file_name,
                    }
                )
            else:
                doc.update(
                    {
                        "user_id": receiver_id,
                        "conversation_type": "Personal",
                        "caption": caption,
                        "file_name": file_name,
                    }
                )
    doc.before_save()
    doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    doc.send()
    return {"result": message_type}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/personal_whatsapp_send_message.py
import json

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import (
    format_with_variables,
)
from nextai.personal_whatsapp_integration.doctype.cn_pw_whatsapp_message.cn_pw_whatsapp_message import (
    format_whatsapp_group_id,
    format_whatsapp_id,
)


def personal_whatsapp_send_message(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)

    session_id = node_data["session_id"]
    body = node_data["message"]

    session_id = format_with_variables(session_id, variables)
    body = format_with_variables(body, variables)
    user_id = frappe.get_value(
        "CN PW Whatsapp Session", {"name": session_id}, "user_id"
    )
    doc = frappe.new_doc("CN PW Whatsapp Message")
    doc.update(
        {
            "session": session_id,
            "body": body,
            "user_id": user_id,
            "type": "Outgoing",
            "message_type": "Text",
        }
    )
    group_id = None
    if node_data["conversation_type"] == "personal":
        user_id = format_with_variables(node_data["user_id"], variables)
        user_id = format_whatsapp_id(user_id)
        doc.update(
            {
                "user_id": user_id,
                "conversation_type": "Personal",
            }
        )
    elif node_data["conversation_type"] == "group":
        group_id = format_with_variables(node_data["group_id"], variables)
        group_id = format_whatsapp_group_id(group_id)
        doc.update({"group_id": group_id, "conversation_type": "Group"})
    else:
        receiver_id = format_with_variables(node_data["receiver_id"], variables)
        if receiver_id.endswith("@g.us"):
            doc.update({"group_id": receiver_id, "conversation_type": "Group"})
        else:
            receiver_id = format_with_variables(node_data["receiver_id"], variables)
            receiver_id = format_whatsapp_id(receiver_id)
            doc.update(
                {
                    "user_id": receiver_id,
                    "conversation_type": "Personal",
                }
            )
    doc.before_save()
    doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    message_id = doc.send()
    return {"result": message_id}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/personal_whatsapp_send_poll.py
import json

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import (
    format_with_variables,
)
from nextai.personal_whatsapp_integration.doctype.cn_pw_whatsapp_message.cn_pw_whatsapp_message import (
    format_whatsapp_id,
    format_whatsapp_group_id,
)


def personal_whatsapp_send_poll(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)

    session_id = node_data["session_id"]
    session_id = format_with_variables(session_id, variables)

    chat_type = node_data["chatType"]

    poll_name = node_data["poll_name"]
    poll_name = format_with_variables(poll_name, variables)

    poll_options = format_with_variables(node_data["poll_options"], variables)

    multiple_answers = False
    if node_data["multiple_answers"]:
        multiple_answers = True

    if chat_type == "personal":
        user_id = format_with_variables(node_data["user_id"], variables)
        user_id = format_whatsapp_id(user_id)
        poll_data = {
            "session": session_id,
            "poll_name": poll_name,
            "user_id": user_id,
            "type": "Outgoing",
            "conversation_type": "Personal",
            "message_type": "Poll",
            "options": poll_options,
            "multiple_answers": multiple_answers,
        }
    else:
        group_id = format_with_variables(node_data["group_id"], variables)
        group_id = format_whatsapp_group_id(group_id)
        poll_data = {
            "session": session_id,
            "poll_name": poll_name,
            "user_id": group_id,
            "type": "Outgoing",
            "conversation_type": "Group",
            "message_type": "Poll",
            "options": poll_options,
            "multiple_answers": multiple_answers,
        }
    doc = frappe.new_doc("CN PW Whatsapp Message")
    doc.update(poll_data)
    doc.before_save()
    doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    doc.send()
    return {}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/reply_to_mail.py
import json

import frappe
from frappe.core.doctype.communication.email import make as create_email

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import format_with_variables


def reply_to_mail(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)

    select_template = node_data.get("select_template")
    email_template = node_data.get("email_template")
    email_subject = node_data.get("email_subject")
    email_content = node_data.get("email_content")

    email_account = node_data["email_account"]

    email_account = frappe.get_doc("Email Account", email_account)
    sender_email = email_account.email_id

    if select_template:
        email_template_doc = frappe.get_doc("Email Template", email_template)
        subject = email_template_doc.subject
        use_html = email_template_doc.use_html
        response_html = email_template_doc.response_html
        response = email_template_doc.response

        if use_html:
            message = response_html
        else:
            message = response
    else:
        subject = email_subject
        message = email_content

    message = format_with_variables(message, variables)
    subject = format_with_variables(subject, variables)

    extra_email_args = variables.get('extra_email_args', {})
    if extra_email_args.get("attachments"):
        extra_email_args["attachments"] = [frappe.attach_print(**attachment) for attachment in
                                           extra_email_args.get("attachments")]

    # FOR EMAIL THREADING
    # reply_to_id = None
    # try:
    #     last_email = variables.get("last_email_data")
    #     if last_email:
    #         reply_to_id = last_email.get("message_id") or None
    #         if reply_to_id:
    #             reply_to_id = '<' + reply_to_id.strip() + '>'
    # except:
    #     pass

    create_email(
        recipients=variables['funnel_email_id'],
        subject=subject,
        content=message,
        sender=sender_email,
        # in_reply_to=reply_to_id,
        send_email=True,
        read_receipt=True,
        **extra_email_args
    )

    return {
        action_return_keys.result: {
            "recepients": variables['funnel_email_id'],
        }
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/send_audio_message_whatsapp.py
import json

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import format_with_variables
from nextai.whatsapp_business_api_integration.doctype.whatsapp_message.whatsapp_message import send_message


def send_funnel_whatsapp_message(action_node, variables, message_type):
    if not variables.get("contact_name"):
        raise Exception("No WhatsApp contact found in variables")

    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
    file_url = node_data.get("filePath")

    if file_url:
        file_url = format_with_variables(file_url, variables)

    if file_url:
        file_name = file_url.split("/")[-1]
        file_doc = frappe.get_doc("File", {"file_url": file_url, "file_name": file_name})
        media_image = file_doc.get_full_path()

        if not media_image:
            raise Exception(f"No image reference where found {file_url}")

    message_body = node_data.get("message")

    if message_body:
        message_body = format_with_variables(message_body, variables)

    if message_type == "Image":
        send_message(
            variables.get("contact_name"),
            message_body,
            True,
            message_type="Image",
            media_type="image",
            media_image=file_url
        )
    else:
        send_message(
            variables.get("contact_name"),
            message_body,
            True,
            message_type=message_type,
            media_type=message_type.lower(),
            media_file=file_url
        )

    return {}


def send_audio_message_whatsapp(action_node, variables):
    return send_funnel_whatsapp_message(action_node, variables, "Audio")

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/send_bulk_mail.py
import json
import random
from hashlib import blake2b

import frappe
import spintax
from frappe.core.doctype.communication.email import make as create_email
from frappe.utils import now

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import format_with_variables
from nextai.utils import get_host_with_protocol


def create_email_image_id(recepients):
    rec_str = recepients if isinstance(recepients, str) else ",".join(recepients)
    current_time_str = str(now())
    site_hash = blake2b(digest_size=15)
    site_hash.update(bytes(rec_str, 'utf-8'))
    site_hash.update(bytes(current_time_str, 'utf-8'))
    return site_hash.hexdigest()


def send_bulk_mail(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)

    select_specific_mail_accounts = node_data.get("selectSpecificMailAccounts")
    mail_accounts = node_data.get("mail_accounts")
    select_template = node_data.get("select_template")
    email_template = node_data.get("email_template")
    email_subject = node_data.get("email_subject")
    email_content = node_data.get("email_content")
    recipients = node_data["recipients"]
    cc = node_data.get("cc")
    bcc = node_data.get("bcc")

    sequence = variables.get("sequence")
    if select_specific_mail_accounts:
        mail_accs = [mail_account.get("email_account") for mail_account in mail_accounts]
        sequence = sequence or random.randint(1, len(mail_accs))
        selected_mail = mail_accs[(sequence - 1) % len(mail_accs)]
        selected_mail_account = frappe.get_doc("Email Account", selected_mail).as_dict()
    else:
        email_accounts = frappe.get_all("Email Account", fields=["*"], filters={"enable_outgoing": 1})
        sequence = sequence or random.randint(1, len(email_accounts))
        selected_mail_account = email_accounts[(sequence - 1) % len(email_accounts)]

    variables[sequence] = sequence

    if select_template:
        email_template_doc = frappe.get_doc("Email Template", email_template)
        subject = email_template_doc.subject
        use_html = email_template_doc.use_html
        response_html = email_template_doc.response_html
        response = email_template_doc.response

        if use_html:
            message = response_html
        else:
            message = response
    else:
        subject = email_subject
        message = email_content

    variables['sender_email_account'] = selected_mail_account

    message = format_with_variables(message, variables)
    message = spintax.spin(message)

    subject = format_with_variables(subject, variables)
    recipients = format_with_variables(recipients, variables)
    cc = format_with_variables(cc, variables)
    bcc = format_with_variables(bcc, variables)

    email_recepients = [r for r in recipients.split(",") if r]
    email_recepients_cc = [r for r in cc.split(",") if r]
    email_recepients_bcc = [r for r in bcc.split(",") if r]

    extra_email_args = variables.get('extra_email_args', {})
    attachments = extra_email_args.get("attachments") or []
    if extra_email_args.get("attachments"):
        attachments = [frappe.attach_print(**attachment) for attachment in extra_email_args.get("attachments")]

    email_image_id = create_email_image_id(email_recepients)
    host = get_host_with_protocol()
    message = message + '<img alt="" src="' + host + '/email/image?' + email_image_id + '">'
    make_response = create_email(
        recipients=email_recepients,
        cc=email_recepients_cc,
        bcc=email_recepients_bcc,
        subject=subject,
        content=message,
        sender=selected_mail_account.get("email_id"),
        send_email=True,
        read_receipt=True,
        **{
            **extra_email_args,
            "attachments": attachments,
        }
    )

    comm_name = make_response.get("name")

    if comm_name:
        ei_map_doc = frappe.new_doc("CN Email Image Map")
        ei_map_doc.communication = comm_name
        ei_map_doc.image_id = email_image_id
        ei_map_doc.save()

    variables['funnel_email_id'] = email_recepients[0]

    return {
        action_return_keys.result: {
            "cc": email_recepients_cc,
            "recepients": email_recepients,
            "bcc": email_recepients_bcc,
            "communication": comm_name
        }
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/send_fb_conversion_offline.py
import time
import requests
import hashlib
import json
import frappe

from datetime import datetime, timedelta

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys, task_states
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import create_new_task
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import format_with_variables


# Define constants
API_VERSION = "v18.0"  # Update API version as needed
BASE_URL = f"https://graph.facebook.com/{API_VERSION}/"

def send_fb_conversion_offline(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
    # frappe.logger("funnel").exception(json.dumps(node_data))


    # frappe.logger("funnel").exception(json.dumps(variables,default=str))

    #the node_data is the data of the action node can be change according to the form name
    event_name = node_data.get("eventName")
    currency = node_data.get("currency")
    value = node_data.get("value")
    fetch_from_contact = node_data.get("fetchFromContact")
    custom_data = node_data.get("customdata")
    order_id = node_data.get("orderid")
    item_id = node_data.get("itemId")
    contact = node_data.get("contact")
    contact_details=node_data.get("contactDetails")

    # frappe.logger("funnel").exception("value")

    contact = format_with_variables(contact, variables)
    custom_data = format_with_variables(custom_data, variables)
    value = format_with_variables(value, variables)
    order_id = format_with_variables(order_id, variables)
    item_id = format_with_variables(item_id, variables)
    contact_details = format_with_variables(contact_details, variables)



    contact_data=None
    phone=None
    email=None

    values = {
        'contact': contact,
    }
    if fetch_from_contact:
        contact_data = frappe.db.sql("""
            SELECT *
            FROM `tabContact`
            WHERE `from` = %(contact)s
        """, values=values, as_dict=1)

        if not len(contact_data):
            raise Exception("No contact_details found")

        phone= contact
        email = contact_data[0].get("email")
    else:
        #change according to the obj structure
        contact_details_obj = json.loads(contact_details)

        phone = contact_details_obj.get("phone")
        email = contact_details_obj.get("email")

        if not phone:
            raise Exception("Phone number not found")
        if not email:
            raise Exception("Email not found")


    dataset_id= frappe.db.get_single_value("Whatsapp Settings", "dataset_id")

    # Set the endpoint URL
    url = f"{BASE_URL}{dataset_id}/events" if dataset_id else f"{BASE_URL}me/events"


    # Set headers with access token and content type
    access_token = frappe.utils.password.get_decrypted_password(
            "Whatsapp Settings", "Whatsapp Settings", "access_token"
        ) # just a overview that can be change with the given path


    headers = {"Authorization":f"Bearer {access_token}","Content-Type": "application/json"}

    event_time = int(time.time())  # Current time as event time

    # Prepare data payload
    data = {
        "action_source": "physical_store",  # Set action_source as per documentation
        "event_name": event_name,
        "event_time": event_time,
        "user_data": {
            "em": hashlib.sha256(email.encode()).hexdigest(),
            "ph": hashlib.sha256(phone.encode()).hexdigest(),
        },
        "custom_data": {
            "currency": currency,
            "value": value,
            "custom_data": custom_data,
            "order_id": order_id,
            "item_number": item_id
        }
    }


    # Send the POST request
    response = requests.post(url, headers=headers, json={"data": [data]})

    # Check the status code of the response
    if response.status_code == 200:
        return {
            action_return_keys.result:"Success",
        }
    else:
        if response.content:
            raise Exception(f"Error sending event: {response.content}")
        raise Exception(f"Error sending event: {response.status_code}")

    return{}
# Example usage (uncomment if needed)
# send_offline_event("Purchase", "INR", 123.45, {"email": "user@example.com", "phone": "1234567890", "contents": [{"id": "product123", "quantity": 1}]}, "order123", "item123", "<DATASET_ID>")

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/send_fb_conversion_wa.py
import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
import frappe
import time
import requests


from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys, task_states
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import create_new_task
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import format_with_variables

# Define constants
API_VERSION = "v18.0"  # Update API version as needed
BASE_URL = f"https://graph.facebook.com/{API_VERSION}/"


def send_fb_conversion_wa(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
    # frappe.logger("funnel").exception(json.dumps(node_data))
    # frappe.logger("funnel").exception("variables")

    frappe.logger("funnel").exception(json.dumps(variables,default=str))
    # frappe.logger("funnel").exception("next line")


    contact= variables.get("contact_name")
    if not contact :
        raise Exception("contact_name is needed")

    values = {
        'contact': contact,
    }

    messages = frappe.db.sql("""
        SELECT  referral_ctwa_clid
        FROM `tabWhatsApp Message`
        WHERE `from` = %(contact)s
            and referral_ctwa_clid is not null
        limit 1
    """, values=values, as_dict=1)

    if not len(messages):
        raise Exception("No referral data found")


    event_name = node_data.get("eventName")
    currency = node_data.get("currency")
    value = node_data.get("value")

    value = format_with_variables(value, variables)

    page_id = frappe.db.get_single_value("Whatsapp Settings", "page_id")
    ctwa_clid = messages[0].get("referral_ctwa_clid")

    if not page_id :
        raise Exception("No page_id data found")
    if not ctwa_clid :
        raise Exception("No ctwa_clid data found")

    dataset_id = frappe.db.get_single_value("Whatsapp Settings", "dataset_id")
    # Set the endpoint URL
    url = f"{BASE_URL}{dataset_id}/events"

    # Set headers with access token and content type
    ACCESS_TOKEN = frappe.utils.password.get_decrypted_password(
            "Whatsapp Settings", "Whatsapp Settings", "access_token"
        )

    headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {ACCESS_TOKEN}", "Content-Type": "application/json"}

    # Capture user input for all parameters
    event_time = int(time.time())  # Current time as event time


    data = {
        "data": [
            {
                "event_name": event_name,
                "event_time": event_time,
                "action_source": "business_messaging",
                "messaging_channel": "whatsapp",
                "user_data": {
                    "page_id":page_id ,
                    "ctwa_clid": ctwa_clid
                },
                "custom_data": {
                    "currency": currency,
                    "value": value
                }
            }
        ]
    }
    # Send the POST request
    response = requests.post(url, headers=headers, json=data)

    # Check the status code of the response
    if response.status_code == 200:
        return {
            action_return_keys.result:"Success",
        }
    else:
        if response.content:
            raise Exception(f"Error sending event: {response.content}")
        raise Exception(f"Error sending event: {response.status_code}")

    return{}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/send_mail.py
import json

import frappe
from frappe.core.doctype.communication.email import make as create_email
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import (
    format_with_variables,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_assigned_users import (
    get_assigned_users,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.logger import debug


def send_mail(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
    doc = variables.get("doc")

    select_template = node_data.get("select_template")
    email_template = node_data.get("email_template")
    email_subject = node_data.get("email_subject")
    email_content = node_data.get("email_content")
    send_to_assigned_users = node_data.get("send_to_assigned_users")

    email_account = node_data.get("email_account")
    recepients = node_data.get("recepients")  # older version

    recipients = node_data.get("recipients")
    cc = node_data.get("cc")
    bcc = node_data.get("bcc")
    sender_user_id = node_data.get("sender_user_id")

    email_recepients = []
    email_recepients_cc = []
    email_recepients_bcc = []

    assigned_users = get_assigned_users(doc.get("doctype"), doc.get("name")) if doc else []
    assigned_user_ids = []
    if assigned_users:
        for user_id in assigned_users:
            if user_id:
                # check if an email or not
                if "@" in user_id:
                    assigned_user_ids.append(user_id)
                else:
                    user_doc = frappe.get_doc("User", user_id)
                    email = user_doc.get("email")
                    if "@" in email:
                        assigned_user_ids.append(email)
                    else:
                        debug(
                            "cannot send email to user: "
                            + user_id
                            + " as no email found"
                        )

    variables['doc_assigned_to'] = ','.join(assigned_user_ids) if assigned_users else ''

    if recipients:
        recipients = format_with_variables(recipients, variables)
        cc = format_with_variables(cc, variables)
        bcc = format_with_variables(bcc, variables)
        sender_user_id = format_with_variables(sender_user_id, variables)
        email_recepients = [r.strip() for r in recipients.split(",") if r]
        email_recepients_cc = [r.strip() for r in cc.split(",") if r]
        email_recepients_bcc = [r.strip() for r in bcc.split(",") if r]
    else:
        # to support the old version
        for r in recepients:
            recipient_field = r["recipient"]
            if recipient_field:
                recipient_field = format_with_variables(recipient_field, variables)
                email_recepients.append(recipient_field)

            recipient_cc_field = r.get("cc")
            if recipient_cc_field:
                recipient_cc_field = format_with_variables(
                    recipient_cc_field, variables
                )
                recipient_cc_field = [c.strip() for c in recipient_cc_field.split(",")]
                email_recepients_cc += recipient_cc_field

    if send_to_assigned_users:
        email_recepients_cc += assigned_user_ids

    sender_email = None
    if email_account:
        email_account = frappe.get_doc("Email Account", email_account)
        sender_email = email_account.email_id

    if select_template:
        email_template_doc = frappe.get_doc("Email Template", email_template)
        subject = email_template_doc.subject
        use_html = email_template_doc.use_html
        response_html = email_template_doc.response_html
        response = email_template_doc.response

        if use_html:
            message = response_html
        else:
            message = response
    else:
        subject = email_subject
        message = email_content

    message = format_with_variables(message, variables)
    subject = format_with_variables(subject, variables)

    extra_email_args = variables.get("extra_email_args", {})
    email_attachments = extra_email_args.get("attachments", [])
    if extra_email_args.get("attachments"):
        email_attachments = [
            (
                attachment
                if isinstance(attachment, str)
                else frappe.attach_print(**attachment)
            )
            for attachment in extra_email_args.get("attachments", [])
        ]

    if node_data.get("link_doctype"):
        doctype = node_data["doctype"]
        docname = node_data["docname"]
        docname = format_with_variables(docname, variables)
        extra_email_args["doctype"] = doctype
        extra_email_args["name"] = docname

        if node_data.get("attach_print") and not node_data.get("attach_prints"):
            print_format = node_data.get("print_format")
            email_attachments.append(
                frappe.attach_print(
                    doctype,
                    docname,
                    print_format=print_format if print_format else None,
                )
            )

    if node_data.get("attach_prints"):
        attachments = node_data.get("attachments", []) or []
        for attachment in attachments:
            doctype = attachment["doctype"]
            docname = attachment["docname"]
            docname = format_with_variables(docname, variables)
            if attachment.get("is_variable"):
                variable_print_format = attachment.get("variable_print_format")
                print_format = format_with_variables(variable_print_format, variables)
            else:
                print_format = attachment.get("print_format")
            email_attachments.append(
                frappe.attach_print(
                    doctype,
                    docname,
                    print_format=print_format if print_format else None,
                )
            )

    if node_data.get("attach_files"):
        attachments = node_data.get("file_attachments", []) or []
        for attachment in attachments:
            file_path = attachment["file_path"]
            file_path = format_with_variables(file_path, variables)
            if '/' in file_path:
                try:
                    file_doc = frappe.get_doc("File", {"file_url": file_path, })
                    file_path = file_doc.name
                except Exception:
                    pass
            email_attachments.append(file_path)

    prev_user_id = frappe.session.user
    if sender_user_id:
        frappe.session.user = sender_user_id
    create_email(
        recipients=email_recepients,
        subject=subject,
        content=message,
        cc=email_recepients_cc,
        bcc=email_recepients_bcc,
        sender=sender_email,
        send_email=True,
        read_receipt=True,
        **{
            **extra_email_args,
            "attachments": email_attachments,
        }
    )

    variables["funnel_email_id"] = email_recepients[0]

    frappe.session.user = prev_user_id
    return {
        action_return_keys.result: {
            "cc": email_recepients_cc,
            "bcc": email_recepients_bcc,
            "recepients": email_recepients,
        }
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/send_message_whatsapp.py
import json

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import format_with_variables
from nextai.whatsapp_business_api_integration.doctype.whatsapp_message.whatsapp_message import send_message


def send_message_whatsapp(action_node, variables):
    if not variables.get("contact_name"):
        raise Exception("No whatsapp contact found in variables")
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)

    is_buttons = node_data.get("reply_options_type") == "button"
    is_list = node_data.get("reply_options_type") == "list"

    message_type = "Button" if is_buttons else (
        "Interactive" if is_list else "Text")

    interactive_message_data = None

    if is_buttons:
        list_data = json.loads(json.dumps(node_data.get("button")))
        interactive_message_data = []

        for option in list_data:
            option_data = option['option']
            interactive_message_data.append({
                'type': "reply",
                'reply': {
                    'id': option_data,
                    'title': option_data
                }
            })
    elif is_list:
        list_data = json.loads(json.dumps(node_data.get("list")))
        interactive_message_data = []
        list_option_rows = []

        i = 0
        for option in list_data:
            option_data = option['option']
            description = option.get('description') or ""
            if option_data:
                description = format_with_variables(description, variables)
                list_option_rows.append({
                    'id': "ans-" + str(i),
                    'title': option_data,
                    'description': description
                })
                i += 1
        interactive_message_data.append({
            'title': "",
            'rows': list_option_rows
        })
    message_body = node_data.get("message")
    if message_body:
        message_body = format_with_variables(message_body, variables)
    send_message(variables.get("contact_name"), message_body, message_type=message_type,
                 interactive_message_data=interactive_message_data or None)
    return {}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/send_notification.py
import json

import frappe
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel.funnel_user import get_funnel_user_id
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import format_with_variables


def send_notification(action_node, variables):
    try:
        node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
        user_ids = format_with_variables(node_data.get("user_ids"), variables)
        notification_subject = format_with_variables(node_data.get("notification_subject"), variables),
        notification_content = format_with_variables(node_data.get("notification_content"), variables)
        docname = format_with_variables(node_data.get("docname"), variables)

        connect_to_doc = node_data.get("connectToDoc")
        doctype = node_data.get("doctype")

        subject = notification_subject
        context = notification_content

        sent_notifications = []

        if user_ids:
            user_ids = [uid.strip() for uid in user_ids.split(',') if uid.strip()]
            for user_id in user_ids:
                notification = frappe.new_doc("Notification Log")
                notification.update({
                    "type": "Alert",
                    "subject": subject,
                    "from_user": get_funnel_user_id(),
                    "email_content": context,
                    'for_user': user_id
                })

                if connect_to_doc:
                    notification.update({
                        "document_type": doctype,
                        "document_name": docname
                    })

                if notification.for_user != notification.from_user:
                    notification.insert(ignore_permissions=True)

                    sent_notifications.append({
                        'user': notification.for_user,
                        'notification': notification.name
                    })
                else:
                    sent_notifications.append({
                        'user': notification.for_user,
                        'status': 'not-sent as same user'
                    })

        return {
            "result": {
                'notifications': sent_notifications,
                'user_ids': user_ids,
            }
        }

    except Exception as e:
        frappe.logger('utils').exception(e)

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/send_template_whatsapp.py
import json
import re

import frappe
from nextai.api.webhook.whatsapp import create_or_get_contact
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import format_with_variables
from nextai.whatsapp_business_api_integration.doctype.whatsapp_message.whatsapp_message import send_templated_message


def send_template_whatsapp(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)

    whatsapp_template_parameters = []
    for parameter in node_data.get("whatsapp_template_parameters") or []:
        formatted_parameter = format_with_variables(parameter["parameter"], variables)
        whatsapp_template_parameters.append(formatted_parameter)

    template_data = []
    for parameter in whatsapp_template_parameters:
        template_data.append(
            {
                "type": "text",
                "text": str(parameter or "")
            }
        )

    template = node_data["whatsapp_template"]
    whatsapp_id = node_data["whatsapp_id"]

    # Process file attachment if provided
    media_file = None
    media_type = None
    file_name = None

    attachment_file = node_data.get("attachment_file")
    if attachment_file:
        attachment_file = format_with_variables(attachment_file, variables)

        if attachment_file:
            # attachment_file can be either a file ID/name or a file path

            # First check if it's a file ID/name (no slashes in the value)
            if "/" not in attachment_file:
                # Check if file exists in database by name
                if frappe.db.exists("File", attachment_file):
                    file_doc = frappe.get_doc("File", attachment_file)
                    media_file = file_doc.get("file_url")
                    file_name = file_doc.get("file_name")
                else:
                    # Treat as direct file reference
                    media_file = attachment_file
                    file_name = attachment_file
            else:
                # It's a file path - check if exists by file_url
                file_doc_name = frappe.db.exists("File", {"file_url": attachment_file})
                if file_doc_name:
                    file_doc = frappe.get_doc("File", file_doc_name)
                    media_file = file_doc.get("file_url")
                    file_name = file_doc.get("file_name")
                else:
                    # Use the path directly
                    media_file = attachment_file
                    file_name = attachment_file.split("/")[-1]

            # Determine media type based on file extension (must match MEDIA_TYPES)
            if file_name:
                file_ext = file_name.split('.')[-1].lower() if '.' in file_name else ''

                if file_ext in ['jpg', 'jpeg', 'png', 'gif', 'webp']:
                    media_type = 'image'  # matches MEDIA_TYPES
                elif file_ext in ['mp4', 'avi', 'mov', 'wmv', 'flv', 'mkv']:
                    media_type = 'video'  # matches MEDIA_TYPES
                elif file_ext in ['mp3', 'wav', 'ogg', 'm4a', 'aac']:
                    media_type = 'audio'  # matches MEDIA_TYPES
                else:
                    # Everything else is document (matches MEDIA_TYPES)
                    media_type = 'document'

    if whatsapp_id:
        whatsapp_id = format_with_variables(whatsapp_id, variables)
        whatsapp = str(whatsapp_id).strip()

        if whatsapp.startswith("+"):
            whatsapp = whatsapp[1:]

        if not re.compile("^([0-9]*)$").match(whatsapp):
            raise Exception("Invalid whatsapp id (contains non-numeric characters):" + whatsapp)
        elif len(whatsapp) < 10:
            raise Exception('Invalid whatsapp id (length less than 10):' + whatsapp)
        elif len(whatsapp) > 12:
            raise Exception('Invalid whatsapp id (length greater than 12):' + whatsapp)
        else:
            if len(whatsapp) == 10:
                whatsapp = "91" + whatsapp

            contact = create_or_get_contact(whatsapp, whatsapp)

            # Send templated message with or without media
            if media_file:
                # Capitalize message_type for proper display (Image, Video, Document, Audio)
                display_message_type = media_type.capitalize() if media_type else "Text"
                message = send_templated_message(
                    contact.name,
                    template,
                    json.dumps(template_data),
                    message_type=display_message_type,
                    media_file=media_file,
                    media_type=media_type,
                    media_filename=file_name,
                )
            else:
                message = send_templated_message(contact.name, template, json.dumps(template_data))

            variables["sent_templated_message"] = message.as_dict()
            if not variables.get("contact_name"):
                variables["contact_name"] = contact.name
    else:
        raise Exception("No whatsapp id found")
    return {}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/set_chatbot_status.py
import json

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys


def set_chatbot_status(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)

    if not variables.get("contact_name"):
        raise Exception("No WhatsApp contact found in variables")

    if node_data.get("status") == "enabled":
        frappe.db.set_value("Contact", variables["contact_name"], "chatbot_disabled", 0)
    else:
        frappe.db.set_value("Contact", variables["contact_name"], "chatbot_disabled", 1)

    return {
        action_return_keys.result: node_data.get("status"),
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/split_array.py
import json

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import create_new_task
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import (
    format_with_variables,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_action_nodes import (
    get_next_action_nodes,
)


def split_array(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
    array_json = node_data["array_json"]
    array_element_variable = node_data["array_element_variable"]
    if array_json:
        array_json = format_with_variables(array_json, variables)
        array_json = json.loads(array_json)
    else:
        array_json = []

    current_task_doc = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Task", variables["task_name"])
    current_node_id = current_task_doc.target_node_id
    current_workflow_doc = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Workflow", current_task_doc.workflow)
    for array_element in array_json:
        variable_payload = {**variables, array_element_variable: array_element}
        workflow = frappe.get_doc(
            {
                "doctype": "Funnel Workflow",
                "funnel": current_workflow_doc.funnel,
                "variables": json.dumps(variable_payload, indent=2, default=str),
            }
        )
        workflow.insert(ignore_permissions=True)

        actions = get_next_action_nodes(current_node_id)
        for action_node in actions:
            create_new_task(
                workflow.name,
                action_node.id,
                current_node_id,
                variables["task_name"],
                task_info={
                    "trigger": "array loop",
                },
            )
    return {
        action_return_keys.skip_task_creation: True,
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/stop_current_workflow.py
import frappe


def get_executable_tasks(workflow_name):
    values = {
        'workflow_name': workflow_name
    }
    tasks = frappe.db.sql("""
            select *
                   from `tabFunnel Task`
                   where execution_status != 'completed'
                     and execution_status != 'cancelled'
                     and execution_status != 'failed'
                  and workflow = %(workflow_name)s
    """, values=values, as_dict=1)
    return tasks


def stop_current_workflow(action_node, variables):
    executable_tasks = get_executable_tasks(variables["workflow_name"])
    for task in executable_tasks:
        task_doc = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Task", task.get("name"))
        task_doc.execution_status = "cancelled"
        task_doc.save()

    workflow_doc = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Workflow", variables["workflow_name"])
    workflow_doc.status = "stopped"
    workflow_doc.save()

    return {}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/submit_doc.py
import json

import frappe
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import format_with_variables


def submit_doc(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)

    target_doctype = format_with_variables(node_data["target_doctype"], variables)
    target_docname = format_with_variables(node_data["target_docname"], variables)

    target_doc = frappe.get_doc(target_doctype, target_docname)
    target_doc.flags.ignore_permissions = True
    target_doc.flags.ignore_links = True
    target_doc.flags.ignore_mandatory = True
    target_doc.flags.ignore_children = True
    target_doc.flags.ignore_version = True
    target_doc.submit()

    return {
        action_return_keys.result: {
            'doctype': target_doctype,
            'docname': target_docname
        }
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/text_intent_detection.py
import json

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import format_with_variables
from nextai.utils import send_authorized_core_request


def text_intent_detection(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
    text_data = format_with_variables(node_data["text"], variables)
    intent_list = [intent.get("intent") for intent in node_data["intent_list"] if intent.get("intent")]

    # send api request to detect intent
    selected_intent = "__"
    score = None

    intent_response = send_authorized_core_request({
        "message": text_data,
        "intents": intent_list,
        "threshold": node_data.get("threshold") or 0.6
    }, "POST", "chatnext_core.models.intent.detect_intent", {
        "Content-Type": "application/json"
    })

    if intent_response:
        intent_response = intent_response.json()
        if intent_response and intent_response.get("message") and intent_response.get("message").get("payload"):
            if intent_response.get("message").get("score"):
                score = intent_response.get("message").get("score")
            if intent_response.get("message").get("payload").get("intent"):
                selected_intent = intent_response.get("message").get("payload").get("intent")
        else:
            selected_intent = "__"

    return {
        action_return_keys.source_handle_id: selected_intent,
        action_return_keys.result: {
            "response": intent_response,
            "intent": selected_intent if selected_intent != "__" else None,
            "score": score
        }
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/trigger_funnel.py
import json

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import create_new_task
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import format_with_variables
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_action_nodes import get_action_nodes_from_trigger


def trigger_funnel(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
    selected_funnel = node_data["selected_funnel"]
    variable_payload = node_data["variable_payload"]
    if variable_payload:
        variable_payload = format_with_variables(variable_payload, variables)
        variable_payload = json.loads(variable_payload)
    else:
        variable_payload = {}

    published_funnel = frappe.get_last_doc("Funnel Published", {"funnel": selected_funnel, "published": 1})
    if published_funnel.name:
        funnel_defination = frappe.get_last_doc("Funnel Definition", {
            "parent": published_funnel.name,
            "parenttype": "Funnel Published",
            "type": "on_funnel_trigger"
        })
        if funnel_defination.name:
            actions = get_action_nodes_from_trigger(funnel_defination.id)

            workflow = frappe.get_doc({
                "doctype": "Funnel Workflow",
                "funnel": funnel_defination.parent,
                "variables": json.dumps(variable_payload, indent=2, default=str)
            })
            workflow.insert()
            for action_node in actions:
                create_new_task(workflow.name, action_node.id, funnel_defination.id, "", task_info={
                    "trigger": 'by_funnel',
                    "funnel": selected_funnel,
                    "published_funnel": published_funnel.name,
                })
    return {}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/update_doc.py
import json

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import format_with_variables


def update_doc(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
    src_doc = variables['doc']
    doc = frappe.get_doc(src_doc["doctype"], src_doc["name"])
    value = format_with_variables(node_data["value"], variables)
    doc.set(node_data["field"], format_with_variables(value, variables))
    doc.save()
    return {}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/update_doctype.py
import json
import frappe
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import (
    format_with_variables,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys


def update_doctype(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)

    doctype_info = node_data.get("docTypeJson")
    if doctype_info:
        doctype_info = format_with_variables(doctype_info, variables)

    doctype_info = json.loads(doctype_info)

    existing_doc = frappe.get_doc(doctype_info["doctype"], doctype_info["name"])

    doctype_info.pop("doctype")
    doctype_info.pop("name")

    existing_doc.update(doctype_info)

    existing_doc.save(ignore_permissions=True)

    return {action_return_keys.result: existing_doc.as_dict()}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/var_filter.py
import copy
import json
import re

from frappe.utils.safe_exec import safe_exec

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import format_with_variables


def exec_py_exp(py_exp, variables):
    py_exp = py_exp.replace('\n', '\n  ')
    py_exp = "def expression_function(variables):\n  frappe = None\n  " + py_exp
    _g, _l = safe_exec(py_exp)
    expression_function = _g.get("expression_function")
    variables = copy.deepcopy(variables)
    result = expression_function(variables)
    return True if result else False


def var_filter(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
    target_handle_id = "yes"
    if node_data.get("use_python_expression"):
        py_expr = node_data["expression"]
        passed = exec_py_exp(py_expr, variables)
        if not passed:
            target_handle_id = "no"
        return {
            action_return_keys.result: target_handle_id,
            action_return_keys.source_handle_id: target_handle_id
        }

    for filter in node_data["filters"]:
        variable = filter.get("variable")
        match = filter.get("match")
        value = filter.get("value")
        if variable:
            variable = format_with_variables(variable, variables)
        if value:
            value = format_with_variables(value, variables)

        if match == "equals":
            if str(variable).strip() != str(value).strip():
                target_handle_id = "no"
                break
        elif match == "not_equals":
            if str(variable).strip() == str(value).strip():
                target_handle_id = "no"
                break
        elif match == "contains":
            if str(value) not in str(variable):
                target_handle_id = "no"
                break
        elif match == "not_contains":
            if str(value) in str(variable):
                target_handle_id = "no"
                break
        elif match == "regex":
            try:
                if not re.compile(value).match(str(variable)):
                    target_handle_id = "no"
                    break
            except Exception as e:
                raise Exception("Invalid regex (" + value + ") in filter, " + str(e))
        # elif match == "is_set":
        #     if not variable and str(variable) != "0":
        #         target_handle_id = "no"
        #         break
        # elif match == "is_not_set":
        #     if variable or str(variable) == "0":
        #         target_handle_id = "no"
        #         break
        else:
            raise Exception("Invalid match type in filter: " + str(match))

    return {
        action_return_keys.result: target_handle_id,
        action_return_keys.source_handle_id: target_handle_id
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/wait_for_nodes_to_complete.py
import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys, task_states
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import (
    update_task_status,
)


def get_parent_nodes(action_node_id):
    values = {"id": action_node_id}
    nodes = frappe.db.sql(
        """
        select * from `tabFunnel Definition`
        where id in (
            select source
            from `tabFunnel Definition`
            WHERE `element_type` = 'edge'
            and parenttype = 'Funnel Published'
            and target in (
                SELECT id
                FROM `tabFunnel Definition`
                WHERE parenttype = 'Funnel Published'
                and  id = %(id)s
            )
        );
        """,
        values=values,
        as_dict=1,
    )
    return nodes


def wait_for_nodes_to_complete(action_node, variables):
    node_id = action_node.id
    variable_name = f"node_wait_for_at-{node_id}"
    executed_nodes = variables.get(variable_name, [])

    current_task_doc = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Task", variables["task_name"])
    current_parent_node_id = current_task_doc.trigger_node_id

    executed_nodes.append(current_parent_node_id)
    variables[variable_name] = executed_nodes

    parent_nodes = get_parent_nodes(node_id)
    parent_node_ids = [pn.id for pn in parent_nodes]

    is_all_executed = True
    for parent_node_id in parent_node_ids:
        is_all_executed = is_all_executed and parent_node_id in executed_nodes

    need_to_wait_for_oter_nodes = not is_all_executed

    if need_to_wait_for_oter_nodes:
        update_task_status(
            variables["task_name"],
            task_states.CANCELLED,
            {
                "cancel_reason": "all parent nodes not executed",
                "exec_details": {
                    "parent_node_ids": parent_node_ids,
                    "current_parent_node_id": current_parent_node_id,
                    "current_node_id": node_id,
                    "executed_parent_nodes": executed_nodes,
                },
            },
        )

    return {
        action_return_keys.skip_task_creation: need_to_wait_for_oter_nodes,
        action_return_keys.skip_task_update: need_to_wait_for_oter_nodes,
        action_return_keys.result: {
            "parent_node_ids": parent_node_ids,
            "executed_parent_nodes": executed_nodes,
            "info": "all parent nodes are executed",
        },
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/wait_node.py
import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys, task_states
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import create_new_task
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import format_with_variables


def wait_node(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)

    task_name = variables["task_name"]
    workflow_name = variables["workflow_name"]
    minutes = str(node_data.get("minutes", '0'))
    minutes = format_with_variables(minutes, variables)
    minutes = float(minutes)
    execution_time = datetime.now() + timedelta(0, minutes * 60)
    create_new_task(
        workflow_name,
        action_node.id,
        action_node.id,
        task_name,
        execution_time,
        status=task_states.AWAITING_TRIGGER,
    )

    return {
        action_return_keys.skip_task_creation: True,
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/wait_till_date.py
import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys, task_states
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import (
    update_task_status,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import (
    format_with_variables,
)


def wait_till_date(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)

    task_name = variables["task_name"]

    target_date = node_data["target_date"]
    target_date = format_with_variables(target_date, variables)
    execute_at = node_data["execute_at"]
    days = node_data.get("days", 0)
    if execute_at == "same_day":
        days = 0
    elif execute_at == "days_before":
        days = -days

    execution_time = datetime.strptime(target_date, "%Y-%m-%d") + timedelta(days)

    update_task_status(
        task_name,
        task_states.AWAITING_TRIGGER,
        {
            "execution_time": execution_time,
            "days": days,
            "execute_at": execute_at,
            "target_date": target_date,
            "current_time": datetime.now(),
        },
        {"trigger_after": execution_time},
    )

    return {
        action_return_keys.skip_task_creation: True,
        action_return_keys.skip_task_update: True,
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/whatsapp_question_ai_qa_agent.py
import json

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys, task_states
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.chat_agent_apis import create_thread, interact_with_ai
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import create_new_task
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import format_with_variables
from nextai.whatsapp_business_api_integration.doctype.whatsapp_message.whatsapp_message import send_message


def whatsapp_question_ai_qa_agent(action_node, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
    assistant_id = node_data["assistant_id"]
    openai_thread_id = create_thread()

    task_name = variables["task_name"]
    workflow_name = variables["workflow_name"]
    variables["openai_thread_id"] = openai_thread_id
    message_body = node_data.get("first_message_to_send")
    if message_body:
        message_body = format_with_variables(message_body, variables)
    if message_body:
        ai_response = interact_with_ai(assistant_id, variables["openai_thread_id"], message_body)
        variables["last_ai_response"] = ai_response
        response_type = ai_response.get("type")
        if response_type == 'message':
            message_text = ai_response.get("content")
            if message_text:
                send_message(variables.get("contact_name"), message_text)
        else:
            raise Exception("Invalid response type received from AI assistant")
    create_new_task(workflow_name, action_node.id, action_node.id, task_name, status=task_states.AWAITING_TRIGGER)

    return {
        action_return_keys.result: {
            "openai_thread_id": openai_thread_id,
        },
        action_return_keys.skip_task_creation: True,
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/actions/whatsapp_question_boolean.py
import json

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import action_return_keys, task_states
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import create_new_task
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import format_with_variables
from nextai.whatsapp_business_api_integration.doctype.whatsapp_message.whatsapp_message import send_message


def whatsapp_question_boolean(action_node, variables):
    if not variables.get("contact_name"):
        raise Exception("No whatsapp contact found in variables")
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)

    yes_field = node_data.get("yes_field")
    no_field = node_data.get("no_field")

    interactive_message_data = [{
        'type': "reply",
        'reply': {
            'id': "yes_field",
            'title': yes_field or "Yes"
        }
    }, {
        'type': "reply",
        'reply': {
            'id': "no_field",
            'title': no_field or "No"
        }
    }]

    message_body = node_data.get("message")
    if message_body:
        message_body = format_with_variables(message_body, variables)

    send_message(variables.get("contact_name"), message_body, message_type="Button",
                 interactive_message_data=interactive_message_data)

    task_name = variables["task_name"]
    workflow_name = variables["workflow_name"]
    create_new_task(workflow_name, action_node.id, action_node.id,
                    task_name, status=task_states.AWAITING_TRIGGER)

    return {
        action_return_keys.skip_task_creation: True,
    }

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.executable_trigger_nodes import (
    get_executable_trigger_nodes,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_fields import (
    get_all_fields,
    get_fields,
)


# nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.api.get_doc_fields
@frappe.whitelist()
def get_doc_fields(doctype):
    return get_all_fields(doctype)


# nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.api.get_doc_whatsapp_fields
@frappe.whitelist()
def get_doc_whatsapp_fields(doctype):
    # TODO: send only probable whatsapp fields
    values = {"doctype": doctype}
    fields = frappe.db.sql(
        """
        select
            fieldname, fieldtype, label
        from `tabDocField`
        where
            parent= %(doctype)s
            and hidden=0
        """,
        values=values,
        as_dict=1,
    )
    fields2 = frappe.db.sql(
        """
        select
            fieldname, fieldtype, label
        from `tabCustom Field`
        where
          dt=%(doctype)s
           and hidden=0
        """,
        values=values,
        as_dict=1,
    )
    all_fields = []
    for f in fields + fields2:
        field_name = f.get("fieldname")
        field_label = f.get("label")
        field_type = f.get("fieldtype")
        if field_name and field_label:
            all_fields.append(
                {
                    "fieldname": "trigger." + str(field_name),
                    "label": str(doctype) + "." + str(field_label),
                    "fieldtype": field_type,
                }
            )
    all_fields.append(
        {
            "label": "Connected Contact",
            "fieldname": "connected_contact",
            "fieldtype": "Contact",
        }
    )
    return all_fields


# nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.api.get_doc_field_values
@frappe.whitelist()
def get_doc_field_values(doctype, field_name, limit=100, skip=0, unique=True):
    if not field_name:
        return []
    if not doctype:
        return []

    all_values = []
    values = {
        "doctype": f"tab%s" % doctype,
        "field_name": field_name,
        "limit": int(limit),
        "skip": int(skip),
    }
    sql = (
        "select `"
        + field_name
        + "` from `tab"
        + doctype
        + "` limit %(limit)s offset %(skip)s"
    )
    fetched_values = frappe.db.sql(sql, values=values, as_dict=1)

    # add unique values
    for value in fetched_values:
        field_value = value.get(field_name)
        if unique:
            if field_value not in all_values:
                all_values.append(field_value)
        else:
            all_values.append(field_value)

    return [{"value": value} for value in all_values]


# nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.api.get_trigger_field_list_with_contact
@frappe.whitelist()
def get_trigger_field_list_with_contact(trigger_doc, field_type=None):
    trigger_fields = get_fields(trigger_doc, "trigger.")
    contact_fields = get_fields("Contact", "contact.")
    all_fields = [
        {"label": "Connected Contact", "value": "connected_contact", "type": "Contact"}
    ]

    if not field_type:
        all_fields = trigger_fields + contact_fields
    else:
        for trigger_field in trigger_fields:
            if trigger_field["type"] == field_type:
                all_fields.append(trigger_field)

    return all_fields


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_manual_trigger_funnels(doctype):
    if not isinstance(doctype, (list, tuple)):
        doctype = doctype.split(",")

    values = {
        "doctypes": doctype,
    }

    nodes = frappe.db.sql(
        """
        SELECT *, (SELECT funnel FROM `tabFunnel Published` WHERE name = `tabFunnel Definition`.parent) AS funnel_name
        FROM `tabFunnel Definition`
        WHERE `element_type` = 'Trigger'
        AND parenttype = 'Funnel Published'
        AND parent IN (
            SELECT name
            FROM `tabFunnel Published`
            WHERE published = 1
        )
        AND type = 'manual_trigger'
        AND JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`data`, '$.doctype')) IN %(doctypes)s
    """,
        values=values,
        as_dict=1,
    )

    return get_executable_trigger_nodes(nodes)


# nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.api.get_chatnext_assistant_trigger_list
@frappe.whitelist()
def get_chatnext_assistant_trigger_list(
    route: str,
    page_type: str = "",
    page_name: str = "",
):
    if not route:
        raise frappe.ValidationError("Missing route")
    if page_type not in ["doctype", "doclist", "others", "workspace", ""]:
        raise frappe.ValidationError("Invalid page type")
    if page_type in ["doctype", "doclist", "workspace"]:
        if not page_name:
            raise frappe.ValidationError("Missing page name")
    values = {"page_name": page_name, "page_type": page_type, "route": route}

    # TODO: optimize this query
    nodes = frappe.db.sql(
        """
            SELECT *, JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`data`, '$.name_of_action')) as name_of_action,
             (SELECT funnel FROM `tabFunnel Published` WHERE name = `tabFunnel Definition`.parent) AS funnel_name
            FROM `tabFunnel Definition`
            WHERE `element_type` = 'Trigger'
            AND parenttype = 'Funnel Published'
            AND parent IN (
                SELECT name
                FROM `tabFunnel Published`
                WHERE published = 1
            )
            AND type = 'chatnext_assistant_trigger'
            AND (
                JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`data`, '$.page_type')) = 'all' or (
                    %(page_type)s in ('doctype', 'doclist') and
                    JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`data`, '$.page_type')) = %(page_type)s and
                    JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`data`, '$.doctype')) = %(page_name)s
                ) or (
                    JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`data`, '$.page_type')) = 'route' and
                    JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`data`, '$.route')) = %(route)s
                ) or (
                    JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`data`, '$.page_type')) = 'regex' and
                    %(route)s regexp JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`data`, '$.regex'))
                ) or (
                    %(page_type)s = 'workspace' and
                    JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`data`, '$.page_type')) = %(page_type)s and
                    JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`data`, '$.workspace')) = %(page_name)s
                )
            )
        """,
        values=values,
        as_dict=1,
    )
    # TODO: optimize this
    return get_executable_trigger_nodes(nodes)


# nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.api.get_on_submit_assistant_trigger_list
@frappe.whitelist()
def get_on_submit_assistant_trigger_list(doctype):
    if not doctype:
        raise frappe.ValidationError("Missing doctype")

    nodes = frappe.db.sql(
        """
            SELECT *
            FROM `tabFunnel Definition`
            WHERE
                element_type = 'Trigger'
                AND parenttype = 'Funnel Published'
                AND parent in (
                    select name
                    from `tabFunnel Published`
                    where
                        published = 1
                )
                AND `type` = 'on_submit_assistant_trigger'
                and JSON_UNQUOTE(
                    JSON_EXTRACT(`data`, '$.selectDoctype')
                ) = %(doctype)s
        """,
        values={"doctype": doctype},
        as_dict=1,
    )

    return nodes

# nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.assistant_api.get_assistant_messages
@frappe.whitelist(allow_guest=True)
def get_assistant_messages(session_id):
    user_id = frappe.session.user
    filters = [
        ["session", "=", session_id],
    ]
    or_filters = [
        ["user", "=", user_id],
        ["show_in_chat_history", "=", 1]
    ]
    return frappe.get_all(
        "CNA Conversation",
        filters=filters,
        or_filters=or_filters,
        fields=[
            "name",
            "creation",
            "status",
            "id",
            "session",
            "user",
            "author",
            "message_type",
            "message",
        ],
        order_by="creation",
    )


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_assigned_tasks():
    # get tasks
    values = {
        "user_id": f"%{frappe.session.user}%",
    }
    tasks = frappe.db.sql(
        """
        SELECT name, subject, project, _assign
        FROM `tabTask`
        WHERE `_assign` like %(user_id)s
        """,
        values=values,
        as_dict=1,
    )
    return tasks


# nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.assistant_api.get_assistant_session_info
@frappe.whitelist(allow_guest=True)
def get_assistant_session_info(session_id):
    user_id = frappe.session.user
    values = {
        "user_id": user_id,
        "session_id": session_id,
    }
    workflows = frappe.db.sql(
        """
            SELECT
                name, status
            FROM
                `tabFunnel Workflow`
            WHERE
                JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`variables`, '$.session_id')) = %(session_id)s and
                JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`variables`, '$.user_id')) = %(user_id)s
            order by creation desc
            limit 1
        """,
        values=values,
        as_dict=1,
    )

    if not len(workflows):
        return {"awaiting_task_count": 0}
    workflow = workflows[0]
    values = {
        "workflow_id": workflow.name,
    }
    awaiting_counts = frappe.db.sql(
        """
            SELECT
                count(*) as count
            FROM
                `tabFunnel Task`
            WHERE
                execution_status =  'awaiting_trigger' and
                workflow = %(workflow_id)s
            limit 1
        """,
        values=values,
        as_dict=1,
    )
    awaiting_count = awaiting_counts[0].count
    return {"workflow": workflow, "awaiting_task_count": awaiting_count}


# nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.assistant_api.get_chatnext_assistant_private_doc_trigger_list
@frappe.whitelist()
def get_chatnext_assistant_private_doc_trigger_list(
    doctype: str,
):
    values = {"doctype": doctype}

    # TODO reduce un necessary json extract
    nodes = frappe.db.sql(
        """
            SELECT *, JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`data`, '$.name_of_action')) as name_of_action,
            JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`data`, '$.button_label')) as button_label,
            (SELECT funnel FROM `tabFunnel Published` WHERE name = `tabFunnel Definition`.parent) AS funnel_name
            FROM `tabFunnel Definition`
            WHERE `element_type` = 'Trigger'
            AND parenttype = 'Funnel Published'
            AND parent IN (
                SELECT name
                FROM `tabFunnel Published`
                WHERE published = 1
            )
            AND type = 'chatnext_assistant_trigger'
            AND JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`data`, '$.page_type')) ='doctype'
            and JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`data`, '$.doctype')) = %(doctype)s
            and JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`data`, '$.show_as_button')) = 'true'
        """,
        values=values,
        as_dict=1,
    )
    executable_trigger_nodes = get_executable_trigger_nodes(nodes)
    for trigger_node in executable_trigger_nodes:
        try:
            data = json.loads(trigger_node['data'])
            trigger_node['data_obj'] = data
            trigger_category = data.get('trigger_category')
            if trigger_category:
                trigger_node['trigger_category_name'] = trigger_category
                trigger_node['trigger_category'] = frappe.get_doc('Funnel Trigger Category', trigger_category).as_dict()
        except:
            pass
    return executable_trigger_nodes


# nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.assistant_api.get_chatnext_assistant_private_triggers_doctype_list

def get_chatnext_assistant_private_triggers_doctype_list():

            SELECT *, JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`data`, '$.doctype')) as doctype_name
            FROM `tabFunnel Definition`
            WHERE `element_type` = 'Trigger'
            AND parenttype = 'Funnel Published'
            AND parent IN (
                SELECT name
                FROM `tabFunnel Published`
                WHERE published = 1
            )
            AND type = 'chatnext_assistant_trigger'
            AND JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`data`, '$.page_type')) ='doctype'
        """,
        values={},
        as_dict=1,
    )
    return list(
        set([node["doctype_name"] for node in get_executable_trigger_nodes(nodes)])
    )
