# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/awaiting_actions/chatnext_assistant_ai_qa_agent.py
import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import task_states
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.actions.whatsapp_question_ai_qa_agent import (
    interact_with_ai,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.funnel_task import queue_execute_pending_tasks
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.add_chatnext_assistant_message import (
    add_bot_message,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import (
    update_task_status,
    create_new_task,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_action_nodes import (
    get_next_action_nodes,
)


def chatnext_assistant_ai_qa_agent(action_node, task, assistant_message, variables):
    node_data = frappe.parse_json(action_node.data)
    assistant_id = node_data["assistant_id"]
    workflow_name = task.workflow
    variables["last_user_message"] = assistant_message
    ai_response = interact_with_ai(
        assistant_id, variables["openai_thread_id"], assistant_message
    )
    variables["last_ai_response"] = ai_response
    response_type = ai_response.get("type")
    if response_type == "finish":
        variable_name = node_data.get("variable")
        function_args = ai_response.get("args")
        variables[variable_name] = function_args

        update_task_status(task, task_states.COMPLETED, assistant_message)

        # create new task
        next_action_nodes = get_next_action_nodes(action_node.id)
        for next_node in next_action_nodes:
            create_new_task(workflow_name, next_node.id, action_node.id, task.name)
        if len(next_action_nodes):
            queue_execute_pending_tasks()
    else:
        message_text = ai_response.get("content")
        if message_text:
            user_id = variables.get("user_id")
            session_id = variables.get("session_id")
            add_bot_message(session_id, message_text, user_id, "pending")

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/awaiting_actions/chatnext_assistant_document_approval.py
import json
import uuid

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import task_states
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.funnel_task import (
    queue_execute_pending_tasks,
    set_funnel_user,
    create_new_task,

    update_task_status,
)

# nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.awaiting_actions.chatnext_assistant_document_approval.get_approve_reject_list
@frappe.whitelist()
def get_approve_reject_list(doc_type: str, doc_name: str):
    user_id = frappe.session.user
    values = {"doctype": doc_type, "docname": doc_name, "userid": user_id}
    fields = frappe.db.sql(
        """
        select
            name, required_fields
        from `tabCN Doc Approval`
        where
            doc_type = %(doctype)s and
            doc_name = %(docname)s and
            status = 'pending' and
            ((use_role = 0 and user = %(userid)s) or
            (use_role = 1 and role in (
                select role from `tabHas Role`
                where
                    parenttype = 'user' and parent = %(userid)s
            )))
        """,
        values=values,
        as_dict=1,
    )
    return fields


# nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.awaiting_actions.chatnext_assistant_document_approval.approve_reject
@frappe.whitelist()
def approve_reject(docname: str, approved: bool):
    user_id = frappe.session.user
    doc = frappe.get_doc("CN Doc Approval", docname)
    if doc.user != user_id:
        if (
            len(
                frappe.get_all(
                    "Has Role",
                    filters={"parent": user_id, "parenttype": "user", "role": doc.role},
                )
            )
            == 0
        ):
            raise frappe.PermissionError(
                frappe._(f"You cannot approve or reject this {doc.user} {user_id}")
            )
    if doc.status != "pending":
        raise frappe.ValidationError(frappe._(f"This is already {doc.status}"))

    doc.status = "approved" if approved else "rejected"
    doc.save(ignore_permissions=True)

    funnel_task = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Task", doc.task)

    set_funnel_user()

    # execute next nodes
    actions = get_next_action_nodes_with_source_handle_id(
        funnel_task.target_node_id, doc.status
    )

    update_task_status(
        funnel_task.name,
        task_states.COMPLETED,
        f"{doc.doc_type}({doc.doc_name}) {doc.status} by {user_id}",
    )

    funnel_workflow = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Workflow", funnel_task.workflow)
    workflow_variables = json.loads(funnel_workflow.variables)
    session_id = None
    if len(actions) > 0 and doc.open_chatnext_assistant:
        session_id = workflow_variables.get("session_id") or str(uuid.uuid4())
        conversation = frappe.get_doc(
            {
                "doctype": "CNA Conversation",
                "status": "default",
                "user": user_id,
                "session": session_id,
                "id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
                "author": "Bot",
                "message_type": "Loader",
                "message": "Fetching data...",
            }
        )
        conversation.insert(ignore_permissions=True)

    funnel_workflow.variables = json.dumps(
        {
            **workflow_variables,
            "event": "chatnext_trigger",
            "session_id": session_id,
            "user_id": user_id,
            "trigger_user_id": user_id,
        }
    )
    funnel_workflow.save(ignore_permissions=True)

    for action_node in actions:
        create_new_task(
            funnel_task.workflow, action_node.id, funnel_task.target_node_id, ""
        )

    queue_execute_pending_tasks()

    return {"session": session_id}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/awaiting_actions/chatnext_assistant_form_question.py
import json
import traceback

import frappe
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import task_states, node_names
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.awaiting_actions.chatnext_assistant_text_question import (
    get_awaiting_assistant_tasks,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.funnel_task import queue_execute_pending_tasks
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import (
    update_task_status,
    create_new_task,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_action_nodes import (
    get_next_action_nodes,
    get_node_from_id,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.update_workflow import update_workflow


def chatnext_assistant_form_question(session_id=None, submission=None, workflow_id=None):
    awaiting_tasks = get_awaiting_assistant_tasks(
        session_id,
        [
            node_names.chatnext_assistant_form_question,
            node_names.chatnext_assistant_trigger,
        ],
        workflow_id=workflow_id,
    )
    if len(awaiting_tasks):
        for task in awaiting_tasks:
            # this would execute only once
            no_matched_action = False
            try:
                action_node = get_node_from_id(task.target_node_id)[0]
                if action_node.type == node_names.chatnext_assistant_form_question:
                    workflow_name = task.workflow
                    workflow_doc = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Workflow", workflow_name)
                    variables = frappe.parse_json(workflow_doc.variables)
                    node_data = json.loads(action_node.get("data"))
                    answer_var = node_data["answer_var"]
                    variables[answer_var] = submission
                    update_workflow(workflow_name, variables)
                    update_task_status(task, task_states.COMPLETED, submission)
                elif action_node.type == node_names.chatnext_assistant_trigger:
                    # TODO: recheck this use case, probably with openai qa-agent re-trigger
                    workflow_name = task.workflow
                    workflow_doc = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Workflow", workflow_name)
                    variables = frappe.parse_json(workflow_doc.variables)
                    variables["docname"] = submission["docname"]
                    doc = frappe.get_doc(variables["doctype"], submission["docname"])
                    variables["doc"] = doc.as_dict()
                    update_workflow(workflow_name, variables)
                    update_task_status(task, task_states.COMPLETED, submission)
                else:
                    no_matched_action = True

                if not no_matched_action:
                    next_action_nodes = get_next_action_nodes(action_node.id)
                    for next_node in next_action_nodes:
                        create_new_task(
                            task.workflow, next_node.id, action_node.id, task.name
                        )
                    queue_execute_pending_tasks(True)

            except Exception as e:
                exec_trace = traceback.format_exc()
                stack_trace = traceback.format_stack()
                update_task_status(
                    task,
                    task_states.FAILED,
                    {
                        "error": str(e),
                        "node": task.target_node_id,
                        "exec_trace": exec_trace,
                        "stack_trace": stack_trace,
                    },
                )
            if not no_matched_action:
                break

import json

    update_task_status,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_action_nodes import (
    get_next_action_nodes_with_source_handle_id, get_node_from_id,
)
from nextai.utils import get_translated_doc_name


def is_for_current_user(todo_doc):
    user_id = frappe.session.user
    if todo_doc.allocated_to == user_id:
        return True
    user_roles = frappe.get_roles(user_id)
    if todo_doc.role in user_roles:
        return True
    return False


def get_pending_actions(doctype=None, docname=None, status=None, role=None, user=None, fields=None):
    filters = {
        'custom_approval_type': ("in", ["Multi Actions", "Approval Matrix"]),
        'status': "Open",
    }
    if doctype: filters["reference_type"] = doctype
    if docname: filters["reference_name"] = docname
    if status: filters["status"] = status
    if role: filters["role"] = role
    if user: filters["allocated_to"] = user
    return frappe.get_all("ToDo", filters=filters, fields=fields or ["*"], order_by="creation")


def restrict_modification_on_approval_pending(doc, event):
    user_id = frappe.session.user
    # allow administrator and funnel user only to update always
    if user_id == 'Administrator' or user_id == get_funnel_user_id():
        return

    todo_actions_list = get_pending_actions(doctype=doc.doctype, docname=doc.name, status="Open")
    if not len(todo_actions_list):
        return
    for todo_action in todo_actions_list:
        if is_for_current_user(todo_action):
            return

    frappe.throw("You cannot update this document at the moment, please wait for the approval to be completed")


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_permitted_multi_actions(doctype, docname):
    # fetch only the funnel actions this doctype , docname and user or role
    funnel_actions_list = get_pending_actions(doctype=doctype, docname=docname, fields=['*'])
    if not len(funnel_actions_list):
        return

    for action in funnel_actions_list:
        if is_for_current_user(action):
            return {
                **action,
                'options_data': action.custom_doctype_actions
            }


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_all_permitted_multi_actions():
    return get_pending_actions()


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_doctype_list():
    funnel_actions_list = get_pending_actions(fields=['reference_type'])
    actions_visible_doctypes = [a.reference_type for a in funnel_actions_list]
    return list(set(actions_visible_doctypes))


def complete_doc_action(doc_action_name, selected_option, user_id, variables, session_id):
    doc = frappe.get_doc("ToDo", doc_action_name)
    funnel_task = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Task", doc.custom_funnel_task)

    funnel_workflow = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Workflow", funnel_task.workflow)
    workflow_variables = json.loads(funnel_workflow.variables)

    update_task_status(
        funnel_task.name,
        task_states.COMPLETED,
        f"{user_id} selected `{selected_option}` on {doc.reference_type}({doc.reference_name})",
    )
    actions = get_next_action_nodes_with_source_handle_id(funnel_task.target_node_id, selected_option)
    funnel_workflow.variables = json.dumps(
        {
            "trigger_user_id": user_id,  # it can be replaced by the actual used who triggered the funnel
            "event": "chatnext_trigger",
            **workflow_variables,
            **variables,
            "session_id": session_id,
            "user_id": user_id,  # last user who performed any action
        }
    )
    funnel_workflow.save(ignore_permissions=True)
    for action_node in actions:
        create_new_task(
            funnel_task.workflow, action_node.id, funnel_task.target_node_id, funnel_task.name
        )
    queue_execute_pending_tasks()

    doc.status = "Closed"
    doc.custom_selected_doctype_action = selected_option
    doc.save(ignore_permissions=True)


def on_doc_action_form_submitted(
    submission, doc_action_name, selected_option, session_id, user_id,
    submission_variable,
):
    complete_doc_action(
        doc_action_name,
        selected_option,
        user_id,
        {submission_variable: submission},
        session_id
    )


@frappe.whitelist()
def select_event_from_options(selected_option, data, variables=None, doctype=None):
    user_id = frappe.session.user
    set_funnel_user()

    data = json.loads(data)
    doc_action_name = data["name"]
    todo_doc = frappe.get_doc("ToDo", doc_action_name)

    if todo_doc.custom_approval_type == "Approval Matrix":
        return action_select_handler(todo_doc, selected_option, user_id)

    if variables is None:
        variables = {}
    funnel_task = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Task", todo_doc.custom_funnel_task)

    funnel_workflow = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Workflow", funnel_task.workflow)
    workflow_variables = json.loads(funnel_workflow.variables)
    session_id = workflow_variables.get("session_id")

    if session_id:
        exist = frappe.db.exists("CNA Conversation", {"session": session_id, "user": user_id, "message_type": "Form", "status": "pending"})
        if exist:
            return {"session": session_id}

    if todo_doc.custom_open_chatnext_assistant_on_action:
        session_id = session_id or str(uuid.uuid4())
        target_node_id = funnel_task.target_node_id
        target_node = get_node_from_id(target_node_id)[0]
        target_node_data = json.loads(target_node.data)
        buttons = target_node_data['buttons']
        selected_button = None
        for button in buttons:
            if button['option'] == selected_option:
                selected_button = button
                break

        if not selected_button:
            raise Exception('select a valid option')

        form_question = selected_button.get('formQuestion', {})
        is_info_required = form_question.get('info_required')
        if is_info_required:
            container_data = form_question.get('container', {})
            submission_variable = container_data.get('answer_var')
            enable_data_update_on_submit = container_data.get('enableDataUpdateOnSubmit')

            # fetch form json
            formio_data = get_form_json(container_data, workflow_variables)
            prefill_data = create_form_prefill_data(formio_data, workflow_variables)
            create_form_conversation(
                container_data, workflow_variables, user_id, session_id, {
                    "method": 'nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.awaiting_actions.chatnext_assistant_multi_actions.on_doc_action_form_submitted',
                    "payload": {
                        'doc_action_name': doc_action_name,
                        'selected_option': selected_option,
                        'submission_variable': submission_variable,
                        "show_in_chat_history": True if container_data.get('show_in_chat_history') else False
                    }
                },
                # show_in_chat_history=True if container_data.get('show_in_chat_history') else False,
                workflow_id=funnel_task.workflow, prefill_data=prefill_data,
                formio_data=formio_data, enable_data_update_on_submit=enable_data_update_on_submit
            )
        else:
            # show loading
            conversation = frappe.get_doc(
                {
                    "doctype": "CNA Conversation",
                    "status": "default",
                    "user": user_id,
                    "session": session_id,
                    "id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
                    "workflow": funnel_task.workflow,
                    "author": "Bot",
                    "message_type": "Loader",
                    "message": "Fetching data...",
                }
            )
            conversation.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
            complete_doc_action(doc_action_name, selected_option, user_id, variables, session_id)
        return {"session": session_id}

    complete_doc_action(doc_action_name, selected_option, user_id, variables, session_id)
    return {
        "docname": get_translated_doc_name(doctype) if doctype else None,
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/awaiting_actions/chatnext_assistant_question_single_choice.py
import json

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import task_states
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.funnel_task import queue_execute_pending_tasks
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.add_chatnext_assistant_message import (
    add_bot_message,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import (
    update_task_status,
    create_new_task,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_action_nodes import (
    get_next_action_nodes_with_source_handle_id,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.update_workflow import update_workflow


def chatnext_assistant_question_single_choice(action_node, task, message, variables):
    node_data = json.loads(action_node.get("data"))
    available_options = [op.get("option") for op in node_data.get("options") or []]
    if message in available_options:
        workflow_name = task.workflow
        workflow_doc = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Workflow", workflow_name)
        variables = frappe.parse_json(workflow_doc.variables)
        answer_var = node_data["answer_var"]
        variables[answer_var] = message
        update_workflow(workflow_name, variables)
        update_task_status(task, task_states.COMPLETED, message)

        next_action_nodes = get_next_action_nodes_with_source_handle_id(
            action_node.id, message
        )
        for next_node in next_action_nodes:
            create_new_task(workflow_name, next_node.id, action_node.id, task.name)
        queue_execute_pending_tasks(True)
    else:
        reply_if_wrong_option_received = node_data.get("reply_if_wrong_option_received")
        if reply_if_wrong_option_received:
            user_id = variables.get("user_id")
            session_id = variables.get("session_id")
            add_bot_message(session_id, reply_if_wrong_option_received, user_id)
            update_task_status(
                task,
                task_states.AWAITING_TRIGGER,
                {
                    "error": "Invalid input",
                },
            )

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/awaiting_actions/chatnext_assistant_text_question.py
import json
import traceback

import frappe
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import node_names
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import task_states
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.funnel_task import queue_execute_pending_tasks
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.add_chatnext_assistant_message import (
    add_bot_message,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import (
    update_task_status,
    create_new_task,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_action_nodes import (
    get_action_node_from_id,
    get_next_action_nodes,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.update_workflow import update_workflow
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.validate_input import is_valid_input
from .chatnext_assistant_ai_qa_agent import chatnext_assistant_ai_qa_agent
from .chatnext_assistant_question_single_choice import (
    chatnext_assistant_question_single_choice,
)


def get_awaiting_assistant_tasks(session_id=None, node_types=None, workflow_id=None, order_by="creation", desc=1):
    if node_types is None:
        node_types = []
    values = {
        "session_id": session_id,
        "workflow_id": workflow_id,
        "execution_status": task_states.AWAITING_TRIGGER,
        "order_by": order_by,
        "desc": "desc" if desc else "",
        "target_node_name": node_types,
    }
    workflow_from_session = '''
        workflow in (
            SELECT name FROM `tabFunnel Workflow`
            WHERE JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`variables`, '$.session_id')) = %(session_id)s
        )
    '''
    nodes = frappe.db.sql(
        f"""
        SELECT * FROM `tabFunnel Task`
        WHERE
            execution_status =  %(execution_status)s and
            target_node_name in %(target_node_name)s and
            {"workflow = %(workflow_id)s" if workflow_id else workflow_from_session}
        ORDER BY %(order_by)s %(desc)s
    """, values=values,
        as_dict=1,
    )
    return nodes


def handle_chatnext_assistant_text_response(session_id, message, user_id):
    assistant_text_answer_node_types = [
        node_names.chatnext_assistant_text_question,
        node_names.chatnext_assistant_question_single_choice,
        node_names.chatnext_assistant_ai_qa_agent,
    ]
    awaiting_tasks = get_awaiting_assistant_tasks(
        session_id, assistant_text_answer_node_types
    )
    had_awaiting_task = False
    if len(awaiting_tasks):
        for task in awaiting_tasks:
            try:
                workflow_name = task.workflow
                workflow_doc = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Workflow", workflow_name)
                variables = frappe.parse_json(workflow_doc.variables)
                action_node = get_action_node_from_id(task.target_node_id)[0]

                if task.target_node_name == node_names.chatnext_assistant_ai_qa_agent:
                    had_awaiting_task = True
                    chatnext_assistant_ai_qa_agent(
                        action_node, task, message, variables
                    )
                    continue

                if (
                        task.target_node_name
                        == node_names.chatnext_assistant_question_single_choice
                ):
                    had_awaiting_task = True
                    chatnext_assistant_question_single_choice(
                        action_node, task, message, variables
                    )
                    continue

                if task.target_node_name != node_names.chatnext_assistant_text_question:
                    continue

                # handle text question answer
                had_awaiting_task = True

                # this would execute only once
                node_data = json.loads(action_node.get("data"))

                is_validate_answer = False
                validation_type = None
                if "validate_answer" in node_data:
                    is_validate_answer = node_data["validate_answer"]
                if "validation_type" in node_data:
                    validation_type = node_data["validation_type"]

                if (
                        is_validate_answer
                        and validation_type
                        and not is_valid_input(message, validation_type)
                ):
                    error_reply = node_data["reply_if_wrong_option_received"]
                    if error_reply:
                        add_bot_message(session_id, error_reply, user_id)
                        update_task_status(
                            task,
                            task_states.AWAITING_TRIGGER,
                            {
                                "error": "Invalid input",
                            },
                        )
                    break

                else:
                    answer_var = node_data["answer_var"]
                    variables[answer_var] = message

                    update_workflow(workflow_name, variables)
                    update_task_status(task, task_states.COMPLETED, message)

                    next_action_nodes = get_next_action_nodes(action_node.id)
                    for next_node in next_action_nodes:
                        create_new_task(
                            workflow_name, next_node.id, action_node.id, task.name
                        )
                    queue_execute_pending_tasks(True)

            except Exception as e:
                exec_trace = traceback.format_exc()
                stack_trace = traceback.format_stack()
                update_task_status(
                    task,
                    task_states.FAILED,
                    {
                        "error": str(e),
                        "exec_trace": exec_trace,
                        "stack_trace": stack_trace,
                    },
                )

    return had_awaiting_task

import traceback

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import task_states, node_names
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.awaiting_actions.chatnext_assistant_text_question import (
    get_awaiting_assistant_tasks,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.funnel_task import queue_execute_pending_tasks
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import (
    update_task_status,
    create_new_task,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_action_nodes import (
    get_action_node_from_id,
    get_next_action_nodes,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.update_workflow import update_workflow


def chatnext_assistant_trigger(session_id, data):
    awaiting_tasks = get_awaiting_assistant_tasks(
        session_id, [node_names.chatnext_assistant_trigger]
    )
    if len(awaiting_tasks):
        for task in awaiting_tasks:
            # this would execute only once
            no_matched_action = False
            try:
                action_node = get_action_node_from_id(task.target_node_id)[0]
                if action_node.type == node_names.chatnext_assistant_trigger:
                    workflow_name = task.workflow
                    workflow_doc = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Workflow", workflow_name)
                    variables = frappe.parse_json(workflow_doc.variables)
                    variables["docname"] = data["docname"]
                    update_workflow(workflow_name, variables)
                    update_task_status(task, task_states.COMPLETED, data)

                    next_action_nodes = get_next_action_nodes(action_node.id)
                    for next_node in next_action_nodes:
                        create_new_task(
                            workflow_name, next_node.id, action_node.id, task.name
                        )
                    queue_execute_pending_tasks(True)
                else:
                    no_matched_action = True
            except Exception as e:
                exec_trace = traceback.format_exc()
                stack_trace = traceback.format_stack()
                update_task_status(
                    task,
                    task_states.FAILED,
                    {
                        "error": str(e),
                        "exec_trace": exec_trace,
                        "stack_trace": stack_trace,
                    },
                )
            if not no_matched_action:
                break

# This function handles the selection of an action from a ToDo item
def action_select_handler(todo_doc, selected_action, user_id, variables=None):
    if variables is None:
        variables = {}
    action_map = {
        "approve": {"todo_status": "Closed", "outcome": "Approved"},
        "reject": {"todo_status": "Cancelled", "outcome": "Rejected"},
        "sendback": {"todo_status": "Closed", "outcome": "Send Back"},
    }
    norm = (selected_action or "").strip().lower().replace(" ", "")
    if norm not in action_map:  # <-- add this guard
        raise ValueError(f"Invalid action selected: {selected_action}")

    status = action_map[norm]["todo_status"]
    todo = frappe.get_doc("ToDo", todo_doc.name)
    return process_approval_action(
        todo, user_id, status, outcome=action_map[norm]["outcome"]
    )


# This function processes the approval action based on the ToDo item and selected status
def process_approval_action(todo, user_id, status, outcome=None):
    if status not in ["Closed", "Cancelled"]:
        return

    log_name = frappe.db.get_value(
        "Approval Log Entry", {"todo_reference": todo.name}, "name"
    )
    if not log_name:
        raise ValueError(f"Approval Log Entry not found for ToDo {todo.name}")

    approval_log = frappe.get_doc("Approval Log Entry", log_name)

    # Decide which form (if any) to show before committing the outcome
    form_link = None
    generate_form_data = None
    update_data_on_form_submit = False
    form_prefill_data = None
    if outcome == "Approved":
        form_link = getattr(approval_log, "form_for_approval", None)
        if form_link and getattr(approval_log, "update_data_on_approval_form_submit", False):
            update_data_on_form_submit = True
    elif outcome == "Rejected":
        form_link = getattr(approval_log, "form_for_rejection", None)
        if form_link and getattr(approval_log, "update_data_on_reject_form_submit", False):
            update_data_on_form_submit = True
    elif outcome == "Send Back":
        generate_form_data = {
            "components": [
                {
                    "label": "Select Approver/Creator",
                    "widget": "choicesjs",
                    "tableView": True,
                    "dataSrc": "values",
                    "data": {"values": []},
                    "valueProperty": "value",
                    "template": "<span>{{ item.label }}</span>",
                    "validate": {"required": True},
                    "key": "approver",
                    "type": "select",
                    "input": True,
                    "selectValues": "data.values",
                    "clearOnHide": False,
                    "persistent": True,
                    # If you ever want multi-select: add "multiple": True
                },
                {
                    "type": "button",
                    "label": "Submit",
                    "key": "submit",
                    "disableOnInvalid": True,
                    "input": True,
                },
            ]
        }
        get_privous_approver = get_previous_approver(approval_log)
        generate_form_data["components"][0]["data"]["values"] = get_privous_approver
    if form_link or generate_form_data:
        funnel_task = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Task", todo.custom_funnel_task)
        privous_session_id = None
        if form_link:
            form_data = frappe.get_value(
                "Microapp Form Widget", form_link, "custom_form_data"
            )
            funnel_workflow = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Workflow", funnel_task.workflow)
            workflow_variables = json.loads(funnel_workflow.variables)
            privous_session_id = workflow_variables.get("session_id")
            form_prefill_data = create_form_prefill_data(json.loads(form_data), workflow_variables)
        else:
            form_data = json.dumps(generate_form_data)

        new_session_id = trigger_form_conversation(
            form_data,
            user_id,
            form_prefill_data=form_prefill_data,
            enable_data_update_on_submit=update_data_on_form_submit,
            workflow_id=funnel_task.workflow,
            session_id=privous_session_id,
            on_submit={
                "method": "nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.awaiting_actions.chatnext_dynamic_multi_actions.on_doc_action_form_submitted",
                "payload": {
                    "doc_action_name": todo.name,
                    "selected_option": outcome,  # pass semantic result now
                    "todo_status": status,  # keep ToDo status for later
                    "approval_log_name": approval_log.name,
                    "user_id": user_id,
                    "workflow_id": funnel_task.workflow,
                },
            },
        )
        return {"session": new_session_id}
    else:
        return process_after_form_submission(
            approval_log=approval_log,
            status=outcome,
            user_id=user_id,
            doc_action_name=todo.name,
            todo_status=status,
        )


# --- on_doc_action_form_submitted: selected_option now equals the outcome; use todo_status too
def on_doc_action_form_submitted(
    submission,
    doc_action_name,
    selected_option,  # "Approved" | "Rejected" | "Send Back"
    approval_log_name,
    user_id,
    session_id=None,
    todo_status=None,  # new
    workflow_id=None,
):
    approval_log = frappe.get_doc("Approval Log Entry", approval_log_name)

    if isinstance(submission, str):
        try:
            submission_data = json.loads(submission)
        except Exception:
            submission_data = {}
    elif isinstance(submission, dict):
        submission_data = submission
    else:
        submission_data = {}
    if selected_option == "Approved":
        approval_log.approval_response_data = submission_data
    elif selected_option == "Rejected":
        approval_log.rejection_response_data = submission_data
    elif selected_option == "Send Back":
        template_name = approval_log.sendback_email_template
        if template_name:
            recipients = submission_data.get("approver")
            if not recipients:
                frappe.log_error("Send Back recipients not found in submission data")
            if isinstance(recipients, str):
                recipients = [r.strip() for r in recipients.split(",") if r.strip()]
            elif not isinstance(recipients, (list, tuple)):
                recipients = [str(recipients)]
            tracker = frappe.get_doc("Approval Tracker", approval_log.parent)
            doc = frappe.get_doc(tracker.doc_type, tracker.doc_name)

            args = {
                "doc": doc,
                "tracker": tracker,
                "approval_log": approval_log,
                "comment": submission_data.get("comment"),
            }

            rendered = get_email_template(template_name, args)

            subject = rendered.get("subject") or f"Sent Back: {getattr(doc, 'name', tracker.doc_name)}"
            message = rendered.get("message") or "This request has been sent back for changes."

            frappe.sendmail(
                recipients=recipients,
                subject=subject,
                message=message,
                reference_doctype=tracker.doc_type,
                reference_name=tracker.doc_name,
            )

    approval_log.save(ignore_permissions=True)
    log_form_submission(
        reference_doctype=approval_log.parenttype,
        reference_docname=approval_log.parent,
        reference_form=(
            approval_log.form_for_approval
            if selected_option == "Approved"
            else (
                    getattr(approval_log, "form_for_sendback", None)
                    or approval_log.form_for_rejection
            )
        ),
        submitted_by=user_id,
        form_data=submission_data,
    )

    close_assistant_action(doc_action_name, selected_option, user_id, session_id, workflow_id=workflow_id)

    # selected_option is the outcome now
    process_after_form_submission(
        approval_log=approval_log,
        status=selected_option,
        user_id=user_id,
        new_session_id=session_id,
        doc_action_name=doc_action_name,
        todo_status=todo_status
                    or ("Closed" if selected_option in ("Approved", "Send Back") else "Cancelled"),
        send_back_seleted_approver=submission_data.get("approver")
    )


# This function sends a UI action to close the modal after approval/rejection
def close_assistant_action(doc_action_name, selected_option, user_id, session_id=None, workflow_id=None):
    """Sends UI action to close the modal after approval/rejection."""
    conversation = frappe.get_doc(
        {
            "doctype": "CNA Conversation",
            "status": "pending",
            "user": user_id,
            "session": session_id,
            "workflow": workflow_id,
            "id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
            "author": "Bot",
            "message_type": "UI Action",
            "message": json.dumps(
                {"action_type": "close_modal"}, indent=2, default=str
            ),
        }
    )
    conversation.insert(ignore_permissions=True)


# This function processes the form submission after an approval/rejection action
def process_after_form_submission(
    approval_log,
    status,
    user_id,
    new_session_id=None,
    doc_action_name=None,
    todo_status=None,
    send_back_seleted_approver=None,
):
    """Handles the document updates, tracker updates, and email notifications after approval/rejection."""
    approval_log.status = status
    approval_log.approval_time = now()
    approval_log.user = user_id

    # 🔽 Update target document if configured
    try:
        target_doc = frappe.get_doc(approval_log.parenttype, approval_log.parent)
        doc = frappe.get_doc(target_doc.doc_type, target_doc.doc_name)

        if status == "Approved":
            if approval_log.update_field and approval_log.update_value:
                doc.set(approval_log.update_field, approval_log.update_value)
        elif status == "Rejected":
            if (
                    approval_log.field_to_update_on_rejection
                    and approval_log.new_value_on_rejection
            ):
                doc.set(
                    approval_log.field_to_update_on_rejection,
                    approval_log.new_value_on_rejection,
                )

        doc.save(ignore_permissions=True)
        if doc_action_name:
            todo = frappe.get_doc("ToDo", doc_action_name)
            todo.status = todo_status
            todo.custom_selected_doctype_action = status
            todo.save(ignore_permissions=True)
        if send_back_seleted_approver:
            approval_log.send_back_user = send_back_seleted_approver
    except Exception as e:
        frappe.log_error(
            frappe.get_traceback(), "Failed to update document field from approval log"
        )
        frappe.throw(f"Failed to update target document: {str(e)}")

    approval_log.save()
    approval_log.reload()

    tracker = frappe.get_doc("Approval Tracker", approval_log.parent)

    # 📩 Post-action email
    target_type = (
        approval_log.approval_email_recipient
        if approval_log.status == "Approved"
        else approval_log.rejection_email_recipient
    )
    recipients = get_post_action_recipients(tracker, approval_log, target_type)
    if recipients:
        send_custom_post_action_email(
            tracker, approval_log, recipients, approval_log.status.lower()
        )

    # Terminal outcomes
    if status == "Rejected":
        tracker.status = "Rejected"
        tracker.save()
        return

    if status == "Send Back":
        tracker.status = "Send Back"
        tracker.save()
        return
    # Sequential approval
    if tracker.approval_mode == "Sequential":
        tracker.current_approval_step += 1
        if tracker.current_approval_step < len(tracker.approval_stages):
            tracker.save()
            process_next_approval_stage(tracker)
        else:
            tracker.status = "Approved"
            tracker.save()
            task = update_task_status(tracker.task, task_states.COMPLETED)
            next_action_nodes = get_next_action_nodes(task.target_node_id)
            for next_node in next_action_nodes:
                create_new_task(task.workflow, next_node.id, tracker.task, task.name)
            queue_execute_pending_tasks()

    # Parallel approval
    elif tracker.approval_mode == "Parallel":
        if all(log.status == "Approved" for log in tracker.approval_logs):
            tracker.status = "Approved"
            tracker.save()
            task = update_task_status(tracker.task, task_states.COMPLETED)
            next_action_nodes = get_next_action_nodes(task.target_node_id)
            for next_node in next_action_nodes:
                create_new_task(task.workflow, next_node.id, tracker.task, task.name)
            queue_execute_pending_tasks()


# This function handles the API action for bulk processing of ToDo items
@frappe.whitelist()
def action_api_handler(todo_ids, selected_action, variables=None):
    user_id = frappe.session.user
    set_funnel_user()
    for todo_id in todo_ids:
        if todo_id:
            todo = frappe.get_doc("ToDo", todo_id)
            action_select_handler(todo, selected_action, user_id, variables)

    return {"status": "success", "message": "Bulk action processed successfully."}


# This function processes the next approval stage in the approval tracker
def process_next_approval_stage(tracker):
    while tracker.current_approval_step < len(tracker.approval_stages):
        next_stage = tracker.approval_stages[tracker.current_approval_step]
        metrix_doc = frappe.get_doc("Approval Policy Matrix", tracker.approval_matrix)
        approver_type = next_stage.get("approver_type")
        update_field = next_stage.get("update_field")
        update_value = next_stage.get("update_value")
        email_template = next_stage.get("email_template")
        approval_email_recipient = next_stage.get("approval_email_recipient")
        field_to_update_on_rejection = next_stage.get("field_to_update_on_rejection")
        new_value_on_rejection = next_stage.get("new_value_on_rejection")
        rejection_email_template = next_stage.get("rejection_email_template")
        rejection_email_recipient = next_stage.get("rejection_email_recipient")
        form_for_approval = next_stage.get("form_for_approval")
        form_for_rejection = next_stage.get("form_for_rejection")
        approval_timeline_in_days = next_stage.get("approval_timeline_in_days")
        before_approval_email_template = next_stage.get(
            "before_approval_email_template"
        )
        update_data_on_approval_form_submit = next_stage.get("update_data_on_approval_form_submit")
        update_data_on_reject_form_submit = next_stage.get("update_data_on_reject_form_submit")
        cc_applicable = next_stage.get("cc_applicable")
        cc_based_on = next_stage.get("cc_based_on")
        cc_role = next_stage.get("cc_role")
        cc_employee_field = next_stage.get("cc_employee_field")
        allow_revoke = next_stage.get("allow_revoke")
        allow_sendback = next_stage.get("allow_sendback")
        sendback_email_template = next_stage.get("sendback_email_template")
        todo_category = metrix_doc.todo_category
        escalation_applicable = next_stage.get("escalation_applicable")
        auto_approvereject_applicable = next_stage.get("auto_approvereject_applicable")
        auto_action_type = next_stage.get("auto_action_type")
        custom_escalation_user = ""
        if next_stage.get("escalation_applicable") and next_stage.get("escalation_employee_field") and next_stage.get("escalation_based_on") == "Employee Field":
            custom_escalation_user = get_employee_or_user_field(tracker.doc_type, tracker.doc_name, next_stage.get("escalation_employee_field"))
        if next_stage.get("escalation_applicable") and next_stage.get("escalation_based_on") == "User":
            custom_escalation_user = next_stage.get("escalation_user")
        custom_doctype_actions_with_form = []
        if form_for_approval:
            custom_doctype_actions_with_form.append("Approve")
        if form_for_rejection:
            custom_doctype_actions_with_form.append("Reject")
        if allow_sendback:
            custom_doctype_actions_with_form.append("Send Back")

        def create_pending_log(**kwargs):
            log = tracker.append(
                "approval_logs",
                {
                    "status": "Pending",
                    "approver_type": approver_type,
                    "update_field": update_field,
                    "update_value": update_value,
                    "email_template": email_template,
                    "approval_email_recipient": approval_email_recipient,
                    "field_to_update_on_rejection": field_to_update_on_rejection,
                    "new_value_on_rejection": new_value_on_rejection,
                    "rejection_email_template": rejection_email_template,
                    "rejection_email_recipient": rejection_email_recipient,
                    "form_for_approval": form_for_approval,
                    "update_data_on_approval_form_submit": update_data_on_approval_form_submit,
                    "update_data_on_reject_form_submit": update_data_on_reject_form_submit,
                    "form_for_rejection": form_for_rejection,
                    "cc_applicable": cc_applicable,
                    "sendback_email_template": sendback_email_template,
                    "cc_based_on": cc_based_on,
                    "cc_role": cc_role,
                    "cc_employee_field": cc_employee_field,
                    "allow_revoke": allow_revoke,
                    "allow_sendback": allow_sendback,
                    "todo_category": todo_category,
                    "approval_timeline_in_days": approval_timeline_in_days,
                    "before_approval_email_template": before_approval_email_template,
                    "custom_doctype_actions_with_form": custom_doctype_actions_with_form,
                    "escalation_applicable": escalation_applicable,
                    "custom_escalation_user": custom_escalation_user,
                    "auto_approvereject_applicable": auto_approvereject_applicable,
                    "auto_action_type": auto_action_type,
                    **kwargs,
                },
            )
            log.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
            create_approval_todo(tracker.doctype, tracker.name, log)
            return log

        if approver_type == "Level":
            employee = (
                fetch_employee_from_doc(
                    tracker.doc_type, tracker.doc_name, metrix_doc.select_employee_field
                )
                if metrix_doc.parent_employee_link_field == "doc"
                else fetch_employee_from_linked_doc(
                    tracker.doc_type,
                    tracker.doc_name,
                    metrix_doc.select_employee_field,
                    metrix_doc.parent_employee_link_field,
                )
            )
            if employee:
                user = fetch_reporting_manager_user(employee, next_stage.get("level"))
                if user:
                    create_pending_log(user=user, role=next_stage.get("role"))
                    return
        elif approver_type == "Role":
            create_pending_log(role=next_stage.get("role"))
            return
        elif approver_type == "User":
            user = next_stage.get("user")
            if user:
                create_pending_log(user=user)
                return
        elif approver_type == "Field":
            field_name = next_stage.get("user_field")
            field_doc = frappe.get_doc(tracker.doc_type, tracker.doc_name)
            user = field_doc.get(field_name)
            if user:
                create_pending_log(user=user)
                return
        elif approver_type == "Employee Doc Field":
            u = next_stage.get("employee_doc_field")
            user = get_employee_or_user_field(tracker.doc_type, tracker.doc_name, u)
            if user and frappe.db.exists("User", user):
                create_pending_log(user=user)
                return
        # Handle skipped stage
        if tracker.allow_skip_missing_level:
            tracker.append(
                "approval_logs",
                {
                    "status": "Skipped",
                    "role": None,
                    "user": None,
                    "approver_type": approver_type,
                    "update_field": update_field,
                    "update_value": update_value,
                    "email_template": email_template,
                    "approval_email_recipient": approval_email_recipient,
                    "field_to_update_on_rejection": field_to_update_on_rejection,
                    "new_value_on_rejection": new_value_on_rejection,
                    "rejection_email_template": rejection_email_template,
                    "rejection_email_recipient": rejection_email_recipient,
                    "cc_applicable": cc_applicable,
                    "cc_based_on": cc_based_on,
                    "sendback_email_template": sendback_email_template,
                    "cc_role": cc_role,
                    "cc_employee_field": cc_employee_field,
                    "allow_revoke": allow_revoke,
                    "allow_sendback": allow_sendback,
                    "form_for_approval": form_for_approval,
                    "update_data_on_approval_form_submit": update_data_on_approval_form_submit,
                    "update_data_on_reject_form_submit": update_data_on_reject_form_submit,
                    "form_for_rejection": form_for_rejection,
                    "todo_category": todo_category,
                },
            ).insert(ignore_permissions=True)
            tracker.current_approval_step += 1
            tracker.save()
            continue
        else:
            break

    # End of approval stages
    if tracker.current_approval_step >= len(tracker.approval_stages):
        tracker.status = "Approved"
        tracker.save()
        task = update_task_status(tracker.task, task_states.COMPLETED)
        next_action_nodes = get_next_action_nodes(task.target_node_id)
        for next_node in next_action_nodes:
            create_new_task(task.workflow, next_node.id, tracker.task, task.name)
        queue_execute_pending_tasks()


# This function retrieves the recipients for post-action emails based on the approval log and tracker
def get_post_action_recipients(tracker, approval_log, target_type):
    recipients = []

    if target_type == "Owner":
        doc = frappe.get_doc(tracker.doc_type, tracker.doc_name)
        user_id = get_user_from_doc(tracker.doc_type, tracker.doc_name)
        if user_id and user_id != "Administrator":
            recipients.append(user_id)

    elif target_type == "Previous Approver":
        # find the last approval log before current that has status Approved
        prev_logs = [
            log
            for log in tracker.approval_logs
            if log.name != approval_log.name and log.status == "Approved"
        ]
        if prev_logs:
            prev_user = prev_logs[-1].user
            if prev_user and prev_user != "Administrator":
                recipients.append(prev_user)

    elif target_type == "Current Approver":
        if approval_log.user and approval_log.user != "Administrator":
            recipients.append(approval_log.user)

    elif target_type == "Next Approver":
        # Find current stage index based on approval_log
        current_stage_idx = None

        approval_log_index = approval_log.idx
        if len(tracker.approval_stages) > approval_log_index:
            next_stage = tracker.approval_stages[approval_log_index]
            if next_stage:
                approver_type = next_stage.get("approver_type")
                if approver_type == "User":
                    user = next_stage.get("user")
                    if user and user != "Administrator":
                        recipients.append(user)

                elif approver_type == "Role":
                    role_users = frappe.get_all(
                        "Has Role",
                        filters={"role": next_stage.get("role")},
                        fields=["parent"],
                    )
                    recipients.extend(
                        [r.parent for r in role_users if r.parent != "Administrator"]
                    )

                elif approver_type == "Field":
                    doc = frappe.get_doc(tracker.doc_type, tracker.doc_name)
                    field_user = doc.get(next_stage.get("user_field"))
                    if field_user and field_user != "Administrator":
                        recipients.append(field_user)

                elif approver_type == "Employee Doc Field":
                    u = next_stage.get("employee_doc_field")
                    user = get_employee_or_user_field(
                        tracker.doc_type, tracker.doc_name, u
                    )
                    if user and user != "Administrator":
                        recipients.append(user)

                elif approver_type == "Level":
                    metrix_doc = frappe.get_doc(
                        "Approval Policy Matrix", tracker.approval_matrix
                    )
                    employee = (
                        fetch_employee_from_doc(
                            tracker.doc_type,
                            tracker.doc_name,
                            metrix_doc.select_employee_field,
                        )
                        if metrix_doc.parent_employee_link_field == "doc"
                        else fetch_employee_from_linked_doc(
                            tracker.doc_type,
                            tracker.doc_name,
                            metrix_doc.select_employee_field,
                            metrix_doc.parent_employee_link_field,
                        )
                    )
                    if employee:
                        user = fetch_reporting_manager_user(
                            employee, next_stage.get("level")
                        )
                        if user and user != "Administrator":
                            recipients.append(user)

    return list(set(recipients))


EMPLOYEE_FIELDNAME_GUESSES = ("employee", "employee_id", "emp", "emp_id")


def get_employee_from_doc(doctype, docname):
    if not doctype or not docname:
        return None
    meta = frappe.get_meta(doctype)
    employee_field = next(
        (
            df.fieldname
            for df in meta.fields
            if df.fieldname in EMPLOYEE_FIELDNAME_GUESSES
               or (
                       df.fieldtype == "Link" and df.options in ("Employee", "Employee Master")
               )
        ),
        None,
    )
    if not employee_field:
        return None
    return frappe.db.get_value(doctype, docname, employee_field)


# This function sends a custom post-action email based on the approval log and tracker
def is_valid_email(email):
    """Return True only if the email is a valid address."""
    try:
        validate_email_address(email, throw=True)
        return True
    except Exception:
        return False


def send_custom_post_action_email(tracker, approval_log, recipients, action_status):
    # --- Normalize and validate recipients ---
    recipients = list({r for r in (recipients or []) if r and r != "Administrator"})
    recipients = [r for r in recipients if is_valid_email(r)]

    if not recipients:
        return

    # --- Pick correct email template ---
    template_name = None
    if action_status == "approved":
        template_name = approval_log.email_template
    elif action_status == "rejected":
        template_name = approval_log.rejection_email_template

    if not template_name:
        return

    # --- Build CC list safely ---
    cc_recipients = []
    if getattr(approval_log, "cc_applicable", False) and getattr(approval_log, "cc_based_on", None):
        if approval_log.cc_based_on == "Role" and getattr(approval_log, "cc_role", None):
            user_ids = frappe.get_all(
                "Has Role",
                filters={"role": approval_log.cc_role},
                pluck="parent"
            )

            if user_ids:
                role_users = frappe.get_all(
                    "User",
                    filters={"name": ["in", user_ids]},
                    fields=["email"]
                )
                cc_recipients.extend(
                    u.email for u in role_users if u.email and u.email != "Administrator"
                )

        elif approval_log.cc_based_on == "Employee Doc Field" and getattr(approval_log, "cc_employee_field", None):
            fieldname = approval_log.cc_employee_field
            user = get_employee_or_user_field(tracker.doc_type, tracker.doc_name, fieldname)
            if user and user != "Administrator":
                cc_recipients.append(user)

    cc_list = [
        c for c in set(cc_recipients)
        if c not in recipients and is_valid_email(c)
    ] or None

    # --- Render email content ---
    doc = frappe.get_doc(tracker.doc_type, tracker.doc_name)
    args = {"doc": doc, "tracker": tracker, "approval_log": approval_log}
    rendered = get_email_template(template_name, args) or {}

    subject = rendered.get("subject") or f"{tracker.doc_type} {tracker.doc_name}"
    message = rendered.get("message") or ""

    # -------------------------
    # ✅ CREATE COMMUNICATION
    # -------------------------
    communication = frappe.get_doc({
        "doctype": "Communication",
        "communication_type": "Automated Message",
        "communication_medium": "Email",
        "subject": subject,
        "content": message,
        "sent_or_received": "Sent",
        "reference_doctype": tracker.doc_type,
        "reference_name": tracker.doc_name
    }).insert(ignore_permissions=True)

    # -------------------------
    # ✅ SEND EMAIL LINKED TO COMMUNICATION
    # -------------------------
    frappe.sendmail(
        recipients=recipients,
        subject=subject,
        message=message,
        communication=communication.name,  # ✔ correct link
        cc=cc_list,
    )
# This function triggers a conversation for form submission
def trigger_form_conversation(formio_data, user_id, on_submit=None, form_prefill_data=None, enable_data_update_on_submit=None, workflow_id=None, session_id=None):
    session_id = session_id or str(uuid.uuid4())
    exist = frappe.db.exists("CNA Conversation", {"session": session_id, "user": user_id, "message_type": "Form", "status": "pending"})
    if exist:
        return session_id
    conversation = frappe.get_doc(
        {
            "doctype": "CNA Conversation",
            "status": "pending",
            "user": user_id,
            "session": session_id,
            "workflow": workflow_id,
            "id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
            "author": "Bot",
            "message_type": "Form",
            "show_in_chat_history": True,
            "message": json.dumps(
                {
                    "form": json.loads(formio_data) if formio_data else {},
                    "on_submit": on_submit,
                    "prefill_data": form_prefill_data,
                    "show_in_chat_history": True,
                    "enable_data_update_on_submit": enable_data_update_on_submit
                },
                indent=2,
                default=str,
            ),
        }
    )
    conversation.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    return session_id


@frappe.whitelist()
def revoke_event(doctype, docname, data):
    try:
        if not doctype or not docname:
            return {"ok": False, "error": "missing_doctype_or_docname"}

        # Parse incoming data (string or dict)
        if isinstance(data, str):
            try:
                data = frappe.parse_json(data)
            except Exception:
                data = json.loads(data)
        if not isinstance(data, dict):
            return {"ok": False, "error": "invalid_data_payload"}

        # Resolve ToDo reference
        todo_name = data.get("name") or data.get("todo") or data.get("todo_name")
        if not todo_name:
            return {"ok": False, "error": "missing_todo_reference"}

        if not frappe.db.exists("ToDo", todo_name):
            return {"ok": False, "error": "todo_not_found", "todo": todo_name}

        # Cancel the ToDo
        todo_doc = frappe.get_doc("ToDo", todo_name)
        todo_doc.db_set("status", "Cancelled", update_modified=False)

        # Update Approval Log Entry (Rejected)
        log_name = frappe.db.get_value(
            "Approval Log Entry",
            {"todo_reference": todo_doc.name},
            "name",
        )
        if not log_name:
            return {
                "ok": False,
                "error": "approval_log_not_found",
                "todo": todo_doc.name,
            }

        frappe.db.set_value(
            "Approval Log Entry", log_name, "status", "Rejected", update_modified=False
        )

        # Update Approval Tracker (Revoked)
        tracker_name = frappe.db.get_value("Approval Log Entry", log_name, "parent")
        if not tracker_name or not frappe.db.exists("Approval Tracker", tracker_name):
            return {"ok": False, "error": "tracker_not_found", "log": log_name}

        frappe.db.set_value(
            "Approval Tracker", tracker_name, "status", "Revoked", update_modified=False
        )

        # --- Helpers ---------------------------------------------------------
        def _has_field(meta, fieldname: str) -> bool:
            try:
                if hasattr(meta, "has_field"):
                    return bool(meta.has_field(fieldname))
            except Exception:
                pass
            try:
                return bool(meta.get_field(fieldname))
            except Exception:
                return False

        def _set_cancel_fields(meta, dt: str, dn: str):
            """Set status/custom_status to 'Cancelled' if fields exist (no modified bump)."""
            updates = {}
            for f in ("status", "custom_status"):
                if _has_field(meta, f):
                    updates[f] = "Cancelled"
            if updates:
                frappe.db.set_value(dt, dn, updates, update_modified=False)

        # --- Target document action -----------------------------------------
        if not frappe.db.exists(doctype, docname):
            target_action = "not_found"
        else:
            target_meta = frappe.get_meta(doctype)
            target_doc = frappe.get_doc(doctype, docname)
            target_action = "noop"

            is_submittable = int(getattr(target_meta, "is_submittable", 0)) == 1

            # Case 1: Submitted docs -> cancel
            if is_submittable and target_doc.docstatus == 1:
                target_doc.flags.ignore_permissions = True
                target_doc.cancel()
                _set_cancel_fields(target_meta, doctype, docname)
                target_action = "cancelled"

            # Case 2: Non-submittable -> set fields then delete
            elif not is_submittable:
                _set_cancel_fields(target_meta, doctype, docname)
                frappe.delete_doc(doctype, docname, ignore_permissions=True, force=1)
                target_action = "deleted"

            else:
                # Submittable but already cancelled
                if target_doc.docstatus == 2:
                    _set_cancel_fields(target_meta, doctype, docname)
                    target_action = "already_cancelled"

                # Submittable draft -> force cancel by setting docstatus=2
                elif target_doc.docstatus == 0:
                    frappe.db.set_value(
                        doctype, docname, "docstatus", 2, update_modified=False
                    )
                    _set_cancel_fields(target_meta, doctype, docname)
                    frappe.db.commit()  # ensure consistency if called in a larger flow
                    target_doc.reload()
                    target_action = "force_cancelled_draft"

        return {
            "ok": True,
            "message": "revoked",
            "todo": todo_doc.name,
            "log": log_name,
            "tracker": tracker_name,
            "target_doctype": doctype,
            "target_docname": docname,
            "target_action": target_action,
        }

    except Exception as e:
        frappe.log_error(frappe.get_traceback(), "revoke_event failed")
        return {"ok": False, "error": "unexpected_error", "detail": str(e)}


def get_permitted_revoke_docs(doctype=None, docname=None):
    filters = {
        "custom_approval_type": ("in", ["Multi Actions", "Approval Matrix"]),
        "status": "Open",
        "custom_allow_revoke": 1,
    }
    if doctype:
        filters["reference_type"] = doctype
    if docname:
        filters["reference_name"] = docname
    return frappe.get_all("ToDo", filters=filters, fields=["*"], order_by="creation")


def is_for_current_user(todo_action):
    user_id = frappe.session.user
    if todo_action.reference_type and todo_action.reference_name:
        employee_user_id = get_user_from_doc(
            todo_action.reference_type, todo_action.reference_name
        )
        if user_id == employee_user_id:
            return True


# This function returns the first action that is permitted to be revoked
@frappe.whitelist()
def get_permitted_revoke_actions(doctype, docname):
    action_list = get_permitted_revoke_docs(doctype, docname)
    if not len(action_list):
        return
    for action in action_list:
        if is_for_current_user(action):
            return action


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_doctype_list():
    action_list = get_permitted_revoke_docs()
    actions_visible_doctypes = [a.reference_type for a in action_list]
    return list(set(actions_visible_doctypes))


def get_resubmit_list():
    action_list = frappe.get_all(
        "Approval Tracker",
        filters={"status": "Send Back"},
        fields=["*"],
        order_by="creation",
    )
    return action_list


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_permitted_resubmit_approval_list():
    action_list = get_resubmit_list()
    action_visible_sendback_doctypes = [a.doc_type for a in action_list]
    return list(set(action_visible_sendback_doctypes))


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_permitted_resubmit_approval(doctype, docname):
    current_session_user = frappe.session.user
    tracker = frappe.db.get_value(
        "Approval Tracker",
        {"doc_type": doctype, "doc_name": docname, "status": "Send Back"},
        "name",
    )
    if not tracker:
        return
    tracker_doc = frappe.get_doc("Approval Tracker", tracker)
    get_sendback_log = latest_sendback_log(tracker_doc)
    if not get_sendback_log:
        return
    if get_sendback_log.send_back_user == current_session_user:
        return frappe.get_all("Approval Tracker", filters={"status": "Send Back", "doc_type": doctype, "doc_name": docname}, fields=["*"], order_by="creation")


def latest_sendback_log(tracker_doc):
    rows = tracker_doc.get("approval_logs") or []
    sendbacks = [r for r in rows if (r.get("status") or "").lower() == "send back"]
    if not sendbacks:
        return None
    sendbacks.sort(
        key=lambda r: (r.get("creation") or "", r.get("idx") or 0), reverse=True
    )
    return sendbacks[0]


def duplicate_todo_open(old_todo_name, tracker_doc):
    if old_todo_name and frappe.db.exists("ToDo", old_todo_name):
        old = frappe.get_doc("ToDo", old_todo_name)
        new_todo = frappe.copy_doc(old)
        new_todo.name = None
        new_todo.docstatus = 0
        new_todo.status = "Open"
        new_todo.custom_selected_doctype_action = None
        new_todo.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
        return new_todo.name
    todo = frappe.new_doc("ToDo")
    todo.status = "Open"
    todo.priority = "Medium"
    todo.description = frappe._("Approval required for {0} {1}").format(
        tracker_doc.doc_type, tracker_doc.doc_name
    )
    todo.reference_type = tracker_doc.doc_type or "Approval Tracker"
    todo.reference_name = tracker_doc.doc_name or tracker_doc.name
    if tracker_doc.get("todo_category"):
        todo.todo_category = tracker_doc.get("todo_category")
    todo.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    return todo.name


@frappe.whitelist()
def resubmit_approval_event(doctype, docname, data):
    payload = frappe.parse_json(data) if isinstance(data, str) else (data or [])
    if not isinstance(payload, list):
        frappe.throw(frappe._("Payload must be a list"))
    results, errors = [], []
    for row in payload:
        try:
            tracker_name = row.get("name") or frappe.db.get_value(
                "Approval Tracker",
                {
                    "doc_type": row.get("doc_type") or doctype,
                    "doc_name": row.get("doc_name") or docname,
                    "status": "Send Back",
                },
                "name",
            )
            if not tracker_name:
                errors.append({"input": row, "error": "No 'Send Back' tracker found"})
                continue
            tracker = frappe.get_doc("Approval Tracker", tracker_name)
            log = latest_sendback_log(tracker)
            if not log:
                errors.append(
                    {"tracker": tracker.name, "error": "No 'Send Back' log found"}
                )
                continue
            new_todo = duplicate_todo_open(log.get("todo_reference"), tracker)
            log.status = "Pending"
            log.approval_time = None
            log.todo_reference = new_todo
            tracker.status = "Pending"
            tracker.save(ignore_permissions=True)
            results.append(
                {
                    "tracker": tracker.name,
                    "log_name": log.get("name"),
                    "new_todo": new_todo,
                    "status": tracker.status,
                }
            )
        except Exception as e:
            errors.append({"input": row, "error": str(e)})
    frappe.db.commit()
    return {"ok": bool(results), "updated": results, "errors": errors}


def get_previous_approver(approval_log):
    tracker = frappe.get_doc("Approval Tracker", approval_log.parent)
    owner_id = get_user_from_doc(tracker.doc_type, tracker.doc_name)

    rows = tracker.get("approval_logs") or []
    approved_user_ids = [
        r.get("user") for r in rows if (r.get("status") or "").lower() == "approved"
    ]

    # de-dup while preserving order (owner first)
    user_ids = list(dict.fromkeys([owner_id] + approved_user_ids))

    # map user_id -> label
    values = []
    for uid in user_ids:
        full_name = frappe.get_value("User", uid, "full_name") or uid
        values.append({"label": full_name, "value": uid})

    return values

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/awaiting_actions/on_email_reply.py
import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import task_states
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.funnel_task import queue_execute_pending_tasks
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import update_task_status, create_new_task
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_action_nodes import get_next_action_nodes


def on_email_reply(action_node, task, email_data, variables):
    # save answer in workflow variables
    node_data = frappe.parse_json(action_node.data)
    workflow_name = task.workflow

    variables["last_email_doc_name"] = email_data.get("name")
    variables["last_email_data"] = email_data

    variable_path_for_output = node_data.get("variable_path_for_output")
    if variable_path_for_output:
        variables[variable_path_for_output] = email_data

    # update task status
    update_task_status(task, task_states.COMPLETED, email_data)

    # create new task
    next_action_nodes = get_next_action_nodes(action_node.id)
    for next_node in next_action_nodes:
        create_new_task(workflow_name, next_node.id, action_node.id, task.name)
    queue_execute_pending_tasks()

import frappe

    whatsapp_answer_docs[answer_key] = whatsapp_message.name
    variables["whatsapp_answer_docs"] = whatsapp_answer_docs
    variables["last_whatsapp_message"] = whatsapp_message.message_body

    variable_path_for_output = node_data.get("variable_path_for_output")
    if variable_path_for_output:
        variables[variable_path_for_output] = whatsapp_message.message_body

    # update task status
    update_task_status(task, task_states.COMPLETED, whatsapp_message.message_body)

    # create new task
    next_action_nodes = get_next_action_nodes(action_node.id)
    for next_node in next_action_nodes:
        create_new_task(workflow_name, next_node.id, action_node.id, task.name)
    queue_execute_pending_tasks()

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/awaiting_actions/personal_whatsapp_ai_qa_agent.py
import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import task_states
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.actions.personal_whatsapp_ai_qa_agent import (
    get_contact_id,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.chat_agent_apis import interact_with_ai
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.funnel_task import queue_execute_pending_tasks, set_funnel_user
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import (
    update_task_status,
    create_new_task,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.format_with_variables import (
    format_with_variables,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_action_nodes import (
    get_next_action_nodes,
)
from nextai.personal_whatsapp_integration.doctype.cn_pw_whatsapp_message.cn_pw_whatsapp_message import (
    send_personal_whatsapp_message,
)


def personal_whatsapp_ai_qa_agent(action_node, variables, task, whatsapp_message):
    user_id = frappe.session.user
    set_funnel_user()
    node_data = frappe.parse_json(action_node.data)
    assistant_id = node_data["assistant_id"]
    workflow_name = task.workflow
    variables["last_message"] = whatsapp_message.body
    ai_response = interact_with_ai(
        assistant_id, variables["openai_thread_id"], whatsapp_message.body
    )
    variables["last_ai_response"] = ai_response
    response_type = ai_response.get("type")
    if response_type == "finish":
        variable_name = node_data.get("variable")
        function_args = ai_response.get("args")
        variables[variable_name] = function_args

        update_task_status(task, task_states.COMPLETED, whatsapp_message.body)

        # create new task
        next_action_nodes = get_next_action_nodes(action_node.id)
        for next_node in next_action_nodes:
            create_new_task(workflow_name, next_node.id, action_node.id, task.name)
        if len(next_action_nodes):
            queue_execute_pending_tasks()
    else:
        message_text = ai_response.get("content")
        if message_text:
            session_id = node_data["session_id"]
            session_id = format_with_variables(session_id, variables)
            contact_id = get_contact_id(node_data, variables)
            send_personal_whatsapp_message(contact_id, session_id, message_text)

    frappe.session.user = user_id

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import task_states
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.actions.whatsapp_question_ai_qa_agent import interact_with_ai
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.funnel_task import queue_execute_pending_tasks
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import update_task_status, create_new_task
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_action_nodes import get_next_action_nodes
from nextai.whatsapp_business_api_integration.doctype.whatsapp_message.whatsapp_message import send_message


def whatsapp_question_ai_qa_agent(action_node, task, whatsapp_message, variables):
    node_data = frappe.parse_json(action_node.data)
    assistant_id = node_data["assistant_id"]
    workflow_name = task.workflow
    message_body = whatsapp_message.message_body
    variables["last_whatsapp_message"] = message_body
    ai_response = interact_with_ai(assistant_id, variables["openai_thread_id"], message_body)
    variables["last_ai_response"] = ai_response
    response_type = ai_response.get("type")
    if response_type == 'finish':
        variable_name = node_data.get("variable")
        function_args = ai_response.get("args")
        variables[variable_name] = function_args

        update_task_status(task, task_states.COMPLETED, whatsapp_message.message_body)

        # create new task
        next_action_nodes = get_next_action_nodes(action_node.id)
        for next_node in next_action_nodes:
