        queue_execute_pending_tasks()
    else:
        message_text = ai_response.get("content")
        if message_text:
            send_message(variables.get("contact_name"), message_text)

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/awaiting_actions/whatsapp_question_audio.py
import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import task_states
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.funnel_task import queue_execute_pending_tasks
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import create_new_task, update_task_status
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_action_nodes import get_next_action_nodes
from nextai.whatsapp_business_api_integration.doctype.whatsapp_message.whatsapp_message import send_message


def has_audio(whatsapp_doc):
    if not whatsapp_doc.media_id:
        return False
    if not whatsapp_doc.media_file:
        whatsapp_doc.download_media()
        whatsapp_doc.reload()
    if not whatsapp_doc.media_file:
        raise Exception("Cannot download media")
    if not whatsapp_doc.media_mime_type:
        return False
    if not whatsapp_doc.media_mime_type.startswith("audio/"):
        return False
    return True


def whatsapp_question_audio(action_node, task, whatsapp_message, variables):
    node_data = frappe.parse_json(action_node.data)
    workflow_name = task.workflow
    whatsapp_doc = frappe.get_doc("WhatsApp Message", whatsapp_message.name)

    if has_audio(whatsapp_doc):
        connected_nodes = get_next_action_nodes(action_node.id)
        if len(connected_nodes):
            # save answer in workflow variables
            answer_key = node_data.answer_key
            whatsapp_answers = variables.get("whatsapp_answers") or {}
            whatsapp_answers[answer_key] = whatsapp_doc.media_file
            variables["whatsapp_answers"] = whatsapp_answers
            whatsapp_answer_docs = variables.get("whatsapp_answer_docs") or {}
            whatsapp_answer_docs[answer_key] = whatsapp_message.name
            variables["whatsapp_answer_docs"] = whatsapp_answer_docs
            variables["last_whatsapp_message"] = whatsapp_doc.media_file

            variable_path_for_output = node_data.get("variable_path_for_output")
            if variable_path_for_output:
                variables[variable_path_for_output] = whatsapp_doc.media_file

            update_task_status(task, task_states.COMPLETED, whatsapp_doc.media_file)
            for next_node in connected_nodes:
                create_new_task(workflow_name, next_node.id, action_node.id, task.name)
            queue_execute_pending_tasks()
    else:
        reply_if_wrong_option_received = node_data.get("reply_if_wrong_option_received")
        if reply_if_wrong_option_received:
            send_message(variables.get("contact_name"), reply_if_wrong_option_received)
            update_task_status(task, task_states.AWAITING_TRIGGER, {
                "message": "Invalid file received. Waiting for a valid option."
            })

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/awaiting_actions/whatsapp_question_boolean.py
import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import task_states
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.funnel_task import queue_execute_pending_tasks
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import create_new_task, update_task_status
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_action_nodes import get_next_action_nodes_with_source_handle_id
from nextai.whatsapp_business_api_integration.doctype.whatsapp_message.whatsapp_message import send_message


def whatsapp_question_boolean(action_node, task, whatsapp_message, variables):
    node_data = frappe.parse_json(action_node.data)
    workflow_name = task.workflow

    yes_field = node_data.get("yes_field")
    no_field = node_data.get("no_field")

    # check if valid answer
    message_body = whatsapp_message.message_body
    available_options = [yes_field, no_field]
    if message_body in available_options:
        connected_nodes = get_next_action_nodes_with_source_handle_id(
            action_node.id, message_body)
        if len(connected_nodes):
            # save answer in workflow variables
            answer_key = node_data.answer_key
            whatsapp_answers = variables.get("whatsapp_answers") or {}
            whatsapp_answers[answer_key] = whatsapp_message.message_body
            variables["whatsapp_answers"] = whatsapp_answers
            whatsapp_answer_docs = variables.get("whatsapp_answer_docs") or {}
            whatsapp_answer_docs[answer_key] = whatsapp_message.name
            variables["whatsapp_answer_docs"] = whatsapp_answer_docs
            variables["last_whatsapp_message"] = whatsapp_message.message_body

            variable_path_for_output = node_data.get(
                "variable_path_for_output")
            if variable_path_for_output:
                variables[variable_path_for_output] = whatsapp_message.message_body

            for next_node in connected_nodes:
                create_new_task(workflow_name, next_node.id,
                                action_node.id, task.name)
            queue_execute_pending_tasks()
        update_task_status(task, task_states.COMPLETED,
                           whatsapp_message.message_body)
    else:
        reply_if_wrong_option_received = node_data.get(
            "reply_if_wrong_option_received")
        if reply_if_wrong_option_received:
            send_message(variables.get("contact_name"),
                         reply_if_wrong_option_received)
            update_task_status(task, task_states.AWAITING_TRIGGER, {
                "message": "Invalid option received. waiting for valid option."
            })

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/awaiting_actions/whatsapp_question_email.py
import re

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import task_states
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.funnel_task import queue_execute_pending_tasks
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import create_new_task, update_task_status
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_action_nodes import get_next_action_nodes
from nextai.whatsapp_business_api_integration.doctype.whatsapp_message.whatsapp_message import send_message


def is_valid_email(email):
    email_pattern = r'^[\w\.-]+@[\w\.-]+\.\w+$'
    return re.match(email_pattern, email) is not None


def whatsapp_question_email(action_node, task, whatsapp_message, variables):
    node_data = frappe.parse_json(action_node.data)
    workflow_name = task.workflow
    # check if valid answer
    message_body = whatsapp_message.message_body
    if is_valid_email(message_body):
        connected_nodes = get_next_action_nodes(action_node.id)
        if len(connected_nodes):
            # save answer in workflow variables
            answer_key = node_data.answer_key
            whatsapp_answers = variables.get("whatsapp_answers") or {}
            whatsapp_answers[answer_key] = whatsapp_message.message_body
            variables["whatsapp_answers"] = whatsapp_answers
            whatsapp_answer_docs = variables.get("whatsapp_answer_docs") or {}
            whatsapp_answer_docs[answer_key] = whatsapp_message.name
            variables["whatsapp_answer_docs"] = whatsapp_answer_docs
            variables["last_whatsapp_message"] = whatsapp_message.message_body

            variable_path_for_output = node_data.get("variable_path_for_output")
            if variable_path_for_output:
                variables[variable_path_for_output] = whatsapp_message.message_body

            update_task_status(task, task_states.COMPLETED, whatsapp_message.message_body)
            for next_node in connected_nodes:
                create_new_task(workflow_name, next_node.id, action_node.id, task.name)
            queue_execute_pending_tasks()
    else:
        reply_if_wrong_option_received = node_data.get("reply_if_wrong_option_received")
        if reply_if_wrong_option_received:
            send_message(variables.get("contact_name"), reply_if_wrong_option_received)
            update_task_status(task, task_states.AWAITING_TRIGGER, {
                "message": "Invalid email received. waiting for valid option."
            })

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/awaiting_actions/whatsapp_question_file.py
import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import task_states
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.funnel_task import queue_execute_pending_tasks
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import create_new_task, update_task_status
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_action_nodes import get_next_action_nodes
from nextai.whatsapp_business_api_integration.doctype.whatsapp_message.whatsapp_message import send_message


def has_media(whatsapp_doc):
    if not whatsapp_doc.media_id:
        return False
    if not whatsapp_doc.media_file:
        whatsapp_doc.download_media()
        whatsapp_doc.reload()
    if not whatsapp_doc.media_file:
        raise Exception("Cannot download media")
    return True


def whatsapp_question_file(action_node, task, whatsapp_message, variables):
    node_data = frappe.parse_json(action_node.data)
    workflow_name = task.workflow
    whatsapp_doc = frappe.get_doc("WhatsApp Message", whatsapp_message.name)

    if has_media(whatsapp_doc):
        connected_nodes = get_next_action_nodes(action_node.id)
        if len(connected_nodes):
            # save answer in workflow variables
            answer_key = node_data.answer_key
            whatsapp_answers = variables.get("whatsapp_answers") or {}
            whatsapp_answers[answer_key] = whatsapp_doc.media_file
            variables["whatsapp_answers"] = whatsapp_answers
            whatsapp_answer_docs = variables.get("whatsapp_answer_docs") or {}
            whatsapp_answer_docs[answer_key] = whatsapp_message.name
            variables["whatsapp_answer_docs"] = whatsapp_answer_docs
            variables["last_whatsapp_message"] = whatsapp_doc.media_file

            variable_path_for_output = node_data.get("variable_path_for_output")
            if variable_path_for_output:
                variables[variable_path_for_output] = whatsapp_doc.media_file

            update_task_status(task, task_states.COMPLETED, whatsapp_doc.media_file)
            for next_node in connected_nodes:
                create_new_task(workflow_name, next_node.id, action_node.id, task.name)
            queue_execute_pending_tasks()
    else:
        reply_if_wrong_option_received = node_data.get("reply_if_wrong_option_received")
        if reply_if_wrong_option_received:
            send_message(variables.get("contact_name"), reply_if_wrong_option_received)
            update_task_status(task, task_states.AWAITING_TRIGGER, {
                "message": "Invalid file received. Waiting for a valid option."
            })

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/awaiting_actions/whatsapp_question_image.py
import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import task_states
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.funnel_task import queue_execute_pending_tasks
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import create_new_task, update_task_status
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_action_nodes import get_next_action_nodes
from nextai.whatsapp_business_api_integration.doctype.whatsapp_message.whatsapp_message import send_message


def has_image(whatsapp_doc):
    if not whatsapp_doc.media_id:
        return False
    if not whatsapp_doc.media_file:
        whatsapp_doc.download_media()
        whatsapp_doc.reload()
    if not whatsapp_doc.media_file:
        raise Exception("Cannot download media")
    if not whatsapp_doc.media_mime_type:
        return False
    if not whatsapp_doc.media_mime_type.startswith("image/"):
        return False
    return True


def whatsapp_question_image(action_node, task, whatsapp_message, variables):
    node_data = frappe.parse_json(action_node.data)
    workflow_name = task.workflow
    whatsapp_doc = frappe.get_doc("WhatsApp Message", whatsapp_message.name)

    if has_image(whatsapp_doc):
        connected_nodes = get_next_action_nodes(action_node.id)
        if len(connected_nodes):
            # save answer in workflow variables
            answer_key = node_data.answer_key
            whatsapp_answers = variables.get("whatsapp_answers") or {}
            whatsapp_answers[answer_key] = whatsapp_doc.media_file
            variables["whatsapp_answers"] = whatsapp_answers
            whatsapp_answer_docs = variables.get("whatsapp_answer_docs") or {}
            whatsapp_answer_docs[answer_key] = whatsapp_message.name
            variables["whatsapp_answer_docs"] = whatsapp_answer_docs
            variables["last_whatsapp_message"] = whatsapp_doc.media_file

            variable_path_for_output = node_data.get("variable_path_for_output")
            if variable_path_for_output:
                variables[variable_path_for_output] = whatsapp_doc.media_file

            update_task_status(task, task_states.COMPLETED, whatsapp_doc.media_file)
            for next_node in connected_nodes:
                create_new_task(workflow_name, next_node.id, action_node.id, task.name)
            queue_execute_pending_tasks()
    else:
        reply_if_wrong_option_received = node_data.get("reply_if_wrong_option_received")
        if reply_if_wrong_option_received:
            send_message(variables.get("contact_name"), reply_if_wrong_option_received)
            update_task_status(task, task_states.AWAITING_TRIGGER, {
                "message": "Invalid file received. Waiting for a valid option."
            })

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/awaiting_actions/whatsapp_question_mobile.py
import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import task_states
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.funnel_task import queue_execute_pending_tasks
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import create_new_task, update_task_status
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_action_nodes import get_next_action_nodes
from nextai.whatsapp_business_api_integration.doctype.whatsapp_message.whatsapp_message import send_message


def is_valid_phone_number(phone_number):
    if phone_number.startswith("+"):
        phone_number = phone_number[1:]
    if len(phone_number) != 10 and len(phone_number) != 11 and len(phone_number) != 12:
        return False
    return phone_number.isdigit()


def whatsapp_question_mobile(action_node, task, whatsapp_message, variables):
    node_data = frappe.parse_json(action_node.data)
    workflow_name = task.workflow
    # check if valid answer
    message_body = whatsapp_message.message_body
    if is_valid_phone_number(message_body):
        connected_nodes = get_next_action_nodes(action_node.id)
        if len(connected_nodes):
            # save answer in workflow variables
            answer_key = node_data.answer_key
            whatsapp_answers = variables.get("whatsapp_answers") or {}
            whatsapp_answers[answer_key] = whatsapp_message.message_body
            variables["whatsapp_answers"] = whatsapp_answers
            whatsapp_answer_docs = variables.get("whatsapp_answer_docs") or {}
            whatsapp_answer_docs[answer_key] = whatsapp_message.name
            variables["whatsapp_answer_docs"] = whatsapp_answer_docs
            variables["last_whatsapp_message"] = whatsapp_message.message_body

            variable_path_for_output = node_data.get("variable_path_for_output")
            if variable_path_for_output:
                variables[variable_path_for_output] = whatsapp_message.message_body

            update_task_status(task, task_states.COMPLETED, whatsapp_message.message_body)
            for next_node in connected_nodes:
                create_new_task(workflow_name, next_node.id, action_node.id, task.name)
            queue_execute_pending_tasks()
    else:
        reply_if_wrong_option_received = node_data.get("reply_if_wrong_option_received")
        if reply_if_wrong_option_received:
            send_message(variables.get("contact_name"), reply_if_wrong_option_received)
            update_task_status(task, task_states.AWAITING_TRIGGER, {
                "message": "Invalid phone received. waiting for valid option."
            })

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/awaiting_actions/whatsapp_question_single_choice.py
import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import task_states
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.actions.send_message_whatsapp import send_message_whatsapp
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.funnel_task import queue_execute_pending_tasks
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import create_new_task, update_task_status
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_action_nodes import get_next_action_nodes_with_source_handle_id
from nextai.whatsapp_business_api_integration.doctype.whatsapp_message.whatsapp_message import send_message


def whatsapp_question_single_choice(action_node, task, whatsapp_message, variables):
    node_data = frappe.parse_json(action_node.data)
    workflow_name = task.workflow
    # check if valid answer
    message_body = whatsapp_message.message_body
    available_options = [op.get("option") for op in node_data.get(node_data.get("reply_options_type"))]
    if message_body in available_options:
        connected_nodes = get_next_action_nodes_with_source_handle_id(action_node.id, message_body)
        if len(connected_nodes):
            # save answer in workflow variables
            answer_key = node_data.answer_key
            whatsapp_answers = variables.get("whatsapp_answers") or {}
            whatsapp_answers[answer_key] = whatsapp_message.message_body
            variables["whatsapp_answers"] = whatsapp_answers
            whatsapp_answer_docs = variables.get("whatsapp_answer_docs") or {}
            whatsapp_answer_docs[answer_key] = whatsapp_message.name
            variables["whatsapp_answer_docs"] = whatsapp_answer_docs
            variables["last_whatsapp_message"] = whatsapp_message.message_body

            variable_path_for_output = node_data.get("variable_path_for_output")
            if variable_path_for_output:
                variables[variable_path_for_output] = whatsapp_message.message_body

            for next_node in connected_nodes:
                create_new_task(workflow_name, next_node.id, action_node.id, task.name)
            queue_execute_pending_tasks()
        update_task_status(task, task_states.COMPLETED, whatsapp_message.message_body)
    else:
        reply_if_wrong_option_received = node_data.get("reply_if_wrong_option_received")
        if reply_if_wrong_option_received:
            send_message(variables.get("contact_name"), reply_if_wrong_option_received)
            send_message_whatsapp(action_node, variables)
            update_task_status(task, task_states.AWAITING_TRIGGER, {
                "message": "Invalid option received. waiting for valid option.",
                "reply": message_body
            })

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/chat_agent_apis.py
import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.set_funnel_user import set_funnel_user
from nextai.utils import send_authorized_core_request


def interact_with_ai(assistant_id, thread_id, user_input):
    core_method = 'chatnext_core.clients.ai_assistant_client.send_message'
    payload = {
        'assistant_id': assistant_id,
        'thread_id': thread_id,
        'user_input': user_input
    }
    response = send_authorized_core_request(payload, "POST", core_method)
    return response.json().get('message')


def create_thread():
    core_method = 'chatnext_core.clients.ai_assistant_client.create_thread'
    response = send_authorized_core_request({}, "GET", core_method)
    return response.json().get('message')


def frappe_type_to_openai_type(frappe_type):
    if frappe_type == 'Data' or frappe_type == 'JSON' or frappe_type == 'HTML':
        return 'string'
    if frappe_type == 'Check':
        return 'boolean'
    if frappe_type == 'Int' or frappe_type == 'Float':
        return 'number'
    if frappe_type == 'Select':
        return 'string'  # TODO: make this enum
    return None


def format_field(field):
    field_label = field.get('name').get('label')
    field_name = field.get('name').get('value')
    field_type = field.get('name').get('type')
    is_required = field.get('required')
    field_description = field.get('description')
    parameter_type = frappe_type_to_openai_type(field_type)
    if not parameter_type:
        raise Exception("Field", field_name, "has an unsupported type", field_type)
    return {
        'name': field_name,
        'type': parameter_type,
        'required': is_required,
        'description': field_description or field_label
    }


def format_custom_field(field):
    field_name = field.get('name')
    field_type = field.get('type')
    is_required = field.get('required')
    field_description = field.get('description')
    return {
        'name': field_name,
        'type': field_type,
        'required': is_required,
        'description': field_description or field_name
    }


def get_openai_api_key():
    return frappe.db.get_single_value('Chatnext Settings', 'openai_api_key')


@frappe.whitelist()
def create_chat_agent(**kwargs):
    set_funnel_user()
    api_key = get_openai_api_key()
    agent_name = kwargs.get('agent_name')
    is_custom = kwargs.get('is_custom')
    doctypefields = kwargs.get('doctypefields')
    fields = kwargs.get('fields')
    chat_bot_personality = kwargs.get('chat_bot_personality')
    other_instructions_for_the_bot = kwargs.get('other_instructions_for_the_bot')
    doc_parameters = [format_custom_field(field) for field in fields] if is_custom \
        else [format_field(field) for field in doctypefields]
    function_parameters = {}
    for parameter in doc_parameters:
        function_parameters[parameter.get('name')] = {
            'type': parameter.get('type'),
            'description': parameter.get('description')
        }
    required_params = [parameter.get('name') for parameter in doc_parameters if parameter.get('required')]
    parameter_instructions = ''
    if len(doc_parameters) > 0:
        parameter_instructions = '\n'.join(
            [f"""{parameter.get('name')} ({parameter.get('type')}) - {parameter.get('description')}""" for parameter in
             doc_parameters])

        parameter_instructions = f"""
Ask the user for the following information:
{parameter_instructions}
"""

    required_instructions = ''
    if len(required_params) > 0:
        required_instructions = f"""
User Must Provide: {', '.join(required_params)}
"""

    instructions = f"""{chat_bot_personality}
{parameter_instructions}{required_instructions}
{other_instructions_for_the_bot}"""
    tools = [{
        "type": "function",
        "function": {
            "name": "create_json_from_user_input",
            "description": "Create a JSON object from the users answers",
            "parameters": {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": function_parameters,
                "required": required_params
            }
        }
    }]

    core_method = 'chatnext_core.clients.ai_assistant_client.create_assistant?all_fields=1'
    response = send_authorized_core_request({
        'name': agent_name,
        'instructions': instructions,
        'tools': tools,
        'api_key': api_key or ""
    }, "POST", core_method)
    return response.json().get('message')


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_ai_assistant_list():
    set_funnel_user()
    api_key = get_openai_api_key()
    core_method = 'chatnext_core.clients.ai_assistant_client.get_list'
    if api_key:
        core_method += '?api_key=' + api_key
    response = send_authorized_core_request({}, "GET", core_method)
    return response.json().get('message')


@frappe.whitelist()
def delete_ai_assistant(**kwargs):
    assistant_id = kwargs.get('id')
    api_key = get_openai_api_key()
    set_funnel_user()
    core_method = 'chatnext_core.clients.ai_assistant_client.delete_assistant?assistant_id=' + assistant_id
    if api_key:
        core_method += '&api_key=' + api_key
    response = send_authorized_core_request({}, "DELETE", core_method)
    return response.json().get('message')

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/funnel_task.py
# Copyright (c) 2023, Hybrowlabs Technologies and contributors
# For license information, please see license.txt
import json
import time
import traceback
from datetime import datetime
from hashlib import blake2b

import frappe
from frappe.model.document import Document
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel.funnel_user import check, get_funnel_user_id
from . import task_states
from .utils.create_update_task import update_task_status, create_new_task
from .utils.format_with_variables import format_with_variables
from .utils.get_action_nodes import (
    get_action_node_from_id,
    get_next_action_nodes_with_source_handle_id,
    get_next_action_nodes,
    ON_ERROR_HANDLE_NAME,
)
from .utils.get_pending_tasks import get_all_pending_tasks
from .utils.logger import error
from .utils.update_workflow import update_workflow


class FunnelTask(Document):

    def execute(self):
        execute_task(self)

    def autoname(self):
        try:
            site_hash = blake2b(digest_size=8)
            site_hash.update(bytes(str(self.target_node_id), "utf-8"))
            if self.parent_task:
                hash_part = self.parent_task.split("_")[-1]
                if hash_part:
                    site_hash.update(bytes(str(hash_part), "utf-8"))
            task_hash = site_hash.hexdigest()
            task_name = str(self.workflow) + "_" + str(task_hash)
            self.name = task_name
        except:
            pass


def execute_custom_action_node(action_node, variables):
    node_doc = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Node", action_node.type)

    if not node_doc:
        raise Exception("No node found with id:" + action_node.type)

    if not node_doc.get("is_custom"):
        raise Exception("Node is not custom node:" + node_doc.name)

    python_function_dotted_path = node_doc.get("python_function_dotted_path")
    if not python_function_dotted_path:
        raise Exception("No python function defined for node:" + node_doc.name)

    try:
        return frappe.call(
            python_function_dotted_path, action_node=action_node, variables=variables
        )
    except Exception as e:
        raise Exception("Error executing custom node:" + str(e))


def execute_node(action_node, variables):
    funnel_node = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Node", action_node.type)

    if variables.get("contact_name") and funnel_node.is_chatbot_node:
        contact_name = variables.get("contact_name")
        contact_doc = frappe.get_doc("Contact", contact_name)
        if contact_doc:
            if contact_doc.chatbot_disabled:
                return {
                    "skip_task_creation": True,
                    "mark_as_cancelled": True,
                }

    if funnel_node.is_custom:
        return execute_custom_action_node(action_node, variables)
    action_node_name = action_node.type.lower()
    return frappe.call(
        f"nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.actions.{action_node_name}.{action_node_name}",
        action_node=action_node, variables=variables
    )


def execute_task(task):
    try:
        task = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Task", task.get("name")).as_dict()
        if task.execution_status != task_states.PENDING:
            return
    except:
        pass

    exec_start_time = datetime.now()
    exec_delays = []
    update_task_status(task, task_states.EXECUTING)

    target_node_id = task.target_node_id

    return_data = {}
    has_tasks_to_execute = False
    prev_user = frappe.session.user
    execute_user = prev_user
    user_change_reason = "Funnel Default"
    try:
        # get variables from workflow data
        variables = {}
        if not task.workflow:
            raise Exception("No workflow found for task:" + task.name)

        workflow_doc = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Workflow", task.workflow)
        if workflow_doc.variables:
            variables = json.loads(workflow_doc.variables)

        variables["workflow_name"] = workflow_doc.name
        variables["task_name"] = task.name

        # get node data
        action_nodes = get_action_node_from_id(target_node_id)
        if not len(action_nodes):
            raise Exception("No action found with id:" + target_node_id)

        action_node = action_nodes[0]

        try:
            if variables.get("doc"):
                variables["doc"] = frappe.get_doc(
                    variables["doc"]["doctype"], variables["doc"]["name"]
                ).as_dict()
        except:
            pass

        try:
            exec_delays.append((datetime.now() - exec_start_time).total_seconds())

            # execute node as user
            node_data = json.loads(action_node.data)
            if node_data.get("execute_current_node_as_trigger_user") and variables.get("trigger_user_id"):
                execute_user = variables.get("trigger_user_id")
                if execute_user:
                    frappe.session.user = execute_user
                    user_change_reason = "trigger_user_id"
            elif node_data.get("execute_current_node_as_user"):
                execute_user = format_with_variables(
                    node_data["execute_current_node_as_user"], variables
                )
                if execute_user:
                    frappe.session.user = execute_user
                    user_change_reason = "execute_current_node_as_user"
            elif variables.get("execute_current_workflow_as_user"):
                execute_user = variables["execute_current_workflow_as_user"]
                if execute_user:
                    frappe.session.user = execute_user
                    user_change_reason = "execute_current_workflow_as_user"
            return_data = execute_node(action_node, variables)

            # reset to prev user
            frappe.session.user = prev_user
            exec_delays.append((datetime.now() - exec_start_time).total_seconds())
            node_data = frappe.parse_json(action_node.data)
            variable_path_for_output = node_data.get("variable_path_for_output")
            if variable_path_for_output:
                variables[variable_path_for_output] = return_data.get("result")
            update_workflow(task.workflow, variables)
        except Exception as e:
            # reset to prev user
            frappe.session.user = prev_user
            # on exec error - try to run error flow then raise same error for the error handler
            action_nodes = get_next_action_nodes_with_source_handle_id(
                target_node_id, ON_ERROR_HANDLE_NAME
            )
            if len(action_nodes):
                variables['error'] = str(e)
                update_workflow(task.workflow, variables)
                # create new tasks
                for action_node in action_nodes:
                    create_new_task(
                        task.workflow,
                        action_node.id,
                        target_node_id,
                        task.name,
                        task_info={
                            "error": str(e),
                            "parent_exec_delays": exec_delays,
                        },
                        target_node_name=action_node.type,
                        target_node=action_node,
                    )
                has_tasks_to_execute = True
            raise e
    except Exception as e:
        exec_trace = str(traceback.format_exc()).split("\n")
        stack_trace = traceback.format_stack()
        exec_delays.append((datetime.now() - exec_start_time).total_seconds())
        return update_task_status(
            task,
            task_states.FAILED,
            {
                "error": str(e),
                "exec_trace": exec_trace,
                "stack_trace": stack_trace,
                "exec_delays": exec_delays,
                "execute_user": execute_user,
                "user_change_reason": user_change_reason,
            },
        )

    result = return_data.get("result") or None
    # execute_after = return_data.get("execute_after") or datetime.now()
    task_info = return_data.get("task_info") or None
    source_handle_id = return_data.get("source_handle_id") or None
    skip_task_creation = return_data.get("skip_task_creation") or False
    skip_task_update = return_data.get("skip_task_update") or False
    mark_as_cancelled = return_data.get("mark_as_cancelled") or False

    exec_delays.append((datetime.now() - exec_start_time).total_seconds())
    if not skip_task_update:
        new_state = (
            task_states.CANCELLED if mark_as_cancelled else task_states.COMPLETED
        )
        update_task_status(
            task, new_state, {"exec_result": result, "exec_delays": exec_delays}
        )

    if skip_task_creation:
        return False

    # get next following actions
    next_action_nodes = []
    if source_handle_id:
        next_action_nodes = get_next_action_nodes_with_source_handle_id(
            target_node_id, source_handle_id
        )
    else:
        next_action_nodes = get_next_action_nodes(target_node_id)
    # create new tasks
    exec_delays.append((datetime.now() - exec_start_time).total_seconds())
    for action_node in next_action_nodes:
        try:
            updated_task_info = {**task_info, "parent_exec_delays": exec_delays}
        except Exception as e:
            updated_task_info = {
                "task_info": task_info,
                "parent_exec_delays": exec_delays,
            }
        create_new_task(
            task.workflow,
            action_node.id,
            target_node_id,
            task.name,
            task_info=updated_task_info,
            target_node_name=action_node.type,
            target_node=action_node,
        )

    has_tasks_to_execute = has_tasks_to_execute or len(
        next_action_nodes
    )  # and not return_data.get("execute_after")
    return has_tasks_to_execute


def queue_execute_pending_tasks(at_front=False):
    try:
        # queue with only 1s delay
        cache = frappe.cache()
        last_task_queued_time = cache.get("last_funnel_tasks_task_queued_time")
        if last_task_queued_time:
            last_task_queued_time = float(last_task_queued_time)
            if time.time() - last_task_queued_time < 1:
                return

        cache.set("last_funnel_tasks_task_queued_time", time.time())
    except:
        pass

    queue_name = "long"
    try:
        if (
                frappe.get_site_config()
                        .get("workers")
                        .get("funnel")
                        .get("background_workers")
        ):
            queue_name = "funnel"
    except:
        pass

    frappe.enqueue(
        "nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.funnel_task.execute_all_pending_tasks",
        queue='long',
        is_async=True,
        timeout=3600,
        at_front=at_front,
    )


def set_funnel_user():
    has_funnel_user = check()
    if not has_funnel_user:
        raise Exception("Funnel user not found")
    frappe.session.user = get_funnel_user_id()


def get_finished_workflows():
    values = {}
    workflows = frappe.db.sql(
        """
        select *  from `tabFunnel Workflow`
            where status = 'running'
            and name not in (select workflow
                from `tabFunnel Task`
                where execution_status != 'completed'
                    and execution_status != 'cancelled'
                    and execution_status != 'failed')
        """,
        values=values,
        as_dict=1,
    )
    return workflows


def execute_all_pending_tasks():
    try:
        set_funnel_user()
        frappe.db.commit()
        has_executed = False
        MAX_LOOP_COUNT = 10
        loop_count = 0
        for priority in [0, 1, 2]:
            pending_tasks = get_all_pending_tasks(priority=priority)
            while len(pending_tasks) and loop_count <= MAX_LOOP_COUNT:
                loop_count += 1
                has_executed = True
                for task in pending_tasks:
                    execute_task(task)
                frappe.db.commit()
                pending_tasks = get_all_pending_tasks(priority=priority)
        if has_executed:
            queue_execute_pending_tasks()

        workflows = get_finished_workflows()
        for workflow in workflows:
            update_workflow(workflow.name, status="finished")

    except Exception as e:
        error(str(e))


def on_cron_trigger():
    queue_execute_pending_tasks(True)

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/triggers/chatnext_assistant_public_trigger.py
import json
import uuid

import frappe
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel.funnel_user import get_funnel_user_id
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.assistant_api import get_chatnext_assistant_public_trigger_list
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.funnel_task import queue_execute_pending_tasks
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import create_new_task
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.extract_data_from_text import extract_data_from_text
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.find_best_text_match import find_best_text_match
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_action_nodes import (
    get_action_nodes_from_trigger,
)

cna_conversation_doctype = "CNA Conversation"


# nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.triggers.chatnext_assistant_public_trigger.link_trigger
@frappe.whitelist(allow_guest=True)
def link_trigger(
    trigger_id,
):
    user_id = frappe.session.user

    if not frappe.db.exists(
            "CN Link Funnel Trigger", {"assistant_trigger_id": trigger_id}
    ):
        session_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        conversation = frappe.get_doc(
            {
                "doctype": cna_conversation_doctype,
                "status": "default",
                "user": user_id,
                "session": session_id,
                "id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
                "author": "Bot",
                "message_type": "Text",
                "message": "This is a invalid link",
            }
        )
        conversation.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
        return {"session": session_id}
    trigger_link_doc = frappe.get_doc(
        "CN Link Funnel Trigger", {"assistant_trigger_id": trigger_id}
    )
    is_executable = (
            trigger_link_doc.multiple_usage or trigger_link_doc.execution_count == 0
    )
    if not is_executable:
        session_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        conversation = frappe.get_doc(
            {
                "doctype": cna_conversation_doctype,
                "status": "default",
                "user": user_id,
                "session": session_id,
                "id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
                "author": "Bot",
                "message_type": "Text",
                "message": "You cannot used this link more than once. This link is already used",
            }
        )
        conversation.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
        return {"session": session_id}

    variables = json.loads(trigger_link_doc.get("variables", "{}") or "{}")
    trigger_definition_doc = frappe.get_doc(
        "Funnel Definition", trigger_link_doc.funnel_definition
    )

    session_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    variables = {
        "event": "chatnext_trigger",
        "session_id": session_id,
        "user_id": user_id,
        "trigger_user_id": user_id,
        **variables,
    }
    workflow = frappe.get_doc(
        {
            "doctype": "Funnel Workflow",
            "funnel": trigger_definition_doc.parent,
            "variables": json.dumps(variables, default=str),
        }
    )
    workflow.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    workflow.reload()

    conversation = frappe.get_doc(
        {
            "doctype": cna_conversation_doctype,
            "status": "default",
            "user": user_id,
            "session": session_id,
            "id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
            "author": "Bot",
            "message_type": "Loader",
            "message": "Loading...",
        }
    )
    conversation.insert(ignore_permissions=True)

    trigger_link_doc.execution_count = trigger_link_doc.execution_count + 1
    trigger_link_doc.save(ignore_permissions=True)

    actions = get_action_nodes_from_trigger(trigger_definition_doc.id)
    for action_node in actions:
        create_new_task(
            workflow.name,
            action_node.id,
            trigger_definition_doc.id,
            "",
            task_info={
                "trigger": "chatnext_trigger",
            },
        )
    queue_execute_pending_tasks()

    return {"session": session_id}


# nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.triggers.chatnext_assistant_public_trigger.trigger
@frappe.whitelist(allow_guest=True)
def trigger(
    definition_name,
    variables=None,
    previous_session=None,
):
    variables = variables or {}
    user_id = frappe.session.user

    trigger_definition_doc = frappe.get_doc(
        "Funnel Definition", definition_name
    )

    session_id = previous_session or str(uuid.uuid4())
    variables = {
        "event": "chatnext_trigger",
        "session_id": session_id,
        "user_id": user_id,
        "trigger_user_id": user_id,
        **variables,
    }
    workflow = frappe.get_doc(
        {
            "doctype": "Funnel Workflow",
            "funnel": trigger_definition_doc.parent,
            "variables": json.dumps(variables, default=str),
        }
    )
    workflow.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    workflow.reload()

    conversation = frappe.get_doc(
        {
            "doctype": cna_conversation_doctype,
            "status": "default",
            "user": user_id,
            "session": session_id,
            "id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
            "author": "Bot",
            "message_type": "Loader",
            "message": "Loading...",
        }
    )
    conversation.insert(ignore_permissions=True)

    actions = get_action_nodes_from_trigger(trigger_definition_doc.id)
    for action_node in actions:
        create_new_task(
            workflow.name,
            action_node.id,
            trigger_definition_doc.id,
            "",
            task_info={
                "trigger": "chatnext_trigger",
            },
        )
    queue_execute_pending_tasks()

    return {"session": session_id}


def create_conversation_async(function_args):
    message = function_args["message"]
    previous_session_id = function_args["previous_session_id"]
    user_id = function_args["user_id"]
    session_id = function_args["session_id"]

    frappe.session.user = user_id

    executable_triggers = get_chatnext_assistant_public_trigger_list()

    trigger_commands = [
        {"command": t.name_of_action, "definition": t}
        for t in executable_triggers
    ]
    frappe.session.user = get_funnel_user_id()
    best_suitable_command = find_best_text_match(
        [t["command"] for t in trigger_commands], message
    )
    frappe.session.user = user_id
    if best_suitable_command:
        best_suitable_trigger_definition = [
            t["definition"]
            for t in trigger_commands
            if best_suitable_command.strip().lower() == t["command"].strip().lower()
        ]
        if len(best_suitable_trigger_definition) > 0:
            published_funnel = frappe.get_doc(
                "Funnel Published", best_suitable_trigger_definition[0].parent
            )
            funnel = frappe.get_doc("Funnel", published_funnel.funnel)
            frappe.session.user = get_funnel_user_id()
            extracted_data_string = extract_data_from_text(
                message, best_suitable_command, funnel.assistant_form_fields
            )
            frappe.session.user = user_id
            extracted_session_data = {}
            if previous_session_id:
                pwf_data = {"session_id": previous_session_id, "user_id": user_id}
                previous_workflows = frappe.db.sql(
                    """
                        SELECT
                            name, status, variables
                        FROM
                            `tabFunnel Workflow`
                        WHERE
                            JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`variables`, '$.session_id')) = %(session_id)s and
                            JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`variables`, '$.user_id')) = %(user_id)s
                        order by creation desc
                        limit 1
                    """,
                    values=pwf_data,
                    as_dict=1,
                )
                for previous_workflow in previous_workflows:
                    workflow_variables = json.loads(previous_workflow.variables)
                    if "common_assistant_prefill_data" in workflow_variables:
                        extracted_session_data = {
                            **extracted_session_data,
                            **workflow_variables["common_assistant_prefill_data"],
                        }
            extracted_data = json.loads(extracted_data_string)
            common_assistant_prefill_data = {**extracted_session_data, **extracted_data}
            variables = {
                "common_assistant_prefill_data": common_assistant_prefill_data,
            }

            return trigger(
                best_suitable_trigger_definition[0].name,
                variables,
                previous_session_id
            )

    conversation = frappe.get_doc(
        {
            "doctype": cna_conversation_doctype,
            "status": "default",
            "user": user_id,
            "session": session_id,
            "id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
            "author": "Bot",
            "message_type": "Text",
            "message": "Could not understand, please try again with different query",
        }
    )
    conversation.insert(ignore_permissions=True)

@frappe.whitelist(allow_guest=True)
def create_new_conversation(
    message: str,
    previous_session_id: str = None,
):
    # show error message to user
    session_id = previous_session_id or str(uuid.uuid4())
    user_id = frappe.session.user

    # create conversation
    conversation = frappe.get_doc(
        {
            "doctype": cna_conversation_doctype,
            "status": "default",
            "user": user_id,
            "session": session_id,
            "id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
            "author": "User",
            "message_type": "Text",
            "message": message,
        }
    )
    conversation.insert(ignore_permissions=True)

    # create conversation
    conversation = frappe.get_doc(
        {
            "doctype": cna_conversation_doctype,
            "status": "default",
            "user": user_id,
            "session": session_id,
            "id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
            "author": "Bot",
            "message_type": "Loader",
            "message": "Loading...",
        }
    )
    conversation.insert(ignore_permissions=True)

    frappe.db.commit()

    async_function_args = {
        "message": message,
        "previous_session_id": previous_session_id,
        "user_id": user_id,
        "session_id": session_id,
    }

    frappe.enqueue(
        "nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.triggers.chatnext_assistant_public_trigger.create_conversation_async",
        function_args=async_function_args,
    )
    # print(create_conversation_async(async_function_args))

    return {"session": session_id}

import json

    handle_chatnext_assistant_text_response,
)

# nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.triggers.chatnext_assistant_trigger.trigger
@frappe.whitelist()
def trigger(
    definition_name,
    variables=None,
    skip_first_message=False,
    session_id=None,
    need_docname=False,
):
    if variables is None:
        variables = {}

    if type(variables) is str:
        variables = json.loads(variables)

    session_id = session_id or str(uuid.uuid4())
    trigger_doc = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Definition", definition_name)
    published_funnel = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Published", trigger_doc.parent)

    if not can_execute(published_funnel.funnel):
        raise frappe.PermissionError(
            frappe._("You do not have permission to access this resource.")
        )

    user_id = frappe.session.user
    set_funnel_user()

    definition_data = json.loads(trigger_doc.data)
    page_type = definition_data.get("page_type")

    # validate variable
    if definition_data.get("doctype"):
        variables["doctype"] = definition_data.get("doctype")

    if page_type == "doctype" and not variables.get("docname") and not need_docname:
        raise frappe.ValidationError("Missing docname")

    if (
            page_type == "doctype"
            and "docname" in variables
            and "doctype" in definition_data
    ):
        docname = variables.get("docname")
        doctype = definition_data.get("doctype")
        doc = frappe.get_doc(doctype, docname)
        if doc:
            variables["doc"] = doc.as_dict()

    variables = {
        "event": "chatnext_trigger",
        "session_id": session_id,
        "user_id": user_id,
        "trigger_user_id": user_id,
        **variables,
    }
    # create workflow
    workflow = frappe.get_doc(
        {
            "doctype": "Funnel Workflow",
            "funnel": trigger_doc.parent,
            "variables": json.dumps(variables, default=str),
        }
    )
    workflow.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    workflow.reload()

    # create conversation
    if not skip_first_message:
        conversation = frappe.get_doc(
            {
                "doctype": cna_conversation_doctype,
                "status": "default",
                "user": user_id,
                "session": session_id,
                "id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
                "author": "User",
                "message_type": "Trigger",
                "message": json.dumps(
                    {
                        "name_of_action": definition_data.get("name_of_action"),
                        "page_type": definition_data.get("page_type"),
                        "definition": trigger_doc.name,
                        **variables,
                    },
                    default=str,
                    indent=2,
                ),
            }
        )
        conversation.insert(ignore_permissions=True)

    variables = {
        "workflow": workflow.name,
        **variables,
    }

    if need_docname:
        chatnext_assistant_trigger(trigger_doc, variables)
    else:
        # enqueue next actions
        actions = get_action_nodes_from_trigger(trigger_doc.id)
        for action_node in actions:
            create_new_task(
                workflow.name,
                action_node.id,
                trigger_doc.id,
                "",
                task_info={
                    "trigger": "chatnext_trigger",
                },
            )
    queue_execute_pending_tasks()
    return {"session": session_id}


def create_form_prefill_data(form_json, workflow_variables):
    form_components = form_json.get('components', [])
    # TODO: handle/find nested components & containers
    fetchable_user_fields = [
        f for f in form_components if
        f.get('isCustomUserField') and f.get("fetchDataOnFormLoad")
    ]
    if not fetchable_user_fields: return None
    submission_data = {}
    for user_field in fetchable_user_fields:
        selected_sync_field, selected_sync_doctype, target_doctype_name, value_field = \
            user_field["selectedSyncField"], user_field['selectedSyncDoctype'], user_field["targetDoctypeName"], user_field['key']
        target_doctype_name = format_with_variables(target_doctype_name, workflow_variables)
        target_doc = frappe.get_doc(selected_sync_doctype, target_doctype_name)
        field_value = target_doc.get(selected_sync_field)
        submission_data[value_field] = field_value
    return submission_data


def iso_to_frappe_datetime(date_str: str) -> str | None:
    if not date_str:
        return None
    return frappe.utils.format_datetime(date_str, "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss")


def update_with_data_from_form_submission(form_json, submission, workflow_id):
    if not workflow_id or not form_json: return
    form_components = form_json.get('components', [])
    # TODO: handle/find nested components & containers
    updatable_user_fields = [
        f for f in form_components if
        f.get('isCustomUserField') and f.get("updateFieldOnFormSubmit")
    ]
    if not updatable_user_fields: return
    workflow_doc = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Workflow", workflow_id)
    workflow_variables = json.loads(workflow_doc.variables)

    for user_field in updatable_user_fields:
        selected_sync_field, selected_sync_doctype, target_doctype_name, value_field = \
            user_field["selectedSyncField"], user_field['selectedSyncDoctype'], \
                user_field["targetDoctypeName"], user_field['key']

        target_doctype_name = format_with_variables(target_doctype_name, workflow_variables)
        applicable_from_time = submission.get('onSubmitFieldApplicableFromTime')
        applicable_to_time = submission.get('onSubmitFieldApplicableToTime')
        if applicable_from_time:
            field_value_applicablity = frappe.get_doc(
                {
                    "doctype": "Field Value Update Applicablity",
                    "status": "Pending",
                    "from_date_time": iso_to_frappe_datetime(applicable_from_time),
                    "to_date_time": iso_to_frappe_datetime(applicable_to_time),
                    "applicable_value": json.dumps([submission[value_field]]),
                    "target_doctype": selected_sync_doctype,
                    "target_docname": target_doctype_name,
                    "target_field": selected_sync_field,
                }
            )
            field_value_applicablity.insert(ignore_permissions=True, ignore_links=True)
        else:
            update_field_value(
                doctype=selected_sync_doctype,
                docname=target_doctype_name,
                field=selected_sync_field,
                value=submission[value_field]
            )


# on_form_submit, on_submit
# nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.triggers.chatnext_assistant_trigger.submit_form
@frappe.whitelist(allow_guest=True)
def submit_form(message_id, data):
    user_id = frappe.session.user
    set_funnel_user()

    form_conversation = frappe.get_doc(
        cna_conversation_doctype,
        {
            "id": message_id,
            "message_type": "Form",
            "user": user_id,
            "status": "pending",
        },
    )
    session_id = form_conversation.session

    # create conversation
    conversation = frappe.get_doc(
        {
            "doctype": cna_conversation_doctype,
            "status": "submitted",
            "user": user_id,
            "session": session_id,
            "id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
            "author": "User",
            "message_type": "Submission",
            "workflow": form_conversation.workflow,
            "message": json.dumps(
                {"submission": data, "form_message_id": message_id},
                default=str,
                indent=2,
            ),
        }
    )
    conversation.insert(ignore_permissions=True)

    form_conversation.status = "submitted"
    prev_message = json.loads(form_conversation.message)

    on_submit_data = prev_message.get('on_submit')
    submission_payload = on_submit_data.get('payload', {}) if on_submit_data else {}

    if submission_payload.get('show_in_chat_history'):
        form_conversation.show_in_chat_history = True

    form_conversation.message = json.dumps(
        {
            **prev_message,
            "submission_data": data,
            "submission_message_id": conversation.id,
        },
        default=str,
        indent=2,
    )
    form_conversation.save(ignore_permissions=True)

    frappe.db.commit()

    # update with data from form submission
    form_json = prev_message['form']
    if prev_message.get("enable_data_update_on_submit"):
        update_with_data_from_form_submission(form_json, data, form_conversation.workflow)

    if on_submit_data:
        submission_method = on_submit_data['method']
        frappe.call(
            submission_method, **{
                **submission_payload,
                'submission': data,
                'session_id': session_id,
                'user_id': user_id,
                'form_message': prev_message,
                'workflow_id': form_conversation.workflow
            }
        )
    else:
        chatnext_assistant_form_question(session_id, data, form_conversation.workflow)
    return True


# nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.triggers.chatnext_assistant_trigger.add_user_message
@frappe.whitelist(allow_guest=True)
def add_user_message(session_id: str, message: str):
    user_id = frappe.session.user
    set_funnel_user()

    if not frappe.db.exists(
            cna_conversation_doctype, {"session": session_id, "user": user_id}
    ):
        raise frappe.ValidationError("Conversation not found")

    # create conversation
    conversation = frappe.get_doc(
        {
            "doctype": cna_conversation_doctype,
            "status": "default",
            "user": user_id,
            "session": session_id,
            "id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
            "author": "User",
            "message_type": "Text",
            "message": message,
        }
    )
    conversation.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    frappe.db.commit()

    handle_chatnext_assistant_text_response(session_id, message, user_id)
    return True


def create_conversation_async(function_args):
    route = function_args["route"]
    page_type = function_args["page_type"]
    page_name = function_args["page_name"]
    message = function_args["message"]
    variables = function_args["variables"]
    previous_session_id = function_args["previous_session_id"]
    user_id = function_args["user_id"]
    session_id = function_args["session_id"]

    frappe.session.user = user_id

    executable_triggers = get_chatnext_assistant_trigger_list(
        route, page_type, page_name
    )

    doctype_specific_triggers = frappe.db.sql(
        """
            SELECT *, (SELECT funnel FROM `tabFunnel Published` WHERE name = `tabFunnel Definition`.parent) AS funnel_name
            FROM `tabFunnel Definition`
            WHERE `element_type` = 'Trigger'
            AND parenttype = 'Funnel Published'
            AND parent IN (
                SELECT name
                FROM `tabFunnel Published`
                WHERE published = 1
            )
            AND type = 'chatnext_assistant_trigger'
            AND (
                JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`data`, '$.page_type')) = 'doctype'
            )
        """,
        values={},
        as_dict=1,
    )

    trigger_commands = [
        {"command": json.loads(t.data).get("name_of_action"), "definition": t}
        for t in (executable_triggers + doctype_specific_triggers)
    ]
    best_suitable_command = find_best_text_match(
        [t["command"] for t in trigger_commands], message
    )
    if best_suitable_command:
        best_suitable_trigger_definition = [
            t["definition"]
            for t in trigger_commands
            if best_suitable_command.strip().lower() == t["command"].strip().lower()
        ]
        if len(best_suitable_trigger_definition) > 0:
            published_funnel = frappe.get_doc(
                "Funnel Published", best_suitable_trigger_definition[0].parent
            )
            funnel = frappe.get_doc("Funnel", published_funnel.funnel)
            extracted_data_string = extract_data_from_text(
                message, best_suitable_command, funnel.assistant_form_fields
            )
            if not variables:
                variables = {}

            extracted_session_data = {}
            if previous_session_id:
                pwf_data = {"session_id": previous_session_id, "user_id": user_id}
                previous_workflows = frappe.db.sql(
                    """
                        SELECT
                            name, status, variables
                        FROM
                            `tabFunnel Workflow`
                        WHERE
                            JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`variables`, '$.session_id')) = %(session_id)s and
                            JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`variables`, '$.user_id')) = %(user_id)s
                        order by creation desc
                        limit 1
                    """,
                    values=pwf_data,
                    as_dict=1,
                )
                for previous_workflow in previous_workflows:
                    workflow_variables = json.loads(previous_workflow.variables)
                    if "common_assistant_prefill_data" in workflow_variables:
                        extracted_session_data = {
                            **extracted_session_data,
                            **workflow_variables["common_assistant_prefill_data"],
                        }
            extracted_data = json.loads(extracted_data_string)
            common_assistant_prefill_data = {**extracted_session_data, **extracted_data}
            def_data = json.loads(best_suitable_trigger_definition[0].data)
            doctype = def_data.get("doctype")
            variables = {
                "common_assistant_prefill_data": common_assistant_prefill_data,
                "doctype": doctype,
                **variables,
            }

            return trigger(
                best_suitable_trigger_definition[0].name,
                variables,
                True,
                session_id,
                best_suitable_trigger_definition[0] in doctype_specific_triggers,
            )

    conversation = frappe.get_doc(
        {
            "doctype": cna_conversation_doctype,
            "status": "default",
            "user": user_id,
            "session": session_id,
            "id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
            "author": "Bot",
            "message_type": "Text",
            "message": "Could not understand, please try again with different query",
        }
    )
    conversation.insert(ignore_permissions=True)

# nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.triggers.chatnext_assistant_trigger.on_complete_ui_action
