# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/triggers/email_trigger.py
import json
import traceback

import frappe
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import task_states, node_names
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.awaiting_actions.on_email_reply import on_email_reply
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.awaiting_actions.on_email_seen import on_email_seen
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.funnel_task import set_funnel_user, queue_execute_pending_tasks
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import create_new_task, update_task_status
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_action_nodes import get_action_nodes_from_trigger, \
    get_action_node_from_id
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.logger import debug
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.update_workflow import update_workflow


def get_awaiting_email_tasks(funnel_email_id, order_by="creation", desc=1):
    values = {
        'funnel_email_id': funnel_email_id,
        "execution_status": task_states.AWAITING_TRIGGER,
        'order_by': order_by,
        'desc': "desc" if desc else "",
    }
    nodes = frappe.db.sql("""
      SELECT
            *
        FROM
            `tabFunnel Task`
        WHERE
            execution_status =  %(execution_status)s and
            workflow in (
                SELECT
                    name
                FROM
                    `tabFunnel Workflow`
                WHERE
                    JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`variables`, '$.funnel_email_id')) = %(funnel_email_id)s
            )
        ORDER BY
            %(order_by)s %(desc)s
    """, values=values, as_dict=1)
    return nodes


def get_all_email_triggers():
    values = {}
    nodes = frappe.db.sql("""
        SELECT *
        FROM `tabFunnel Definition`
        WHERE type = 'email_trigger'
        and parenttype = 'Funnel Published'
        and parent in (
            SELECT name
            FROM `tabFunnel Published`
            WHERE published = 1
        )
    """, values=values, as_dict=1)
    return nodes


def email_trigger(email_doc):
    # print("resolve_awaiting_email_tasks", email_doc.as_dict())
    sender = email_doc.sender

    contact_doc = frappe.get_doc("Contact", {
        "email_id": email_doc.sender,
    })
    if not contact_doc:
        return

    triggers = get_all_email_triggers()

    for trigger in triggers:
        # emailIds
        trigger_data = json.loads(trigger.data)
        target_emails_str: str = trigger_data.get("emailIds", '')
        if target_emails_str:
            target_emails = [e.strip() for e in target_emails_str.split(',')]
            if sender not in target_emails:
                continue

        data = {
            "trigger_doc_name": email_doc.name,
            "event": "after_insert",
            "contact_name": contact_doc.name,
            "funnel_email_id": sender,
            "last_email_doc_name": email_doc.name,
            "last_email_data": email_doc.as_dict(),
        }
        workflow = frappe.get_doc({
            "doctype": "Funnel Workflow",
            "funnel": trigger.parent,
            "variables": json.dumps(data, indent=2, default=str)
        })
        workflow.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
        actions = get_action_nodes_from_trigger(trigger.id)
        for action_node in actions:
            create_new_task(workflow.name, action_node.id, trigger.id, "")
    if len(triggers):
        queue_execute_pending_tasks()


def resolve_awaiting_email_tasks(email_doc):
    # print("resolve_awaiting_email_tasks", email_doc.as_dict())
    sender = email_doc.sender

    tasks = get_awaiting_email_tasks(sender, desc=0)
    if len(tasks):
        for task in tasks:
            # this would execute only once
            no_matched_action = False
            try:
                # execute task
                action_node = get_action_node_from_id(task.target_node_id)[0]

                workflow_name = task.workflow
                workflow_doc = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Workflow", workflow_name)
                variables = frappe.parse_json(workflow_doc.variables)
                try:
                    contact_doc = frappe.get_doc("Contact", {
                        "email_id": email_doc.sender,
                    })
                    variables['contact_name'] = contact_doc.name
                except:
                    pass

                if action_node.type == node_names.on_email_reply:
                    on_email_reply(action_node, task, email_doc.as_dict(), variables)
                else:
                    no_matched_action = True

                # would not reach here if something went wrong
                update_workflow(workflow_name, variables)
            except Exception as e:
                exec_trace = traceback.format_exc()
                stack_trace = traceback.format_stack()
                update_task_status(task, task_states.FAILED, {
                    "error": str(e),
                    "exec_trace": exec_trace,
                    "stack_trace": stack_trace
                })
            if not no_matched_action:
                break
    else:
        # # check if chatbot enabled and need to proceed
        # if contact_doc.chatbot_disabled:
        #     return
        email_trigger(email_doc)


def update_contact(doc):
    sender_emails = [e.strip() for e in doc.sender.split(",") if e.strip()]
    recipient_emails = [e.strip() for e in doc.recipients.split(",") if e.strip()]
    all_emails = sender_emails + recipient_emails
    contacts = frappe.get_all("Contact", fields=["name"], filters={"email_id": ["in", all_emails]})
    for contact in contacts:
        try:
            contact_doc = frappe.get_doc("Contact", contact.name)
            contact_doc.custom_last_email_interaction_time = doc.creation
            contact_doc.save()
            frappe.db.commit()
        except:
            pass


def on_email_marked_as_read(email_doc):
    # print("resolve_awaiting_email_tasks", email_doc.as_dict())
    sender = email_doc.sender
    tasks = get_awaiting_email_tasks(sender, desc=0)
    if len(tasks):
        for task in tasks:
            # this would execute only once
            no_matched_action = False
            try:
                # execute task
                action_node = get_action_node_from_id(task.target_node_id)[0]

                workflow_name = task.workflow
                workflow_doc = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Workflow", workflow_name)
                variables = frappe.parse_json(workflow_doc.variables)
                try:
                    contact_doc = frappe.get_doc("Contact", {
                        "email_id": email_doc.sender,
                    })
                    variables['contact_name'] = contact_doc.name
                except:
                    pass

                if action_node.type == node_names.on_email_seen:
                    on_email_seen(action_node, task, email_doc.as_dict(), variables)
                else:
                    no_matched_action = True

                # would not reach here if something went wrong
                update_workflow(workflow_name, variables)
            except Exception as e:
                exec_trace = traceback.format_exc()
                stack_trace = traceback.format_stack()
                update_task_status(task, task_states.FAILED, {
                    "error": str(e),
                    "exec_trace": exec_trace,
                    "stack_trace": stack_trace
                })
            if not no_matched_action:
                break


def on_new_email_updated(doc, event):
    # check of if marked as read
    try:
        prev_doc = doc.get_doc_before_save()
        if prev_doc and doc.read_by_recipient and not prev_doc.read_by_recipient:
            on_email_marked_as_read(doc)
    except:
        pass


def on_new_email_created(doc, event):
    try:
        update_contact(doc)
    except:
        pass

    # return if outgoing message
    if doc.get("sent_or_received") != "Received":
        return

    try:
        set_funnel_user()
        resolve_awaiting_email_tasks(doc)
    except Exception as e:
        debug(str(e))

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/triggers/on_cron_trigger.py
import traceback

import frappe
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import task_states, node_names
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.awaiting_actions.wait_node import wait_node
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.awaiting_actions.wait_till_date import (
    wait_till_date,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_tasks_from_triggers import (
    create_tasks_from_triggers,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import (
    update_task_status,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_action_nodes import (
    get_action_node_from_id,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_pending_tasks import (
    get_all_awaiting_tasks,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.logger import debug


def get_cron_trigger_nodes(interval):
    values = {"interval": interval}
    nodes = frappe.db.sql(
        """
        SELECT *
        FROM `tabFunnel Definition`
        WHERE `element_type` = 'Trigger'
        and parenttype = 'Funnel Published'
        and parent in (
            select name from `tabFunnel Published`
            where published = 1
        )
        and type = 'cron_node'
        and JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`data`, '$.interval')) = %(interval)s
    """,
        values=values,
        as_dict=1,
    )
    return nodes


#  interval = monthly | weekly | daily | hourly | every_min
def execute_corn_triggers(interval):
    try:
        triggers = get_cron_trigger_nodes(interval)
        data = {"event": "cron", "interval": interval}
        create_tasks_from_triggers(triggers, data)
    except Exception as e:
        debug(str(e))


def resolve_timeout_awaiting_tasks():
    awaiting_tasks = get_all_awaiting_tasks()
    for task in awaiting_tasks:
        try:
            # execute task
            action_node = get_action_node_from_id(task.target_node_id)[0]

            if action_node.type == node_names.wait_node:
                wait_node(action_node, task)
            elif action_node.type == node_names.wait_till_date:
                wait_till_date(action_node, task)
        except Exception as e:
            exec_trace = traceback.format_exc()
            stack_trace = traceback.format_stack()
            update_task_status(
                task,
                task_states.FAILED,
                {"error": str(e), "exec_trace": exec_trace, "stack_trace": stack_trace},
            )


def every_month():
    execute_corn_triggers("every_month")


def every_week():
    execute_corn_triggers("every_week")


def every_day():
    execute_corn_triggers("every_day")


def every_hour():
    execute_corn_triggers("every_hour")


def every_minute():
    execute_corn_triggers("every_minute")
    resolve_timeout_awaiting_tasks()

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/triggers/on_doctype_events.py
import json
import traceback

import frappe
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_tasks_from_triggers import create_tasks_from_triggers
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import create_new_task
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_action_nodes import get_action_nodes_from_trigger_with_source_handle_id
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.logger import error


def get_doc_event_trigger_nodes(doctype, action):
    values = {
        'doctype': doctype,
        'action': action
    }
    nodes = frappe.db.sql(
        """
                SELECT *
                FROM `tabFunnel Definition`
                WHERE `element_type` = 'Trigger'
                and parenttype = 'Funnel Published'
                and parent in (
                    select name
                    from `tabFunnel Published`
                    where published = 1
                )
                and type = 'document_event_trigger'
                and  JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`data`, '$.doctype')) = %(doctype)s
                and  JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`data`, '$.action')) = %(action)s
            """, values=values, as_dict=1
    )
    return nodes


def get_custom_event_trigger_nodes(doctype, event_name):
    values = {
        'doctype': doctype,
        'event_name': event_name
    }
    nodes = frappe.db.sql(
        """
                select *
                from `tabFunnel Definition`
                where  type in (
                    select name
                    from `tabFunnel Node`
                    where is_custom = 1
                    and parenttype = 'Funnel Published'
                    and parent in (
                        select name from `tabFunnel Published`
                        where published = 1
                    )
                    and element_type = 'Trigger'
                    and trigger_event_name = %(event_name)s
                )
                and  JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`data`, '$.doctype')) = %(doctype)s
            """, values=values, as_dict=1
    )
    return nodes


# for async call
def create_after_email_insert_tasks(workflows_ids, triggers_ids, doc_name, doc_type):
    try:
        # handle email insert - find if doc is associated with communication doc
        communication_docs = frappe.get_all(
            'Communication', filters={
                "reference_doctype": doc_type,
                "reference_name": doc_name,
            }, fields=['*']
        )
        if communication_docs:
            communication_doc = communication_docs[0]
            attachments = frappe.get_all(
                'File', filters={
                    'attached_to_doctype': 'Communication',
                    'attached_to_name': communication_doc.name
                }, fields=['name', 'file_url']
            )
            data = {
                'communication': communication_doc,
                'attachments': attachments,
            }
            for i in range(len(triggers_ids)):
                triggers_id = triggers_ids[i]
                workflows_id = workflows_ids[i]
                workflow = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Workflow", workflows_id)

                actions = get_action_nodes_from_trigger_with_source_handle_id(triggers_id, 'on_email_insert')
                if actions:
                    variables = json.loads(workflow.variables)
                    workflow.update(
                        {
                            "variables": json.dumps(
                                {
                                    **variables,
                                    **data
                                }, default=str
                            )
                        }
                    )
                    workflow.save(ignore_permissions=True)

                for action_node in actions:
                    create_new_task(workflows_id, action_node.id, triggers_id, "")
    except Exception as e:
        error(str(e))
        error(traceback.format_exc().split('\n'))


# nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.triggers.on_doctype_events.on_trigger_doc_events
def on_trigger_doc_events(doc, event):
    if event == 'on_cancel':
        on_trigger_doc_events(doc, "after_cancel")
    try:
        # set_funnel_user()
        triggers = get_doc_event_trigger_nodes(doc.doctype, event)
        data = {
            "doc": doc.as_dict(),
            "event": event
        }
        valid_triggers = []
        for trigger in triggers:
            node_data = frappe.parse_json(trigger.data)
            updated_field = node_data.get("updated_field")
            if updated_field and (event == "on_update" or event == "on_update_after_submit"):
                doc_before_update = doc.get_doc_before_save()
                if not doc_before_update:
                    continue
                if doc.get(updated_field) != doc_before_update.get(updated_field):
                    valid_triggers.append(trigger)
            else:
                valid_triggers.append(trigger)
        created_workflow_ids = create_tasks_from_triggers(valid_triggers, data)
        payload = {
            'doc_name': doc.name,
            'doc_type': doc.doctype,
            'workflows_ids': created_workflow_ids,
            'triggers_ids': [t.id for t in valid_triggers],
        }
        if event == "after_insert":
            frappe.enqueue(
                'nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.triggers.on_doctype_events.create_after_email_insert_tasks',
                queue="long",
                is_async=True,
                **payload
            )
            # create_after_email_insert_tasks(**payload)
    except Exception as e:
        error(str(e))
        error(traceback.format_exc().split('\n'))

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/triggers/on_manual_trigger.py
import json

import frappe
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.funnel_task import (
    create_new_task,
    queue_execute_pending_tasks,
    set_funnel_user,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.can_execute_funnel import can_execute
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_action_nodes import (
    get_action_nodes_from_trigger,
)


# nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.triggers.on_manual_trigger.trigger
@frappe.whitelist()
def trigger(trigger_name, doctype, doctype_name, variables=None):
    if variables is None:
        variables = {}

    trigger_doc = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Definition", trigger_name)
    actions = get_action_nodes_from_trigger(trigger_doc.id)
    published_funnel = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Published", trigger_doc.parent)

    if not can_execute(published_funnel.funnel):
        raise frappe.PermissionError(
            frappe._("You do not have permission to access this resource.")
        )

    user_id = frappe.session.user
    set_funnel_user()

    doc = frappe.get_doc(doctype, doctype_name)
    variable_string = json.dumps(
        {
            "doc": doc.as_dict(),
            "event": "manual_trigger",
            "trigger_user_id": user_id,
            **variables,
        },
        default=str,
    )

    log_doc = frappe.get_doc(
        {
            "doctype": "Funnel Manual Execution Logs",
            "time_and_date": frappe.utils.now_datetime(),
            "funnel_definition": trigger_name,
            "variables": variable_string,
            "funnel": published_funnel.name,
        }
    )
    log_doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)

    workflow = frappe.get_doc(
        {
            "doctype": "Funnel Workflow",
            "funnel": trigger_doc.parent,
            "variables": variable_string,
            "priority": 2,
        }
    )
    workflow.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    workflow.reload()
    for action_node in actions:
        create_new_task(
            workflow.name,
            action_node.id,
            trigger_doc.id,
            "",
            task_info={"trigger": "manual", "doctype": doctype, "name": doctype_name},
        )
    queue_execute_pending_tasks()
    return workflow

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/triggers/on_submit_assistant_trigger.py
import json
import uuid
import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.funnel_task import (
    queue_execute_pending_tasks,
    set_funnel_user,
    create_new_task,
)

# nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.triggers.on_submit_assistant_trigger.trigger
@frappe.whitelist()
def trigger(trigger_name, variables=None):
    if variables is None:
        variables = {}
    else:
        variables = json.loads(variables)

    user_id = frappe.session.user

    set_funnel_user()

    trigger_doc = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Definition", trigger_name)
    actions = get_action_nodes_from_trigger(trigger_doc.id)
    session_id = str(uuid.uuid4())

    variable_string = json.dumps(
        {
            "event": "chatnext_trigger",
            "trigger_user_id": user_id,
            "session_id": session_id,
            "user_id": user_id,
            **variables,
        },
        default=str,
    )

    workflow = frappe.get_doc(
        {
            "doctype": "Funnel Workflow",
            "funnel": trigger_doc.parent,
            "variables": variable_string,
        }
    )
    workflow.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    workflow.reload()

    conversation = frappe.get_doc(
        {
            "doctype": "CNA Conversation",
            "status": "default",
            "user": user_id,
            "session": session_id,
            "id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
            "author": "Bot",
            "message_type": "Loader",
            "message": "Fetching data...",
        }
    )
    conversation.insert(ignore_permissions=True)

    for action_node in actions:
        create_new_task(workflow.name, action_node.id, trigger_doc.id, "")

    queue_execute_pending_tasks()

    return {"session": session_id}

import json
import uuid

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.funnel_task import (
    set_funnel_user,
    queue_execute_pending_tasks,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import create_new_task

)


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_trigger_list():
    values = {"doctype": "CN PW Whatsapp Message"}

    # TODO reduce un necessary json extract
    nodes = frappe.db.sql(
        """
            SELECT *, JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`data`, '$.nameOfAction')) as name_of_action
            FROM `tabFunnel Definition`
            WHERE `element_type` = 'Trigger'
            AND parenttype = 'Funnel Published'
            AND parent IN (
                SELECT name
                FROM `tabFunnel Published`
                WHERE published = 1
            )
            AND type = 'personal_whatsapp_message_action_trigger'
        """,
        values=values,
        as_dict=1,
    )
    return nodes


# nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.triggers.personal_whatsapp_message_action_trigger.trigger
@frappe.whitelist()
def trigger(
    definition_name,
    docname,
    variables=None,
):
    user_id = frappe.session.user
    set_funnel_user()

    if variables is None:
        variables = {}

    if type(variables) is str:
        variables = json.loads(variables)

    session_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    trigger_doc = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Definition", definition_name)
    published_funnel = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Published", trigger_doc.parent)
    definition_data = json.loads(trigger_doc.data)
    doctype = "CN PW Whatsapp Message"
    doc = frappe.get_doc(doctype, docname)
    contact = doc.user_id
    contact_deatils = frappe.get_doc("CN PW Chat", contact)
    group = doc.group_id
    group_details = frappe.get_doc("CN PW Chat", group)
    variables["doc"] = doc.as_dict()
    variables["contact"] = contact_deatils.as_dict()
    variables["group"] = group_details.as_dict()
    if definition_data.get("open_chatnext_assistant") == 1:
        variables["session_id"] = session_id
        conversation = frappe.get_doc(
            {
                "doctype": "CNA Conversation",
                "status": "default",
                "user": user_id,
                "session": session_id,
                "id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
                "author": "Bot",
                "message_type": "Loader",
                "message": "Fetching data...",
            }
        )
        conversation.insert(ignore_permissions=True)

    variables = {
        "event": "chatnext_trigger",
        "user_id": user_id,
        "trigger_user_id": user_id,
        **variables,
    }

    workflow = frappe.get_doc(
        {
            "doctype": "Funnel Workflow",
            "funnel": trigger_doc.parent,
            "variables": json.dumps(variables, default=str),
        }
    )
    workflow.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    workflow.reload()
    actions = get_action_nodes_from_trigger(trigger_doc.id)

    for action_node in actions:
        create_new_task(workflow.name, action_node.id, trigger_doc.id, "")
    if len(actions):
        queue_execute_pending_tasks()
    if definition_data.get("open_chatnext_assistant") == 1:
        return {"session_id": session_id}
    return {}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/triggers/personal_whatsapp_trigger.py
import json
import traceback

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import task_states
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.awaiting_actions.personal_whatsapp_ai_qa_agent import (
    personal_whatsapp_ai_qa_agent,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.awaiting_actions.personal_whatsapp_text_question import (
    personal_whatsapp_text_question,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.funnel_task import queue_execute_pending_tasks
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import (
    create_new_task,
    update_task_status,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_action_nodes import (
    get_action_nodes_from_trigger,
    get_action_node_from_id,
)
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.logger import debug
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.update_workflow import update_workflow


def get_all_whatsapp_triggers():
    values = {}
    nodes = frappe.db.sql(
        """
        SELECT *
        FROM `tabFunnel Definition`
        WHERE type = 'personal_whatsapp_trigger'
        and parenttype = 'Funnel Published'
        and parent in (
            SELECT name
            FROM `tabFunnel Published`
            WHERE published = 1
        )
    """,
        values=values,
        as_dict=1,
    )
    return nodes


def get_previous_message_count(sender_id):
    values = {
        "sender_id": sender_id,
    }
    nodes = frappe.db.sql(
        """
         SELECT
                count(*)
            FROM
                `tabCN PW Whatsapp Message`
            WHERE `user_id` = %(contact)s or `group_id` = %(contact)s
        """,
        values=values,
        as_dict=1,
    )
    if len(nodes) == 0:
        return 0
    return nodes[0].get("count(*)") or 0


def personal_whatsapp_trigger(whatsapp_message):
    triggers = get_all_whatsapp_triggers()
    for trigger in triggers:
        trigger_data = json.loads(trigger.data)
        trigger_on_any_message = trigger_data.get("trigger_on_any_message")
        trigger_on_first_message_only = trigger_data.get("trigger_on_first_message")
        trigger_on_message_type = trigger_data.get("message_type")

        if trigger_on_message_type == "personal":
            if whatsapp_message.conversation_type.lower() != "personal":
                return
        if trigger_on_message_type == "group":
            if whatsapp_message.conversation_type.lower() != "group":
                return
        can_create_task = False
        if trigger_on_any_message:
            if trigger_on_first_message_only:
                total_message_count = get_previous_message_count(
                    whatsapp_message.group_id
                    if whatsapp_message.conversation_type == "Group"
                    else whatsapp_message.user_id
                )
                if total_message_count == 1:
                    can_create_task = True
            else:
                can_create_task = True
        else:
            trigger_on_specific_message = trigger_data.get(
                "trigger_on_specific_message"
            )
            trigger_message = trigger_data.get("trigger_message") or None

            message_body = whatsapp_message.body or ""
            can_match_trigger_text = (
                trigger_on_specific_message and message_body and trigger_message
            )
            if (
                can_match_trigger_text
                and str(message_body).lower() == str(trigger_message).lower()
            ):
                can_create_task = True

        if can_create_task:
            data = {
                "doc": whatsapp_message.as_dict(),
                "event": "after_insert",
                "session_id": whatsapp_message.session,
                "whatsapp_id": whatsapp_message.user_id,
                "group_id": (
                    whatsapp_message.group_id
                    if whatsapp_message.conversation_type == "Group"
                    else None
                ),
                "message_id": whatsapp_message.message_id,
                "last_message": whatsapp_message.body or "",
                "sender_id": (
                    whatsapp_message.group_id
                    if whatsapp_message.conversation_type == "Group"
                    else whatsapp_message.user_id
                ),
            }
            workflow = frappe.get_doc(
                {
                    "doctype": "Funnel Workflow",
                    "funnel": trigger.parent,
                    "variables": json.dumps(data, indent=2, default=str),
                }
            )
            workflow.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
            actions = get_action_nodes_from_trigger(trigger.id)
            for action_node in actions:
                create_new_task(workflow.name, action_node.id, trigger.id, "")
        # if len(actions):
        #     # set whatsapp status to marked as read
        #     wa_doc = frappe.get_doc("CN PW Whatsapp Message", whatsapp_message.name)
        #     # wa_doc.mark_as_seen()
    if len(triggers):
        queue_execute_pending_tasks()


def get_awaiting_personal_whatsapp_tasks(sender_id, order_by="creation", desc=1):
    values = {
        "sender_id": sender_id,
        "execution_status": task_states.AWAITING_TRIGGER,
        "order_by": order_by,
        "desc": "desc" if desc else "",
    }
    nodes = frappe.db.sql(
        """
      SELECT
            *
        FROM
            `tabFunnel Task`
        WHERE
            execution_status =  %(execution_status)s and
            workflow in (
                SELECT
                    name
                FROM
                    `tabFunnel Workflow`
                WHERE
                    JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`variables`, '$.sender_id')) = %(sender_id)s
            )
        ORDER BY
            %(order_by)s %(desc)s
    """,
        values=values,
        as_dict=1,
    )
    return nodes


def trigger_or_resolve_personal_whatsapp_task(whatsapp_message):
    sender_id = (
        whatsapp_message.group_id
        if whatsapp_message.conversation_type == "Group"
        else whatsapp_message.user_id
    )
    awaiting_tasks = get_awaiting_personal_whatsapp_tasks(sender_id)
    is_awaiting_task_executed = False
    for awaiting_task in awaiting_tasks:
        if is_awaiting_task_executed:
            break
        try:
            workflow_name = awaiting_task.workflow
            workflow_doc = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Workflow", workflow_name)
            variables = frappe.parse_json(workflow_doc.variables)

            action_node = get_action_node_from_id(awaiting_task.target_node_id)[0]
            if awaiting_task["target_node_name"] == "personal_whatsapp_text_question":
                personal_whatsapp_text_question(
                    action_node, variables, awaiting_task, whatsapp_message
                )
                is_awaiting_task_executed = True
            elif awaiting_task["target_node_name"] == "personal_whatsapp_ai_qa_agent":
                personal_whatsapp_ai_qa_agent(
                    action_node, variables, awaiting_task, whatsapp_message
                )
                is_awaiting_task_executed = True

            # would not reach here if something went wrong
            update_workflow(workflow_name, variables)
        except Exception as e:
            exec_trace = str(traceback.format_exc()).split("\n")
            stack_trace = traceback.format_stack()
            return update_task_status(
                awaiting_task,
                task_states.FAILED,
                {
                    "error": str(e),
                    "exec_trace": exec_trace,
                    "stack_trace": stack_trace,
                },
            )

    if not is_awaiting_task_executed:
        personal_whatsapp_trigger(whatsapp_message)


def on_new_whatsapp_message(doc, event):
    if doc.type != "Incoming":
        return
    try:
        # personal_whatsapp_trigger(doc)
        trigger_or_resolve_personal_whatsapp_task(doc)
    except Exception as e:
        debug(str(e))
        print(e)

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/triggers/whatsapp_trigger.py
import json
import traceback

import frappe
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import node_names, task_states
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.awaiting_actions.on_whatsapp_reply import on_whatsapp_reply
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.awaiting_actions.whatsapp_question_ai_qa_agent import \
    whatsapp_question_ai_qa_agent
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.awaiting_actions.whatsapp_question_audio import whatsapp_question_audio
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.awaiting_actions.whatsapp_question_boolean import whatsapp_question_boolean
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.awaiting_actions.whatsapp_question_email import whatsapp_question_email
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.awaiting_actions.whatsapp_question_file import whatsapp_question_file
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.awaiting_actions.whatsapp_question_image import whatsapp_question_image
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.awaiting_actions.whatsapp_question_mobile import whatsapp_question_mobile
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.awaiting_actions.whatsapp_question_raw import whatsapp_question_raw
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.awaiting_actions.whatsapp_question_single_choice import \
    whatsapp_question_single_choice
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.awaiting_actions.whatsapp_question_video import whatsapp_question_video
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.funnel_task import queue_execute_pending_tasks, set_funnel_user
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import create_new_task, update_task_status

    get_action_nodes_from_trigger

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.update_workflow import update_workflow


def get_awaiting_whatsapp_tasks(whatsapp_number, order_by="creation", desc=1):
    values = {
        'whatsapp_number': whatsapp_number,
        "execution_status": task_states.AWAITING_TRIGGER,
        'order_by': order_by,
        'desc': "desc" if desc else "",
    }
    nodes = frappe.db.sql("""
      SELECT
            *
        FROM
            `tabFunnel Task`
        WHERE
            execution_status =  %(execution_status)s and
            workflow in (
                SELECT
                    name
                FROM
                    `tabFunnel Workflow`
                WHERE
                    JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(`variables`, '$.whatsapp_number')) = %(whatsapp_number)s
            )
        ORDER BY
            %(order_by)s %(desc)s
    """, values=values, as_dict=1)
    return nodes


def get_all_whatsapp_triggers():
    values = {}
    nodes = frappe.db.sql("""
        SELECT *
        FROM `tabFunnel Definition`
        WHERE type = 'whatsapp_trigger'
        and parenttype = 'Funnel Published'
        and parent in (
            SELECT name
            FROM `tabFunnel Published`
            WHERE published = 1
        )
    """, values=values, as_dict=1)
    return nodes


def get_previous_message_count(contact):
    values = {
        'contact': contact,
    }
    nodes = frappe.db.sql("""
     SELECT
            count(*)
        FROM
            `tabWhatsApp Message`
        WHERE `from` = %(contact)s or `to` = %(contact)s
    """, values=values, as_dict=1)
    if len(nodes) == 0:
        return 0
    return nodes[0].get("count(*)") or 0


def whatsapp_trigger(whatsapp_message, contact_name, whatsapp_number):
    triggers = get_all_whatsapp_triggers()
    for trigger in triggers:
        trigger_data = json.loads(trigger.data)
        if trigger_data.get("container"):
            trigger_data = {
                **trigger_data,
                **trigger_data.get("container")
            }
        trigger_on_any_message = trigger_data.get("trigger_on_any_message")
        trigger_on_first_message_only = trigger_data.get("trigger_on_first_message_only")

        can_create_task = False
        if trigger_on_any_message:
            if trigger_on_first_message_only:
                total_message_count = get_previous_message_count(contact_name)
                if total_message_count == 1:
                    can_create_task = True
            else:
                can_create_task = True
        else:
            trigger_on_text_match = trigger_data.get("trigger_on_text_match")
            trigger_text = trigger_data.get("trigger_text") or None

            message_body = whatsapp_message.message_body or ""
            can_match_trigger_text = trigger_on_text_match and message_body and trigger_text
            if can_match_trigger_text and str(message_body).lower() == str(trigger_text).lower():
                can_create_task = True

        if can_create_task:
            data = {
                "doc": whatsapp_message.as_dict(),
                "event": "after_insert",
                "contact_name": contact_name,
                "whatsapp_number": whatsapp_number,
                "last_whatsapp_message": whatsapp_message.message_body or ""
            }
            workflow = frappe.get_doc({
                "doctype": "Funnel Workflow",
                "funnel": trigger.parent,
                "variables": json.dumps(data, indent=2, default=str)
            })
            workflow.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
            actions = get_action_nodes_from_trigger(trigger.id)
            for action_node in actions:
                create_new_task(workflow.name, action_node.id, trigger.id, "")
            if len(actions):
                # set whatsapp status to marked as read
                wa_doc = frappe.get_doc("WhatsApp Message", whatsapp_message.name)
                wa_doc.mark_as_seen()
        # if len(get_awaiting_whatsapp_tasks(contact_name)) != 0:
        #     break
    if len(triggers):
        queue_execute_pending_tasks()


whatsapp_awaiting_nodes = [
    node_names.on_whatsapp_reply,
    node_names.whatsapp_question_single_choice,
    node_names.whatsapp_question_boolean,
    node_names.whatsapp_question_mobile,
    node_names.whatsapp_question_raw,
    node_names.whatsapp_question_email,
    node_names.whatsapp_question_file,
    node_names.whatsapp_question_video,
    node_names.whatsapp_question_image,
    node_names.whatsapp_question_audio,
    node_names.whatsapp_question_ai_qa_agent,
]


def resolve_awaiting_whatsapp_tasks(whatsapp_message, contact_name):
    contact_doc = frappe.get_doc("Contact", contact_name)
    if not contact_doc:
        return

    whatsapp_number = contact_doc.get("whatsapp_id")
    if not whatsapp_number:
        return

    tasks = get_awaiting_whatsapp_tasks(whatsapp_number, desc=0)
    if len(tasks):
        # TODO: ?? execute a single task | last create task
        for task in tasks:
            # this would execute only once
            no_matched_action = False
            try:
                action_node = get_action_node_from_id(task.target_node_id)[0]

                # check if chatbot enabled and need to proceed
                funnel_node = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Node", action_node.type)
                if funnel_node.get("is_chatbot_node"):
                    if contact_doc.chatbot_disabled:
                        # mark task as cancelled
                        update_task_status(task, task_states.CANCELLED, {
                            "info": "Chatbot disabled for this contact"
                        })
                        continue

                if action_node.type in whatsapp_awaiting_nodes:
                    # set whatsapp status to marked as read
                    wa_doc = frappe.get_doc("WhatsApp Message", whatsapp_message.name)
                    wa_doc.mark_as_seen()

                workflow_name = task.workflow
                workflow_doc = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Workflow", workflow_name)
                variables = frappe.parse_json(workflow_doc.variables)
                variables['contact_name'] = contact_name

                if action_node.type == node_names.on_whatsapp_reply:
                    on_whatsapp_reply(action_node, task, whatsapp_message.as_dict(), variables)
                elif action_node.type == node_names.whatsapp_question_single_choice:
                    whatsapp_question_single_choice(action_node, task, whatsapp_message.as_dict(), variables)
                elif action_node.type == node_names.whatsapp_question_boolean:
                    whatsapp_question_boolean(action_node, task, whatsapp_message.as_dict(), variables)
                elif action_node.type == node_names.whatsapp_question_mobile:
                    whatsapp_question_mobile(action_node, task, whatsapp_message.as_dict(), variables)
                elif action_node.type == node_names.whatsapp_question_raw:
                    whatsapp_question_raw(action_node, task, whatsapp_message.as_dict(), variables)
                elif action_node.type == node_names.whatsapp_question_email:
                    whatsapp_question_email(action_node, task, whatsapp_message.as_dict(), variables)
                elif action_node.type == node_names.whatsapp_question_file:
                    whatsapp_question_file(action_node, task, whatsapp_message.as_dict(), variables)
                elif action_node.type == node_names.whatsapp_question_video:
                    whatsapp_question_video(action_node, task, whatsapp_message.as_dict(), variables)
                elif action_node.type == node_names.whatsapp_question_image:
                    whatsapp_question_image(action_node, task, whatsapp_message.as_dict(), variables)
                elif action_node.type == node_names.whatsapp_question_audio:
                    whatsapp_question_audio(action_node, task, whatsapp_message.as_dict(), variables)
                elif action_node.type == node_names.whatsapp_question_ai_qa_agent:
                    whatsapp_question_ai_qa_agent(action_node, task, whatsapp_message.as_dict(), variables)
                else:
                    no_matched_action = True

                # would not reach here if something went wrong
                update_workflow(workflow_name, variables)
            except Exception as e:
                exec_trace = traceback.format_exc()
                stack_trace = traceback.format_stack()
                update_task_status(task, task_states.FAILED, {
                    "error": str(e),
                    "exec_trace": exec_trace,
                    "stack_trace": stack_trace
                })
            if not no_matched_action:
                break
    else:
        # check if chatbot enabled and need to proceed
        if contact_doc.chatbot_disabled:
            return
        whatsapp_trigger(whatsapp_message, contact_name, whatsapp_number)


def on_new_whatsapp_message_created(doc, event):
    # return if outgoing message
    if doc.get("type") != "Incoming":
        return

    contact_name = doc.get("from")
    if not contact_name:
        return

    try:
        set_funnel_user()
        resolve_awaiting_whatsapp_tasks(doc, contact_name)
    except Exception as e:
        debug(str(e))

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/utils/add_chatnext_assistant_message.py
import uuid

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.funnel_task import set_funnel_user


def add_bot_message(
    session_id: str, message: str, user_id: str, status: str = "default"
):
    set_funnel_user()

    if not frappe.db.exists(
        "CNA Conversation", {"session": session_id, "user": user_id}
    ):
        raise frappe.ValidationError("Conversation not found")

    # create conversation
    conversation = frappe.get_doc(
        {
            "doctype": "CNA Conversation",
            "status": status,
            "user": user_id,
            "session": session_id,
            "id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
            "author": "Bot",
            "message_type": "Text",
            "message": message,
        }
    )

    conversation.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    frappe.db.commit()

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/utils/auto_approval_corn_for_todo.py
# your_app/auto_approval.py
import frappe
from frappe.utils import getdate, nowdate
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.awaiting_actions.chatnext_dynamic_multi_actions import action_select_handler

@frappe.whitelist()
def auto_process_todos():
    today = getdate(nowdate())
    todos = frappe.get_all(
        "ToDo",
        fields=["name", "custom_auto_action_type"],
        filters=[
            ["custom_auto_approvereject_applicable", "=", 1],
            ["status", "not in", ["Closed", "Cancelled"]],
            ["date", "<=", today],
        ],
        limit=10000,
    )
    user_id = "Administrator"
    for t in todos:
        action = (t.get("custom_auto_action_type") or "").strip().lower()
        if not action:
            continue
        todo_doc = frappe.get_doc("ToDo", t["name"])
        try:
            action_select_handler(todo_doc, action, user_id, {})
        except Exception:
            frappe.log_error("Auto approval failed", t["name"])

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/utils/can_execute_funnel.py
import frappe
from nextai.utils import is_admin_user


@frappe.whitelist()
def can_execute(funnel):
    user_id = frappe.session.user
    if is_admin_user():
        return True

    funnel_access = frappe.get_doc("Funnel", funnel).funnel_access

    filtered_access_list = [access for access in funnel_access if access.can_execute]

    if len(filtered_access_list):
        roles = frappe.get_roles(user_id)

        for access in filtered_access_list:
            if access.role in roles:
                return True

    return False

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/utils/create_tasks_from_triggers.py
import json

import frappe
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.funnel_task import queue_execute_pending_tasks
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.create_update_task import create_new_task
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.get_action_nodes import get_action_nodes_from_trigger


def create_tasks_from_triggers(triggers, data=None):
    if data is None:
        data = {}
    if len(triggers):
        workflows = []
        for trigger in triggers:
            workflow = frappe.get_doc({
                "doctype": "Funnel Workflow",
                "funnel": trigger.parent,
                "variables": json.dumps(data, default=str)
            })
            workflow.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
            actions = get_action_nodes_from_trigger(trigger.id)
            for action_node in actions:
                create_new_task(workflow.name, action_node.id, trigger.id, "")
            workflows.append(workflow.name)
        queue_execute_pending_tasks()
        return workflows
    return []

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/utils/create_update_task.py
import json
from datetime import datetime

import frappe
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import task_states


def create_new_task(
    workflow_id,
    target_node_id,  # TODO: remove usage
    trigger_node_id,
    parent_task_name,
    after=datetime.now(),
    task_info=None,
    status=task_states.PENDING,
    target_node_name=None,  # TODO: remove usage
    target_node=None,
):
    if not target_node:
        target_node = frappe.get_doc("Funnel Definition", {"id": target_node_id})

    if not target_node_id:
        target_node_id = target_node.get("id")

    if not target_node_name:
        target_node_name = target_node.get("type")

    target_node_data = frappe.parse_json(target_node.data)
    execute_immediately = target_node_data.get("skip_queue_and_execute_immediately")

    task = json.loads(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "doctype": "Funnel Task",
                "workflow": workflow_id,
                "trigger_after": str(after),
                "target_node_id": target_node_id,
                "trigger_node_id": trigger_node_id,
                "execution_status": status,
                "target_node_name": target_node_name,
                "parent_task": parent_task_name,
                "info": json.dumps(
                    {
                        "task_was_queued": not execute_immediately,
                        **(task_info if task_info else {}),
                    },
                    default=str,
                    indent=6,
                ),
            },
            default=str,
        )
    )
    task_doc = frappe.get_doc(task)
    task_doc.flags.ignore_links = True
    task_doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    frappe.db.commit()


def update_task_status(task, status, result=None, update_data=None):
    task = frappe.get_doc(
        "Funnel Task", task if isinstance(task, str) else task.get("name")
    )
    task.flags.ignore_links = True
    task.execution_status = status
    if result:
        task.result = json.dumps(result, default=str, indent=4)
    if update_data:
        task.update(update_data)
    task.save(ignore_permissions=True)
    frappe.db.commit()
    return task

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/utils/executable_trigger_nodes.py
from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.can_execute_funnel import can_execute
from nextai.utils import is_admin_user


def get_executable_trigger_nodes(nodes):
    if is_admin_user():
        return nodes

    executable_trigger_nodes = []
    for node in nodes:
        # TODO: optimize this
        if can_execute(node.funnel_name):
            executable_trigger_nodes.append(node)

    return executable_trigger_nodes

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/utils/extract_data_from_text.py
from nextai.config.api import HOST_NAME
from nextai.utils import send_authorized_core_request


def extract_data_from_text(text, trigger_text, data_type):
    messages = [
        {
            "role": "system",
            "content": "you have to create prefill data for a form from a a text provided which is returned by user,"
            + "when user sends that text a command is triggered other than the trigger associated part from the"
            + "text can be use to extract data, you would get the text, data structure and the text by user,"
            + "all the fields are optional, if no matched data is found for certain keys then ignore those keys",
        },
        {
            "role": "user",
            "content": f"""trigger text:
{trigger_text}""",
        },
        {
            "role": "user",
            "content": f"""text:
{text}""",
        },
        {
            "role": "user",
            "content": f"""data structure:
{data_type}""",
        },
        {
            "role": "user",
            "content": "return a single json object maintaining the data structure, "
            + "if no matched data found return empty object",
        },
    ]

    payload = {
        "messages": messages,
    }
    headers = {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        "origin": HOST_NAME,
    }

    response = send_authorized_core_request(
        payload,
        "POST",
        "chatnext_core.clients.ai_client.json_answer",
        headers,
    ).json()
    return response["message"]

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/utils/filter.py
def match_filter(filter_data, text):
    filter_type = filter_data.get("filterType")
    filter_value = filter_data.get("filterValue")
    passed_value = text
    if filter_type == "regex":
        import re
        value = filter_value
        if not value:
            return False
        return re.match(value, passed_value)
    if filter_type == "contains":
        value = filter_value
        if not value:
            return False
        return value in passed_value
    if filter_type == "equals":
        value = filter_value
        if not value:
            return False
        return value == passed_value
    if filter_type == "notEquals":
        value = filter_value
        if not value:
            return False
        return value != passed_value
    if filter_type == "set":
        if not passed_value:
            return False
        return True
    if filter_type == "notSet":
        if passed_value:
            return False
        return True
    return False


def match_filters(filters_data, text):
    if not filters_data:
        return False
    for filter_data in filters_data:
        if not match_filter(filter_data, text):
            return False
    return True

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/utils/find_best_text_match.py
import json

from nextai.config.api import HOST_NAME
from nextai.utils import send_authorized_core_request


def find_best_ai_text_match(options: list[str], text: str):
    text = text.strip().lower()
    options = [option.strip().lower() for option in options]
    options_string = "\n".join(options)
    messages = [
        {
            "role": "system",
            "content": "you have to find the best match for the given text from the provided options. "
            + "you have match with not only the text but also the intent. "
            + "you have to return the best match and the percentage of similarity. "
            + "if you cannot find any match return 0 as percentage and empty string as option.",
        },
        {
            "role": "user",
            "content": f"""text:
{text}

options:
{options_string}""",
        },
        {
            "role": "user",
            "content": 'return a single json object with the keys "percentage" (float number) and "option" (string)',
        },
    ]

    payload = {
        "messages": messages,
    }
    headers = {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        "origin": HOST_NAME,
    }

    response = send_authorized_core_request(
        payload,
        "POST",
        "chatnext_core.clients.ai_client.json_answer",
        headers,
    ).json()

    if not response.get("message"):
        raise Exception(json.dumps(response))

    reply = response["message"]
    match_data = json.loads(reply)

    matched_option = match_data.get("option")
    matched_percentage = match_data.get("percentage")

    if matched_percentage > 20:
        return matched_option
    else:
        return None


def find_best_text_match(options: list[str], text: str):
    text = text.strip().lower()
    options = [option.strip().lower() for option in options]

    best_matches = []
    for option in options:
        if option == text:
            return option

    for option in options:
        if option in text:
            best_matches.append(option)
    if len(best_matches) == 1:
        return best_matches[0]

    if len(best_matches) == 0:
        for option in options:
            if text in option:
                best_matches.append(option)
        if len(best_matches) == 1:
            return best_matches[0]

    return find_best_ai_text_match(options, text)

import frappe

ON_ERROR_HANDLE_NAME = "$__on__error__$"


def get_next_action_nodes(action_node_id):
    return get_next_action_nodes_with_source_handle_id(action_node_id, 'output')


def get_next_action_nodes_with_source_handle_id(action_node_id, handle_id):
    values = {"id": action_node_id, "handle_id": handle_id}
    nodes = frappe.db.sql(
        """
        select * from `tabFunnel Definition`
        where element_type = 'Action'
        and parenttype = 'Funnel Published'
        and id in (
            select target
            from `tabFunnel Definition`
            WHERE `element_type` = 'edge'
            and sourcehandle = %(handle_id)s
            and parenttype = 'Funnel Published'
            and source in (
                SELECT id
                FROM `tabFunnel Definition`
                WHERE `element_type` = 'Action'
                and parenttype = 'Funnel Published'
                and  id = %(id)s
            )
        );
        """,
        values=values,
        as_dict=1,
    )
    return nodes


def get_action_nodes_from_trigger(trigger_id):
    values = {
        "trigger_id": trigger_id,
    }
    # TODO: update for multiple triggers targeting one action
    nodes = frappe.db.sql(
        """
        select * from `tabFunnel Definition`
        where element_type = 'Action'
        and parenttype = 'Funnel Published'
        and id in (
            select target
            from `tabFunnel Definition`
            WHERE `element_type` = 'edge'
            and sourcehandle = 'output'
            and source = %(trigger_id)s
            and parenttype = 'Funnel Published'
        );
    """,
        values=values,
        as_dict=1,
    )
    return nodes

import frappe


def get_assigned_users(doctype, doc_name):
    assigned_users = frappe.get_all(
        "ToDo",
        fields=["allocated_to"],
        filters={
            "reference_type": doctype,
            "reference_name": doc_name,
            "status": ("=", "Open"),
        },
        pluck="allocated_to",
    )
    return list(set(assigned_users))

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/utils/get_associated_contacts.py
import frappe


def get_associated_contacts(doctype, doc_name):
    values = {
        'doctype': doctype,
        'doc_name': doc_name
    }

    contacts = frappe.db.sql("""
        select *
        from
        `tabDynamic Link` where
        link_doctype = %(doctype)s and parenttype = 'Contact' and link_name = %(doc_name)s
    """, values=values, as_dict=1)
    return [contact.parent for contact in contacts]

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/utils/get_fields.py
import frappe


def get_all_fields(doctype):
    all_fields = [{
        "label": "Status",
        "value": 'status',
        "type": 'Select'
    }]
    metadata = frappe.get_meta(doctype)
    fields = metadata.fields
    for field in fields:
        fieldname, label, fieldtype = field.fieldname, field.label, field.fieldtype
        if not label:
            continue
        if not fieldname:
            continue
        if fieldname == 'status':
            continue
        all_fields.append({
            "label": str(label),
            "value": str(fieldname),
            "type": fieldtype
        })
    return all_fields


def get_fields(doctype, value_prefix):
    fields = get_all_fields(doctype)
    all_fields = []
    for field in fields:
        all_fields.append({
            "label": str(doctype) + "." + str(field.get("label")),
            "value": value_prefix + str(field.get("value")),
            "type": field.get("type")
        })
    return all_fields

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/utils/get_pending_tasks.py
import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task import task_states


# priority can be 0, 1 or 2
def get_all_pending_tasks(order_by="creation", desc=0, priority=1):
    values = {
        "execution_status": task_states.PENDING,
        "order_by": order_by,
        "desc": "desc" if desc else "",
        "priority": priority,
    }
    tasks = frappe.db.sql(
        """
        SELECT *
        FROM `tabFunnel Task` AS ft
        WHERE
            execution_status = %(execution_status)s
            AND trigger_after <= NOW() + INTERVAL 1 SECOND
            AND EXISTS (
                SELECT 1
                FROM `tabFunnel Workflow` AS fw
                WHERE
                    fw.name = ft.workflow
                    AND (
                        fw.priority IS NULL
                        OR fw.priority = %(priority)s
                    )
            )
        ORDER BY
            %(order_by)s %(desc)s
    """,
        values=values,
        as_dict=1,
    )
    return tasks


def get_all_awaiting_tasks(order_by="creation", desc=0):
    values = {
        "execution_status": task_states.AWAITING_TRIGGER,
        "order_by": order_by,
        "desc": "desc" if desc else "",
    }
    tasks = frappe.db.sql(
        """
        SELECT
            *
        FROM
            `tabFunnel Task`
        WHERE
            execution_status = %(execution_status)s and
            trigger_after <= now() + INTERVAL 1 SECOND
        ORDER BY
            %(order_by)s %(desc)s
    """,
        values=values,
        as_dict=1,
    )
    return tasks

import frappe

from nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel_task.utils.is_on_dev import is_on_dev

    if is_on_dev():
        print(*args)
    else:
        frappe.logger("funnel-info").info(frappe.as_json(args))

    if is_on_dev():
        print(*args)
    else:
        frappe.logger("funnel-debug").debug(frappe.as_json(args))

    if is_on_dev():

    else:
        frappe.logger("funnel-warning").warning(frappe.as_json(args))

    else:
        frappe.logger("funnel-error").error(frappe.as_json(args))

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/utils/submit_form_data.py
import frappe
import json
from frappe.utils import now

def log_form_submission(reference_doctype, reference_docname, reference_form, form_data, submitted_by=None):
    try:
        if not all([reference_doctype, reference_docname, form_data]):
            raise ValueError("Missing required values: reference_doctype, reference_docname, reference_form, or form_data")

        submitted_by = submitted_by or frappe.session.user
        # Ensure form_data is stored as a JSON string
        form_data_json = json.dumps(form_data, indent=2) if isinstance(form_data, dict) else str(form_data)
        get_doc=frappe.get_doc(reference_doctype, reference_docname)
        doc = frappe.get_doc({
            "doctype": "Submitted Forms",
            "reference_doctype": get_doc.doc_type,
            "reference_docname": get_doc.doc_name,
            "reference_form": reference_form,
            "submitted_by": submitted_by,
            "submitted_at": now(),
            "form_data_json": form_data_json
        })

        doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)

    except Exception as e:
        frappe.log_error(
            title="Error in log_form_submission",
            message=f"{str(e)}\n\n{frappe.get_traceback()}"
        )

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/utils/todo_utils.py
import frappe
from frappe.core.doctype.communication.email import make as create_email
from frappe.utils import add_days, now_datetime


EMPLOYEE_FIELDNAME_GUESSES = ("employee", "employee_id", "emp", "emp_id")
def get_employee_name_from_doc(doctype: str, docname: str) -> str | None:
    if not (doctype and docname):
        return None
    meta = frappe.get_meta(doctype)
    employee_field = next(
        (
            df.fieldname
            for df in meta.fields
            if df.fieldname in EMPLOYEE_FIELDNAME_GUESSES
            or (df.fieldtype == "Link" and df.options in ("Employee", "Employee Master"))
        ),
        None,
    )
    if employee_field:
        employee_id = frappe.db.get_value(doctype, docname, employee_field)
        if employee_id:
            for emp_dt in ("Employee", "Employee Master"):
                user_vals = frappe.db.get_value(emp_dt, employee_id, "employee_name")
                if isinstance(user_vals, (tuple, list)):
                    user_vals = next((u for u in user_vals if u), None)
                if user_vals:
                    return user_vals
    return docname


def create_approval_todo(reference_type, reference_name, log):
    try:
        if not all([reference_type, reference_name, log]):
            raise ValueError(
                "Missing required parameters: reference_type, reference_name, or log"
            )
        reference_doc = frappe.get_doc(reference_type, reference_name)
        discription_name= get_employee_name_from_doc(reference_doc.doc_type, reference_doc.doc_name)
        approval_days = getattr(log, "approval_timeline_in_days", None)
        todo_category = getattr(log, "todo_category", "Approval Matrix")
        before_approval_email_template = getattr(log, "before_approval_email_template", None)
        allow_revoke = getattr(log, "allow_revoke", 0)
        allow_sendback = getattr(log, "allow_sendback", 0)
        auto_approvereject_applicable = getattr(log, "auto_approvereject_applicable", 0)
        auto_action_type = getattr(log, "auto_action_type", None)
        custom_doctype_actions_with_form = getattr(log, "custom_doctype_actions_with_form", [])
        escalation_applicable = getattr(log, "escalation_applicable", 0)
        custom_escalation_user = None
        if escalation_applicable:
            custom_escalation_user = getattr(log, "custom_escalation_user", "")
        if allow_sendback:
            custom_doctype_actions = '["Approve", "Reject", "Send Back"]'
        else:
            custom_doctype_actions = '["Approve", "Reject"]'

        todo_fields = {
            "doctype": "ToDo",
            "status": "Open",
            "reference_type": reference_doc.doc_type,
            "reference_name": reference_doc.doc_name,
            "custom_doctype_actions": custom_doctype_actions,
            "custom_funnel_task": reference_doc.task,
            "custom_todo_type": todo_category,
            "custom_approval_type": "Approval Matrix",
            "assigned_by": frappe.session.user,
            "custom_doctype_actions_with_form": str(custom_doctype_actions_with_form),
            "custom_allow_revoke": allow_revoke,
            "custom_enable_auto_escalation": escalation_applicable,
            "custom_escalation_target": custom_escalation_user,
            "custom_auto_approvereject_applicable": auto_approvereject_applicable,
            "custom_auto_action_type": auto_action_type,
            "description": f"Approval required for {reference_doc.doc_type} {discription_name}",
            "date": add_days(now_datetime(), approval_days) if approval_days else now_datetime(),
        }

        if log.user:
            todo_fields["allocated_to"] = log.user
            target_users = [log.user]
        elif log.role:
            todo_fields["role"] = log.role
            user_roles = frappe.get_all(
                "Has Role",
                filters={"role": log.role, "parenttype": "user"},
                fields=["parent"],
            )
            target_users = [ur.parent for ur in user_roles]
        else:
            raise ValueError("Approval log missing both user and role")

        user_id = frappe.session.user
        frappe.session.user = "Administrator"
        for _target_user in target_users:
            already_shared = frappe.db.exists(
                "DocShare",
                {
                    "user": _target_user,
                    "share_doctype": reference_doc.doc_type,
                    "share_name": reference_doc.doc_name,
                },
            )
            if not already_shared:
                share_doc = frappe.new_doc("DocShare")
                share_doc.user = _target_user
                share_doc.share_doctype = reference_doc.doc_type
                share_doc.share_name = reference_doc.doc_name
                share_doc.read = 1
                share_doc.save(ignore_permissions=True)
        frappe.session.user = user_id

        frappe.enqueue(
            "frappe.desk.doctype.notification_log.notification_log.make_notification_logs",
            enqueue_after_commit=True,
            users=target_users,
            now=False,
            doc={
                "type": "Share",
                "document_type": reference_doc.doc_type,
                "subject": "Approval Required for "
                           + reference_doc.doc_type
                           + " "
                           + discription_name,
                "document_name": reference_doc.doc_name,
                "from_user": frappe.session.user,
            },
        )

        todo = frappe.get_doc(todo_fields)
        todo.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
        log.db_set("todo_reference", todo.name)

        if before_approval_email_template:
            rows = frappe.get_all(
                "User",
                filters={"name": ["in", target_users], "enabled": 1},
                fields=["name", "email"],
            )
            recipients = []
            for r in rows:
                cand = (r.get("email") or r.get("name") or "").strip()
                if cand and "@" in cand:
                    recipients.append(cand.lower())
            recipients = sorted(set(recipients))
            if recipients:
                template_doc = frappe.get_cached_doc("Email Template", before_approval_email_template)
                doc = frappe.get_doc(reference_doc.doc_type, reference_doc.doc_name)
                context = frappe._dict(
                    doc=doc,
                    log=log,
                    todo_name=todo.name,
                    doctype=reference_doc.doc_type,
                    name=reference_doc.doc_name,
                    recipients=recipients,
                )
                subject = frappe.render_template(template_doc.subject or "", context)
                content = frappe.render_template(template_doc.response or "", context)
                cur_user = frappe.session.user
                frappe.session.user = "Administrator"
                try:
                    create_email(
                        recipients=", ".join(recipients),
                        subject=subject,
                        content=content,
                        doctype=reference_doc.doc_type,
                        name=reference_doc.doc_name,
                        communication_medium="Email",
                        send_email=True,
                    )
                finally:
                    frappe.session.user = cur_user

        return todo.name

    except Exception as e:
        frappe.log_error(frappe.get_traceback(), "create_approval_todo")
        raise Exception(f"[ToDo Creation Failed] {str(e)}")

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/utils/update_workflow.py
import json

import frappe


# always keep the execution time as less possible no db or api calls after first line
def update_workflow(workflow_name, variables=None, status=None):
    if variables:
        prev_variables = frappe.db.get_value("Funnel Workflow", workflow_name, 'variables')
        updated_variables = {
            **frappe.parse_json(prev_variables or "{}"),
            **variables
        }
        variables_str = json.dumps(updated_variables, default=str)
        frappe.db.set_value("Funnel Workflow", workflow_name, "variables", variables_str)

    if status:
        frappe.db.set_value("Funnel Workflow", workflow_name, "status", status)

    if status or variables:
        frappe.db.commit()

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/doctype/funnel_task/utils/validate_input.py
import re


# Checks if the message is a numeric string or a number
def is_numeric_string(message):
    if isinstance(message, str):
        return message.isdigit()
    else:
        return isinstance(message, (int, float))


# Checks if the message is a valid email
def is_valid_email(email):
    email_pattern = r'^[\w\.-]+@[\w\.-]+\.\w+$'
    return re.match(email_pattern, email) is not None


# Function to validate input based on specified validation type
def is_valid_input(message, validation_type):
    switcher = {
        "number": is_numeric_string(message),
        "email": is_valid_email(message)
    }
    return switcher.get(validation_type, True)

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/funnel/page/funnel_builder/funnel_builder.js
const showFunnelBuilderDeniedDialog = () => {
  var modal = new frappe.ui.Dialog({
    title: __('Access Denied'),
    fields: [
      {
        fieldname: 'message',
        fieldtype: 'HTML',
        options: 'You do not have access to view this page.',
      },
    ],
    primary_action: function () {
      modal.hide()
    },
    primary_action_label: __('OK'),
  })
  modal.show()
}

frappe.pages['funnel-builder'].on_page_load = function (wrapper) {
  fetch('/api/resource/Funnel')
    .then((resp) => resp.json())
    .then((data) => {
      if (data?.data?.length) {
        const builder = document.createElement('funnel-builder')
        wrapper.appendChild(builder)
      } else {
        frappe.call({
          method: 'nextai.funnel.doctype.funnel.funnel.check_funnel_permission',
          callback: function (response) {
            if (response.message) {
              const builder = document.createElement('funnel-builder')
              wrapper.appendChild(builder)
            } else {
              showFunnelBuilderDeniedDialog()
            }
          },
        })
      }
    })
}
