# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/personal_whatsapp_integration/api/groups_and_contacts.py
from enum import Enum

import frappe

from nextai.personal_whatsapp_integration.doctype.cn_pw_whatsapp_session.cn_pw_whatsapp_session import (
    check_is_system_manager,
)
from nextai.personal_whatsapp_integration.doctype.utils import waha_api_dispatcher


class OrderBy(Enum):
    ALL = "All"
    CONTACTS = "Contacts"
    GROUPS = "Groups"
    UNANSWERED = "Un Answered"


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_list(
    session: str,
    offset: int = 0,
    limit: int = 10,
    search: str = None,
    order_by: str = OrderBy.ALL.value,
    is_archived: bool = False,
    create_new_groups: bool = False,
):
    user = frappe.session.user
    query_data = {
        "session": session,
        "user": user,
        "limit": limit,
        "offset": offset,
        "search": f"%{search}%" if search else None,
    }

    is_system_manager = check_is_system_manager(user)
    session_doc = frappe.get_doc("CN PW Whatsapp Session", session)
    full_access = any(
        child.user_assign == user and child.all_access
        for child in session_doc.session_assign_to
    )

    type_filter = ""
    if order_by == OrderBy.CONTACTS.value or create_new_groups:
        type_filter = "AND c.type = 'Contact'"
    elif order_by == OrderBy.GROUPS.value:
        type_filter = "AND c.type = 'Group'"
    elif order_by == OrderBy.UNANSWERED.value:
        type_filter = "AND uc.unread_count > 0"

    access_conditions = ""
    if not (is_system_manager or user == "Administrator" or full_access):
        access_conditions = "LEFT JOIN `tabCN PW Contact Access` ca ON ca.parent = c.name AND ca.user_assign = %(user)s"

    search_conditions = ""
    if search:
        search_conditions = (
            "AND (c.chat_id LIKE %(search)s OR c.chat_name LIKE %(search)s)"
        )
    archived_filter = ""
    if is_archived:
        archived_filter = "AND ar.session_name IS NOT NULL"
    else:
        archived_filter = ""

    query = f"""
        SELECT
            c.name,
            c.chat_id,
            c.chat_name,
            c.mark_as_read,
            c.profile_picture,
            c.type as type,
            COALESCE(uc.unread_count, 0) AS unread_count,
            lm.message_type AS last_message_type,
            lm.message_body AS last_message_body,
            lm.message_time AS last_message_time,
            lm.type AS last_message_category,
            IF(ar.session_name IS NOT NULL, TRUE, FALSE) AS is_archived
        FROM `tabCN PW Chat` c
        LEFT JOIN `tabCN PW Unread Count` uc
            ON uc.parent = c.name AND uc.session = %(session)s
        LEFT JOIN `tabCN PW Last Message` lm
            ON lm.parent = c.name AND lm.session = %(session)s
        LEFT JOIN `tabCN PW Archived` ar
            ON ar.parent = c.name AND ar.session_name = %(session)s
        {access_conditions}
        WHERE 1=1
            {"AND ca.user_assign = %(user)s" if access_conditions else ""}
            {search_conditions}
            {type_filter}
            {archived_filter}
        GROUP BY c.name
        ORDER BY lm.message_time DESC, c.modified DESC
        LIMIT %(limit)s OFFSET %(offset)s;
    """

    data = frappe.db.sql(query, query_data, as_dict=True)
    return data


def is_group_contact_access_available(chat_id: str, session: str):
    doctype = "CN PW Chat"
    user = frappe.session.user
    is_system_manager = check_is_system_manager(user)
    try:
        contact_doc = frappe.get_doc(doctype, {"chat_id": chat_id})
    except frappe.DoesNotExistError:
        return f"{doctype} with ID {chat_id} does not exist"

    chat_assign_field = "chat_assign_to"
    chat_access = any(
        child.user_assign == user for child in getattr(contact_doc, chat_assign_field)
    )
    try:
        session_doc = frappe.get_doc("CN PW Whatsapp Session", session)
    except frappe.DoesNotExistError:
        return f"Session {session} does not exist"

    session_has_access = any(
        child.user_assign == user for child in session_doc.session_assign_to
    )
    full_access = any(
        child.user_assign == user and child.all_access == True
        for child in session_doc.session_assign_to
    )
    if (
        is_system_manager
        or user == "Administrator"
        or (chat_access and session_has_access)
        or full_access
    ):
        return True
    return False


@frappe.whitelist()
def archive_user(chat_id: str, session: str):
    if not is_group_contact_access_available(chat_id, session):
        raise frappe.PermissionError("You are not allowed to archive this user")
    doctype_name = "CN PW Chat"
    chat_doc = frappe.get_doc(doctype_name, {"chat_id": chat_id})
    session_exists = any(
        child.session_name == session for child in chat_doc.archived_by
    )
    if session_exists:
        return "Session already exists in archived list"
    else:
        url = f"/api/{session}/chats/{chat_id}/archive"
        return waha_api_dispatcher("POST", url)


@frappe.whitelist()
def unarchive_user(chat_id: str, session: str):
    if not is_group_contact_access_available(chat_id, session):
        raise frappe.PermissionError("You are not allowed to un archive this user")
    doctype_name = "CN PW Chat"
    chat_doc = frappe.get_doc(doctype_name, chat_id)
    session_exists = any(
        child.session_name == session for child in chat_doc.archived_by
    )
    if session_exists:
        url = f"/api/{session}/chats/{chat_id}/unarchive"
        response = waha_api_dispatcher("POST", url)
        return response
    else:
        return "Session does not exists in archived list"


@frappe.whitelist()
def assign_user_to_contact(user: str, chat_id: str):
    doctype_name = "CN PW Chat"
    contact_doc = frappe.get_doc(doctype_name, chat_id)
    chat_assign_field = "chat_assign_to"
    user_data = frappe.db.get_value("User", user, ["full_name"], as_dict=True)
    user_exists = False
    for row in getattr(contact_doc, chat_assign_field):
        if row.user_assign == user:
            user_exists = True
            break
    if not user_exists:
        try:
            contact_doc.append(
                chat_assign_field,
                {"user_assign": user, "full_name": user_data["full_name"]},
            )
            contact_doc.save()
            frappe.db.commit()
        except Exception as e:
            return f"error assigning user to contact  {str(e)}"
        return f"User {user} has been assigned to {chat_id}"
    else:
        return f"User {user} has been already assigned to the contact {chat_id}"


@frappe.whitelist()
def un_assign_user_to_contact(user: str, chat_id: str):
    doctype_name = "CN PW Chat"
    contact_doc = frappe.get_doc(doctype_name, chat_id)
    chat_assign_field = "chat_assign_to"
    user_exists = False
    for row in getattr(contact_doc, chat_assign_field):
        if row.user_assign == user:
            user_exists = True
            break
    if not user_exists:
        return f"User Doesn't exist in contact: {chat_id}"
    else:
        try:
            removed = False
            for row in getattr(contact_doc, chat_assign_field):
                if row.user_assign == user:
                    contact_doc.remove(row)
                    removed = True
                    break
            if removed:
                contact_doc.save()
                frappe.db.commit()
                return f"User {user} has been removed from the chat Id : {chat_id}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"error assigning user to contact  {str(e)}"


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_detail(chat_id: str, session: str):
    doctype_name = "CN PW Chat"
    user = frappe.session.user
    is_system_manager = check_is_system_manager(user)
    session_doc = frappe.get_doc("CN PW Whatsapp Session", session)
    full_access = any(
        child.user_assign == user and child.all_access == True
        for child in session_doc.session_assign_to
    )
    try:
        contact_doc = frappe.get_doc(doctype_name, chat_id)
    except frappe.DoesNotExistError:
        return
    except Exception as e:
        frappe.log_error(message=str(e), title="Error in get_detail")
        return

    chat_assign_field = "chat_assign_to"

    if not hasattr(contact_doc, chat_assign_field):
        return

    chat_access = any(
        child.user_assign == user
        for child in getattr(contact_doc, chat_assign_field, [])
    )
    session_has_access = any(
        child.user_assign == user for child in session_doc.session_assign_to
    )
    if (
        is_system_manager
        or user == "Administrator"
        or (chat_access and session_has_access)
        or full_access
    ):
        return contact_doc
    else:
        return f"You don't have access {user} to this contact."


@frappe.whitelist()
def Mark_as_read_conditional(chat_id: str, mark_as_read: bool):
    doctype_name = "CN PW Chat"
    chat_doc = frappe.get_doc(doctype_name, {"chat_id": chat_id})
    chat_doc.mark_as_read = mark_as_read
    chat_doc.save()
    frappe.db.commit()

    return chat_doc

import json

import frappe
from frappe.model.document import Document

from nextai.api.webhook.personal_whatsapp import save_contact_by_session
from nextai.personal_whatsapp_integration.doctype.cn_pw_whatsapp_message.cn_pw_whatsapp_message import (
    format_whatsapp_group_id,
    format_whatsapp_id,
)
from nextai.personal_whatsapp_integration.doctype.cn_pw_whatsapp_session.cn_pw_whatsapp_session import (
    check_is_system_manager,
)
from nextai.personal_whatsapp_integration.doctype.utils import waha_api_dispatcher


class CNPWChat(Document):
    pass


@frappe.whitelist()
def create_whatsapp_group(group_name: str, group_members: str, session_id: str):
    url = f"/api/{session_id}/groups"

    participants = json.loads(group_members)
    new_participants = []
    for participant in participants:
        new_participants.append({"id": format_whatsapp_id(participant)})

    payload = json.dumps({"name": group_name, "participants": new_participants})

    response = waha_api_dispatcher("POST", url, payload)
    if response is not None:
        response = response.json()
    group_id = format_whatsapp_group_id(response["gid"]["user"])

    return {"result": group_id}


@frappe.whitelist()
def leave_whatsapp_group(group_id: str, session_id: str):
    user = frappe.session.user
    is_system_manager = check_is_system_manager(user)
    doctype_name = "CN PW Chat"
    group_doc = frappe.get_doc(doctype_name, {"chat_id": group_id})
    if (
        is_system_manager
        or user == "Administrator"
        or any(child.user_assign == user for child in group_doc.session_assign_to)
    ):
        response = waha_api_dispatcher(
            "POST", f"/api/{session_id}/groups/{group_id}/leave"
        )
        if response.ok:
            return {"result": response}
    else:
        return "you dont have permission to do this"


@frappe.whitelist()
def create_whatsapp_contact(user_id: str, session: str):
    if not frappe.db.exists("CN PW Chat", {"chat_id": user_id}):
        save_contact_by_session(user_id, session)
        return 1
    return "Contact Already Exists"


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_whatsapp_contact(user_id: str, session: str):
    url = f"/api/contacts/check-exists?phone={user_id}&session={session}"
    response = waha_api_dispatcher("GET", url)
    contact_data = response.json()
    if contact_data["numberExists"]:
        user_id = contact_data["chatId"]
        url = f"/api/contacts?contactId={user_id}&session={session}"
        response = waha_api_dispatcher("GET", url)
        user_data = response.json()
        return user_data
    else:
        return contact_data

# Copyright (c) 2024, Hybrowlabs Technologies and contributors

import json

import frappe
from frappe.model.document import Document

from nextai.personal_whatsapp_integration.doctype.cn_pw_whatsapp_message.cn_pw_whatsapp_message import (
    format_whatsapp_group_id,
    format_whatsapp_id,
)
from nextai.personal_whatsapp_integration.doctype.cn_pw_whatsapp_session.cn_pw_whatsapp_session import (
    check_is_system_manager,
)
from nextai.personal_whatsapp_integration.doctype.utils import waha_api_dispatcher


class CNPWWhatsappGroup(Document):
    pass


@frappe.whitelist()
def create_whatsapp_group(group_name: str, group_members: str, session_id: str):
    url = f"/api/{session_id}/groups"

    participants = json.loads(group_members)

    new_participants = []
    for participant in participants:
        new_participants.append({"id": format_whatsapp_id(participant)})

    payload = json.dumps({"name": group_name, "participants": new_participants})

    response = waha_api_dispatcher("POST", url, payload).json()
    group_id = format_whatsapp_group_id(response["gid"]["user"])

    return {"result": group_id}


@frappe.whitelist()
def leave_whatsapp_group(group_id: str, session_id: str):
    user = frappe.session.user
    is_system_manager = check_is_system_manager(user)
    doctype_name = "CN PW Whatsapp Group"
    group_doc = frappe.get_doc(doctype_name, group_id)
    if (
        is_system_manager
        or user == "Administrator"
        or any(child.user_assign == user for child in group_doc.session_assign_to)
    ):
        response = waha_api_dispatcher(
            "POST", f"/api/{session_id}/groups/{group_id}/leave"
        )
        if response.ok:
            return {"result": response}
    else:
        return "you dont have permission to do this"

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/personal_whatsapp_integration/doctype/cn_pw_whatsapp_message/cn_pw_whatsapp_message.py

import json
import random
import time
from datetime import datetime

import frappe
from frappe.model.document import Document
from frappe.utils import now

from nextai.api.webhook.personal_whatsapp import (
    save_contact_by_session,
    save_group,
    is_chat_available,
    update_unread_count,
    update_last_message,
)
from nextai.personal_whatsapp_integration.api.groups_and_contacts import (
    is_group_contact_access_available,
)
from nextai.personal_whatsapp_integration.doctype.utils import waha_api_dispatcher


def format_options(data):
    ids = data.split(",")
    option_list = []
    for id_ in ids:
        trimmed_option = id_.strip()
        option_list.append(trimmed_option)

    return option_list


def format_whatsapp_id(number_or_id):
    if "@" not in number_or_id:
        if len(number_or_id) == 10:
            number_or_id = "91" + number_or_id
        number_or_id = number_or_id + "@c.us"
    return number_or_id


def format_whatsapp_group_id(group_or_id):
    if "@" not in group_or_id:
        group_or_id = group_or_id + "@g.us"
    return group_or_id


class CNPWWhatsappMessage(Document):
    def validate(self):
        if self.message_id:
            existing_message_name = frappe.db.exists(
                "CN PW Whatsapp Message",
                {
                    "session": self.session,
                    "message_id": self.message_id,
                    "user_id": self.user_id,
                },
            )
            if existing_message_name and existing_message_name != self.name:
                raise frappe.ValidationError("Message already exists")

    def before_save(self):
        if self.group_id:
            if not is_chat_available(self.group_id):
                save_group(self.session, self.group_id)
            self.group_id = format_whatsapp_group_id(self.group_id)
        if self.user_id:
            if not is_chat_available(self.user_id):
                save_contact_by_session(self.user_id, self.session)
            self.user_id = format_whatsapp_id(self.user_id)

    @frappe.whitelist()
    def send(self):
        url = None
        data = None
        chat_id = self.group_id if self.conversation_type == "Group" else self.user_id
        if self.message_type == "Text":
            start_typing_url = "/api/startTyping"
            stop_typing_url = "/api/stopTyping"
            typing_payload = json.dumps(
                {
                    "chatId": chat_id,
                    "session": self.session,
                }
            )
            url = "/api/sendText"
            data = json.dumps(
                {
                    "chatId": chat_id,
                    "text": self.body,
                    "session": self.session,
                }
            )
            length_body = len(self.body)
            time_per_letter = random.uniform(0.15, 0.20)
            wait_time = length_body * time_per_letter
            waha_api_dispatcher("POST", start_typing_url, typing_payload)
            time.sleep(wait_time)
            waha_api_dispatcher("POST", stop_typing_url, typing_payload)

        elif self.message_type == "Poll":
            url = "/api/sendPoll"
            data = json.dumps(
                {
                    "chatId": chat_id,
                    "poll": {
                        "name": self.poll_name,
                        "options": format_options(self.options),
                        "multipleAnswers": self.multiple_answers,
                    },
                    "session": self.session_id,
                }
            )

        elif self.message_type == "Image":

            url = "/api/sendImage"
            data = json.dumps(
                {
                    "chatId": chat_id,
                    "file": {
                        "mimetype": self.mime_type,
                        "filename": self.file_name,
                        "url": self.media_url,
                    },
                    "caption": self.caption,
                    "session": self.session,
                }
            )

        elif self.message_type == "Video":

            url = "/api/sendVideo"
            data = json.dumps(
                {
                    "chatId": chat_id,
                    "file": {
                        "mimetype": self.mime_type,
                        "filename": self.file_name,
                        "url": self.media_url,
                    },
                    "caption": self.caption,
                    "session": self.session,
                }
            )

        elif self.message_type == "Audio":

            url = "/api/sendVoice"
            data = json.dumps(
                {
                    "chatId": chat_id,
                    "file": {
                        "mimetype": self.mime_type,
                        "url": self.media_url,
                    },
                    "session": self.session,
                }
            )

        elif self.message_type == "Document":

            url = "/api/sendFile"
            data = json.dumps(
                {
                    "chatId": chat_id,
                    "file": {
                        "mimetype": self.mime_type,
                        "filename": self.file_name,
                        "url": self.media_url,
                    },
                    "caption": self.caption,
                    "session": self.session,
                }
            )

        response = waha_api_dispatcher("POST", url, data).json()
        update_last_message(
            chat_id,
            self.session,
            self.message_type,
            "Outgoing",
            self.body if self.message_type == "Text" else None,
        )
        if not response.get("_data", {}).get("id", {}).get("id"):
            raise Exception(json.dumps(response))
        self.delete()
        return response["_data"]["id"]["id"]


def send_personal_whatsapp_message(chat_id: str, session_id: str, body: str):
    group_suffix = "@g.us"
    user_id = frappe.db.get_value(
        "CN PW Whatsapp Session", {"session_id": session_id}, "user_id"
    )
    message_data = {
        "session": session_id,
        "body": body,
        "user_id": (user_id if chat_id.endswith(group_suffix) else chat_id),
        "type": "Outgoing",
        "group_id": chat_id if chat_id.endswith(group_suffix) else None,
        "conversation_type": "Group" if chat_id.endswith(group_suffix) else "Personal",
        "message_type": "Text",
    }
    doc = frappe.new_doc("CN PW Whatsapp Message")
    doc.update(message_data)
    doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    doc.send()
    return True


@frappe.whitelist()
def mark_as_seen(name: str, session_id: str):
    message_doc = frappe.get_doc("CN PW Whatsapp Message", name)
    conversation_type = message_doc.conversation_type
    chat_id = (
        message_doc.group_id if conversation_type == "Group" else message_doc.user_id
    )
    if not is_group_contact_access_available(chat_id, session_id):
        raise frappe.PermissionError(
            "You are not allowed to marked as seen this user message"
        )
    if message_doc.status != "Received":
        return False
    url = "/api/sendSeen"
    data = json.dumps(
        {
            "chatId": chat_id,
            "session": message_doc.session,
            "messageId": message_doc.message_id,
        }
    )
    waha_api_dispatcher("POST", url, data)
    message_doc.status = "Seen"
    message_doc.save(ignore_permissions=True)
    update_unread_count(chat_id, message_doc.session)
    return True


def send_scheduled_message():
    doc_type = "CN PW Whatsapp Message"
    messages = frappe.get_all(
        doc_type, filters={"status": "Scheduled", "type": "Outgoing"}
    )
    current = now()
    current_datetime = datetime.strptime(current, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S.%f")
    for message in messages:
        message_doc = frappe.get_doc(doc_type, message.name)
        if message_doc.scheduled_at <= current_datetime:
            message_doc.status = "Unknown"
            message_doc.save()
            message_doc.send()


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_whatsapp_msg(chat_id, session):
    conversation_type = "Group" if chat_id.endswith("@g.us") else "Personal"
    if is_group_contact_access_available(chat_id, session):
        all_chat = frappe.get_all(
            "CN PW Whatsapp Message",
            filters={
                "conversation_type": conversation_type,
                "session": session,
                "group_id" if conversation_type == "Group" else "user_id": chat_id,
            },
            fields=["*"],
        )
        return all_chat
    raise frappe.PermissionError("You are not allowed to see this message")

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/personal_whatsapp_integration/doctype/cn_pw_whatsapp_session/cn_pw_whatsapp_session.js
// Copyright (c) 2024, Hybrowlabs Technologies and contributors
// For license information, please see license.txt

frappe.ui.form.on('CN PW Whatsapp Session', {
  refresh: function(frm) {
    if (frm.doc.status === 'WORKING') {
      const btn = frm.add_custom_button('Logout', () => {
        frm
          .call({
            doc: frm.doc,
            method: 'logout',
            btn,
          })
          .then((m) => frm.refresh())
      })
    }
    if (frm.doc.status === 'STOPPED' || frm.doc.status === 'FAILED') {
      const btn = frm.add_custom_button('Start', () => {
        frm
          .call({
            doc: frm.doc,
            method: 'start',
            btn,
          })
          .then((m) => frm.refresh())
      })
    }
    if (frm.doc.status === 'SCAN_QR_CODE' || frm.doc.status === 'WORKING') {
      const btn = frm.add_custom_button('Stop', () => {
        frm
          .call({
            doc: frm.doc,
            method: 'stop',
            btn,
          })
          .then((m) => frm.refresh())
      })
    }
  },
})

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/personal_whatsapp_integration/doctype/cn_pw_whatsapp_session/cn_pw_whatsapp_session.py
# Copyright (c) 2024, Hybrowlabs Technologies and contributors
# For license information, please see license.txt
import json

import frappe
import requests
from frappe.model.document import Document
from werkzeug.wrappers import Response

from nextai.config.api import WAHA_API_KEY, WAHA_HOST, HOST_NAME
from nextai.personal_whatsapp_integration.doctype.utils import waha_api_dispatcher
from nextai.utils import is_admin_user


class CNPWWhatsappSession(Document):
    def validate(self):
        if self.default:
            frappe.db.set_value("CN PW Whatsapp Session", {"default": 1}, "default", 0)

    def before_insert(self):
        session_count = frappe.db.count("CN PW Whatsapp Session")
        if session_count == 0:
            self.default = 1

    @frappe.whitelist()
    def logout(self):
        url = "/api/sessions/logout"
        payload = json.dumps({"name": self.name})
        response = waha_api_dispatcher("POST", url, payload)
        if not response.ok:
            return response.text

    @frappe.whitelist()
    def start(self):
        url = "/api/sessions/start"
        webhook_url = (
            f"{HOST_NAME}/api/method/nextai.api.webhook.personal_whatsapp.handle"
        )
        payload = json.dumps(
            {
                "name": self.name,
                "config": {
                    "proxy": None,
                    "noweb": {"store": {"enabled": True, "fullSync": True}},
                    "webhooks": [
                        {
                            "url": webhook_url,
                            "events": [
                                "message.any",
                                "message",
                                "session.status",
                                "message.reaction",
                                "message.ack",
                                "message.revoked",
                                "state.change",
                                "group.join",
                                "group.leave",
                                "presence.update",
                                "poll.vote",
                                "poll.vote.failed",
                                "chat.archive",
                                "call.accepted",
                                "label.deleted",
                                "label.chat.added",
                                "label.chat.deleted",
                            ],
                            "hmac": None,
                            "retries": None,
                            "customHeaders": None,
                        }
                    ],
                    "debug": False,
                },
            }
        )
        response = waha_api_dispatcher("POST", url, payload)
        if not response.ok:
            return response.text

    @frappe.whitelist()
    def stop(self):
        url = "/api/sessions/stop"
        payload = json.dumps({"name": self.name})
        response = waha_api_dispatcher("POST", url, payload)
        if not response.ok:
            return response.text


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_qr_code(url: str):
    updated_url = f"{WAHA_HOST}{url}"
    headers = {
        "X-Api-Key": WAHA_API_KEY,
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
    }
    try:
        response = requests.get(updated_url, headers=headers)
        if response.status_code == 200:
            return Response(
                response.content, mimetype=response.headers.get("Content-Type")
            )
        else:
            frappe.throw(
                f"Failed to fetch image from WAHA. Status code: {response.status_code}"
            )
    except Exception as e:
        frappe.throw(f"Error while fetching image: {str(e)}")


def get_session_present_in_local(local_session, server_session):
    reference_names = {entry["name"] for entry in local_session}
    result = []
    for entry in server_session:
        if entry["name"] in reference_names:
            result.append({"name": entry["name"], "status": entry["status"]})

    return result


def sync_whatsapp_session_with_waha():
    url = "/ping"
    response = waha_api_dispatcher("GET", url)
    if not response:
        return
    doc_type = "CN PW Whatsapp Session"
    local_sessions = frappe.get_all(doc_type)
    get_all_session_url = "/api/sessions"
    session_in_server = waha_api_dispatcher("GET", get_all_session_url).json()
    filtered_sessions = get_session_present_in_local(local_sessions, session_in_server)
    for local_session in local_sessions:
        session_doc = frappe.get_doc(doc_type, local_session.name)
        local_sessions_status = session_doc.status
        session_server_status = next(
            (
                entry["status"]
                for entry in filtered_sessions
                if entry["name"] == local_session.name
            ),
            None,
        )
        running_state = {"WORKING", "STARTING", "SCAN_QR_CODE"}

        if local_sessions_status in running_state:
            if session_server_status in running_state:
                session_doc.status = session_server_status
            else:
                session_doc.start()
        else:
            if session_server_status in running_state:
                session_doc.status = session_server_status
        session_doc.save()


def check_is_system_manager(user):
    roles = frappe.get_roles(user)
    if "System Manager" in roles:
        return True
    else:
        return False


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_selected_session(session):
    user = frappe.session.user
    is_system_manager = check_is_system_manager(user)

    session_data = frappe.get_doc("CN PW Whatsapp Session", session)

    if is_system_manager or user == "Administrator":
        return session_data

    if any(child.user_assign == user for child in session_data.session_assign_to):
        return session_data

    return None


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_assigned_session():
    user = frappe.session.user
    is_system_manager = check_is_system_manager(user)
    doctype_name = "CN PW Whatsapp Session"
    has_access = []
    all_sessions = frappe.get_all(
        doctype_name,
        fields=[
            "name",
            "session_id",
            "user_id",
            "user_name",
            "status",
            "qr_image_link",
            "default",
        ],
    )
    if is_system_manager or user == "Administrator":
        has_access = all_sessions
        return has_access
    for session in all_sessions:
        session_doc = frappe.get_doc(doctype_name, session.name)
        if any(child.user_assign == user for child in session_doc.session_assign_to):
            has_access.append(
                {
                    "name": session.name,
                    "session_id": session.session_id,
                    "user_id": session.user_id,
                    "user_name": session.user_name,
                    "status": session.status,
                    "image_link": session.qr_image_link,
                }
            )
    return has_access


def get_session_present_in_local(local_session, server_session):
    reference_names = {entry["name"] for entry in local_session}
    result = []
    for entry in server_session:
        if entry["name"] in reference_names:
            result.append({"name": entry["name"], "status": entry["status"]})

    return result


@frappe.whitelist()
def assign_user_to_session(user, session, full_access=False):
    session_doc = frappe.get_doc("CN PW Whatsapp Session", session)
    user_exists = False
    for row in session_doc.session_assign_to:
        if row.user_assign == user:
            user_exists = True
            break
    if not user_exists:
        try:
            session_doc.append(
                "session_assign_to",
                {
                    "user_assign": user,
                    "all_access": True if full_access else False,
                },
            )
            session_doc.save()
            frappe.db.commit()
        except Exception as e:
            return f"Failed to assign user to session: {str(e)}"
        return f"User {user} has been added to the session {session}"
    else:
        return f"User {user} has been already assigned to the session {session}"


@frappe.whitelist()
def un_assign_user_to_session(user, session):
    session_doc = frappe.get_doc("CN PW Whatsapp Session", session)
    user_exists = False
    for row in session_doc.session_assign_to:
        if row.user_assign == user:
            user_exists = True
            break
    if not user_exists:
        return f"User {user} does not exist in the session {session}"
    else:
        try:
            removed = False
            for row in session_doc.session_assign_to:
                if row.user_assign == user:
                    session_doc.remove(row)
                    removed = True
                    break
            if removed:
                session_doc.save()
                frappe.db.commit()
                return f"User {user} has been removed from the session {session}"
            else:
                return f"Error while un-assigning user: {user}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"Failed to un-assign user: {str(e)}"


@frappe.whitelist()
def start_session(doc_name):
    user = frappe.session.user
    session_doc = frappe.get_doc("CN PW Whatsapp Session", doc_name)
    if is_admin_user(user):
        session_doc.start()
        return f"Session {doc_name} has been started."
    for row in session_doc.session_assign_to:
        if row.user_assign == user:
            session_doc.start()
            return f"Session {doc_name} has been started."
    return f"you Dont Have access to this session {doc_name}."


@frappe.whitelist()
def create_session(session_name):
    user = frappe.session.user
    if not is_admin_user(user):
        return "you Dont Have access to create new session."
    data = {"session_id": session_name}
    doc = frappe.new_doc("CN PW Whatsapp Session")
    doc.update(data)
    doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    doc.start()
    return f"Session {session_name} has been created."

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/personal_whatsapp_integration/doctype/utils.py
import requests

from nextai.config.api import WAHA_HOST, WAHA_API_KEY


def waha_api_dispatcher(method: str, url: str, payload=None):
    if payload is None:
        payload = {}

    header = {
        "X-Api-Key": WAHA_API_KEY,
        "accept": "*/*",
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
    }

    full_url = f"{WAHA_HOST}{url}"
    try:
        res = requests.request(method, full_url, headers=header, data=payload)
    except Exception as e:
        return None
    return res

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/token.py
import frappe
from nextai.utils import create_response
import json


@frappe.whitelist()
def register(**kwargs):
    try:
        if not kwargs.get("token"):
            return create_response("Error", "Please Specify Token")
        if frappe.db.exists(
            "Mobile Push Token",
            {"user_id": frappe.session.user, "token": kwargs.get("token")},
        ):
            return ("Success", "User Id Already Registered")
        mp_doc = frappe.new_doc("Mobile Push Token")
        mp_doc.token = kwargs.get("token")
        mp_doc.user_id = frappe.session.user
        mp_doc.save(ignore_permissions=True)
        return create_response("Success", "User Id Token Registered")
    except Exception as e:
        return create_response("Error", e)


@frappe.whitelist()
def de_register(token):
    try:
        token = json.loads(token)
        if not token.get("data"):
            return "Please Specify EXPO Token"
        token = token.get("data")
        if not frappe.db.exists("Mobile Push Token", {"token": token}):
            return f"Mobile Push Token {token.get('data')} Not Present !"
        frappe.db.delete("Mobile Push Token", {"token": token})
        return True
    except Exception as e:
        return e

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/utils.py
import base64
import json

import requests

import frappe
from frappe.core.api.file import create_new_folder
from frappe.sessions import Session
from frappe.utils.file_manager import save_file
from nextai.config.api import API_URL


def is_admin_user(user_id=None):
    user_id = user_id or frappe.session.user
    if user_id == 'Administrator':
        return True
    has_role = frappe.get_all("Has Role", filters={"parent": user_id, "role": "System Manager"})
    return len(has_role) > 0


def send_authorized_core_request(payload, request_type, method, headers=None):
    if headers is None:
        headers = {}
    user = frappe.get_doc("User", frappe.session.user)
    headers = {
        "Authorization": "Token "
                         + str(user.chatnext_api_key)
                         + ":"
                         + str(user.chatnext_api_secret),
        **headers,
    }

    url = f"{API_URL}/api/method/{method}"
    response = send_request(url, payload, method=request_type, headers=headers)
    return response


def send_authed_core_request(payload, request_type, method, headers=None):
    return send_authorized_core_request(payload, request_type, method, headers)


def send_core_request(payload, request_type, method, headers=None):
    url = f"{API_URL}/api/method/{method}"
    response = send_request(url, payload, method=request_type, headers=headers)
    return response


def create_response(type, message):
    return {"msg": type, "data": message}


def get_host_with_protocol():
    host_name = frappe.get_site_config().get("host_name")
    if host_name:
        return host_name

    try:
        current_host = frappe.get_request_header("Host")
        current_protocol = frappe.get_request_header("X-Forwarded-Proto")

        if current_protocol:
            current_url = f"{current_protocol}://{current_host}"
        else:
            current_url = f"http://{current_host}"
        return current_url
    except:
        return frappe.get_site_config().get("localhost_name", "http://localhost:8000")


@frappe.whitelist()
def create_session(user, host):
    try:
        user = frappe.get_doc("User", user)
        settings = frappe.get_doc("ChatNext Settings")
        print(host)
        session = Session(
            user=user.name,
            resume=False,
            full_name=user.full_name,
            user_type=user.user_type,
        )
        dataString = '{"host":"' + host + '", "login": "' + session.sid + '"}'
        return (
                settings.deeplink_url
                + "/login?loginData="
                + base64.b64encode(dataString.encode("utf-8")).decode()
        )
    except Exception as e:
        frappe.logger("session").exception(e)


def send_request(url, payload, method="GET", headers=None):
    if payload is None:
        payload = {}
    if headers is None:
        headers = {}
    headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json", **headers}
    if method == "POST" or method == "PUT":
        response = requests.request(
            method, url, headers=headers, data=json.dumps(payload, default=str)
        )
    else:
        response = requests.request(method, url, headers=headers)
    return response


def create_folder(folder, parent):
    """Make sure the folder exists and return it's name."""
    new_folder_name = "/".join([parent, folder])

    if not frappe.db.exists("File", new_folder_name):
        create_new_folder(folder, parent)

    return new_folder_name


def create_print_file_url(doctype, docname, file_name, format):
    doctype_folder = create_folder(doctype, "Home")

    html = frappe.get_print(doctype, docname, format)
    pdf_data = frappe.utils.pdf.get_pdf(html)

    pdf_url = save_file(
        file_name, pdf_data, doctype, docname, folder=doctype_folder, is_private=0
    )
    return pdf_url.file_url

def get_translated_doc_name(doctype):
    """Retrieve the translated name of a doctype if available"""
    if not doctype:
        return doctype

    # Get the user's preferred language
    lang = frappe.local.lang or frappe.get_system_settings("language") or "en"

    # Fetch translation from the database without caching
    translated_text = frappe.get_value(
        "Translation",
        {"source_text": doctype, "language": lang},
        "translated_text"
    ) or doctype

    return translated_text

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/whatsapp_business_api_integration/doctype/whatsapp_message/whatsapp_message.js

frappe.ui.form.on("WhatsApp Message", {
  refresh: function (frm) {
    if (!frm.doc.id) {
      const btn = frm.add_custom_button("Send Message", () => {
        frm
          .call({
            doc: frm.doc,
            method: "send",
            btn,
          })
          .then((m) => frm.refresh())
      })
    }
    frm.add_custom_button(__("Generate OCR"), function () {
      let uploader = new frappe.ui.FileUploader({
        on_success: async (data) => {
          const image = await fetch(data.file_url)
          const imageData = await image.blob()

          function blobToBase64(blob) {
            return new Promise((resolve, _) => {
              const reader = new FileReader()
              reader.onloadend = () => resolve(reader.result)
              reader.readAsDataURL(blob)
            })
          }

          const base64Image = await blobToBase64(imageData)
          var myHeaders = new Headers()
          myHeaders.append("Content-Type", "application/json")

          const raw = JSON.stringify({
            docName: frm.doctype,
            file: base64Image,
          })
          var requestOptions = {
            method: "POST",
            headers: myHeaders,
            body: raw,
            redirect: "follow",
          }

          fetch(
            "https://chatnext.hybrowlabs.com/api/method/chatnext_core.chatnext_core.doctype.ocr.api.create_ocr_request",
            requestOptions
          )
            .then((response) => response.text())

            .catch((error) => console.log("error", error))
        },
      })
    })

    if (
      frm.doc.type === "Incoming" &&
      ["Image", "Video", "Audio", "Document"].includes(frm.doc.message_type) &&
      !frm.doc.media_file
    ) {
      const btn = frm.add_custom_button("Download Attachment File", () => {
        frm
          .call({
            doc: frm.doc,
            method: "download_media",
            btn,
          })
          .then((data) => {
            const file = data.message
            frm.refresh()
            frappe.msgprint({
              title: "Attachment downloaded successfully.",
              message: `Attachment File: <a href="${file.file_url}" target="_blank">${file.file_name}</a>`,
              indicator: "green",
            })
          })
      })
    }

    if (frm.doc.preview_html) {
      let wrapper = frm.get_field("preview_html_rendered").$wrapper
      wrapper.html(frm.doc.preview_html)
    }

    if (
      frm.doc.type === "Outgoing" &&
      frm.doc.media_file &&
      !frm.doc.media_uploaded
    ) {
      const btn = frm.add_custom_button("Upload Attachment File", () => {
        frm
          .call({
            doc: frm.doc,
            method: "upload_media",
            btn,
          })
          .then(() => {
            frm.refresh()
            frappe.msgprint({
              title: "Attachment uploaded successfully.",
              message: "You can send this message now!",
              indicator: "green",
            })
          })
      })
    }

    if (frm.doc.type === "Incoming" && frm.doc.status !== "Marked As Seen") {
      const btn = frm.add_custom_button("Mark As Seen", () => {
        frm
          .call({
            doc: frm.doc,
            method: "mark_as_seen",
            btn,
          })
          .then(() => frm.refresh())
      })
    }
  },
})

  },

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/whatsapp_business_api_integration/doctype/whatsapp_message/whatsapp_message.py
# Copyright (c) 2023, Hybrowlabs Technologies and contributors
# For license information, please see license.txt

import json
import mimetypes
from datetime import datetime
from functools import lru_cache
from typing import Dict

import requests

import frappe
from frappe.model.document import Document
from frappe.utils import now
from nextai.api.webhook.whatsapp import create_message
from nextai.utils import get_host_with_protocol, create_print_file_url, create_folder

MEDIA_TYPES = ("image", "sticker", "document", "audio", "video")
MEDIA_TITLE_TYPES = ("Image", "Sticker", "Document", "Audio", "Video")
MESSAGE_TYPES = ("image", "sticker", "document", "audio", "video", "button", "interactive")

META_WA_API_URL = "https://graph.facebook.com/v18.0"


class WhatsAppMessage(Document):

    def validate(self):
        self.validate_image_attachment()

        if self.message_type == "Audio" and self.media_file:
            self.preview_html = f"""
				<audio controls>
					<source src="{self.media_file}" type="{self.media_mime_type}">
					Your browser does not support the audio element.
				</audio>
			"""

        if self.message_type == "Video" and self.media_file:
            self.preview_html = f"""
				<video controls>
					<source src="{self.media_file}" type="{self.media_mime_type}">
					Your browser does not support the video element.
				</video>
			"""

    def validate_image_attachment(self):
        if self.media_image:
            self.media_file = self.media_image

        if self.media_file and self.message_type == "Image":
            self.media_image = self.media_file

    # preferred using overwrite instead of uploading media to whatsapp and generating id as it will save bandwidth
    def send_templated_message(self, host_overwrite: str = None):
        if not self.template:
            frappe.throw(
                "Template Name (`template`) is required for templated message."
            )

        access_token = frappe.utils.password.get_decrypted_password(
            "Whatsapp Settings", "Whatsapp Settings", "access_token"
        )

        phone_number_id = frappe.db.get_single_value(
            "Whatsapp Settings", "phone_number_id"
        )

        endpoint = f"{META_WA_API_URL}/{phone_number_id}/messages"

        template = frappe.get_doc("WhatsApp Template", self.template)
        language_code = template.language_code

        template_data = json.loads(self.template_data or "[]") or []

        request_data = {
            "messaging_product": "whatsapp",
            "recipient_type": "individual",
            "to": frappe.get_value('Contact', self.to, 'whatsapp_id'),
            "type": "template",
            "template": {
                "name": self.template,
                "language": {
                    "code": language_code or "en"
                },
                "components": [
                    {
                        "type": "body",
                        "parameters": template_data
                    }
                ]
            }
        }

        message_type = str(self.message_type.lower())

        if message_type in MEDIA_TYPES:
            current_url = host_overwrite or get_host_with_protocol()

            parameter = {}
            parameter["type"] = message_type
            parameter[message_type] = {
                "link": f"{current_url}{self.media_file}",
            }
            updated_header = {
                "type": "header",
                "parameters": [
                    parameter
                ]
            }
            request_data["template"]["components"].insert(0, updated_header)

        if template.category == "AUTHENTICATION" and template_data:
            otp = template_data[0]['text']
            data = {
                "type": "BUTTON",
                "sub_type": "url",
                "index": 0,
                "parameters": [{"type": "text", "text": otp}],
            }
            request_data["template"]['components'].append(data)

        response = requests.post(
            endpoint,
            json=request_data,
            headers={
                "Authorization": "Bearer " + access_token,
                "Content-Type": "application/json",
            },
        )

        if response.ok:
            self.id = response.json().get("messages")[0]["id"]
            self.status = "Sent"
            self.send_error = ''
            self.save(ignore_permissions=True)
            return response.json()
        else:
            if (response.json().get("error").get("message")):
                self.send_error = response.json().get("error").get("message")
                self.save(ignore_permissions=True)
            if response.json().get("error").get("message") == "(#131030) Recipient phone number not in allowed list":
                contact_person = frappe.get_doc("Contact", self.to)
                contact_person.mark_whatsapp_unregistered()
            return response.json().get("error").get("message")

    @frappe.whitelist()
    def send(self) -> Dict:
        if not self.to:
            frappe.throw("Recepient (`to`) is required to send message.")

        if self.is_template:
            return self.send_templated_message()

        access_token = frappe.utils.password.get_decrypted_password(
            "Whatsapp Settings", "Whatsapp Settings", "access_token"
        )

        phone_number_id = frappe.db.get_single_value(
            "Whatsapp Settings", "phone_number_id"
        )

        endpoint = f"{META_WA_API_URL}/{phone_number_id}/messages"

        request_data = {
            "messaging_product": "whatsapp",
            "recipient_type": "individual",
            "to": frappe.get_value('Contact', self.to, 'whatsapp_id'),
            "type": get_message_type(self.message_type),
        }

        if self.message_type == "Text":
            request_data["text"] = {"preview_url": False, "body": self.message_body}

        if self.message_type in MEDIA_TITLE_TYPES:
            if not self.media_id:
                frappe.throw(
                    "Please attach and upload the media before sending this message."
                )

            request_data[get_message_type(self.message_type)] = {
                "id": self.media_id,
            }

            if self.message_type == "document":
                request_data[self.message_type.lower()]["filename"] = self.media_filename
                request_data[self.message_type.lower()]["caption"] = self.media_caption

        if self.message_type in ("Button", "Interactive"):
            interactive = {
                'body': {
                    'text': self.message_body
                },
                'action': {}
            }

            if self.message_type == "Button":
                interactive["type"] = "button"
                interactive["action"]["buttons"] = self.interactive_message_data
            else:
                interactive["type"] = "list"
                # Default title for list view
                interactive["action"]["button"] = "Select a Option"
                interactive["action"]["sections"] = self.interactive_message_data

            request_data["interactive"] = interactive

        response = requests.post(
            endpoint,
            json=request_data,
            headers={
                "Authorization": "Bearer " + access_token,
                "Content-Type": "application/json",
            },
        )

        self.reload()
        if response.ok:
            self.id = response.json().get("messages")[0]["id"]
            self.status = "Sent"
            self.send_error = ''
            self.save(ignore_permissions=True)
            return response.json()
        else:
            try:
                if (response.json().get("error").get("message")):
                    self.send_error = response.json().get("error").get("message")
                    self.save(ignore_permissions=True)
                return response.json().get("error").get("message")
            except Exception as e:
                frappe.logger("Whatsapp_Send").exception(e)
            # frappe.throw(response.json().get("error").get("message"))

    def attach_media(self):
        url = self.get_media_url()
        access_token = self.get_access_token()
        response = requests.get(
            url,
            headers={
                "Authorization": "Bearer " + access_token,
            },
        )

        file_name = get_media_name(self, response.headers.get("Content-Type"))
        file_doc = frappe.get_doc(
            {
                "doctype": "File",
                "file_name": file_name,
                "content": response.content,
            }
        ).insert(ignore_permissions=True)

        self.set("media_file", file_doc.file_url)

        # Will be used to display image preview
        if self.message_type == "Image":
            self.set("media_image", file_doc.file_url)

        return file_doc.as_dict()

    @frappe.whitelist()
    def download_media(self) -> Dict:
        self.reload()
        file_doc = self.attach_media()
        self.save()
        self.reload()
        return file_doc

    def get_media_url(self) -> str:
        if not self.media_id:
            frappe.throw("`media_id` is missing.")

        access_token = self.get_access_token()
        response = requests.get(
            f"{META_WA_API_URL}/{self.media_id}",
            headers={
                "Authorization": "Bearer " + access_token,
            },
        )

        if not response.ok:
            frappe.throw("Error fetching media URL")

        return response.json().get("url")

    @lru_cache
    def get_access_token(self) -> str:
        return frappe.utils.password.get_decrypted_password(
            "WhatsApp Settings", "WhatsApp Settings", "access_token"
        )

    @frappe.whitelist()
    def upload_media(self):
        if not self.media_file:
            frappe.throw("`media_file` is required to upload media.")

        media_file_path = frappe.get_doc(
            "File", {"file_url": self.media_file}
        ).get_full_path()
        access_token = self.get_access_token()

        phone_number_id = frappe.db.get_single_value(
            "WhatsApp Settings", "phone_number_id"
        )

        if not self.media_mime_type:
            self.media_mime_type = mimetypes.guess_type(self.media_file)[0]

        # Way to send multi-part form data
        # Ref: https://stackoverflow.com/a/35974071
        form_data = {
            "file": (
                "file",
                open(media_file_path, "rb"),
                self.media_mime_type,
            ),
            "messaging_product": (None, "whatsapp"),
            "type": (None, self.media_mime_type),
        }
        response = requests.post(
            f"{META_WA_API_URL}/{phone_number_id}/media",
            files=form_data,
            headers={
                "Authorization": "Bearer " + access_token,
            },
        )

        if response.ok:
            self.media_id = response.json().get("id")
            self.media_uploaded = True
            self.save(ignore_permissions=True)
        else:
            frappe.throw(response.json().get("error").get("message"))

    @frappe.whitelist()
    def mark_as_seen(self):
        if self.type != "Incoming":
            frappe.throw("Only incoming messages can be marked as seen.")

        phone_number_id = frappe.db.get_single_value(
            "WhatsApp Settings", "phone_number_id"
        )
        endpoint = f"{META_WA_API_URL}/{phone_number_id}/messages"
        access_token = self.get_access_token()

        response = requests.post(
            endpoint,
            json={
                "messaging_product": "whatsapp",
                "status": "read", "message_id": self.id
            },
            headers={
                "Authorization": "Bearer " + access_token,
                "Content-Type": "application/json",
            },
        )

        # TODO: ?? cane be marked as seen even if api fails
        if response.ok:
            self.reload()
            self.status = "Marked As Seen"
            self.save(ignore_permissions=True)
            frappe.db.set_value(
                "WhatsApp Message", {"id": self.id}, "status", "Marked As Seen"
            )
            self.notify_update()
        else:
            frappe.throw(response.json().get("error").get("message"))

    def after_insert(self):
        contact_id = self.get('from') or self.get('to')
        if contact_id:
            receive_time = None
            if self.type == "Incoming":
                receive_time = self.creation
                subject = f"Message From <b>{self.get('from')}</b>"
                notify_to_assignees(contact_id, subject, self.get("message_body"), self.doctype, self.name)
            try:
                contact = frappe.get_doc("Contact", contact_id)
                if receive_time:
                    contact.last_whatsapp_message_received = receive_time
                contact.custom_last_message_interaction = self.creation
                contact.save(ignore_permissions=True)
            except:
                pass

    def before_insert(self):
        is_message_media_type = self.message_type == "Image" or self.message_type == "Audio" or self.message_type == "Document" or self.message_type == "Video"

        if is_message_media_type and self.media_id:
            try:
                current_user = frappe.session.user
                frappe.session.user = "Administrator"
                self.attach_media()
                frappe.session.user = current_user
            except Exception as e:
                try:
                    frappe.log_error(f"Problem downloading {self.message_type}", frappe.get_traceback())
                except:
                    pass

        # resolve template
        if self.is_template:
            doc = frappe.get_doc("WhatsApp Template", self.template)

            actual_message = doc.template
            data = json.loads(self.template_data)
            if data:
                for item in data:
                    placeholder = "{{" + str(data.index(item) + 1) + "}}"
                    actual_message = actual_message.replace(
                        placeholder, item["text"]
                    )

            self.message_body = actual_message

        # resolve interactive messages
        try:
            if self.type == "Incoming":
                if self.message_type == "Button":
                    data = json.loads(self.interactive_message_data)
                    self.message_body = data["text"]
                if self.message_type == "Interactive":
                    data = json.loads(self.interactive_message_data)
                    if data.get("list_reply") and data["list_reply"].get("title"):
                        self.message_body = data["list_reply"]["title"]
                    elif data.get("button_reply") and data["button_reply"].get("title"):
                        self.message_body = data["button_reply"]["title"]
                    elif data.get("body") and data["body"].get("text"):
                        self.message_body = data["body"]["text"]
        except:
            pass

    def on_update(self):
        user_id = self.get('from') or self.get('to')
        if not user_id:
            return

        try:
            last_message = None
            try:
                if self.type == "Incoming":
                    last_message = frappe.get_last_doc(
                        "WhatsApp Message",
                        {"from": self.get('from'), "status": "Marked As Seen"}
                    )
                else:
                    last_message = frappe.get_last_doc("WhatsApp Message", {"to": self.get('to'), "status": "Read"})
            except:
                pass

            if last_message:
                if last_message.type == "Incoming":
                    received_messages = frappe.get_all(
                        "WhatsApp Message",
                        {"from": self.get('from'), "status": "Received"}
                    )
                    for message in received_messages:
                        message_doc = frappe.get_doc("WhatsApp Message", message.get("name"))
                        try:
                            self_datetime_object = datetime.strptime(last_message.creation, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S.%f") \
                                if isinstance(last_message.creation, str) else last_message.creation
                            if self_datetime_object >= message_doc.creation:
                                message_doc.db_set('status', "Marked As Seen")
                        except:
                            pass

                    whatsapp_unread_count = frappe.db.count(
                        "WhatsApp Message",
                        {"from": self.get('from'), "status": "Received"}
                    )
                    contact = frappe.get_doc("Contact", user_id)
                    contact.whatsapp_unread_count = whatsapp_unread_count
                    contact.save(ignore_permissions=True)
                else:
                    sent_messages = frappe.get_all("WhatsApp Message", {"to": self.get('to'), "status": "Delivered"})
                    for message in sent_messages:
                        message_doc = frappe.get_doc("WhatsApp Message", message.get("name"))
                        try:
                            self_datetime_object = datetime.strptime(last_message.creation, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S.%f") \
                                if isinstance(last_message.creation, str) else last_message.creation
                            if self_datetime_object >= message_doc.creation:
                                message_doc.db_set('status', "Read")
                        except:
                            pass
            else:
                whatsapp_unread_count = frappe.db.count(
                    "WhatsApp Message",
                    {"from": self.get('from'), "status": "Received"}
                )
                contact = frappe.get_doc("Contact", user_id)
                contact.whatsapp_unread_count = whatsapp_unread_count
                contact.save(ignore_permissions=True)
        except:
            pass

        # for fetching after save
        previous = self._doc_before_save
        if not previous:
            if self.type == "Incoming":
                frappe.publish_realtime(
                    'NEW_MESSAGE_RECEIVED', {
                        'user': self.get('from'), 'type': self.get('type')
                    }
                )
        else:
            if self.type == "Incoming":
                frappe.publish_realtime(
                    'MESSAGE_UPDATED', {'user': self.get('from'), 'type': self.get('type')}
                )

        if self.link_doctype and self.link_doctype_name:
            original_doc = self.get_doc_before_save()
            if original_doc.link_doctype_name != self.link_doctype_name:
                comment = f"""
                <div style="padding:8px; background:white; border-radius:8px; border:1px solid #EEEEEE;">
                <div style="display:flex; align-items:flex-start;">
                    {
                '<img style="margin-top:3px;"  src="/assets/nextai/assets/icons/Whatsapp_o.svg" alt="whatsapp-icon"/>' if self.type == "Incoming" else '<img  style="margin-top:3px;" src="/assets/nextai/assets/icons/Whatsapp_g.svg" alt="whatsapp-icon"/>'
                }
                <p style="color:{'orange' if self.type == "Incoming" else 'green'}; margin-bottom:4px;margin-left:4px;">Whatsapp Message {f'Received From {user_id}' if self.type == "Incoming" else 'sent'}:</p>
                </div>
                <p style="margin-bottom:0px;">{self.message_body}</p>
                </div>
                """
                update_comment(self.link_doctype, self.link_doctype_name, comment)


def create_system_notification(self, subject, context, recipients):
    try:
        if not recipients:
            return
        # frappe.local.login_manager.login_as("Administrator")
        notification_doc = {
            "type": "Alert",
            "document_type": self.doctype,
            "document_name": self.name,
            "subject": subject,
            "from_user": "Administrator",
            "email_content": context
        }
        for user in recipients:
            notification = frappe.new_doc("Notification Log")
            notification.update(notification_doc)

            notification.for_user = user
            if (
                    notification.for_user != notification.from_user
                    or notification_doc.get('type') == "Energy Point"
                    or notification_doc.get('type') == "Alert"
            ):
                notification.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    # frappe.local.login_manager.login_as(frappe.session.user)
    except Exception as e:
        frappe.logger('utils').exception(e)
        return


@frappe.whitelist()
def create_whatsapp_message(message: Dict, contacts: Dict = None, outgoing=False, bot=False) -> WhatsAppMessage:
    return create_message(message, contacts, outgoing, bot)


def get_media_name(message_doc: WhatsAppMessage, content_type: str) -> str:
    return message_doc.media_filename or (
            "attachment_." + content_type.split(";")[0].split("/")[1]
    )


@frappe.whitelist()
def send_templated_message(
    contact_name: str, template_name: str, template_data: str, message_type: str = "Text",
    media_file: str = None, media_type: str = None, media_mime_type: str = None,
    media_caption: str = None, media_filename: str = None, link_doctype: str = None,
    link_doctype_name: str = None, media_image: str = None,
):
    # Parameter Validation
    if not contact_name or not template_name or not template_data:
        raise ValueError(
            "Contact name, template name, and template data are required."
        )

    contact = frappe.get_doc("Contact", contact_name)

    if not contact.whatsapp_id:
        raise ValueError("Contact does not have a WhatsApp ID.")

    return send_message(
        contact_name, "", has_media=bool(media_file), template_name=template_name,
        template_data=template_data, media_type=media_type, media_mime_type=media_mime_type,
        media_file=media_file, media_caption=media_caption, media_filename=media_filename,
        message_type=message_type, link_doctype=link_doctype, link_doctype_name=link_doctype_name, media_image=media_image
    )


def send_message(
    conatct_name: str, message_body: str, has_media: bool = False, media_file: str = None,
    media_type: str = None, media_mime_type: str = None, media_caption: str = None,
    media_filename: str = None, template_name: str = None, template_data: str = None,
    message_type: str = None, link_doctype: str = None, link_doctype_name: str = None,
    interactive_message_data=None, media_image: str = None,
):
    contact = frappe.get_doc("Contact", conatct_name)

    if not contact.whatsapp_id:
        frappe.throw("Contact does not have a WhatsApp ID.")

    is_image_type = message_type == "Image"

    if is_image_type and not media_image:
        media_image = media_file

    if has_media:
        if not media_file and not is_image_type:
            frappe.throw("Media file is required to send media message.")

        if media_type not in MEDIA_TYPES:
            frappe.throw(
                f"Media type should be one of {', '.join(MEDIA_TYPES)}"
            )

        if not media_mime_type:
            if is_image_type:
                media_mime_type = mimetypes.guess_type(media_image)[0]
            else:
                media_mime_type = mimetypes.guess_type(media_file)[0]

        if not media_filename:
            if is_image_type:
                media_filename = media_image.split("/")[-1]
            else:
                media_filename = media_file.split("/")[-1]

        wa_message = {
            "doctype": "WhatsApp Message",
            "type": "Outgoing",
            "message_type": message_type if message_type else "Text" if not has_media else media_type,
            "to": conatct_name,
            "message_body": message_body,
            "media_file": media_file or media_image,
            "media_mime_type": media_mime_type,
            "media_caption": media_caption,
            "media_filename": media_filename,
        }
        if is_image_type:
            wa_message["media_image"] = media_image
        else:
            wa_message["media_file"] = media_file

        if template_name and template_data:
            wa_message["is_template"] = True
            wa_message["template_data"] = json.loads(json.dumps(template_data))
            wa_message["template"] = template_name

        if link_doctype:
            wa_message["link_doctype"] = link_doctype

        message = frappe.get_doc(json.loads(json.dumps(wa_message)))
        message.upload_media()

    else:
        wa_message = {
            "doctype": "WhatsApp Message",
            "type": "Outgoing",
            "message_type": message_type if message_type else "Text",
            "to": conatct_name,
            "message_body": message_body,
        }
        if interactive_message_data:
            wa_message["interactive_message_data"] = json.dumps(
                interactive_message_data
            )

        if template_name and template_data:
            wa_message["is_template"] = True
            wa_message["template_data"] = json.loads(json.dumps(template_data))
            wa_message["template"] = template_name

        message = frappe.get_doc(wa_message)

    if message.link_doctype and link_doctype_name:
        message.link_doctype_name = link_doctype_name
    message.save(ignore_permissions=True)
    message.reload()
    message.send()
    return message


@frappe.whitelist()
def update_comment(doctype, docname, comment):
    doc = frappe.get_doc(doctype, docname)
    doc.add_comment('Attachment', comment)
    doc.save(ignore_permissions=True)


@frappe.whitelist()
def create_communication_video(video_link, reference_doctype, reference_docname):
    communication_video = frappe.get_doc(
        {
            'doctype': 'Communication Video',
            'video_link': video_link,
            'reference_doctype': reference_doctype,
            'reference_docname': reference_docname,
        }
    )
    communication_video.insert()
    frappe.db.commit()
    return communication_video.name


def send_print(
    doctype: str, docname: str, contact_name: str, format: str, template_name: str = None,
    template_data: str = None,
):
    doctype_folder = create_folder(doctype, "Home")

    html = frappe.get_print(doctype, docname, format)
    pdf_data = frappe.utils.pdf.get_pdf(html)

    file_name = "{to_name}.pdf".format(to_name=docname.replace("/", "-"))
    pdf_url = create_print_file_url(doctype, docname, file_name, format)
    if template_name and template_data:
        message_type = "Document"
        send_templated_message(
            contact_name=contact_name, media_file=pdf_url, template_name=template_name,
            template_data=template_data, media_mime_type="application/pdf", media_filename=file_name,
            message_type=message_type, media_caption=file_name, media_type=message_type.lower(),
            link_doctype=doctype, link_doctype_name=docname
        )
    else:
        send_message(
            contact_name, "", True, pdf_url, "Document", "application/pdf", file_name, file_name,
            template_name=template_name, template_data=template_data
        )


def send_scheduled_messages():
    doc_type = 'WhatsApp Message'
    messages = frappe.get_all(doc_type, filters={"status": "Scheduled"})
    current = now()
    current_datetime = datetime.strptime(current, '%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S.%f')

    if len(messages):
        for message in messages:
            message_obj = frappe.get_doc(doc_type, message.name)
            if message_obj.scheduled_at < current_datetime:
                message_obj.status = "Pending"
                message_obj.save(ignore_permissions=True)
                message_obj.send()

def create_notification_for_users(assignee_list, subject, message_body, from_user, doctype, docname):
    notification_obj = {
        "type": "Alert",
        "subject": {subject},
        "message": message_body,
        "from_user": from_user,
        "document_type": doctype,
        "document_name": docname,
        "seen": 0
    }
    for user in assignee_list:
        notification = frappe.new_doc("Notification Log")
        notification.update(notification_obj)
        notification.for_user = user
        notification.insert(ignore_permissions=True)


def notify_to_assignees(contact_id: str, subject: str, message_body: str, doctype: str, docname: str):
    assignees = frappe.get_all(
        'ToDo', filters={
            'reference_type': "Contact", 'reference_name': contact_id, 'status': 'Open'
        }, fields=['allocated_to']
    )
    assignee_list = [assignee.allocated_to for assignee in assignees] or []
    from_user = frappe.session.user

    frappe.enqueue(
        "nextai.whatsapp_business_api_integration.doctype.whatsapp_message.whatsapp_message.create_notification_for_users",
        assignee_list=assignee_list,
        subject=subject, message_body=message_body, from_user=from_user, doctype=doctype, docname=docname
    )


@frappe.whitelist()
def link_whatsapp_message():
    json_data = json.loads(frappe.request.data)
    data = json_data.get('data')
    message_id = json_data.get("message")
    whatsapp_message = frappe.get_doc("WhatsApp Message", message_id)
    comment = f"""
                <div style="padding:8px; background:white; border-radius:8px; border:1px solid #EEEEEE;">
                <div style="display:flex; align-items:flex-start;">
                    {
    '<img  style="margin-top:3px;" src="/assets/nextai/assets/icons/Whatsapp_g.svg" alt="whatsapp-icon"/>'
    }
                <p style="color:'green'; margin-bottom:4px;margin-left:4px;">Whatsapp Message 'sent':</p>
                </div>
                <p style="margin-bottom:0px;">{whatsapp_message.message_body}</p>
                </div>
                """

    for item in data:
        document = item["documentlist"]["name"]
        document_name = item["document"]["name"]

        link = whatsapp_message.append("links", {})
        link.link_doctype = document
        link.link_name = document_name
        try:
            update_comment(document, document_name, comment)
        except Exception:
            pass

    whatsapp_message.save(ignore_permissions=True)


def get_message_type(type: str) -> str:
    type = type.lower()
    if type == "button":
        return "interactive"

    return type

const generateRandomString = (length = 20) => {
    const letters = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz_'
    let string = ""
    for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) {
        string += letters[Math.floor(Math.random() * letters.length)]
    }
    return string
}

frappe.ui.form.on('Whatsapp Settings', {
    refresh: function (frm) {
        const webHookUrl = `${window.location.origin}/api/method/nextai.api.webhook.whatsapp.handle`
        frm.whatsapp_webhook_url = webHookUrl
        frm.webhook_verify_token = frm?.selected_doc?.webhook_verify_token
        frm.set_intro(`
      <p class="text-dark my-0">
          You need to generate whatsapp settings to set up chatnext whatsapp. Please refer to the following page to set up the same:
        <a class="text-primary" href="https://www.hybrowlabs.com/blog/setting-up-whatsapp-in-erpnext-with-chatnext" >
            www.hybrowlabs.com/blog/setting-up-whatsapp-in-erpnext-with-chatnext
        </a>
      </p>
      <p class="text-dark my-0">
        Weebkook url required to receive events for whatsapp:
        <u>${webHookUrl}</u>
      </p>
    `)
    },
    regenerate_token_copy_to_clipboard: (frm) => {
        const verifyToken = generateRandomString()
        frm.set_value("webhook_verify_token", verifyToken)
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(verifyToken)
            .then(r => frappe.show_alert({
                message: 'Verify Token copied to clip board',
                indicator: "green"
            }, 2))
            .catch(r => frappe.show_alert({
                message: 'cannot copy to clip board',
                indicator: "red"
            }, 5))
    },
    copy_token_to_clipboard: (frm) => {
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(frm.selected_doc.webhook_verify_token)
            .then(r => frappe.show_alert({
                message: 'Verify Token copied to clip board',
                indicator: "green"
            }, 2))
            .catch(r => frappe.show_alert({
                message: 'cannot copy to clip board',
                indicator: "red"
            }, 5))
    },
    copy_webhook_link_to_clipboard: (frm) => {
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(frm.whatsapp_webhook_url)
            .then(r => frappe.show_alert({
                message: 'Webhook Url copied to clip board',
                indicator: "green"
            }, 2))
            .catch(r => frappe.show_alert({
                message: 'cannot copy to clip board',
                indicator: "red"
            }, 5))
    }
});

import frappe

@frappe.whitelist()
def get_print_formats(doctype):
    values = {
        'doc_type': doctype,
    }
    nodes = frappe.db.sql("""
    SELECT *
        FROM `tabPrint Format`
        WHERE `doc_type` = %(doc_type)s
            and disabled = "0"

    """, values=values, as_dict=1)
    return nodes

  refresh: function (frm) {
    frm.add_custom_button("Refresh", function () {

        method: 'nextai.whatsapp_business_api_integration.doctype.whatsapp_template.whatsapp_template.fetchTemplate',
        args: {
