        },
        callback: function (res) {
          frappe.msgprint(res.message)
          frm.refresh()
        }
      });
    });
  }
});

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/whatsapp_business_api_integration/doctype/whatsapp_template/whatsapp_template.py
# Copyright (c) 2023, Hybrowlabs Technologies and contributors
# For license information, please see license.txt

import json
import mimetypes
import re

import frappe
import requests
from frappe.desk.form.utils import get_pdf_link
from frappe.integrations.utils import make_post_request, make_request
from frappe.model.document import Document

from nextai.whatsapp_business_api_integration.doctype.whatsapp_message.whatsapp_message import META_WA_API_URL


class WhatsAppTemplate(Document):
    """Create whatsapp template."""

    def validate(self):
        name = self.name or self.template_name
        if not re.compile("^([_a-z]{1,})([_a-z0-9]*)$").match(name):
            raise Exception("Template name must contain only lowercase letters, underscore and numbers only")

        if self.template:
            template_placeholders = re.findall(r'\{\{\d+\}\}', self.template)
            template_values = self.sample_values.split(
                ",") if self.sample_values else []

            if template_placeholders and not self.sample_values:
                raise Exception("Please enter a valid sample value")

            if len(template_placeholders) != len(template_values):
                raise Exception(
                    "The number of sample values does not match the number of placeholders")
        else:
            frappe.throw("Please provide a template value")

    def after_insert(self):
        """Set template code."""
        self.template_name = self.template_name.lower().replace(' ', '_')
        self.language_code = frappe.db.get_value(
            "Language", self.language
        ).replace('-', '_')

        self.get_settings()
        data = {
            "name": self.template_name,
            "language": self.language_code,
            "category": self.category,
            "components": []
        }

        body = {
            "type": "BODY",
            "text": self.template,
        }
        if self.sample_values:
            body.update({
                "example": {
                    "body_text": [self.sample_values.split(',')]
                }
            })

        data['components'].append(body)
        if self.header_type and self.header:
            data['components'].append(self.get_header())

        # add footer
        if self.footer:
            data['components'].append({
                "type": "FOOTER",
                "text": self.footer
            })

        if self.quick_reply_buttons:
            buttons = []
            for button in self.quick_reply_buttons:
                buttons.append(
                    {
                        "type": "QUICK_REPLY",
                        "text": button.button_text
                    }
                )

            data['components'].append({
                "type": "BUTTONS",
                "buttons": buttons
            })

        try:
            if self.header_type in ["DOCUMENT", "IMAGE", "VIDEO"]:
                sample_media = frappe.get_value(
                    "File", {"file_url": self.sample_media}, "name")
                sample_media_doc = frappe.get_doc("File", sample_media)
                mime_type, _ = mimetypes.guess_type(sample_media_doc.file_url)
                media_file_path = sample_media_doc.get_full_path()

                upload_url = f"{self._url}/{self._version}/{self._app_id}/uploads"
                session_data = {
                    'file_length': sample_media_doc.file_size,
                    'file_type': mime_type,
                    'access_token': self._token
                }

                session_id_res = make_post_request(
                    upload_url, data=session_data)
                session_id = session_id_res.get("id")

                if session_id:
                    media_files = {
                        "file": (
                            "file",
                            open(media_file_path, "rb"),
                            mime_type,
                        )
                    }
                    fb_graph_url = f"{self._url}/{self._version}/{session_id}"
                    response = requests.post(fb_graph_url, headers={
                        "Authorization": "OAuth " + self._token,
                    }, files=media_files)
                    if response.ok:
                        header_media = response.json().get("h")

                if header_media:
                    header_content = {
                        "type": "HEADER",
                        "format": self.header_type,
                        "example": {
                            "header_handle": [
                                header_media
                            ]
                        }
                    }
                    data['components'].append(header_content)

            response = make_post_request(
                f"{self._url}/{self._version}/{self._business_id}/message_templates",
                headers=self._headers, data=json.dumps(data)
            )
            self.id = response['id']
            self.status = response['status']
            self.save()

            # frappe.db.set_value("WhatsApp Template", self.name, "id", response['id'])
        except Exception as e:
            res = frappe.flags.integration_request.json()['error']
            error_message = res.get('error_user_msg', res.get("message"))
            frappe.throw(
                msg=error_message,
                title=res.get("error_user_title", "Error"),
            )

    def get_settings(self):
        """Get whatsapp settings."""
        settings = frappe.get_doc("Whatsapp Settings", "Whatsapp Settings")
        self._token = settings.get_password("access_token")
        self._url = "https://graph.facebook.com"
        self._version = "v18.0"
        self._business_id = settings.business_account_id
        self._app_id = settings.app_id

        self._headers = {
            "authorization": f"Bearer {self._token}",
            "content-type": "application/json"
        }

    def on_trash(self):
        self.get_settings()
        url = f'{META_WA_API_URL}/{self._business_id}/message_templates?name={self.name}'
        try:
            make_request("DELETE", url, headers=self._headers)
        except Exception:
            res = frappe.flags.integration_request.json()['error']
            if res.get("error_user_title") == "Message Template Not Found":
                frappe.msgprint("Deleted locally", res.get(
                    "error_user_title", "Error"), alert=True)
            else:
                frappe.throw(
                    msg=res.get("error_user_msg"),
                    title=res.get("error_user_title", "Error"),
                )

    def get_header(self):
        """Get header format."""
        header = {
            "type": "header",
            "format": self.header_type
        }
        if self.header_type == "TEXT":
            header['text'] = self.header

        else:
            if not self.sample:
                key = frappe.get_doc(
                    self.doctype, self.name).get_document_share_key()
                link = get_pdf_link(self.doctype, self.name)
                self.sample = f'{frappe.utils.get_url()}{link}&key={key}'
            header.update({"example": {
                "header_handle": [self.sample]
            }})

        return header


def create_or_update_template(template):
    # set flag to insert or update
    flags = 1
    if frappe.db.exists("WhatsApp Template", template['name']):
        doc = frappe.get_doc("WhatsApp Template", template['name'])
    else:
        flags = 0
        doc = frappe.new_doc("WhatsApp Template")
        doc.template_name = template['name']
        # saving here to ignore child table error
        doc.db_insert()
    doc.reload()
    doc.status = template['status']
    doc.language_code = template['language']
    doc.category = template['category']
    doc.id = template['id']

    doc.header_type = "NONE"
    # update components
    for component in template['components']:

        # update header
        if component['type'] == "HEADER":
            doc.header_type = component['format']

            # if format is text update sample text
            if component['format'] == 'TEXT':
                doc.header = component['text']
        # Update footer text
        elif component['type'] == 'FOOTER':
            doc.footer = component['text']

        elif component['type'] == "BUTTONS":
            for button in component['buttons']:
                filtered_buttons = [i for i in doc.quick_reply_buttons if i.button_text == button["text"]]
                if not len(filtered_buttons):
                    new_button_doc = frappe.new_doc(
                        "WhatsApp Template Quick Reply Button")
                    new_button_doc.button_text = button['text']
                    new_button_doc.parent = template['name']
                    new_button_doc.parentfield = 'quick_reply_buttons'
                    new_button_doc.parenttype = 'WhatsApp Template'
                    new_button_doc.insert()
        # update template text
        elif component['type'] == 'BODY':
            doc.template = component['text']
            if component.get('example'):
                doc.sample_values = ','.join(
                    component['example']['body_text'][0])

    # if document exists update else insert
    # used db_update to ignore hooks
    doc.db_update()
    frappe.db.commit()


@frappe.whitelist()
def fetch():
    """Fetch templates from meta."""

    # get credentials
    settings = frappe.get_doc("Whatsapp Settings", "Whatsapp Settings")
    token = settings.get_password("access_token")
    url = "https://graph.facebook.com"
    version = "v18.0"
    business_id = settings.business_account_id

    headers = {
        "authorization": f"Bearer {token}",
        "content-type": "application/json"
    }

    try:
        response = make_request(
            "GET",
            f"{url}/{version}/{business_id}/message_templates",
            headers=headers,
        )

        # Set the status of existing documents to "DELETED"
        frappe.db.sql("""
            UPDATE `tabWhatsApp Template`
            SET status = 'DELETED'
        """)

        for template in response['data']:
            create_or_update_template(template)

    except Exception as e:
        res = frappe.flags.integration_request.json()['error']
        error_message = res.get('error_user_msg', res.get("message"))
        frappe.throw(
            msg=error_message,
            title=res.get("error_user_title", "Error"),
        )

    return "Successfully fetched templates from meta"


@frappe.whitelist()
def fetchTemplate(template_name):
    """Fetch templates from meta."""

    # get credentials
    settings = frappe.get_doc("Whatsapp Settings", "Whatsapp Settings")
    token = settings.get_password("access_token")
    url = "https://graph.facebook.com"
    version = "v18.0"
    business_id = settings.business_account_id

    headers = {
        "authorization": f"Bearer {token}",
        "content-type": "application/json"
    }

    found = 0

    try:
        response = make_request(
            "GET",
            f"{url}/{version}/{business_id}/message_templates",
            headers=headers,
        )

        for template in response['data']:
            if template_name == template['name']:
                found = 1
                frappe.db.sql("""
                    UPDATE `tabWhatsApp Template`
                    SET status = 'DELETED'
                    where name = %(name)s
                """, values={"name": template_name})
                create_or_update_template(template)
                return "Successfully fetched template from meta"

        frappe.db.sql("""
            UPDATE `tabWhatsApp Template`
            SET status = 'DELETED'
            where name = %(name)s
        """, values={"name": template_name})
        return "This template not available in meta"

    except Exception as e:
        res = frappe.flags.integration_request.json()['error']
        error_message = res.get('error_user_msg', res.get("message"))
        frappe.throw(
            msg=error_message,
            title=res.get("error_user_title", "Error"),
        )

    return "Successfully fetched template from meta" if found == 1 else "This template not available in meta"

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/whatsapp_business_api_integration/overides/contact.py
from frappe.contacts.doctype.contact.contact import Contact
import frappe


class CustomContact(Contact):
    def mark_whatsapp_unregistered(self):
        # set as unregistered, this will be called from the send message api if the app fails to send message and error is because the contact is not registrered with whatsapp
        self.is_whatsapp_registered = False
        self.save(ignore_permissions=True)
        return

    def before_insert(self):
        if self.whatsapp_id:
            new_phone_object = {
                'name': frappe.generate_hash('Contact Phone'),
                'owner': self.owner,
                'creation': frappe.utils.now(),
                'modified': frappe.utils.now(),
                'modified_by': self.modified_by,
                'docstatus': 0,
                'idx': 1,
                'phone': "+" + self.whatsapp_id,
                'is_primary_phone': 1,
                'is_primary_mobile_no': 1,
                'parent': self.name,
                'parentfield': 'phone_nos',
                'parenttype': 'Contact',
                'doctype': 'Contact Phone'
            }

            self.append('phone_nos', new_phone_object)
        else:
            try:
               if self.phone_nos and len(self.phone_nos) > 0:
                 primary_phone_object = next((phone_obj for phone_obj in self.phone_nos if phone_obj.get('is_primary_phone') == 1), None)

                 if primary_phone_object:
                  phone_number = primary_phone_object.get('phone')
                  phone = str(phone_number).lstrip('+')

                  if len(phone) == 10:
                    phone = '91' + phone
                  self.whatsapp_id = phone

            except: pass


    def after_insert(self):
        self.update_personal_type()
        self.update_is_whatsapp_registered()
        self.update_whatsapp_id()


    def update_whatsapp_id(self):
        try:
            phone = str(self.phone).lstrip('+')
            if not self.whatsapp_id and phone and len(phone) >= 10:
                if len(phone) == 10:
                    phone = '91' + phone
                self.whatsapp_id = phone
                self.save()
        except: pass

    def on_update(self):
        self.update_personal_type()
        self.update_is_whatsapp_registered()

    def update_is_whatsapp_registered(self):
        if self.whatsapp_id:
            if self.is_whatsapp_registered:
                return
            self.is_whatsapp_registered = True
            self.save()
            return
        if not self.is_whatsapp_registered:
            return

        self.is_whatsapp_registered = False
        self.save()
        return

    def update_personal_type(self):
        links = []
        for link in self.links:
            if link.link_doctype not in links:
                links.append(link.link_doctype)

        persona_type = ", ".join(links)
        if persona_type == self.persona_type:
            return
        self.db_set('persona_type', persona_type)

    def send_whatsapp_message(self, template, payload):
        return

    def send_email(self, email_template, payload):
        return

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/www/cn-asset/chatnext/attach.py
import json
from os import path
from urllib.parse import urlparse

import frappe


def get_context(context):
    json_file_path = path.join(
        path.dirname(__file__), '../../../..', "package.json"
    )
    f = open(json_file_path, "r")
    package_json_data = f.read()
    f.close()

    package_json_dict = json.loads(package_json_data)
    chatnext_ui_version = package_json_dict.get("dependencies", {}).get("chatnext-ui", {})

    request_url = frappe.request.url
    url = urlparse(request_url)

    chatnext_app_origin = url.scheme + '://' + url.hostname
    if url.port:
        chatnext_app_origin += ':' + str(url.port)

    context['chatnext_ui_version'] = chatnext_ui_version
    context['chatnext_app_origin'] = chatnext_app_origin

    return context

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/www/email/image.py
import json
from datetime import datetime

import frappe


def get_context(context):
    request_url = frappe.request.url
    request_query = request_url.split('?')[1]

    mi_map_doc = frappe.get_doc('CN Email Image Map', {'image_id': request_query})
    open_timestamps = mi_map_doc.get("open_timestamps", "[]")
    open_timestamps = json.loads(open_timestamps)
    open_timestamps.append(datetime.strftime(frappe.utils.get_datetime(), '%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S'))
    mi_map_doc.open_timestamps = json.dumps(open_timestamps, default=str)
    mi_map_doc.view_count = len(open_timestamps)
    mi_map_doc.save(ignore_permissions=True)
    comm_name = mi_map_doc.get('communication')
    communication_doc = frappe.get_doc('Communication', comm_name)
    communication_doc.read_by_recipient = 1
    communication_doc.save(ignore_permissions=True)
    frappe.db.commit()

    return {}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/nextai/nextai/www/mobile/login.py
# Copyright (c) 2015, Frappe Technologies Pvt. Ltd. and Contributors
# License: MIT. See LICENSE

import frappe
import frappe.utils
from frappe import _
from frappe.auth import LoginManager
from frappe.rate_limiter import rate_limit
from frappe.utils import cint, get_url
from frappe.utils.data import escape_html
from frappe.utils.html_utils import get_icon_html
from frappe.utils.jinja import guess_is_path
from frappe.utils.oauth import get_oauth2_authorize_url, get_oauth_keys, redirect_post_login
from frappe.utils.password import get_decrypted_password
from frappe.website.utils import get_home_page

no_cache = True


def get_context(context):
	redirect_to = frappe.local.request.args.get("redirect-to")

	if frappe.session.user != "Guest":
		if not redirect_to:
			if frappe.session.data.user_type == "Website User":
				redirect_to = get_home_page()
			else:
				redirect_to = "/mobile#"

		if redirect_to != "login":
			frappe.local.flags.redirect_location = redirect_to
			raise frappe.Redirect

	context.no_header = True
	context.for_test = "login.html"
	context["title"] = "Login"
	context["hide_login"] = True  # dont show login link on login page again.
	context["provider_logins"] = []
	context["disable_signup"] = cint(frappe.get_website_settings("disable_signup"))
	context["disable_user_pass_login"] = cint(frappe.get_system_settings("disable_user_pass_login"))
	context["logo"] = frappe.get_website_settings("app_logo") or frappe.get_hooks("app_logo_url")[-1]
	context["app_name"] = (
		frappe.get_website_settings("app_name") or frappe.get_system_settings("app_name") or _("Frappe")
	)

	signup_form_template = frappe.get_hooks("signup_form_template")
	if signup_form_template and len(signup_form_template):
		path = signup_form_template[-1]
		if not guess_is_path(path):
			path = frappe.get_attr(signup_form_template[-1])()
	else:
		path = "frappe/templates/signup.html"

	if path:
		context["signup_form_template"] = frappe.get_template(path).render()

	providers = frappe.get_all(
		"Social Login Key",
		filters={"enable_social_login": 1},
		fields=["name", "client_id", "base_url", "provider_name", "icon"],
		order_by="name",
	)

	for provider in providers:
		client_secret = get_decrypted_password("Social Login Key", provider.name, "client_secret")
		if not client_secret:
			continue

		icon = None
		if provider.icon:
			if provider.provider_name == "Custom":
				icon = get_icon_html(provider.icon, small=True)
			else:
				icon = f"<img src={escape_html(provider.icon)!r} alt={escape_html(provider.provider_name)!r}>"

		if provider.client_id and provider.base_url and get_oauth_keys(provider.name):
			context.provider_logins.append(
				{
					"name": provider.name,
					"provider_name": provider.provider_name,
					"auth_url": get_oauth2_authorize_url(provider.name, redirect_to),
					"icon": icon,
				}
			)
			context["social_login"] = True

	if cint(frappe.db.get_value("LDAP Settings", "LDAP Settings", "enabled")):
		from frappe.integrations.doctype.ldap_settings.ldap_settings import LDAPSettings

		context["ldap_settings"] = LDAPSettings.get_ldap_client_settings()

	login_label = [_("Email")]

	if frappe.utils.cint(frappe.get_system_settings("allow_login_using_mobile_number")):
		login_label.append(_("Mobile"))

	if frappe.utils.cint(frappe.get_system_settings("allow_login_using_user_name")):
		login_label.append(_("Username"))

	context["login_label"] = f" {_('or')} ".join(login_label)

	context["login_with_email_link"] = frappe.get_system_settings("login_with_email_link")

	return context


@frappe.whitelist(allow_guest=True)
def login_via_token(login_token: str):
	sid = frappe.cache.get_value(f"login_token:{login_token}", expires=True)
	if not sid:
		frappe.respond_as_web_page(_("Invalid Request"), _("Invalid Login Token"), http_status_code=417)
		return

	frappe.local.form_dict.sid = sid
	frappe.local.login_manager = LoginManager()

	redirect_post_login(
		desk_user=frappe.db.get_value("User", frappe.session.user, "user_type") == "System User"
	)


@frappe.whitelist(allow_guest=True)
@rate_limit(limit=5, seconds=60 * 60)
def send_login_link(email: str):

	if not frappe.get_system_settings("login_with_email_link"):
		return

	expiry = frappe.get_system_settings("login_with_email_link_expiry") or 10
	link = _generate_temporary_login_link(email, expiry)

	app_name = (
		frappe.get_website_settings("app_name") or frappe.get_system_settings("app_name") or _("Frappe")
	)

	subject = _("Login To {0}").format(app_name)

	frappe.sendmail(
		subject=subject,
		recipients=email,
		template="login_with_email_link",
		args={"link": link, "minutes": expiry, "app_name": app_name},
		now=True,
	)


def _generate_temporary_login_link(email: str, expiry: int):
	assert isinstance(email, str)

	if not frappe.db.exists("User", email):
		frappe.throw(
			_("User with email address {0} does not exist").format(email), frappe.DoesNotExistError
		)
	key = frappe.generate_hash()
	frappe.cache.set_value(f"one_time_login_key:{key}", email, expires_in_sec=expiry * 60)

	return get_url(f"/api/method/frappe.www.login.login_via_key?key={key}")


@frappe.whitelist(allow_guest=True, methods=["GET"])
@rate_limit(limit=5, seconds=60 * 60)
def login_via_key(key: str):
	cache_key = f"one_time_login_key:{key}"
	email = frappe.cache.get_value(cache_key)

	if email:
		frappe.cache.delete_value(cache_key)

		frappe.local.login_manager.login_as(email)

		redirect_post_login(
			desk_user=frappe.db.get_value("User", frappe.session.user, "user_type") == "System User"
		)
	else:
		frappe.respond_as_web_page(
			_("Not Permitted"),
			_("The link you trying to login is invalid or expired."),
			http_status_code=403,
			indicator_color="red",
		)
